śrīvāsudevaparabrajhaṇe namaḥ
śrīvyāsapāṇinibhāvanirṇayaḥ
sanīranīradaprakhyaṃ saccidānandavigraham |
ramāramaṇamīśeśaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ samupāsmahe || 1 ||
jñānamudrāñcitakaraṃ nṛsiṃhārya karā'rcitam |
vyāsaṃ vandārumandāraṃ vande'haṃ buddhiśuddaye || 2 ||
pāṇinyādigurūn vande śabdasāstrapravartakān |
yeṣāṃ sūktiḥ pūrūṣāṇāṃ vāṅamalāni cikitsati || 3 ||
śabdābdhikumbhayoniṃ gautamarāṇādatantraniṣṇātam |
mīmāṃsādvayanipuṇaṃ naumi śrīsubbarāyaguruvaryam || 4 ||
śrīsetumādhavābhikhyaḥ sudhīsteṣāṃ girāṃ smaran |
kurve śrīvyāsapāṇinyorṛṣyorbhāvavinirṇayam || 5 ||
vāsudevaḥ paraṃ tattvamiti vyāsaśca pāṇiniḥ |
abhipraitīti saṃkṣipya sapramāṇamihocyate || 6 ||
granthanirmāṇahetunirūpaṇam
sa bhavān maharṣiḥ pāṇinirācāryaścirakālaṃ tapaścaraṇena prasāditānmaheśvarāt akṣarasamāmnāyamadhigamya sarveṣāṃ pramāṇabhūto darbhapavitrapāṇiḥ śucāvavakse prāṅamukha upaviśaaya mahatā manaḥpraṇidhānena `vṛddirādaic' ityādīni `a a' ityantāni praṇīya puṃsāṃ vāṅamalaṃ vidhūnayan śabdāpaśabdavivekakhyātimudapādayat | uktaṃ hi śikṣāyām--
yenā'kṣarasamāmnāyamadhigamya maheśvarāt |
kṛtsnaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ proktaṃ tasmai pāṇinaye namaḥ ||
yena dhaukā giraḥ puṃsāṃ vimalaiḥ śabdavāribhiḥ |
tamaśvājñānajaṃ bhinnaṃ tasmai pāṇinaye namaḥ || iti
idaṃ ca sārvalaukikam | idamīdṛśaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ vākyavākyārthopayogi, padapadārthāvagatisādhanaṃ sarvapuruṣārthasādhanaparūṣamāhātmya parijñānahetubhūtavedārthavicāre phradhānamaṅgam | uktaṃ hi mahābhāṣye paspaśāhrike--`pradhānaṃ ca ṣaḍaṅgeṣu vyākaraṇam' iti |
etādṛśena śāstreṇa pāṇininā kṛtaṃ śabdavyutpādanamavāstavamtismiṃśca vāstavatvadhiyā prakṛtipratyayārthavicāre pravṛttānāṃ pareṣāṃ vaidikānāṃ laukikānāṃ ca bhrāntatvaṃ ca vaktuṃ vipaścicaḥ kasyacidapi vāṇī sahasā na pravarteta | pradīpa-kaustubha-vaiyākaraṇasiddhāntakārikāvaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇa-tatsāra-mañjūṣādiprabandhapraṇetārastu anena mahatā śāstreṇa pāṇiniryadakarocchabdavyutpādanaṃ tadavāsnavam | etacca vāstavasya brajhā'bhidheyasya sphoṭasya pratipattaye upāyo bhavati, `asatye vartmani sthitvā tataḥ satyaṃ samīhate' ityasatyasya satyavastuprāptyupāyatvokteḥ | tataśca, śabdabrajhākhyaḥ sphoṭa eka eva satyaḥ sa eva tu vācyo vācakaśca--
śakyatva iva śaktatve jātersāghavamīyuṣām |
aupādhiko vā bhedo'stu varṇānāṃ tāramandavat ||
ityukteḥ | tadanyannāmarūpātmakaṃ tu sarvaṃ jagadasatyam (māyikaṃ) iti | avāstaveṃ ca pāṇinīye vyutpādane vāstavatvābhiprāyeṇa prakṛtipratya yārthavicāre pravṛttā bhrāntā iti ca |
phaṇibhāṣitabhāṣyābdheḥ śabdakaustubha uddhataḥ |
tatra nirṇīta evārtaḥ saṃkṣepeṇeha kathyate ||
vaiyākaraṇasiddhāntamañjūṣaiṣā viracyate |
iti pratiśrutya, mahābhāṣyodāharaṇapuraṃ svasvagrantheṣu vipuṇaṃ nirūpayanti sma | yadyapi eteṣu ca grantheṣu bahavaḥ prakārabhedā upalabhyante tathāpi sphoṭasya paramārthatve tadanyasya cātathātve, sarva ete sānakaṇṭhā eva | astu tāvat teṣāṃ tathā'bhiprāyaḥ, tāvatā kā hānrtareṣām, svābhāvikametat, granthakṛtāṃ yat svasvagranteṣu svasvābhimatasyārthasya varṇanamiti | satyam, maiṃvamuccairvocaḥ nāsti hāniriti | saṃbhavedeva mahānanartha ācāryāṇāṃ nindāvacanaśravaṇādau | ataeva ākasmikagurūnindāśraṇādau karṇapidhānādi vidadhatā manunā ācāryanindāśravaṇameva na kāryamiti bodhitam--
guroryatra parīvādo nindāvā'pi pravartate |
karṇau tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato'nyataḥ (2.200) ||
iti |
ācāryanindāśravaṇādiranarthahetuḥ, pratividhātavyamavaśyamatreti tadbhāvaḥ | pratividhānaṃ ca gurornindanasyā''cāryabhaktimatāṃ sarveṣāṃ svasvakṛnasya maivaṃ bhāṣitavyamiti śikṣaṇaṃ na sukaramadyatanaiḥ, viphalaṃ ca tādṛśaṃ prativacanam | adyatve tu anuktamācāryairanabhimataṃ ca teṣāmarthaṃ svasvagrantheṣu nirūpayantaḥ `avāstavameva prakṛtyādyarthamācāryā avocan' iti bruvāṇā arvācīnā nāgośādaya evāvāstavaṃ bhāṣante, na tu ācāryāḥ pāṇinyādayaḥ, te tu vāstavamevārtha prāhuriti pratipādanameva pratividhānamiti pratisandhāya `bahūnāṃ janmanām' iti bhagavadvacanāditātparyavivaraṇamukhena, vyāsasya sūtrakṛtpāṇineścābhimataparatattvasvarūpamidaṃ, tadabhīprāyaścāyamiti sahṛdayānāstikajanānāvedayitumāsmākīno'yamudyamaḥ |
bhāratarāmāyaṇādīnāṃ vāsudevaparatvanirūpaṇam
nanu gītāyāṃ satsu anekeṣu ślokoṣu asya ślokasya vyākhyānaṃ kasmātkāraṇātprastūyate | ucyate-atra mañjūṣāyāṃ nāgeśaḥ ahaṃ mametyadhyāsa evāvidyā `vāsudevaḥ sarvam' iti vidyāyāṃ tasyaiva virodhitvāt iti vadan, sarvasya vāsudevātmakatvavijñānameva ajñānanivartakaṃ samīcīnaṃ jñānamiti gītāvacanatātparyamāviṣkurvan, sarvaśāstrāṇāmakraiva tātparyamiti prapañcayan, sarvasya māyikatvaṃ (mithyātvaṃ) nirūpayati | tadidaṃ sarvamapāṇinīyaṃ tena tatānukteḥ, `vāsudevārjunābhyāṃ vun' (pā.sū.4.3.95) `a a' ( pā.sū.8.4.68 ) iti sūtradvayaṃ kurvataḥ sūtrakṛto'nabhimatamityavagamācceti nirūpaṇaṃ tāvatprāptāvasaramiti niścinvānasya mama prathamaṃ smṛtipathamārūḍho'yaṃ ślokaḥ, ataḥ saṃgatatvācca ādāvasya prastāvaḥ | ślokārthavivaraṇottaraṃ sūtrakārasya pāṇinerabhiprāyo vivariṣyata iti |
ślooko'yaṃ mahābhārate bhīṣmabhārate bhīṣmaparvaṇi gītāparvaṇi saptame'dhyāye vartate | idaṃ ca mahābhāratamākhyānamitihāsaratnaṃ sarvavedārthasaṃbhṛtaṃ sarvaśāstropabṛṃhaṇabhūtaṃ bhagavato vāsudevasya māhātmyaṃ pratipādayati prādhānyeneti avipratipannam | tadidamākhyānaṃ śrīmannārāyaṇo bhagavān karuṇāvaruṇālayo'sādhujanasaṃsargādinā vimūḍhacetasaḥ saṃsāre kliśyamānān puṃsaḥ kālato jñānahīnān anujighṛkṣuḥ satyavatyāṃ parāśarāt vyāsarūpeṇāvataran araracat | tatra-
vede rāmāyaṇe puṇye bhārate bharatarṣabha |
ādāvante ca madhye ca hariḥ sarvatra gīyate ||
bhagavān vāsudevaśca kīrtyate'tra sanātanaḥ |
sa hi satyamṛtaṃ caiva pavitraṃ puṇyameva ca ||
(bhārao 1.1.282)
vāsudevasya māhātmyaṃ pāṇḍavānāṃ ca satyatām |
durvṛttaṃ dhārtarāṣṭrāṇāmuktavān bhagavānṛṣiḥ ||
(bhārao 1.1.76)
ityadibhiḥ sarvavilakṣaṇo harīreva pratipādyata iti svagranthapratipādyaṃ svayameva bhagavānmaharṣiḥ prāha | gītāyāṃ ca bhagavān vāsudeva eva purāṇapurūṣottamaḥ sarvaśrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇarūpapkamāṇapratipādyaguṇaniśiṣṭaḥ bravate | yaiśca saptabhiḥ ślokaśataiḥ śrīkṛṣṇo vasudevanandanaḥ pārthaya tattvamupādiśat tadeva tattvam atra bhārate upanibabandha bādarāyaṇaḥ |
tatra bhāratādau vistareṇa nirūpitaṃ bhagavato mahimānaṃ samāsena nirūpasitukāmaḥ śrīvyāsaḥ bhaktirasabhūyiṣṭhaṃ bhagavadguṇaratnaratnākaraṃ bhāgavataṃ nāma purāṇaratnamakarot | tatra ca--
bhaktyā tvananyayā śak‌ya ahamevaṃvidho'rjana |
jñātaṃ draṣṭuṃ ca tattvena praveṣṭuṃ ca parantapa || (gī. 11.54)
ityuktadiśā abhīṣṭasiddhau bhaktiḥ paramasādhanamiti niścitya tayā śriyaḥpatyarbhagavato nārāyaṇasya pādāravindaprapadanamīptitasarvavidhasukhasādhanamiti sodāharaṇamāha | tathā hi--
natāḥsma te deva padāravindaṃ prapannatāpopaśamātapatram |
yanmūlaketā yatayo'ñjasoru saṃsāraduḥkhaṃ bahirutkṣipanti ||
(bhāga. 3.6.17)
dhyeyaṃ sadā paribhavaghramabhīṣaṭadohaṃ
tīrthāspadaṃ śivaviriñjanutaṃ śaraṇyam |
bhṛtyārtihaṃ praṇatapālabhavābdhipotaṃ
vandāmahe puruṣa te caraṇāravindam || (bhā. 11.5)
namo namaste'khilajñatantave sthtau gṛhītā'malasattvamūrtaye |
diṣṭyā hato'yaṃ jagatāmaruntudastvatpādabhaktyā vayamīśa nirvṛtāḥ ||(bhāga.3.20)
iti |
ācāryā api taddeśīyā arvācīnāḥ svottamaviṣayamanovṛttiranurākābhidhā bhaktiriti lakṣitabhaktisvarūpaṃ yuktamiti manyamānāḥ bhagavadbhaktisvarūpaṃ
snehānubandho yastasmin bahumānapuraḥsaraḥ |
bhaktinityucyate saiva karaṇaṃ paramīśituḥ ||
ityanyatra bhagavaduktadiśā anitarasādhāraṇamāhātmyajñānapūrvakāvicchinnabhagavadviṣayakapremapravāhaḥ, `upāsyaḥ phalapradaśca devo nānyo'sti vāsudevāt' iti, ` ananyasādhāraṇaguṇavattvena bhāvanā' iti ca śāstreṣu vyavaharantaḥ ananyabhaktyā bhagavadupāsanaṃ phalasādhanamiti śāstratattvaṃ siddhāntaytvā tameva śāstrārthaṃ śāsati sādhūn |
saiṣā bhaktirna sulabhā sarvaiḥ, kiṃ tu vihitasvakarmānuṣṭhānena prakṣīṇapāpaiḥ śuddhāntaḥkaraṇaiḥ śamādisaṃpannairadhītavidyaireva | `sve sve karmaṇyabhirataḥ saṃsiddhiṃ sabhate naraḥ' (gī.1.45) `śāntodāntaḥuparataḥ titikṣuḥ samāhito bhūtvā ātmānyevātmānaṃ paśyet' (bṛ.u.6.4.22) `parīkṣya lokānaṃ karmacitān brajhaṇoḥ śrotriyaṃ brajhaniṣaṭham' (ā.2.12) `ācāryavān puruṣo veda' (chā. 6.1.4.2) `tadviddhi praṇipātena paripraśnena sevayā' (gī.4.34) `ātāryopāsanaṃ caiva' (gī.13.7) iti gītādiṣīkteḥ | tatra phrathamaṃ karma sandhyāvandanam | tadakaraṇa sarvakarmasvanadhikāra eva |
sandhyāhīno'śucirnityamanarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
yatkiṃcitkurute karma na tasya phalabhāgbhavet ||
at smṛtyupanyāsena prāyaścittaprakaraṇe vijñāneśvareṇa vyavasthāpitātvāt |
evaṃ kṛtopakāro'pi bhagavān vyāsaḥ paramadayāluḥ ananantāśca vedāḥ, sapādalakṣagranthaṃ ca bhāratam, aṣṭādaśahastragranthaṃ śrībhāgavatam, itarāṇi ca purāṇān bahūni amitagranthāni kathametāni mitāyuṣo'lpaśaktayaḥ prāṇinaḥ pratyahaṃ vimṛśya bhagavadguṇān anusaṃdhātuṃ samarthā bhaveyuriti samālocya, nikhilāgamasārasaṃgrahamanaśyamanisaṃdheyabhagavatsaṃbandhijagatkartṛtvādividhavicitratakaguṇapradhānaṃ brajhasūtranāmadheyaṃ mahāśāstramatanot | tadidaṃ tasya nāmānvarthakam, tathā hi-vedatadanusāriśrutītihārasapurāṇapratipāditajagajjanmādikāraṇatnādyanantakalyāṇaguṇaniśiṣṭaśrīmannārāyaṇaparaṃ brajhapadam, sūtrapadaṃ tu
alpākṣaramasaṃdigdhaṃ sāravadviśvatomukham |
astobhamanavajyaṃ ca sūtra sūtravido viduḥ ||
iti niruktalakṣaṇopetaśāstraparam | tataśca, anitarasādhāraṇaniruktā'vadyā'nantaguṇaniśiṣaṭanārāyaṇavāsudevādipadā 'bhidheyaparabrajhapratipādakaṃ sūtramiti tadarthaḥ atha vā brajha vedaḥ, tena tadartho grājhaḥ, tannirṇāyakaṃ sūtramityartaḥ | vedārtaśca uktaguṇakiśiṣṭo vāsudevaḥ | astyasya vedāntaśāstraṃ, vedāntasūtramityapi vyavahāraḥ | vedaśabdo vedārtaparaḥ, antaśabdo nirṇayārthakaḥ `ubhayorapi dṛṣṭo'ntaḥ' (go.2.17)ityatrā'ntaśabdasya nirṇāyārthakatvena sarvairaṅaagīkārāt | tataścavedasya vedārthasya, anto nirṇayaḥ, yasminniti bahuvrihiḥ, tataḥ śāstarapadena sūtrapadena ca karmadhākayaḥ | vedārthanirṇāyakaṃ śāstramityādirarthaḥ | vedārthastu ukto vāsudeva eva |
asyaiva brajhaṇo vāsudevasya jijñāsā kāryeti `tadvijijñāsasva' (tai.u.3.1)ityādiśrutiḥ, `athāto brajhajijñāsā' (bra.sū.1.11) itisūtraṃ ca vidhatte iti sarve bhāṣyakārāstadanuyāyinaśca ācakṣate | śrīkaṇṭhācāryastu śivo jijñāsya iti bravīti | kecit nārāyaṇasya sarvootkarṣaṃ brajhasūtrādisarvaśāstrādipradipādyatvaṃ ca nirūpayanto bādarāyaṇīrṣādi pratipādayanta idaṃ śāstraṃ bhedaparamiti vadanti | anye tu nārāyaṇaḥ sarvādhyakṣaḥ parā devatā sarvajagatkāraṇatvādinā idamādisarvaśāstrapratipādyaśceti sapramāṇaṃ varmayanta idaṃ śāstraṃ bhedaparamiti saṃgirante | itare tu kaṃcitkaṃcidviṣayaviśeṣaṃ darśayantaḥ svasvābhimatabhedābhedādiparamidaṃ śāstramityāhuḥ |
idamevaṃvidhamapi nā'sahāyaṃ vedārthaṃ nirṇetuṃ kṣamate, api tu upabṛṃhaṇasahitameva, kiṃ tadupabṛṃhaṇamiti cet, itihāsapurāṇāni | uktaṃ hi bhagavatā vyāsena--
itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ vedaṃ samu,bṛṃhayet |
bibhetyalpaśrutādvedo māmayaṃ pratariṣyati || (bhāra. 1.1.213)
iti | ata eva `smṛteśca' ityādīni smṛtipadaghaṭitāni bahūni sūtrāṇi praṇītāni bhagavatā vyāsena | atra vedetyupalakṣaṇaṃ tadanusāriṇo'sya śāstrasyāpi | tataśca etadarthanirṇayo'pi itihāsāriṇonaiva kāryaḥ | ata eva śrīśaṅkarācāryādayo bhāṣyakārāḥ mahābhāratādigatacanāni adhikaraṇārthaṃ nirṇetumupodbalakayopanyalasyanti | tatra śrīśāṅkarabhāṣaaye smṛtyadhikaraṇe (bra.sū.2.1.1) śrīmadrāmānuja śrīmatpūrṇaprajñavijñānabhikṣubhāskarācāryaiḥ bhāṣyeṣu tatra, anyatra cādhikaraṇe udāhṛtāni bahūni purāṇavacanāni |
bhāṣyodāhṛtapramāṇānāmanupalambhe hetunirūpaṇam
nanu tattadbhāṣyakārairudāhṛteṣu purāṇavacaneṣu kānicdupalabhyante ākare, kānicinnopalabhyante, ato'tra viśvāsaḥ kathaṃ bhavet ? ucyateākare'dyatve'nupalambhe bhāṣyakārāḥ kiṃ kuryuḥ | te tu tadātve purāṇādāvupalabdhānyeva vacanāni saṃpratyanupalambhastu lekhane śodhane ca karmaṇi labdhādhikāraṇāṃ viduṣāṃ svasvābhimatamatābhiniśamūlakaḥ, tetu tatratatra vidyamānāni vacanāni svasvābhimatamatānanukūlāni iti matvā tāni pratyāvya, anukūlāni vacanāni kānicidracayitvā prakṣipanti | kvacidvidyamānāni pracyāvayanti, kvacidadhikāni yoojayanti | kvacidvidyamānāni pracyāvayanti, kvacidadhikāni yojayanti | ata eva bhāratarāmāyaṇādigrantheṣu kvacitipāṭhabhedaḥ, kvacit granthapātaḥ, kvacit adhikapāṭhaśca dṛśyate | ata evoktamabhiyuktaiḥ `kvacidgranthān prakṣipanti kvacindantaritānapi' iti | idamatra prathamamudāharaṇam--śrīmanmahābhārate ādiparvaṇi dvitīye adhyāye āśvamedhikaparvasaṃgnahe--`sudarśanaṃ tathākhyānaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ dharmameva ca' iti kuṃbhaghoṇanagaravāsinā ṭi.ār. kagṛṣṇācāryeṇa nirṇayasāgakamudraṇālaye mudrāpite bhāratapustake pāṭhaḥ. tathā tatraiva puktake adhyāyādisaṃkhyābodhakaḥ
adhyāyānāṃ śataṃ caiva trayo'dhyāyāśca kīrtitāḥ |
trīṇi ślokasahastrāṇi tāvantyeva śatāni ca |
viṃśatisca tathā ślokāḥ saṃkhyātāstattvadarśinā ||
(bhāra.1.2.345)
iti adhyardhaśloko dṛśaayate | tatraiva pustake āśvamedhike parvaṇi tu aṣṭādaśādhikaśatādhyāyā dṛśyante | parvasaṃgnahe nirdiṣṭasaṃkhaayātaḥ pañcadaśa adhyāyā adhikāḥsanti | anyena kenaciddākṣiṇātyena devanāgaralipibhirmudrāpite bhāratapustake - `maṇidarśanaṃ tathākhyānaṃ vai...' `aśvamedhe maruddarśanamatraiva vai'...

atrādhyāyaśataṃ rtriṃśat trayo'dhyāyāśca śabditāḥ |
catvāriṃśatsahastrāṇi tāvantyeva śatāni ca ||
iti viṣayasaṃgrahaḥ, adhyāyādisaṅkhyābodhakaślookāśca dṛśyante | evameva kuṃbhaghoṇanarasamīpasthatirunāgeśvaranāmakagrāmavāstavyena navanītaṃ kṛṣṇācāryeṇa āśvamedhikaparvaṇi adhyāyānāṃ trayastriṃśadadhikaśatena bhāvyam | saṃpūrṇatadbhāratakośasyānupalambhādadhaayāyānāṃ nyūnādhikabhāvo na pradarśitaḥ | muṃbayīnagarasthagaṇapatimudrālaye mudritanīlakaṇṭhīyavyākhyāsahitabhāratakośe tu `sadarśanam', ityādi ślokārthaṃ nāstyeva | etadarthakaślokāntaramapi nāsti | prathamamudāhṛtabhāratapustaka iva `adhyāyānām' ityādyadhyardhaślokaḥ parvasaṃgrahe vartate | āśvamedhikaparvaṇi tu nakulopākhyānaparyantaḥ kathābhāgaḥ, dvinavadiradhyāyāśvadṛśyante, tasminneva kośe parvaṃsaṃgrahaprakaraṇapaṭhitādhyardhaślokarītyā ekādaśa adhyāyā nyūnāḥ . nanu tatra parvaṇi sa bhāgo netyeveti brūma iti vacanaṃ tu na śraddheyam | yatastatratyāni vacanāni pramāṇatayodāharanti abhiyuktāḥ | tasmāt saṃprati ākareṣvadarśanamātreṇa nāviśaavāsaḥ kāryo bhāṣyakārodāhṛtavacaneṣu | ata eva mahābhāṣaaye parapaśāhrike `evaṃ hi śrūyata' ityupakramya `bṛhaspatirindrāya givyaṃ varṣasahastraṃ pratipadoktānāṃ śabdānāṃ śabdapārāyaṇaṃ provāca nāntaṃ jagāma' ityudāhṛte, tatraiva ṣaṣṭheṃ `ekaḥ pūrvaparayoḥ' iti sūtre `ekaḥ śabdaḥ samyagjñātaḥ śāstrānvitaḥ suprayuktaḥ, svarge loke kāmadhugbhavati' `vidyā ha vai brājhaṇamājagāma gopāya mā śevadhiṣṭe'hamasmi | asūyakāyānṛjave'yatāya na mā brūyā vīryavatī tathā syām' iti yāskodāhṛte ca pramāṇe kaścidapi vipaścit sandihānaḥ kvāsti pramāṇamidamiti na pṛcchati, ākareṣu anupalambhādapramāṇamidamiti ca na brūte | nanu patañjaliprabhṛtayo maharṣayo bahujñā nāvidyāmānaṃ brūyuriti nirdhārayāma iti ced, hanta tarhi brajhamīmāyābhāṣyakārāḥ parāparatattvajñaśiromaṇayo bahujñatamāḥ caturvidhapuruṣārthasādhanam adhyātmaśāltraṃ vyākhyāya paratattvasvarūpopadeṣṭāro nāvidyamānaṃ brūyuriti sudṛḍhaṃ nirdhāryatām | alamanena prāsaṃgikena |
bhāratarāmāyaṇayorviṣṇuparatve ānandavardhanācāryasaṃmatiḥ
mahābhārataṃ gītā ca vāsudevasya mahimānamācaṣṭa iti purastādavocāma | mahimā ca vicitrataravividhaprapañcanirmāṇasaṃrakṣaṇādisāmarthyāparaparyāyānanyasādhāraṇī śaktiḥ | tadidaṃ mahābhāratādi kecidarvācīnā dhvanimārgamavalambhamānā vāsudevādanyaparaṃ manyante | dhvanimārgaṃ sthāpayitāro hi cirantanāḥ paṇḍitapravarāstu vāsudevaparamiti siddhāntayanti | tatra ānandavardhanācāryoo dhvanyāloke caturtho dyote savimarśamitthamabhihitavān | tathā hi --`prabandhe cāṅ‌gī rasa eka evopanibadhyamāno'rchaviseṣalābhaṃ chāyātiśayaṃ ca puṣṇāti | yathāā rāmāyaṇe yathā vā mahābhārate | rāmāyaṇe karuṇo rasaḥ pradhānaḥ | mahābhārate śānto raso mukhyaḥ, mokṣalakṣaṇapuruṣārthaśca sarvapuruṣārthobhyaḥ pradhānaḥ, vāsudevo bhagavān prādhānyena pratipādyate, vāsudevāpradeśāntare vyavasthāpitam | ataḥ saṃsārātīte parameścare tasminneva bhagavati vāsudeve bhavata bhāvitacetasaḥ, mā bhūta vibhūtiṣu niḥsārāsu, nirṇotaścāyamaṃśaḥśabdatattvavidbhirvyākhyāvidhāyibhiranyaiśca, `bhagavān vāsudevaśca kīrtyate'tra sanātanaḥ' ityukteśca' iti | śrutitadanusāriśāstarapratipādyaḥ vāsudeva eva paraṃ tattvam iti śabdārtatattvajñānibhiḥ sudhībhiḥ pūrvapūrvataraiḥ siddhāntitaḥ, mamāpi sa evābhimata iti ānandavardhanācāryaḥ dhvanayati | evaṃ śrīrāmāyaṇabhāratabhāvaṃ prapañcayan eṣa ānandavardhanaḥ tadubhayaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ mokṣaśāstramityabhipraiti | etena rāmāyaṇaṃ bhārataṃ ca vyaṅgyamaryādayā śivapāramyaparamiti yatkaiścidarvācīnatamai ruktaṃ taccintyam, purātanairvidvajjanaireva vāsudevaparatvasya vyavasthāpitatvāt, `vāsudevaḥ pratipādyata' iti bhāratakṛtaiva maharṣiṇoktatvācceti saṃkṣepaḥ |
bahūnāmitiślokorthaḥ
bahūnāṃ janmanāmante jñānavān sāṃ prapadyate |
vāsudevaḥ sarvamiti sa mahātmā sudurlabhaḥ || (gī. 7.19)
tatra bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ-kurukṣetraṃ saṃgrāmasamucitaṃ deśaṃ manvānāḥ kauravāḥ pāṇāḍavāsca yoddhukāmā aṣṭādaśabhirakṣauhiṇībhiḥ saha tatra samavayanti sma | tatrāgateṣu ke samānāḥ, ke'samānāḥ, ke vā guṇottarāḥ atha vā pūrvaṃ parihaṇiteṣu ke samāgatāḥ, ke nāgatā iti jñātvā tattaducitasamarasāmagrīsaṃpādasaukaryāya samāhatān draṣṭukāmaḥ senādvayamadhye svaṃ rathaṃ sthāpayitvā, tatra sthita eva pārthaḥ sarvānapaśyat | dṛṣṭvā tu tatra samavetān ācāryabandhumutrādīn saṃdātakaruṇo vilakṣaṇabandhusnehaparavaśaḥ `kathaṃ hanyāmimān bāndhavādīn, kimanena bandhujananidhanamātraprayodanena aihikāmuṣ‌mikābhimataphalavidhureṇa pāpamūlenāyodhanena, ato na cikīrṣāmi kalahamimamaphalam' itithamuktvā visṛjya saśaraṃ śarāsanaṃ tūṣṇīmāsāṃcakre . tato bhagavatā sāmavacanaiḥ sāntvito'pi dhanañjayo bandhusnehādviṣīdannitikartavyadīn, na jāne'haṃ dharmamadharmaṃ ca, athāpi nāhaṃ paśyāmi idamāyodhanaṃ śreyaḥsādhanam, yadasmākaṃ śreyaḥ paśyati bhavām tacchāstu bhavāneva, śiṣyo'hiṃ tava, bhavataścaraṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ prapadya' iti | evaṃ bruvate prapannāya pārtāya jijñāsave śreyo'rthine paramārtaṃ sarvaśāstrārthaṃ saṃkṣepeṇopadiśati bhagavān --aśocyānanvaśocastvam (gī o 2.11)
ityārabhya,
sarvadharmān parityajya māmekaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja |
ahaṃ tvā sarvapāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyami mā śucaḥ || (gī o1.8.66)
ityantena |
atra śrīmadbhagavadgītāyāṃ ṣaṭkatrayam | tatra prathame ṣaṭke jñānayogaḥ, dvividhakarmayogaḥ, bhagavatsākṣātkārāṅgabhūtadhyānayogaśca upadiṣṭaḥ, dvitīye ṣaṭke bhaktiyogaḥ tasya hetubhūto bhagavato mahimā ca abhihitaḥ | tṛtīye ṣaṭke pūrvaṣaṭkadvayoktārthakroḍīkraḥ, padārthānāmavaśyajñātavyasāttvikatvādi nirūpaṇaṃ ca kṛtam |
tatrāpi--`aśocyānanvaśocastvam' (gī o 2.11) ityādinā `eṣā te'bhihitā sāṃkhye buddhiḥ' (gī o 2.31) ityanetena jñānayogamupadiśya, `yoge tvīmāṃ śrṛṇu' (gī o2.31) ityādinā āpañcamaparisamāpti jñānasya bahiraṅgakāraṇaṃ dvividhaṃ karmayogaṃ saṃkṣepavistarābhyāmupadiśya,
bhoktāraṃ yajñatapasāṃ sarvalokamaheśvaram |
suhṛdaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ jñātvā māṃ śāntimṛcchati |. (gīo 5.21)
iti pañcamasya antimaśloke `jñātvā māṃ śāntimṛcchati' ityanena paramātmajñānamapanargasādanamityuktyā prasaktaṃ jñānāntaraṅgasādhanaṃ dhyānayogaṃ ṣaṣṭha upadiśya, tatrāntimaśloke
yogināmapi sarveṣāṃ madgatenāntarātmanā |
śraddhāvan labhate yo māṃ sa me yuktatamo mataḥ || (gī o 6.47)
ityatra `madgatenāntarātmanā, śraddāvān' ityāyuktyā dhyānāṅgatayā prasaktabhaktyarthaṃ bhagavanmahimapratipādanāya pravṛtte'tra ṣaṭke jñānavijñāvapadavācyasāmānyaviśeṣarūpabhavanmahimānirūpaṇapare'tra saptame'dhyāye `jñānaṃ te'haṃ savijñānam' (gīo7.2) ityubhayavidhamahimoopadeśaṃ pratiśrutyaca, `manuṣyaṇāṃ sahastreṣu' (gī o 7.3) iti jñānaṃ praśasya, bhūmirāpo'nalo vāyuḥ' (gī o 7.4)ityādinā, `mattaḥ parataraṃ nānyat' (gī o 7.7) ityantena ślokacatuṣṭayena jñānapadāvācyasāmānyamahīmānam, `raso'hamapsu kaunteya' (gī o 7.8) ityādinā `ye caiva sāttvikā bhāvā' (7.12) ityantena ślokapañcakena vijñānapadavācyaviśeṣamahimānaṃ copadiśya, evaṃvidhaṃ jñānaṃ saṃpādayituṃ na sarve sokāḥ prabhāvanti, api tu triguṇādikāryakartāpi, paramātmā na triguṇabaddo|ta uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ, sarvaśaraṇya iti dhiyā bhagavaccaraṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatā, bhagavadanugraheti, yathā--loke kaścitkasyacidati premabhātanaṃ janaḥ `ahaṃ saiti vyapadeśamarhati' iti `tribhirguṇamayairbhāvaiḥ' (gī o 7.13) `udārāḥ sarva evaite jñānī' (gī o 7.18) iti ṣaṅabhiḥ ślokairupadiśya, etādṛśavyapadeśahetubhūtaparamātmadayārūpabhagavatprasādasya kāraṇaṃ bhagavajjñānaṃ na sarvaiḥ sulabhamiti jñānistutidvārā jñānaṃ stauti bhagavān vāsudevaḥ --
bahūnāmiti || iyamatra yojanā--bahūnāṃ janmanām---
ācaturdaśamā dvarṣātkarmāṇi niyamena tu |
daśāvarāṇāṃ dehānāṃ kāraṇāni karotyasau ||
iti pramāṇasiddhabahujanmanām, ante-antime ḍanmani, vāsudevaḥ sarvamiti sarvaṃ carācaruprapañcajātam, vāsudevaḥ tadāyattam, atha vā sarvaṃ pūrṇaṃ vasti, vāsudevaḥ harireva, yadvā sarvaṃ sarvavyāpi vāsudevaḥ, yadvā `pitāhamasya jagato matā dhātā pitāmahaḥ' (gī. 9.17) iti vakṣyamāṇasarnanidhabandhujanaḥ vāsudevaḥ, atha vā sarvaṃ sarvasya utpattilayasthānaṃ, vāsudevaḥ, yadvā sarvaṃ sadā sarvapiṣayakajñānavadvastu, vāsudeva eva, iti 5ānavān evaṃrūpajñānavān evaṃrūpajñānavān muktiyogyaḥ san, māṃ madabhinnaṃ paramātmānaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prapadyate prāpnoti, saḥ īdṛśajñānavān, hetugarbhaṃ viseṣaṇam; ata eva mahātmā pūjaniyaḥ, `yaṃ yaṃ lokaṃ manasā saṃvibhāti viśuddhasatvaḥ kāmayate yaṃśca kāmān | caṃ caṃ lokaṃ jayate tāṃśca kāmān, tasmādātmajñaṃ jharcayedbūtikāmaḥ (ācha o 1.1.10) iti ātmajñāninaḥ pūjyatokteḥ, sudurlabhaḥ nopalabhyata iti | atra durlabhatvokterīdṛśabrajhajñānino viralā eva, ata evoktaṃ `kaścinmāṃ vetti tattvataḥ' (gī o 7.3) iti sarvaśabdasya pūrṇārtakatvam `idaṃ brajhātisāmīpyātsarvapūrṇaguṇatvataḥ | tacca brajha jalān sākṣādyo'sau viṣṇurjalān' iti chāndogyapūrṇaprajñamunibhāṣyānusāreṇa, `sarvaṃ samāpnoṣi tato'si sarvaḥ' (gī. 11.40) iti vakṣyamāṇadisā sarvavyāpitvārthakatvaṃ bodhyam | `tadvā asyaitaditi chandaḥ' (bṛ. 6.3.29) ityupaniṣadvyākhyānaparasya `avidyāyā yadā pravibhakto bhavati tadā sarveṇaikatvamevāsya bavati' iti śrīśaṅkarabhāṣyasya vyākhyāne `sarveṇa pūrṇena paramātmānā sahetyarthaḥ' ityānandagiriṇā vyākhyānāt sarvaśabdaḥ pūrṇarthako'pi saṃpratipannaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -jñānaṃ tu bahujanmasādhyam. idaṃ ca gītāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhe'pi `ayati- śraddayopetaḥ' (gī. 6.37) ityādinā `anekajanmasaṃsiddhaḥ' (gī.6.45) ityantena praśnottarābhyāṃ vivṛtaṃ bhagavatā | phalaprāptestatra janmāntare'pi pratipāditattvāt |
bhagavatprāptimokṣastu jñānānacchedakadehāvasāna eva asati pratibandhake, sati tu tasmin kālāntare | sūtrakṛtā `aihukamaprastapatapratibandhe taddarśanāt' (bra.sū.3.4.50) evaṃ `muktiphalā nyamastadavasthā'vadhṛtestadavastā'vadhṛteḥ' (bra.3.4.51) iti sūtrābhyāṃ tadeva siddāntitam | tathā hi prārabdharūpapratibandhakābhāve dajjñānam aparokṣajñānam, aihikaṃ sādanasaṃpattijanmanyeva bhavati, tathādarśanāt, `śrutvātmānam' iti śrutāvuktatvāt ityādyasyārtaḥ | jñānamityadhyāhāralabdham | asati pratibandhake'sminneva janmani jñānotpattiḥ, sati tu tasmun janmāntare seti tātparyam | śāṅkarepi bhāṣye'syaivamevārtho varṇitaḥ |
evaṃ jñānavadeva, muktiphalāniyamaḥ muktiphalasyāpi aniyamaḥ, jñānotpattideṅāvasāne pratibandhakābhāve muktirbhavati, sati tasmin janmāntare | evaṃ ca jñānotpattijanmanyeva muktiriti śāṅkare | śrīrāmānujīyabhāṣye tu `mokṣātirika tābhyudayaphalaṃ tatsādhanottarameva bavatīti na niyamaḥ, muktiphalamapi tatsādhanottarameva bhavatīti na niyam' iti sūtradvayasyārtha upavarṇitaḥ | evaṃ ca yatra janmani sādhanaṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ bhavati, jñānaṃ ca jāyate, tadavasāna eva jñānamokṣau bhavata iti na niyamaḥ, pratibandhakaṃ ca prārabdhaṃ karma | jñānino daurlabhyanirūpaṇena ātmatvavyapadeśahetubhūtabhagavatprītyatiśayakāraṇaṃ jñānamatidurlabhamiti jñānameva stutaṃ bhavatīti |
gotāyā bhedaparatvam
etena `jñānī tvātmaiva' iti vyapadeśaḥ, `vāsudevaḥ sarvam' ityuktiśca jīveśayorabhede, sarvasya vāsudevasvarūpatve ca tātparyaṃ bodhayatīti na sarvathā mantavyam | tathā sati `jñānavānmāṃ prapadyate' `ye bhajanti tu māṃ nityaṃ māyāmetāṃ taranti te' ityādibhirbrajhajīvayorupāsyopāsakabhāvasya, prāpyaprāparabhāvasya cābhidhānāyogāt | tayorbhedāyattatvāt |
nanu svātmaiva svasya priyo bhavati na tvanya ātmeti hi sarveṣāmanubhavaḥ, tataśca jñānino'nyatve prītiviṣayatvameva na syāditi taduktireva tayorabhede tātparyaṃ grāhayatīti cet, ucyate --`priya' iti vyapadeśaṃ ekaikasminnastuni vidyamāna ekaiko'tiśayito guṇaḥ paracittamāvarjayatīti hi yahṛdayahṛdayaikasākṣiko'nubhavaḥ | laukiko hi guṇo na bhagavataścittasyākarṣakaḥ --
na vedayajñādhyayanairna dānairna ca kriyābhirna tapobhirugraiḥ |
evaṃrūpaḥ śakya ahaṃ nṛloke draṣṭuṃ tvadanyena kurupiravīra || (gī. 11.48)
bhaktyā tvananyayā śakya ahamevaṃvidho'rjuna |
jñātuṃ dṣṭuṃ ca tattvena praveṣṭuṃ ca paraṃtapa || (gī.11.54)
iti kevavedādhyayanādīnāṃ jñānasādhanatāṃ niṣidhya, bhaktereva tatsādhanatābodhanāt | kiṃ tu bhagavadviṣayakaṃ sarvettamatvaprakārakaṃ śāstrasiddaṃ jñānam, tanmūlakasvaviṣayaniravacchinnaparamapremapravāharūpabhaktiśceti anuttamaguṇadvayam `priyo hi jñānino'tyarthamahaṃ sa ca mama priyaḥ' `bhaktyā tvananyayā' ityādyukteḥ ata eva `tamevaṃ vidvān' (tai.ā.13) `ātmā vā are' (bṛ.4.14) `athāto brajhajijñāsā' (bra.sū.1.1.1) ityādi śrutiḥ sūtraṃ ca jñānaṃ puruṣārthasādhanamāha | tataśca bhagavatprītihetuḥ `bhaktimānyaḥ sa me priyaḥ' ityuktā bhaktiḥ, uktaṃ vilakṣaṇaṃ jñānaṃ ca, na tvātmasvarūpatvam | asti jñānina idamitaravailakṣaṇyamiti bodhanāyaiva ātmaivetyuktiḥ, na tu ātmasvarūpatvabodhanāya | anyathā svasya svasvarūpaṃ priyameveti sarvaditatvāt, tadbodhanaṃ viphalaṃ syāt | kiṃ ca ātmaikatvavādino mañjūṣākṛto mate sarveṣāṃ jīvānāṃ brajhābhedasya svataḥ siddhatvena, jñānināṃ viśiṣya tatkathanamanarthakam | kiṃ ca atraivopakrame --
aśocyānanvaśocastvaṃ prajñāvādāṃśca bhāṣase |
gatāsūnagatāsūṃśca nānuśocanti paṇḍitāḥ || (gī. 2.11)
na tvevāhaṃ jātu nāsaṃ na tvaṃ neme janādhipāḥ |
na caiva na bhaviṣyāmaḥ sarve vayamataḥ param ||(gī. 2.12)
iti `aśocyān, janādhipāḥ' ati bahuvacanaprayogeṇa teṣāṃ nityatvasādhanānumāne ātmano dṛṣṭāntatvakathanena ca ātmabhedo nirūpitaḥ, upasaṃhāre'pi --
manmanā bhava madbhakto madyājī māṃ namaskuru |
māmevaiṣyasi satyaṃ te pratijāne priyo'si me || (gī. 18.65)
sarvadharmān parityajya māmekaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja |
ahaṃ tvā sarvapāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śicaḥ || (gī. 18.66)
iti dvābhyāṃ śrīkṛṣaaṇākhyaparabrajhaṇa upāsyatvam, arjunopalakṣitasarvamanujādīnāmupāsakatvaṃ bodhayatā tanmūlabhūtātmabhedo'pi nirūpitaḥ | atastadubhayānurodhena `ātmaiva' ityasya asmaduktārtha eva yuktaḥ | tasmāt atra jñānino vāsudevasnarūpatvavarṇanam,etanmūlakaṃ jīvabrajhaṇorabhede śāstrāṇāṃ tātparyanarṇanaṃ cetyubhayamupakramādinyāyavido na nyāyyamityāhuḥ |
nanu upakramopasaṃhārayornirūpito bhedo'pāramārthika iti `tadubhayānurodhe' tiyādyayuktamiti cenna, śāstrādau avāstavārthakathanasyā'nyāyyatvāt | `śiṣyaste'haṃ śādhi māṃ tvāṃ prapannam' (gī. 2.8) ityanena tattvabubhutsayā bhagavantaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ pārtha iti jñāyate, tasmāt prapannāya śiṣyāya ādāvante ca vāstasyaivārthasyopadeśena samīcinajñānotpādamācāryasya mukhyo dharma iti ubhayatrāvāstava evārtha upadiṣṭa ityasyāyogāt | etena yadatra mañjūṣāyām `ahaṃ mametyadhyāsa evā|ṛvidyā, vāsudevaḥ sarvam iti vidyāyāḥ tasyaiva virodhitvāt' ityānena sararvaṃ vāsudevātmakam iti ślokatātparyavarṇanaṃ, yaccāsakṛdātmaikatvanirūpaṇaṃ tadubhayamubhayoorapyācāryayoranabhimatamiti bodhyam | uktadiśa ślokasya tathārthakatvābhāvena --
adhyeṣyate ca ya imaṃ dharmyaṃ saṃvādamāvayoḥ |
jñānayajñena tenāhamiṣṭaḥsyāmiti me matiḥ || (gī 18.70)
śraddhāvananasūyaśca śrṛṇuyādapi yo naraḥ |
so'pi muktaḥ śubhān lokān pāpnuyātpuṇyakarmaṇām || (gī 18.71)
iti ślokābhyāṃ śāstramidamupadeṣṭṛṇāṃ śrotṛṇāṃ ca uttamalokāvāptereva phalatvapratipādanena ca tadubhayasya śrīvyāsamaharṣyanabhimatatvāvagamāt |
vāsudevādi sūtrārthaḥ
`vāsudevārjunābhyāṃ vun', (pā.sū. 4.3.98) `aa' (pā.sū.8.4.68) iti sūtrayataḥ pāṇinerapyācāryasya bhagavati vāsudeve paramātmatvasarvādhyakṣatvādhyavasāyaḥ, ata eva ca sarvato'bhyarhitatvasarvānyatvādhyavasāyaścāstītyavagamena tasya tadanabhimatatvāvagamācya | tataś‌ca--`vaiyākaraṇasiddhānte' ti pratijñāya tadanabhimatārthavarṇanamayuktam |
nanu tathāsūtrapraṇayanena kathamukto'rthastadabhimata iti nirṇaya iti cet, ucyate-ṣaḍaṅ‌geṣu vyākaraṇaṃ pradhānaṃ tattvaṃ ca, paraṃparayā vedārthanirṇayopayogitvamityavocāma | yathā-idaṃ sūtra śabdasādhutvānuśāsakaṃ, tathā tacceṣṭitamapi arthaviśeṣavyavasthāpakamiti, iheṅ‌gitena ceṣṭitena nimiṣitena mahatā sūtraprabandhenā''cāryāṇāmabhiprāyo gamyate, iti `na saṃprasāraṇe saṃprasāraṇam' (pā.sū.3.1.37) `na mune' (pā.sū.8.2.3) iti ,ūtrayorvadatā bhāṣaayakṛtā vyavasthāpitam | iṅgitaṃ mānaso vyāpāraḥ, ceṣ‌ṭataṃ kāyiko vyāpāraḥ prakṛte uccāraṇam, nimiṣitaṃ netravyāpāraḥ | tatra kvacidācāryo viśiṣṭena sūtreṇa svāśayamāviṣkaroti, yathā-yūna ityādau saṃprasāraṇayogyayaṇamadvayādisamāveśasthale pūrvasya yaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ na saṃprasāraṇamiti svāśayaṃ `na saṃpre' ti viśiṣṭavacanena | kvacittu kāryabhūtasya yatkiṃcicchabdasvarūpasya nimittatvādinopādānena svāśayamaviṣkaroti, yathā `na mune' ityanena nāābhāvasiddhaṃ naśabdaṃ nimititamādāya vihito mutvāsiddhatvābhāvaḥ, nimittatvenāśritanābhāve kartavye'pi mutvaṃ nāsiddhamiti svābhiprāyaṃ smārayatyanumānavidhayā | yadyevaṃ kalpyate tadā nābhāve kartavye mutvasyāsiddhatvena naśabdasyaivābhāvena hetvaprasiddhiriti sūtraṃ nirviṣayaṃ syāt | prakṛte'pi vāsudevaśabdasya pūrvanipātakaraṇaṃ ceṣ‌ṭitaṃ (dvande pūrvaprayoogarūpam) vāsudevapadāvācyasya devatāviśeṣatvaṃ, sarvānyatvaṃ, sarvettamatvamata eva sarvatobhyarhitattavaṃ cācāryo'bhipraitīti bodhayati | anyathā arjunaśabdasya ajādyadantā'lpāctarattvena `ajādyadantam' (pā.sū.2.2.33) `alpāctaram' (pāsū.2.2.34) iti svakṛtāṃ vyavasthāmanusṛtya arjunaśabdasya pūrvanipāte kartavye atathābhūtasya vāsudevaśabdasya pūrvaprayogo'nupapannaḥ syāt | tena jhānīmo vāsudeve'bhyarhitatvaprayoojakaṃ paramātmatvādikamastīti svasiddhāntaḥ sūcita iti | ata eva pradarśitāṃ svīyāmapi vyavasthāmullaṅaghya prathamaṃ vāsudevaśabdaṃ prāyuṅkta | uktābhyarhitātvādiṣu uttarottaraṃ prati pūrvapūrvasya hetutvaṃ draṣṭavyam | nanu samabhivyāhṛtapadārthāpekṣayottamatvābhyarhitatvakalpanaṃ nyāyyaṃ natvevaṃ kalpanamiti cet, ucyate-spaṣṭīkṛtaśaacāyamartho bhāṣyakaiyaṭābhyāmatraiva sūtre | tathā hi-`atha vā naiṣā kṣatriyākhyā, saṃjñaiṣā tatra bhagavata' iti bhāṣyam, `saṃjñaiṣeti || nityaḥ paramātmā devatāviśeṣa iha vāsudevo gṛjhata ityartha iti kaiyaṭaḥ | atra bhāṣye vāsudevapadasya bhagavatpadena vyākhyānāt |
eśvaryasya samagrasya vīryasya yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ |
jñānavijñānayoścaiva ṣaṇṇāṃ bhaga itīraṇā ||
jñānaśaktibhalaiśvaryavīryatejāṃsyaśeṣataḥ |
bhagavacchabdavācyāni vinā heyaguṇādibhiḥ ||
iti pramāṇasiddhaheyaguṇābhāvasamānādhikaraṇānanyasādhāraṇajaganniyamanādiguṇavān,
etāvatālaṃ nanu sūcitena guṇairasāmyānatiśāyanasya |
hitvetarānprārthayatoo vibhūtiryasyāṅghrireṇuṃ juṣate'nabhīpsoḥ || (bhāga.pra. 18.20)
athāpi yatpādanakhāvasṛṣṭaṃ jagadviriñ‌copahṛtārhaṇāmbhaḥ |
seśaṃ punātyanyatamo mukundāt koo nāma loke bhagavatpadrthaḥ ||
(bhāga.pra.18.21)
ahaṃ pṛṣṭo'ryamaṇo bhavadbhirācakṣa ātmāvagamo'tra yāvān |
nabhaḥ patantyātmasamaṃ patatriṇaḥ tathā samaṃ viṣṇugatiṃ vipaścitaḥ ||
(bhāga.pra.18.23)
iti bhāgavate bhagavatpadavācyatayā nirṇeto viṣṇumukundādipadavācyo brajheśānādisarvadevasamārādhyo lakṣmīpatirdevatāviseṣaḥ,
bhagavāniti śabdo'yaṃ tathā puruṣa ityapi |
vartete tāvubhau nityaṃ vāsudeve sanātane ||
iti pramāṇena bhagavacchabdasya mukhyavācyatayā nirṇeto vāsudevo grājha iti bodhitam | devatāviśeṣa iti kaiyaṭasya devatottama ityarthaḥ |yataḥ haryādipadāntaraṃ vihāya bhagavatpadaṃ bhagavān bhāṣyakāraḥ ata uktārtho bhāṣyādisaṃmataḥ, ataḥ kasyacidapi codyasya nāvakāśaḥ |
evaṃ ca sūtre vāsudevapadaṃ kartari, adikāraṇe ca auṇādiko'ṇpratyayāntavāsupadasya devaśabdena karmadhāraye sarvāśrayasya sarvāntaryāmiṇaḥ śriyaḥpatyurnārāyaṇasya vācakam | `mayi sarvamidaṃ protaṃ sutre maṇamigaṇā iva' (gī. 7.7.) `īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānām' (gī18.61)
sarvatrāsau samastaṃ ca vasatyatreti vai yataḥ |
tato'sau vāsudeveti vidvidbhiḥ parigīyate ||
vāsanādvāsudevaḥ syād vāsitaṃ te jagattrayam |
sarvabhūtanivāso'si vāsudeva namo'stu te ||
chādayāmi jagadviśaṃ bhūtvā sūrya ivāṃśubhiḥ |
sarvabhūtādhivāsaś‌ca vāsudevastato jhaham || (bhāra.śā. 350.41)
iti smṛtibhiḥ tasyaiva sarvāntaryāmitvasarvādhāratvaguṇaviśiṣṭatvabodhanāt | śaṅkarācāryairapi `eṣa ta ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛta' (bṛ.u.5.) iti śrutivākyavyākhyāne'ntaryāmitvaṃ nārāyaṇasyaivetyuktatvācca | atha vā-idaṃ vāsudevaśabdasya pūrvaprayogarūpaṃ pāṇinīyamuccāraṇaṃ `putrā me bahukṣīraghṛtamodanaṃ kāṃsyapātryāṃ bhuñjīran' iti `na mu na' iti sūtrabhāṣyoktavṛddhakumārīvacanavat nirūpitayāvadaऱthagarbham | vṛddhakumārīvacanaṃ tu patiputradhanadhānyādiyāvatsaṃpatkāmanāgarbhamiti vyākhyātāraḥ |
śabdasaṃskāropayogyarthānuguṇyena `asya a' iti vaktavye `a a' iti nirvibhaktikaṃ nirdeśaṃ kurvan tatra bhavān pāṇiniḥ yadavaś‌yakartavyaṃ śāstrāntee śāstrasāmāpanadyoootakaṃ saṃpradāyasiddhamabhyāsarūpaṃ dvirvacanam , yacca `maṅgalāntāni prathanta' iti pramāṇasiddaṃ śāstrānte kartavyatayā prāptaṃ sveṣṭadevatāsmaraṇarūpaṃ maṅgalam, yacca svakṛterbhagavatsamarpaṇāya kartavyaṃ bhagavatsaṃbodhanam, yacca svasya vāsudeve bhagavati iṣ‌ṭadevatātvādijñānamastīti śroootṛṇāṃ bodhanena teṣāmamapi tādṛśajñānootpādanam tatsarvaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavatitī manyate | tatrābhyāsaḥ spaṣṭaḥ . `akāro vāsudevaḥ syāt akāro bhagavānviṣṇuḥ' iti vacanāt, `a iti brajha' (ai.ā.2-3-8) iti śruteśca, ante'pi maṅaagalārthaṃ vāsudevavācakāśabdaprayooga iti sāṃ pradāyikavacanācca, aśabdavācye vāsudevatvoottamatveeṣ‌ṭadevatātvabuddhirastīti siddham | `a' ityasya saṃbodhanāntatvasaṃbhavena bhagavati svakṛteḥ samarpaṇāya tatsaṃbodhane ca kṛtaṃ bhavati | atra pakṣe ādarāt‌ dviruktiḥ, śrotāroo'pi āācāryāṇāṃ bhagavati vāsudeve sarvettamatveṣaṭadevattvādhyavasāyasadbhāvena asmābhirapi īdṛśādhyavasāyavadbhistadupāsakaiśca bhavitavyam iti dṛḍhādhyavasāyā bhaveyuranutiṣṭheyuśca tathaiveti sarvaṃ samañaajasam |
bhagavatprasādasya avidyānivartakatvam
kiṃ ca `yaduktam ahaṃ mama' ityavidyayeti, tadyuktam `vāsudevaḥ sarvam' iti (sarvaṃ vāsudevātmakamiti jñānaṃ) vidyā `saiṣā uktā'vidyāvirodhinī' ityayayayayuktistu na yuktā | etasyā api ahaṃ mametivat avidyāviśeṣatvāt | `ahaṃ mama' ityasya avidyātve `vāsudevaḥ sarvam' ityāsya ca vidyātve na kaṃcidapi viśeṣaṃ paśyāmaḥ | `atasmin tattvādhyavasāyo' hi avidyā | sa cooobhayatra samānaḥ | yoo hi yasya nivartakaḥ sa tu tasya virodhī | loke ca `idaṃ rajatam' iti mithyādhyavasiteḥ, auttarakālikaṃ `nedaṃ rajatam' iti bādhajñānam, iyaṃ śuktiketi adhiṣiṭhānayāthātmyajñānaṃ vā nivartakaṃ, na tu tatsamānayogakṣemaṃ `ghaṭaḥ paṭa' ityādyavidyayāntaramiti vadanti vidvāṃsaḥ |
śāstraṃ tu bhagavadanugraho saṃsārabījam `ahaṃ mama' ityajñānaṃ nivartatyāha |
yeṣāṃ sa eva bhagavāndayayedanantaḥ
sarvātmanā śritapado yadi nirvyalīkam |
te vai vidantyatitaranti ca devamāyāṃ
naiṣāṃ mamāhamiti dhīḥ śvasṛgālabhakṣ‌ye || (bhāga.2.7.42)
daivī jheṣā guṇamayī mama māyā duratyayā |
māmeva ye prapadyante māyāmetāṃ taranti te || (gī.7.14)
ye mānavā vigatarāgaparāparajñā
nārāyaṇaṃ suraguruṃ satataṃ smaranti |
dh‌yānena tena hatakilbiṣacenāste
mātuḥ payodhararasaṃ na punaḥ pibanti ||(pāṇḍavagītā)
ye tu mumukṣavo bhagavadbhajanenaiva saṃsārataraṇaṃ sukaramiti ācāryavacane śāstre ca kṛtaviśvāsāḥ, paramaparaṃ ca tattvaṃ samyak vijānantaḥ putramitrakalatrādiviṣayābhilāṣavidhurāḥ, santataṃ bhagavantaṃ smaranto niṣkapaṭaṃ sevamānāḥ, bhagavataścaraṇāravindaniṣ‌yandamānamakarandāsvādamattasvāntāḥ, bhagavaccāraṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ prapannā bhavanti te bhagavadanugraheṇa utpannavivekā dūrīkṛtasaṃsārabījā `ahaṃ mama' ityavidyābandhāḥ svarūpatooooo nirvattaprārabdhetarapuṇyapāpāśca punarjanma na vindanta iti śāltravinirṇaya ityakhaṇḍārthaḥ | devamāyā paramātmādhīnaguṇatrayātmakaṃ mohakaṃ (vastuyāthātmyagrahapratibandhakaṃ) vastu, dayayet dayāṃ kuryāt , prasādena anugṛhṇīyāt | dayāśabdātkarotiṇyantālliṅ | śvasṛgālabhakṣye dehe, idamupalakṣaṇaṃ naśvarasya samastaviṣayasyāpi |
`prapadyante, taranti' ityukteḥ bhagavaccaraṇaprāptermāyātaraṇasya ca `aṣṭavarṣaṃ brājhaṇamupayīta tamadhyāpayīta' ityādau upanayanādhyāpanayoriva hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ sūcitaḥ |
vigatetyādeḥ viṣayavairāgyena parāparatattvavivekavanta ityaऱthaḥ | hatakilbiṣetyādeḥ hareranugraheṇa vinaṣṭāvidyādidoṣā ityarthaḥ | śuddhātmāna iti yāvat | stanyapānanimittajanmarahitā (muktā) bhavantīti | itaratspaṣ‌ṭam |
etena yat sphoṭākhyaṃ padārthamapūrvamaṅgīkṛtya tasya brajhatvaṃ jagadupādānakāraṇatvaṃ tasyaikasyaiva satyatvaṃ ca mañjūṣāyāṃ nāgeśena, anyaiś‌ca vaiyākaraṇaiḥ svasvagranthe ca nirūpitaṃ, tatsarvaṃ pāṇinerapi saṃmatamiti bālānāṃ mohootpādanāyaiveti mantavyam | sarvasyāsya sūtrabhāṣyakṛdabhiprāyaviruddhatvāt | vyāsasya pāṇineśca vāsudeva eva paraṃ tatttvaṃ paraṃ brajha tatsmaraṇadireva puruṣārthaprada iti mukh‌yaṃ tātparyam | bhāṣye'pi sphoṭasya brajhatvādikaṃ kvāpi noktamiti tadabhiprāyo'pi vyāsapāṇinitātparyānurodhīti |
gītāślokārthavivṛterbhāvoo vyāsasya darśitaḥ |
`vāsudeve' tyādi sūtravivṛteḥ pāṇinerapi ||
sāmānādhikaraṇyavirodhodhdhāraḥ
nanu `jñānī tvātmaiva, vāsudevaḥ sarvam' ityādi samāndhikaraṇanirdesena `nīlo ghaṭa' ityādāviva abhedabuddhiḥ svarasata utpadyata iti cet, atra brūmaḥ -sāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśo na sarvatra svarūpaikyapratyayaṃ janayitumīṣṭe | a`mukhaṃ candraḥ', `siho māṇavakaḥ', `aādityoo yūpa' ityādau tathā nirdeśe'pi abhedabhuddheranudayāt | kiṃ cu saṃjñājātiguṇakriyābhedena caturvidheṣu śabdeṣu yatra `sundaraḥ pācako vā devadattaḥ, sundaraḥ pācakooo vā brājhaṇaḥ' ityādau saṃjñājātiśabdābhyāṃ guṇakriyayoḥ samabhivyāhāraḥ `devadatto brājhaṇa' ityādau saṃjñājātyoḥ, `sundaraḥ pācaka' utyādau guṇakriyayoḥ , `nilaḥ sundara' ityādau guṇayoḥ, `pācakaḥpāṭhaka' ityādau kriyayoḥ samabhivyāhāraḥ, tatrābhedadhiyamutpādayati | atra sarvatra dharmāṇāmekasminnadhikaraṇe vṛttisaṃbhavāt | yatra tu jātyoreva saṃjñayoreva samabhivyāhāraḥ na tatra | yathā `kṣatriyo brājhaṇaḥ', `devadattoyajñadatta' iti | atra kṣatriyatvabrājhaṇatvādīnāṃ viruddhadharmatvena ekādhikaraṇavṛtterayogāt | prakṛte ca `jñānī tvātmaiva' ityasya `devadatto yajñadatta' iti prayoogatulyatayā nābhedapratyayaḥ, atra jñānipadasya
caturvidhā bhajante māṃ janāḥ sukṛtino'rjuna |
ārte jijñāsurarthārtho jñāni ca bharatarṣabha || (gī. 7.13)
ityanenoktopāsakaviśeṣajñānijīvaparatvena ātmapadasya bhagavatsvarūpaparatvena ubhayorekādhikaraṇavṛttitvāsaṃbhavāt | kiṃ tu `agrirmāṇavaka' ityādāviva ātmasadṛśa ityeva bodhaḥ, sādṛśyaprayojakaś‌ca dharmaḥ atipriyatvamapahatapāpmatvādikaṃ ca, atrā''dya upāttaḥ, dvitīyo'nupāttaḥ | etena atra jñānipadaṃ jñānisāmān‌yaparam ataḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyopapattiriti mantavyam | evaṃ vilakṣaṇabodhe kāraṇaṃ sarvakraṇabhūta īśvaraḥ, śabdārthatattvavidaḥ sahṛdayā vidvāso vā praṣṭavyāḥ |
nanvevam `ātmavat' iti vatyantanirdeśena bhāvyamiti cet, na tamantareṇāpi sādṛśyapratīterānubhavikatvāt | kiṃ ca akṛtvaiva tathānirdeśaṃ tadarthapratyāyanaṃ śāstrakṛcchailīsiddham | ata eva pāṇiniḥ `gāṅ‌kuṭādibhyo'ñṇinṅti' (pā.sū.1.2.1) `goto ṇit' ityeva prāyuṅkta | tayoreva sūtrayoḥ "na jhantareṇa vatimatideśo gamyate, antareṇāpi vatimatideśo gamyate, tadyathā--`eṣa brajhadattaḥ' abrajhadattaṃ brajhadatta ityāha; te manyāmahee brajhadattavadayaṃ bhavatīti, evamihāpi aṅ‌tiṃ ṅti, aṇitaṃ ṇidityāha, ṅidvat, ṇidvat iti gamyata" iti bhāṣyeṇa sadṛṣṭāntaṃ vatiṃ vināpi vatyarthāvagatiḥ bhavatīti bhāṣ‌‌yakārā āhuḥ | evaṃ `bahugaṇavatuḍati saṃkh‌yā' (pā.sū.1.1.23) iti sūtre'pi atideśapakṣe `yadi asaṃkhyāṃ sekhyāṃ saṃkhyayāmāha' ityupakramya etaddaṣṭāntena `saṃkhyetyukte'pi saṃkhyāvaditi gamyata' ityavocan | evaṃvidhenaiva prayogeṇa vivakṣitārthapratyāyanaṃ kaveḥ kauśalaṃ sphorayati, kāvyasya śobhāṃ puṣṇāti, sahṛdayānāṃṃ manāṃsi rañ‌jayati | ata eva īdṛśānāṃ prayoogāṇamupapattaye bahūni nimittāni śāstrakṛto darśayanti |
tatra mahābhāṣyakārāḥ `puṃyogādākhyāyām' (pā.sū.4.1.48) iti sūtree praṣṭhaśabdasya striyāṃ vartamānatve hetunirūpaṇāvasaree caturbhiḥ prakāraiḥ ` atasmin sa' ityetat tātsthyāt; tāddharmyāt, tatsāmīpyāt, tatsāhacaryāt, mañaacā hasanti, jaṭī devadattaḥ, gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ, kuntān praveśaya iti, atasmin tacchabdaprayogee nimittoodāharaṇe āhuḥ | `ig‌yaṇaḥ saṃprasāraṇas‌ya' (pā.sū.6.33.139) ityādauau varṇagrahaṇaṃ kathamityāśaṅkya yathāā kākājjātaḥ kākaḥ, tathā saṃprasāraṇapadaṃ `saṃprasāraṇasaṃjñakavākyāt jātavarṇaparam' iti tajjanyatvamapi atasmin tacchabdaprayoge nimittamityabhyadhuḥ |
gauautamo'pi nyāyasūtre --sahacaraṇa-sthāna-tādarth‌ya-vṛtta-māna-dhāraṇa-sāmīpya-yoooga-sādhanādhipatyebhyaḥ, brājhaṇa - bāla-kaṭa-rāja-saktu-candana-ghaṅgā-śakaṭā (śāṭakā) nna-puruṣeṣu atadbhāve'pi tadupacāraḥ (nyā.sū.2.2.64) itii lakṣaṇānimuttānyāha | sahacaraṇādidharmeṇa atacchabdasya tena śabdenābhidhānamit‌yartha iti tadbhāṣyakāraḥ | mañjūṣākārastu-taddharmābhāvee'pi tacchabdavyavahāraḥ sa ca tadhdharmāropeṇa āropanimittāni ca sahacaraṇādīni iti sūtrārthamāha | atrāroopeṇetyuktitvācca | yadyapi nāgeśakṛta `tatra saṃyuktasamavetāṃ jātiṃ brājhaṇe samavāyenādhyārooopya brājhṇaṃ yaṣṭiketyāha' iti nyāyavārtikamatānuvādena vārtikakāramate ārooopoo'stīti pratīyate | tathāpi lakṣaṇāsthale nāropa iti tadīyānāṃ bahūnāṃ vādaḥ | ataścintyamidam |
sahacaraṇam-avinābhāvaḥ, sthānam-āśrayaḥ, tādarthyam-tatprayooooojanakatvam, vṛttam-ācāraḥ (vyāpāraḥ), mānaṃ-pramāṇam, dhāraṇam-āśrayaviśeṣaḥ, sāmīpyaṃ-sannidhiḥ, sādhanaṃ-hetuḥ, ādhipatyam-prabhutvam, udāharaṇāni tadbhāṣye draṣṭavyāni |
jaiminirapi-yajamānaḥ prastaraḥ, agnirvai brājhaṇaḥ, ādityo yūpaḥ, ityādauau abhedasya bādhitatvāt sāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśo'nipapanna iiti prāpte tatsiddhiḥ, jātiḥ, sārūpyam ityādīni nimittānyāha, prathamasya turīye pāde | iyaṃ ca tadīyānāṃ saṃgrahakārikā--
tatsiddhijātisārūpyapraśaṃsāliṅ‌gabhūmabhiḥ |
ṣaḍabhiḥ sarvatra śabdānāṃ gauṇīvṛttiḥ prakalpitā || iti |
iyaṃ ca liṅgasamavāyā dityadhikaraṇe (jai.sū. 1.4..1.8) śāstradīpikāyām |
brajhasūtrakṛto'pi--`syāccaikasya brajhaśabdavat' (bra.sū.2.3.4) iti brajhaśabdavaditi dṛṣṭāntaṃ vadantaḥ paramātmani mukhyasyāsya śabdasyānyatra prayogo gauṇa iti manyante | tāddharmyādikaṃ tatra nimittamiti tadbhāvaḥ | ekasminnarthe mukhyāsyaikasya śabdasya anyasminnarthe brajhasabdasyeva gauṇaḥ prayogaḥ syāditisūtrārthaḥ | ayamartho bhāṣ‌yatrayasaṃmataḥ | śaṃkararāmānujabhāṣ‌yarītyā pūrvapakṣasūtramidam | etadbhāṣ‌yadvayepi `ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūta' iti śrutisthaḥ saṃbhūtaśabdo dārṣṭantikaḥ, pūrṇaprajñabhāṣ‌tyā tu `anādirvā ayamākāśa' iti śrutisthānādiśabdaḥ | `tapo brajha' (tai.u.3.2) ityadhikāre'nnādiṣu brajhajñānasādhane tapasi ca brajhaśab‌do gauḥ (śaṃ-), `tasmādetadbrajhanāmarūpa' (ā.1.11) ityatra pradhāne brajhaśabdogauṇaḥ (rāmā-), tatraiva śrutau kāryabrajhaṇi caturmukhe brajhaśabdo gauṇaḥ (pūrṇa-) iti vadantaḥ trayo'pi bhāṣ‌yakārā atadaryakasya śabdas‌ya atasminnarthe, samānādhikaraṇaprayoge ca tādarthyatadadhīnatnādi ca nimittamiti manyante | ākāśasya brajhakāryatvaṃ prasādh‌ya `ākāśa ātmā' iti tu gauṇa iti śaṃkarācāryāḥ viyadadhikaraṇe `yāvadvikāra-' (bra.sū.2.3.7) miti sūtraśeṣe bruvanti | sadṛśaguṇavattvena, tajjanyatvena ca samānādhikaraṇamaprayoga iti tadbhāvaḥ |
aitena `raso'hamapsu kaunteya' (gī.7.8) `ahaṃ kraturahaṃ yajñaḥ' (gī.9.16) `ādityānāmahaṃ viṣṇuḥ' (gī.10.21) ityādi vibhūtinirūpaṇaprakaraṇastha `raso'ham' ityādisāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśasya na rasādyabhedeee tātparyam, kiṃ tu, rasādīnāṃ rasatvādipradatvena, sajātīyeṣūtkarṣādhāyakatvena vā, tadabhimānidevatāniyāmakatvena vā `yadadhīnooo guṇoo yasya tadguṇī so'bhidhīyate' iti nyāyāt rasādipadavācyatvāt‌ | rasaḥ--rasatvādipradaḥ, rasādiniṣiṭhotkarṣādhāyaka ityādirarthaḥ | paramātmanaḥ sarvaśarīrakatvāt rasādiśarīrakatayā tathāvyavahāra iti kecit |
ata eva `mayi sarvamidaṃ protaṃ sūtre maṇigaṇā iva' (gī.7.7) `yābhirvibhūtibhilaukānimāṃstvaṃ vyāpya tiṣiṭhasi' (gī.10.13) `keṣu keṣu ca bhāveṣu cintyo'si bhagavanmayā' (gī.10.17) `sarvasya cāhaṃ hṛdi saṃniviṣiṭo mattaḥ smṛtirjñānamapohanaṃ ca' (gī.15.15) `sarvabhūtāśayasthitaḥ' (gī.10.19) `iś‌varaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ hṛdeśe'rjuna tiṣṭhati' (gī.18.61) ityādibhedanibandhanā vyavahārāḥ saṃgacchante |
atra `mayi sarvam' ityetat, sūtramaṇidṛṣṭāāntena harau protatvarūpasaṃyoogaviśeṣamātramabhidhatte, `yābhiḥ' ityetat sarvalokeṣu bhagavatoo vyāptimāha | `keṣu keṣu' iti tu upāsanādhiṣṭhānapraśaanamukhena kiṃcidadhiṣṭhānaṃ pariikalpya tatra bhagavadupāstiḥ kartavyā ityayācaṣaaṭe | ata eva tat‌ra tat‌ra `sālagrāmādau haripūjanavat' ityādi śaṃkarācāryāā āhuḥ | sūtrakāro'pi `na pratīke na hisaḥ' (bra.sū. 4.1.4) iti sūtreṇa pratīke'dhiṣ‌ṭhāne ātmātvabuddhiḥ (pratīkabrajhaṇoorabhedabud‌dhiḥ) na kāryetyāha | etena brajhakāryeṣu sarvatra brajhatvabuddhirna kār‌yetii sivāśayamāviṣkarooti | `ahamātmā guḍākeśa sarvabhūta-' ityetac‌ca `keṣu keṣu' itii arjunananakṛtapraśnasya prativacanaṃ kṛṣṇasya hareḥ sarvaprāṇiṅṛdi sthitiṃ kathayati, `sarvasya' iti vacanaṃ hareḥ sarvahṛdayavartitvaṃ, smṛtyādihetutvaṃ ca vakti, `īśvaraḥ sarvam' iti tu bhagavataḥ sarvāntaryayāmitvena sarvaniyāmakatvaṃ pratipādayati | yadi prapañ‌caparamātmanoorbedooo na s‌yāt tadā sarva ete vyavahārā anupapannāḥ syuḥ | etena `vāsudevaḥ sarvam' ityanena sarvasya vāsudevātmakatvaṃ (tadabhedaḥ) ucyata iti parāstam | sarvaśabdena asaṃṃkucitavṛttyā bodhitacarācarātmakajagataḥ vāsudevasya ca svarūpaikyāyogāt |
kiṃ ca jīvaparayorbhedābhāve `madbhaktā yānti māmapi' (gīī.7.23) `māmevaiṣ‌yasyasaṃśayaḥ' (gī.8.7)
māmupetya punarjanma duḥkhālayamaśāśvatam |
nāpnuvanti mahātmānaḥ saṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ || (gī.8.15)
manmanā bhava madbhakto madyājī māṃ namaskuru |
māmevaiṣyasi yuktvaivamātmānaṃ matparāyaṇaḥ || (gīī.9.34)
manmanāḥ `satyaṃ te pratidāne priyo'si me' (gī.15.64) ityādinā pratipādita ārādhyārādhakabhāvādiḥ kathamupapadyayeta tasya bhedanibandhanatvāt |
kiṃ ca--
ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayastathā | (gī.7.6)
ahaṃ sarvasya prabhavo mattaḥ sarvaṃ pravartate || (gī.10.5)
iti saptamadaśamayorvibhūtinirūpaṇopakramātpūrvam, navame--`pitāhamasya jagato mātā dhātā pitāmahaḥ' (gī.9.17) iti vibhūtinirūpaṇātparaṃ harerjagatkāraṇatvoktirapi vibhūtiprakaraṇasya nābhede tātparyamiti jñāpantī rasādīnāṃramātmakāryatvāt tadadhīnatvena `yo yadadhinaḥ, sa tadadhīnasattāka' iti nyāyāt, tadadhīnasattākatvāt‌ `rājā rāṣ‌ṭm' itivat‌ sāmānādhikaraṇyena vyapadeśa iti bodhayati | ślokamanyathā vyākurvadbhyayo bhagavān krudh‌yet drujhecca | loke hi `pāṣāṇasamo'yam' iti kenaciduktaḥ kaś‌cit pṛthagjano'pi tathābruvate kupyati drujhati ca |
kiṃ ca
athavā bahunaitena kiṃ jñātena tavārjuna |
viṣṭabhyāhamidaṃ kṛtsnamekāṃśena sthito jagat || (gī.10.42)
iti vibhūtiprakaraṇopasaṃhāraś‌lokena sarvajagaddhārakatvena sarvatra saṃnihitatvameva mama vibhūtiriti vibhūtiprakaraṇatātparyasya bhagavataiva nirūpitatvena tattādātmyaparatayā yojanā gītācāryasya na hṛdayaṃgameti vibhāvyatām | tatra dhārakatvaṃ tattadvastusamavetasattānukūlooooo vyāpāraḥ sa ca pālanāntarbhūtaḥ pālanasya sthityādyanekavyāpārasamudāya rūpatvāt, tataś‌ca bhagavadadhīnasattākatvaṃ rasādīnāmiti vibhūtyadhyāyatātparyaṃṃ veditavyam | sarveṣāṃ bhagavadadhīnasattākatvaṃ ca, `natvahaṃ teṣu ta mayi' (gī. 7.12) iti bhagavataivoktam | vāhaṃ tadadhīnaḥ, sarve bhāvāḥ mayi madadhīnā ityartha iti sarve bhāṣyakārā vyākhyātāraśca | idamatra tattm-vibhūtiprakaraṇapaṭhitā viṣṇvādyāstu bhagavadrūpāṇyeva tadvyatiriktā rasādayastu tatkāryabhūtāstadadhīnā iti |
`sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brajha tajjalān iti śānta upāsīta' (chā.3.14.1) iti śrutāvapi sarvaṃ brajheti vyapadeśo'pi sarvas‌ya brajhakāryatvāt tadadhīnatvenaiva rājā rāṣṭramitivat iti jñeyayam | śrutyarthastu idaṃ paridṛś‌yamānaṃ, sarvaṃ samastaṃ vastu, brajha khalu brajhaiva | kutoo brajhatvavyapadeśa ityata āha tajjeti | yathaḥ tajjlān tato brajhaiveti anvayaḥ | tatoo jātaṃ, tatra līne tena anyamāna (rakṣyamāṇa) miti | sarvasya brajhatvavyapadeśe tajjātatvādi nimittamiti śrutitātparyam | vyākhyātamidamevameva śaṃkarācāryaiḥ chāndogyabhāṣye, `sarvatra prasidhdhopadeśāt' (bra.sū. 1.1.1) iti sūtrabhāṣye ca | tajjatvāt, tallīnatvāt, tadanatvāditi tatratyaṃ bhāṣyam | pūrṇaprajñāstu idaṃ brajha, sarvaṃ pūrṇaṃ (sarvaguṇapūrṇamiti yāvat) tat brajha, jalān jale aniti ceṣṭate (pralayārṇavaśāyī, kṣīrasamudraśāyī veti yāvat) evaṃ jānan śāntaḥ śamādiguṇavān bhūtvā upāsīta pūrṇatvādinā dhyāyediti śruterarthaṃ varṇayanti | `ātmaivedaṃ sarvam' (chā.7.25.2) `brajhaivedaṃṃ sarvam (viśvam)' (mu.2.2.1) iti chāndoog‌yamuṇḍakavākyayorapi tajjatvādināpi sāmānādhikaraṇyavyapadeśa iti tātparyaṃ varṇanīyam | yadyapi ubhayatra `ātmaivādhastāt, purastāt brajha' ityādi sarvavyāptirheturupadiṣ‌ṭaḥ sarvasya ātmatvavyapadeśe athaāpi tat‌ra tenāyamapi hetuḥ samucceeyaḥ | idaṃ bodhyam-śāstreṣu yatra kvāpi sāmānādhikaraṇyena nirdeśe heturipadiṣṭaḥ, tatra tena saha tajjatvādiheturapi samuccetavyaḥ | yatra tu nopadiiṣiṭaḥtatrāyameva hetubaudh‌yaḥ | śrutyādibodhitahetuparityāge kāraṇābhāvāt‌, tattyāgasyāyayuktatvācca | svarūpābhede tāturyaṃ tu na kalpanīyayamiti | asti cātraivārthe viṣṇapurāṇavacanamupabṛṃhaṇam | tathā hi, tatra,
viṣṇoḥ sakāśādudbhūtaṃ jagattatraiva ca sthitam |
sthitisaṃyamakartā'sau jagato'to jagacca saḥ ||
(1-bhā. 1-aṃ. 1-a.35)
ityuktam | tasyāyamarthaḥ-jagat prapañcaḥ, viṣṇoḥ sakāśāt nārāyaṇāt utpannam udabhūt, tatraiva viṣṇāneva sthitaṃ ca prasaye līnaśca, ca yataḥ, asau viṣṇuḥ, jagataḥ prapañcasya, sthatisaṃyamakartā saṃyamo layaḥ, idamupalakṣaṇaṃ janmano'pi, janmasthitilayakartā, ataḥḥ, jagat saḥ tadadhinam | atra jagato hareḥ sakāśāt janmādikaṃ bhavatīti nirūpya `jagacca sa' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena vyavahārakararaṇāt , tādṛśavyavahāraṃ prati tajjatvādirheturiti vpaṣṭamuktaṃ bhavati |
kecitu-khasuśabdo niṣedhe alaṃkhalvoḥ pratiṣedhayoḥ pracāṃ ktvā' (pā.sū.3.4.18) iti sūtre sutrakṛtā tasya pratiṣedhārthakatvokteḥ | eyamarthaḥ-idaṃ sarvaṃ brajha khalu habrajha na | kiṃ tarhi ityata āha-tajjalāniti | tajjaṃ tassīnaṃ tadanamiti | ayamāśayaḥ- idaṃ sarvaṃ brajhakāryameva na tu brajhavat svatantraṃ, vayaṃ sarve kryakoṭipraviṣṭā eveti tadadhinā eveti jñātvā svātantrayābhimānaṃ parityajya śāntaḥ sansarvakāraṇatvena brajhopāsīteti vadanti |
eetena `ātmaivedaṃ sarvam' ati śruterātmanaḥ sarvatādātmyamartha iti kṛtvā, `dravyaṃ hi nityamākṛtiranityā' iti vaspaśāhnikasthamahābhāṣyasya `asatyopādhyavacchinnaṃ brajhatattvaṃ dravyapadāvātyam, brajhadarśanena gotvajāterapi asattvāt anityatvam `ātmaivedam'iti śruteḥ iti kaiyaṭakṛtavyākhyānaṃ cintyam | tacchrutescathārthakatvā bhāvāt | tadbhīṣyasya brajhataditarayornityatvāsatyatvapratipipādayiṣayā'pravṛtteḥ | tathā hi--`atha gaukityatra kaḥ śabda' atyādibhāṣye śabdārthatvena prasakteṣu caturṣu jātyādiṣu kiṃ jātiḥ padārtha ati darśanāśrayaṇena siddhe śabde arthe saṃbandhe' iti vigrahaḥ triyate, uta `vyaktiḥ padārtha' iti darśavāśrayeṇeti praśne `jātiḥ padārtha iti pakṣāśrayaṇenāyaṃ vagraha ityabhiprāyeṇa `ākṛtimutyāha' ityukatvā saiva nityetyuktam, tḥ `dravya eva padārtha' iti paśre'pi dravyasya nityatvaṃ susādhamiti `atha vā dravya eva padārtha' iti bhāṣyaṃ pravṛttam; atra mate kāṣṭhāmalabhamāne bhāṣyakāraḥ phūrvaṃ prakrantaṃ `jātiḥ padrtha' iti pakṣamāśritya `ākṛtāvapi' iti āha-tasyā nityatvaṃ rathamiti praśyane `yasmin tattvaṃ na vihanyate tannityam' iti nityalakṣaṇamabhidhāya, `tadbhāvastattvam, ākṛtāvapi tattvaṃ na vihanyata' iti ākṛterapi ntyatvaṃ sādhitam | tasyāyaṃ bhāvaḥ āśrayavyakti (dravya) nāśaḥ sarvapratyakṣasiddhaḥ tadāśritajānāśastu durnirūpaḥ pratyakṣasiddha dravyāntare tasyāḥ sadbhāvāt iti | yadyapi ākṛtipadaṃ jātivyañjakāvasaṃsthanavuiśeṣavāci, athāpi atra tadvyaṅgyajātiparaṃ bodhyam | evaṃ pakṣadvayamupapādya kimetenā'prasaktavicāreṇa nā'smākaṃ prakṛte nityā'nityavastuvivekena sādhyaṃ kimapi | jātidravyayayormadhye yannityaṃ manyase tadāśrayaṇena eṣa vigraha iti vadāma ityabhiprāyakeṇa `atha vā-kiṃ na ityādi bhāṣyeṇa pūrvabhāṣye'nāsthāsūcanāt , bhāṣye brajhajagatoḥ prasakterevābhāvāt `brajhatatvaṃ brajhadarśanena gotvādijāterasattavādanityatvam' ityādi vyākhyānamayuktam | kiṃ ca upakramopasaṃhārayorākṛternityatvamabhipretaṃ bhāṣyakṛtā | ataḥ jāteranityatvābhidhānaṃ bhāṣyaviruddham | kiṃ ca ---`athavā dravya eva padārtha' ityādi bhāṣyaṃ na siddhāntinaḥ ataḥtadbhāṣyasya tathā'rthavarṇanaṃ kuto hetoḥ kṛtaṃmiti cintyam |
kiṃ ca yadidaṃbhāṣyakārasaṃmataṃ tarhi asya bhāṣyasya kaiyaṭakṛtavyākhyādiśā `brajhaikameva nityam' iti siddhāntisaṃmataprameyapratipādanaparatvaṃ vaktavyam | tataśca, vastuviśeṣasya nityānityatvasādhanena kimasmākaṃ prakṛte bhaviṣyati ityāśayena pūrvabhāṣyenāsthāsūcanāya pravattasya `kiṃ na etena' ityādyuttarabhāṣyasyānutthitiḥ syāt | siddhāntaprameyabodhakabhāṣye'nāsthāsūcanāyogāt | kiṃ ca kaiyaṭarītyā brajhātiriktasyāsatve śabdasya, tatsaṃbandhasya ca śaśaviṣāṇavadasatvena brajhaṇaḥ śabdena saṃbandhasya durnirūutvāt, śabdārthasaṃbandhasyaivāsaṃbhavā pattyā tanmūlakavicāro'pi asaṃbhavaduktikaḥ syāditi kaiyaṭavyākhyānaṃ vārtikabhāṣyaviruddhamiti cintyameva | kiṃ ca jātasphoṭameva siddhāntayadbhiḥ kaiyaṭādibhirjāterbrajharūpatāsvīkārāt tasyā anityatvoktirayuktā | nāgeśartu paramārthadṛṣṭyā sarvamavāstavameva yāvadvyavahāraṃ vartamānatvena tu vutyatvavyavahkaḥ | evaṃ ca vyāvahārikanityatvaṃ brajhetasyeti bhāṣyatātparyamutyāha, tanna, evaṃkalpane sūtre bhāṣye ca pramāṇānupalambhāt , pareṣāṃ prakirayāvalambanena viṣayavicārasya padārthasvīrārasya paraddhāntasya etacchāstrakṛtsiddāntatvena vyavasthāyā ścānucitatvāt | `bhedasvarūpameva durnirūpam' iti nañartavādee siddāntayiṣyan ayaṃ bhedanibandhanapāramārtikavyāvahārikāvasthādvayaṃ kalpayituṃ kathaṃ śaknotitī vibhāvyam |
`vāsudevārjunābhyāṃ vun' iti sūtrabhāṣyābhyāṃ vāsudevasya sarvottamatvatātparyakābhyāṃ sarvasya bhagavatsvarūpatvakalpanaṃ, bhagavato vā sarvarūpatvakalpavamayuktamiti siddatvena tanmūlakasarvāsattvasya (mīthyātvasya) sūtrakārādyabhiprāyaviruddatvāvagamāt kaiyaṭaḍakṛtavyākhyānamanādaraṇīyameva | etna--`apratiṣiddhamanumataṃ bhavati' iti nyāyena kaiyaṭādyuktamācāryasaṃmatamiti kalpanaṃ tu kukalpanam, vāsudevasyottamatvamabhiprayatā pāṇininā vāsudevasya sarvottamatvavirodhi sarvātmatvaṃ pratiṣiddhameveti uktanyāyānavatārāt | kiṃ ca uktanyāyena ācāryapāṇunisamakālar,irāddāntasya tatpūrvakālācāryakṛtasiddhāntasya vā ācāryasaṃmatatvaralpanaṃ yuktam, na tu auttarakālikapuruṣakṛtasiddhāntasya | ata eva pāṇineḥ vyāsoktaṃ sarvaṃ saṃmataṃ, tasya pāṇineḥ pūrvatvāt tena vyāsoktasyāpratiṣiddatvāt | na caivaṃ ktyāyanīyasiddhāntasya | ata eva, `na kvādeḥ' (pā.sū.7.3.59) iti sītre `yadyapi sūtramate viparītaṃ prāptam' ityuktaṃ kaumudyām | `yathoottaraṃ munīnāṃ pramamāṇyam' iti pūrvamunivacanasyāprāmāṇyakalpanaṃ tu iṣṭyādeḥ pramamāṇyavyavasthāpakārvācīnavaiyākaraṇagoṣṭhīnivinodamātraphalakam | ata eva ktyāyanokteṣu bhāṣyakārāḥ sūtrakārāṇāmanugraho'stīti tatra tatra sautraṃ jñāpakamupanyasyanti | evaṃ ca, munivacanānāṃ visaṃvāde `darśanabheda eva nyāyya' iti veditavyam . etna `śabdajñānānupātī vastuśūnyo vikalpaḥ (pā.yo.1.9) iti pātañjalpākhyavṛttirityaṅgikāryam, tena padārtasya mithyātvaṃ sidhyatīti mañjūṣādau nāgeśoktiḥ parāstā ḥ patañjaleḥ pāṇinyācāryādarvācīnatvāt | na ca vyāsooktasya pāṇinisaṃmatatvanirūpaṇena yogasūtrabhāṣyasya vyāsakṛtatvāt pātañjalaṃ prameyaṃ sarvaṃ pāṇinisaṃmatamutu uktaṃ bhavatīti vācyam, patañjalimate jagato mithyātvābhāvāt | vyāsapadena parāśarasūnooreva vivakṣitatvena yoogasūtrabhāṣyakṛtoo vyāsasya pārāśarthādanyatvācca | paraśarasuto vyāsa eva yogasītrabhāṣyakāra iti tu bhrāntapralāpaḥ | ata eva patañjalirmahābhāṣye svapraṇītamapi itaṃ sūtraṃ kvāpi nodājahāra | etena śekhare `aco'ntyādi ṭi' (pā.sū. 1.1.64) iti sūtre `vastuta' ityārabhya `śabdajñāne' ti yogasūtropanyāsena kṛtaṃ viṣayavyavasthāpanaṃ cintyam | samudāyābhāvasthale ṭisaṃjñā tu vyāpadeśi vadbhāvena, ityaṃ sūtrayojanayā vā bhavatīti jñeyam | yojanā tu antyādītyatra antyeti bhinnaṃ ca padaṃ laptaprathamākam | acāṃ madhye yo'ntyaḥ tadādisamudāyaḥ so'ntyaśca varṇaḥ ṭisejña iti | evaṃ ca śakandhurityādau śaketi kakārottarasya kevalākārasyāpi sā saṃjñā sidhyati | adhikamupariṣṭāt vakṣyāmaḥ |
īśajīvajaḍānāṃ ca nābhedastasya sidhyati |
sāmānādhikaraṇyena nirdeśe'pīti bhāṣitam ||
vāsudevasyaiva sarvaśāstraviṣayatā
nanu śrutau sūtre ca brajha pratipādyate, ata evoraktamabhiyuktaiḥ `bajheti vedāntina' iti | gītāyāṃ ti vāsudevaḥ sarvaiḥ tīrthakaraistathā vyākhyātatvāt | tasmāt śrutyādau sarvatra vāsudevaḥ pratipādyata iti kathamutyata iti cet, atra vadanti `adharmeṇa paribhūyamāne dharmepravardhamāne cādharme jagataḥ sthitiṃ paripipālayiṣurādikartā nārāyaṇākhyoo viṣṇurbhaiāmasya brajhaṇo brājhaṇatvasya ca rakṣaṇārthaṃ devakyāṃ vasudevādaṃśena kṛṣṇaḥ kila saṃbabhūva, tadidaṃ gītāśāstraṃ samastavedārthasārasaṃgrahabhūtamimaṃ dviprakāraṃ dharmaṃ niḥśreyamaprayojanaṃ paramārthatatvaṃ ca vāsudevākhyaṃ parabrajhābhidheyabhūtaṃ vyañcayat......gītāśāstram' iti gītāvataraṇaśāṃkarabhāṣyagranthena, pañjadaśe'dhyāye `sarvo|़pi gītāśāstrārtho'sminnadhyāye samāsenoktaḥ, na kevalaṃ gītāśāstrārtha eva kiṃ tu sarvaśca vedārtha iha parisamāpta iti--
iti gujhatamaṃ śāstramidamuktaṃ mayā'nagha |
etadbuddhvā buddimānsyātkṛtakṛtyaśca bhārata || (gī.15.20)
iti ślokīyaśāṃkarabhāṣyeṇa, gītāstheśvarādipadānāṃ `nārāyaṇo vāsudevo viṣṇuḥ' iti vyākhyānaparaśāṃkarabhāṣyeṇa, `īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānām' iti śloke `sarvaprāṇināmīśanaśīloo nārāyaṇa' iti īśvarapadavyākhyānaparaśāṅkarabhāṣyeṇa, īdṛśārthakena tadīyānāṃ madhusūdana sarasvatīprabhṛtīnāṃ vyākhyāgranthena, anyeṣāṃ ca tīrthakarāṇāṃ bhāṣyādi granāthena ca gītāyāṃ vāsudevaḥ pratipādyate, gītārtha eva sarvavedārtha iti sarvapratipannam | na ca vyākhyānamātreṇa kathamevaṃ nirṇaya iti vācyam, `vyākhyānato viseṣapratipattiḥ' iti nyāyena mahatāṃ vyākhyānasyaiva prameyanirṇāyakatvāt | tataśca `sarvavedārthasārasaṃgrahabhūtamitadyā diśāṃkarabhāṣyāditaḥ, `omityekākṣaraṃṃ brajha' (gī.8.13) `paraṃ brajha paradhāma, (gī.10.12) `anādmatparaṃ brajha' (gī. 13.12) ityādau bhagavatā vyāsena brajhaśabdena gītāpratipādyasya vyapadeśāt, `vedaiśca sarvairahameva vedyaḥ' (gī. 15.15) iti gītācāryokteśca, vāsudevasya brajhānanyatvena śrutisūtragītānāmekaviṣayakatvasiddhyā vāsudevābhidho nārāyaṇaḥ sarvaśāstraviṣaya iti tattvaṃ susthamiti |
kiṃ ca sūtrakāro bādarāyaṇaḥ `brajhavidāpnoti param' (tai.u.2.1) `tamevaṃ vidvānamṛta iha bhavati' (tai.ā.13) `tarati śokamātmavit' (chā. 7.1.3) ityādi śrutibodhitaṃ brajhajñānaṃ nḥśreyasasya paramaṃ sādhanamiti matvā brajhamīmāsāyāṃ purūṣārakthahetubhūtātmasākṣātkārasādhanabrajhavicārarūpajñāsāyāḥ kartavyatām, `ayā'too brajhajījñāsā' (bra.sū. 1.1.1) ityādimena sūtreṇa vidhāya, `kiṃ tadbrajha' iti tatsvarūpajījñāsāyāṃ śrṛṅge karṇe vā gṛhītvā nirdeṣṭumaśakyatayā lakṣaṇamukhena tatstavarūpaṃ bubodhayiṣuḥ, `paridṛśyamānasya samāstasya jagataḥ yasmāt sṛṣṭisthitilayādi bhavati tad bajha' ityarthakena `janmādyasya yataḥ' (bra.sū.11.2) iti dvitīyena sūtraṇa jagatkāraṇaṃ brajheti tallakṣaṇamabhidhāya, sthiteḥ pālanādyanekavyāpārarūpatvena tatra niyamanasyāpyantarbhāvāt tasya `yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣiṭhanaṃ' (bṛ.5.7.3) ityādyāntaryāmibrājhaṇe sūtranāmakavāyuniṣṭhatayā śravaṇāt, pālanaṃ na brajhaṇo lakṣaṇaṃṃ bhavitumarhatīti ākṣepaṃ pariharan `antaryāmyadidaivādiṣu taddarmavyapadeśāt' ḥbra.sū. 1.2.18) iti sūtreṇa vājasaneyake pṛthivyādyantaryāmitayoktaṃ vasti jagatkāraṇatvenoktaṃ brajhaiveti siddhāntatanān | sūtrārtastu tattu brajhaiva, taddarmavyapadeśāt `eṣa ca aātmā' iti śrutau brajhadharmasya sarvaniyamanasya uktatvāt iti | idaṃ bodhyam --atra kāramatvena nirṇetaṃ brajhaiva ṇantaryāmubrāṅaṇanākyapaṭhitā''tmapadāvācyam | ataḥ pālanaṃ brajhaniṣṭhamiti tasya tallakṣaṇatvamiti na kaściddo,ḥ | tatropaniṣadi vāyipadena ātmaivotyate, vāyupadavācyasya sarvalokādāratvokteḥ, tacca paramātmana eva saṃbhavati nānyasyeti sūtrakṛdāśaya iti | ata eva trayo'pi bhāṣyakārā asyādhikaraṇasya `eṣa ta' iti vākyaṃ viṣayavākyatayodāhṛtya etaccatyukto'ntaryāmī jagatkāraṇaṃ brajhaiveti sūtrakṛtā nirdaritamiti ekakaṇṭhā vadanti | tatra śaṃkarācāryā upaniṣadbhāṣye---`eṣa ta' iti śruteḥ `eṣa ātmā nārāyaṇaḥ mama sarveṣā ca antaryāmī' iti te akṣarārthamanarṇayan |
kiṃ ca--`utpattyasaṃbhavāt' (bra.su. 2.2.42) iti sūtre `bhagavānevaiko vāsudevo nirañjano jñānasvarūpaḥ paramārthatattvam, tatra yattā vaducyate yo'sau nārāyaṇaḥ paro'vyaktāt prasiddaḥ paramātmā iti.... tat na nirākriyata' ityāhu śaṃkarācāryāḥ |
kiṃ ca prathamenādhyāyena sarvajñasya bhagavataḥ paramātmanaḥ samnvayamukhena jagatkāraṇatvādike pratipādite tadasahamānaiḥ sākyādibhiḥ kāpilasmṛtyādivirodhāt uktamasaṃgatamiti pratyavasthāne tatparihārāya pravṛtte dvitīyasminnadhyāye ādyepāde kāpilasmṛtinirodhaparahārāya pravṛttaṃ `smṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṅgāditi cenna, anyasmṛtyanavakāsadoṣaprasaṅgāt' (bra.sū. 2.1.1) iti prathamamadhikaraṇaṃ vyākurvāṇāḥ śaṃkarācāryāḥ śrutyanusāriprabalasmṛtibasāt nārāyaṇa eva jagatkāraṇamiti siddāntayanti | idaṃ tadīyatatsūtrabhāṣyam --brajhaṇaḥ kāraṇatsvīkore prādānyena sokṣasādhanamadhikṛtya praṇītānāmananyaprayodanatayo niravakośānāṃ kipilasmṛtīnāmānarthakyaprasaṃgaḥ | atastadavirodhena vedāntavākyāni vyākhyeyāmi | tatra hi--pradhānaṃ jagataḥ kāraṇamityuktam, etaḥ pradhānameva jagataḥ kartṛ na brajheti sāṃkhyānāmākṣepe, tasyāyaṃ samādhiḥ--na tadyuktam, anyasmṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṅgat | īśvarakāsaṇatāvādinyaḥ śrutyanusāriṇyo bahvyaḥ smṛtaya upalabhyante | pradhānasya kāraṇatve tāsāmānarthakyaṃ prasajyeta | vipratipattau śrutyanu sāriṇya eva pramāṇam | tāścodāhariṣyāmaḥ --
ataśca saṃkṣepamimaṃ śruṇudhvaṃ nāsāyaṇaḥ sarvamidaṃ purāṇaḥ |
sa sargakāle ca karooti sarvaṃ prayāṇakāle ca tadatti bhūyaḥ ||
iti purāṇe, gītāyāṃ ca ---
ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ prasayastathā
iti, paramātmānameva ca prakṛtya āpastambaḥ paṭhati---
tasmāktāyāḥ (jīvāḥ) prabhavanti sarve sa mūlaṃ śāśvatikaḥ sa nicya (dha.sū. 1.8.13.2) itīti |
ayamatra bhāṣyatātparyasāraḥ --śrutsūtrādīvāmarthasaṃśaye itihāsapurāṇānusāreṇaiva tadartho nirṇetavyaḥ | tataḥ śrutyanusāri ituhāsapurāṇavacanaṃ prabalaṃ pramāṇam | `ataśca saṃkṣeparam' ityādi tu tādṛśaṃ pramāṇam | atra ca saṃkṣepamityuktyā samastaśāstrārthaḥ kroḍīkṛtaḥ | ataḥ sarveṣāṃ kāraṇavākyānāmetadanusāreṇārtho vācyaḥ | tataśca `yato vā imāni' ityādadikāraṇavākyānāṃ nārāyaṇasyaiva samastajagatkāraṇatve tātparyam | tatratyātmabrajhādipadānāṃ nārāyaṇaparatvameva | pradhāvādināṃ kāraṇatā vādastu śrutyādiviruddhaḥ | tataśca nārāyaṇa eva paratattvamiti | etatsarvamabhipretyaiva satnaprabhākhye śākaraśārīrakabhāṣyavyākhyāne `rāmanāmni pare dhāmni kṛtsnāmnāyasamanvaya' iti samanvayādhikaraṇanigamanaṃ kṛtam |
paramārtasārakṛtaḥ śeṣanāgācāryasya, aṇubhāṣyakāraṇāṃ vallabhācāryaṇāṃ, śrīrāmānujamunivaryāṇāṃ śrīmatpūrṇaprajñamunipuṅgavānāṃ ca vāsudeva eva paratattvaṃ, jagatkāraṇaṃ ceti siddhānta ityatra na kācidvipratipattiḥ |
`ataśca saṃkṣepam' iti ślokasya tu ayamarthaḥ | ataḥ anatnatvācchāstrāṇāṃ purapaṣāyuṣaśca parimitatvācca hetoḥ --
anantaśāstraṃ bahuveditavyamalpaśca klo bahavaśca vighnāḥ |
yatsārabhūtaṃ tadupāsitavyaṃ haṃso yathā kṣīramivāmbumiśram ||
ityakteḥ | saṃkṣepaṃ samastaśāstrārthasārasaṃgraham. imaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ, śrṛṇudhvam śrṛṇuta, ārṣamātmanepadam | nārāyaṇaḥ madhyekṣīrasamudraṃ bhujaṅgabhogatalpamadhiśayānaḥ śriyaḥpatiḥpurāṇaḥ ādipuruṣaḥ saḥ nārāyaṇaḥ sarke sṛṣṭikāle idaṃ sarvam paridṛśyamānaṃ samastaṃ jagat, karoti sṛjati, idamupalakṣaṇaṃ sthiterapi, prayāṇakāle pralayasamaye ca tatsarvaṃ sṛṣṭaṃsamastaṃ jagat, atti nāśayati, bhūyaḥ punarapi, evamiti śeṣaḥ, puvaḥ evameva karoti pratukalpam `dhātā yatāpūrvamakalpayatṭa' (ṛ.saṃ 8.8.48) iti śruteriti | tātparyaṃ suvyaktam | idaṃ jñeyam--atrānyatra ca sūtre `smṛtī' tyukteḥ, śaṃkarabhāṣye purāṇanacanopanyāsāt, adhikaraṇapūrvapakṣasiddhāntayoḥ upabṛṃhaṇaṃ purāṇavacanamupanyasanīyamiti sūtrabhāṣyakārāṇāmāśayaḥ | na ca smṛtipadaghaṭite sūtre tadupanyāsoo yuktaḥ, atādṛśe sūtra tadapanyāso na nyāyya iti vātyam,ta, `śrutestu śabdamūsatvāt'(bra.2.1.28) itii sūtre smṛtipadāghaṭite, tathā cāhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ
acintyāḥ khalu ye bhāvā na tāstarkeṇa yojayet |
prakṛtibhyaḥ paraṃ yacca tadacintyasya lakṣaṇam ||
iti śāṃkarabhāṣye purāṇavacanopanyāsena tathākaspanāyogāt | na ca `amātrascaturto'vyavahryaḥ prapañcopaśamaḥ śivo'dvaitaḥ, evamoṅkāra ātmaiṃva saṃvisatyātmanā''tmānaṃ, ya evaṃ veda' (mā.12) iti turīyasyātmanaḥ śiva iti viseṣaṇāt `nāntaḥ prajñaṃ na bahuḥ prajña... śāntaṃ śivamadvaitaṃ caturtaṃ manyante' (mā.8) iti mantre ca śivamityukteśca, śivasyaiva śāktraviṣayatvaṃ yuktamiti vātyam | dvādase mantreśiva ityasya maṅgalakara iti śrīśaṃrakācāryairvyākhyānatvāt, saptame śivamityasya pariśuddamiti ānandagirīye vyākhyātatvāt tasya rudraparatvābhāvena śivasya śāstraviṣayatvakalpakatvābhāvāt |
gītāyāṃ viśvarūpādhyāye `aiśvaraṃ rūpam' iti vākyaśravaṇamapi na gītādīnāṃ śivaparatvasādhakam | tathā hi---
evametadyathā''tta tvamātmnaṃ parameśvara |
dra,ṭumucchāmi te rūpamaisvaraṃ puruṣāttama || (gī. 11.3)
iti viśvarūpadarśanaprārthanāśloke `eśvaraṃ rūpam' ityasya `jñānaiśvaryabalaśaktivīryatejobhiḥ saṃpannaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ rūpam' iti saṃkarācāryairvyānāt prārtanāvākyāsya vaiṣṇavarūpadidṛkṣāviṣayakatvasiddhyā ---
evamuktvā tatoo rājanmahāyogośvaro hariḥ |
darśayāmāsa pararthaya paramaṃ rūpamaisvaram || (gī.11.9)
iti `prartitamarthamapūrayat' ityarthakaslokasthasya `aiśvaraṃ rūpam' ityāsyāpi praśnavākyābyākhyānurodhena jñānaiśvaryādiviśiṣṭaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ rūpamityarthasyaiva yuktatvāt | kiṃ ca `paśya me pārtha rūpāṇu' (gī. 11.5) `na tu māṃ śakyase draṣṭum' (gī.11.8) iti darsayiṣyamāṇarūusya bhagavatā kṛṣṇena svīyatvasvābhinnatvanirūpaṇāt viśvarūpasya vaiṣṇavatvasiddhiriti jñeyam | etena, aiśvaramiti padaṃ śaivamiti vyākhyāya viśavarūpādhyāyasya śivaparatvaṃ prakaspya, etadupalakṣitaṃ śāstraṃ sarvaṃ śivaparaṃ, śina eva paratattvamiti rāmāyaṇabhāratasāra saṃgrahādau koṣāṃcidadvaitināṃ śaṃkārācryamatānuyāyināṃ nirūpaṇaṃ durāgrahamūsakaṃ, svācāryatātparyānanurodhi ca | śiva eva paraṃ tattvamiti śaivānāṃṃ vādastu śaṃkarabhāṣyāditi eva samāhitaḥ | bhāratādīnāṃ vāsudevaparatve upakramoktaṃ kāraṇamatrāpyanusandeyam | evaṃ ca jagatkāraṇaṃ vastu nārāyaṇābhidhaṃ paratattvamiti sarvaśāstratātparyam | vistarastu śrīvijayindrayatīndrapraṇītāyāṃ paratattvaprakāśikāyām ---
dāsabhūtāḥ svataḥ sarvepyātmānaḥ paramātmanaḥ |
eto'hamapi te dāsa iti matvā namāmyaham ||
ityādi śivāvākyodāharaṇena vāsudevasya paratvaṃ vyavasthāpakaiḥ śrīvedāntadeśikaiḥ praṇīte rahasyatrayasāre paradevatāpārāmārthyādhikāre ca draṣṭavyaḥ |
kiṃ ca, bhagavatā vyāsena---
nato'smyahaṃ tvākhisahetuhetuṃ nārāyaṇaṃ pūruṣamādyamavyamam |
yannābhijākājaravindakosādbrajhā''virāsīdyata eṣa lokaḥ ||
(bhāga.10.40.1)
ityādinā bhāgavatādau prapañcanirmācaturacaturānanakāraṇatayā sarvakāraṇatvaṃṃ paratattavaṃ ca nārāyaṇasya prapa़citam | nanu śivasya paramātmābhinnatayāṣa़sti jagatkāraṇateti vadāma itadi cet, acyate kimidaṃ muktau uta saṃsāritve | nādyaḥ, tatra paramātmasvarūpatvena tasya nirdharmakatvasvabhāvāt jagadyvyāpārasyaivāsaṃbhavena `jagadvyāpāravarjam' iti paratattvādanyasya aprasaktajagatsṛṣṭyādivyāpārapratiṣedhavacanavaiyarthyāt | etena muktāvapi muktasya paramātmabheda eva sūtrakṛtsaṃmata iti bhāti | na ca svarūpakīrtanaparaṃ sūtramiti vācyam, varjamityuktyā tadbhinnavyāpārāntarāstitvalābhāt tathākalpanāyogāt | dvitīye tu, tadā abhedasyaivābhāvena evaṃ vaktumaśakyam | kaiścadapi tīrthakaraiḥ śivādeḥ kāraṇatāyā anaṅgīkārācca nedaṃ yuktam | kiṃ ca, brajhamīmāṃsākṛtaiva `patyurasāmañjasyāt' (2.2.37) ityānena paśupatimatanirasamanamukhena paśupate rudrasya jagatkāraṇatā pratiṣiddhā | na ca nimittakāraṇatvamātraṃ tena pratiṣiddam, ubhayavidhakāraṇatvaṃ tu tasyāstīti tātparyamiti vātyam, `janmādyasya yataḥ' (1.12) iti sūtreṇa brajhaṇo yādṛśaṃ kāraṇatvaṃ bodhite tat rudrasya nāstītyarthasyaiva yuktatvena evaṃ kalpane mānābhāvāt | bhagavadguṇakīrtanena diritakṣayamapekṣamāṇā mahānto'pi kavayo'mumeva śāstrārtha bodhayanti | tatā hi---
nārāyaṇāya nalināyatalocanāya
nāmāvaśeṣitamahābalivaibhavāya |
nānātarācaravidhāyakajanmadeśa-
nābhīpuṭāya puruṣāya namaḥ parasmai || (caṃ. rā. 1.16)
viramati mahākalpe nābhīpathaikaniketana-
stribhuvanapuraḥ śilpī yasya pratikṣaṇamātmabhūḥ |
kimadhikaraṇaṃ kīdṛk kasya vyavasthitarityasā-
vudaramaviśaddṛṣṭuṃ tasmai jagannidhaye namaḥ || (anargha.2)
iti | nanu tarhi rudrasya kāraṇatābodhakavākyānāṃ kā gatiriti cet, ucyate--tatra rudrāpadasya tadantaryāmivāsudevāparatvena tānyapi vākyāni nārāyaṇasya kāraṇatvaparāṇyeveti | etena, śabdabrajhaṇo jagatkāramatvamutu mañjūṣādau nāgeśādyuktaṃ brajhavitsiddhāntaviruddhaṃ pāṇinyādyanabhimataṃ caiveti veditavyam |
śrutyādipratipādyastu vāsudevaḥ paraḥ pumān |
iti śaṃkarabhāṣyādipramāṇairnirṇayaḥ kṛtaḥ ||
nimittakāraṇatvavicāraḥ
sarvaśāltrārthasārasaṃgrāhukāyām `ataścasaṃkṣepamaṃ' iti śrutyanusāriṇyāṃ smṛtau `sa sargakāle pariṇāmameti, vivartatāmeti sa sargakose' iti vā vapaktavye `sa sargakāle ca karoti sarvam' ityuktyā ghaṭādeḥ kulālādiriva bhagavānnārāyaṇo jagatoo nimittakāraṇaṃ, na tu dvividhamupādānakāraṇamiti śrutismṛtitātparyamityavagantavyam | evaṃ ca `yato vā' (tai.u.3.1) ityādau `yata' iti pañcamī nimittārthikaiveti śrīvyādibhirvyākhyātaṃ mataṃ, sagrahaślokasya tatkṛtatvāt | pāṇinipatañjalyorapi idameva matamabhimatam, tābhāyāṃ brajhaṇo nimittakāraṇatāyā aniṣedhāt. upādānakāraṇatāyā anabhidhānācca |
nanu, `janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ' (pī.sū.1.4.30) ityātra prakṛtipadaṃ prayuñjānaḥ pāṇinirbrajhaṇa upādānaprayogamātreṇa evamabhiprāyavarṇanāyogāt, evaṃkalpanasyottamatvāvirogamātreṇaevaṃkalpanasyottamatvāvirodhitvāt, ubhayavidhakāraṇasaṃgrahāya tatpadaprayoga iti vaktaṃ śakyatvāt | etena ye tu vaiyākaraṇasaṃgrahāya tatpadaprayoga iti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | etna ye tu vaiyākaraṇā brajhaṇa upādānatvaṃ vadanti te pāṇunerāśayamananurundhānā eva | pāṇinestu ayamāśayaḥ --`yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante' (tai.u.3.1) `ātmāna ākāśāḥ saṃbhūtaḥ' (tai.u.2.3)`sarvaṇi ha vā imāni bhūtānyākāśādeva samutpadyante' (chā.1.9.1) `tesmādetadbrajha nāmarūpamanna ca jāyate' (mu.1.1.9) ityādi sṛṣṭivākyeṣu katipayeṣu `yata' ityādi kāraṇavācakasya pañcamyantatvaśravaṇe'pi, naitānyeva sṛṣṭivākyāni śārtreṣu, api tu--`so'kāmayata bahusyāṃ prajāyeyeti sa tapo'tapyata sa tapastaptvā idaṃ sarvamasṛjata bahapasyāṃ prajāyeyeti sa tapo'tapyata sa tapastaptvā idaṃ sarvamasṛjata yadide kiṃ ca' (tai.u.2.3) `sa īkṣācakre kasmanvahamutkrānta ukrānto bhaviṣyāmi kasminnahaṃpratiṣṭhite pratiṣṭhāsyāmi sa prāṇamasṛjaka prāṇācchraddām' (ṣa.pra.6.3) `ātmā vā idameka evāgra āsīt nānyatviṃcanamiṣat sa aikṣata lokānnu sṛjā iti sa imān lokānasṛjata' (ai.u.1.1.3)`tedaikṣata bahusyāṃ prajāyeyeti tattijo'sṛjata, tatteja aikṣata bahusyāṃ prajāyeyetu tadapo'sṛjata tā āpa aikṣanta bahvyaḥ syāma prajāyemahīti tā annamasṛjanta'(chā.6.2.4) `seyaṃ devataikṣata hantāhamimāstistro devatā anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi' (chā.6...3.3) `yo brajhāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvaṃ yo vai vedāśca prahiṇoti tasmai | te ha devamātmabuddhaprakāśaṃ mumukṣurvai śaraṇamahaṃ praradye' (śe.u.6.18) `sa viśvakṛt viśvavidātmayoniḥ' (śe|u.6.16) asṛjatsalilaṃ pūrvaṃ sa ca nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ | tatastu bhagavān toye brājhaṇamasṛjatsvayam (bhāra.sabhā.43.3)
sarvabhūtāni kauntaya prakṛtiṃ yānti māmikām |
kalpakṣaye punastāni kalpādau visujāmyaham || (gī.9.7)
prakṛtiṃ svāmavaṣṭabhya visṛjāmi punaḥ punaḥ |
bhūtagrāmamimaṃ kṛtsnamavasaṃ prakṛtervaśāt |. (gī.9.8)
`cāturvarṇyaṃ mayā sṛṣṭaṃ guṇakarmavibhāgasaḥ' (gī.4.13) ityādinī kāraṇavācakaprathamāntatṛtīyāntapadaghaṭitāni sṛṣṭivākyāni bahulamupalabhāyante | sarveṣāmapi sṛṣṭivākyānāṃ bhagavān vāsudeva eva kāraṇamityatra tātparyam | tacca nimittaṃ vā upādāuṃ veti viṣayasastu vastusvabhāvaṃ vijñāya parihartavyaḥ | yadi vastu pariṇāmi kryātmanāā apariṇāmi tadā nimittakāraṇameva, yathā-daṇḍo ghaṭasya | vāsudevasya tu acetanātmanā vā anyacetanātmanā vā pariṇāme nābhyupaiti kaścidapi tathāpariṇāmasya uttamatvavirodhitvāt | to nimittakāraṇameva vāsudevo jagataḥ | `yato vā' ityādi pañcamī tu `daṇḍād ghaṭo jāyate putrāt pramodo jāyata' ityādāviva nimuittakāraṇavācikaiva sūtre prakṛtadipadaṃ kāraṇaparamiti |
vṛttikṛttu jānannevāmumāśayamācāryāṇāṃ `janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ' (pā.1.4.30) iti sūtre prakṛtipadaṃ hetuparamiti vyācaṣṭe | canikartuḥ jāyamānasya, prakṛtiḥ hetuḥ apādānamiti sūtrarthaḥ | haradatto'pi mañjaryām `anye tu' ityādinā prakṛtipadasya kāraṇamātraparatvādeva `putrātpramodo jāyata' ityādau putrapadottaramanena pañcamītyāha | putrastu pramodasya nopādānamapi tu nimittamiti spaṣṭaṃ sarveṣām |
nanu--manoramāyāṃ `jānakartuḥ' iti sūtravyākhyāvasaro `prakṛti padamupādānamātraparamiti bhāṣyakaiyaṭamatamityuktam, tatra `bhāṣṭakaiyaṭa' iti pratīkamādāya `idameva vyāyyam' ityādinā prakṛtipadasya tatraiva prasiddhiḥ, pratyākhyānabhāṣyācca | kiṃ ca --vṛttikṛnmate kāraka ityasyā dhikṛtasya kāraṇasāmānyavācinooo nirdāraṇasaptamyantasyānvayo'saṃbhavī, na hi kāramānāṃ madhya upādānakāraṇanitivat kāramānāṃ madhye `kāraṇamiti saṃbhavati' ityantena grantena hetuparaṃ prakṛtirheturiti vyākhyānaṃ lokaprasiddhivirodhabhāṣyavirodhānvayānupapattirūpado,tayagrastamiti ct, ucyate--`pradhāne prakṛtiḥ striyām, saṃsidduprakagṛtītvime, rājyāṅgāni prakṛtayaḥ, prakṛtiryoniliṅge ca' ityamarāt, `prakṛtirguṇasāmye cāmātyādisvabhāvayoḥ' `yonau liṅgapauravarge' iti medinīkośāt, `pabrakṛtyā niyātāḥ svayā' (gī.7.20) ityādau svabhāve, `prārthayāmāsa prāñjaliḥ prakṛtīḥ kṛtī' (ayo.8.2) iti campūrāmāyaṇe rājāyāṅgāmātyādiṣu, `ma kovalā prakṛtiḥ prayoktavyā nāpi kevalaḥ pratyaya' ityādiṣu pratyayapradagvṛttiśabdeṣu prayogadarśanāt `tatraiva prasidde' ritiprathamo heturvyabhicaritaḥ | tataśca--prakṛtipadasya nānārthakatvena avadhāraṇāyogaḥ | nanu--tatra sūtre prakṛtipadaṃ hetusāmānyaparaṃ vā viśeṣakāraṇaparaṃ vetisaṃbhāvitakalpayormadhye viśeṣakāraṇaparameva prasidderatyuktyā kāraṇasāmānyaparatvaṃ vyāvarttyate avadhāraṇeneti cejucyate --`tadarthaṃ vikagṛteḥ prakṛtau' (pā.sū.51.12) iti sūtrasthena `vikṛtipadasamabhivyāhārāt prakṛti padamupādānakāraṇaparam' iti vṛttigkanthena, `prakṛtivikṛtisabdau parasparasamabhivyāhārāt upādānatatkāryaparau' iti tatsūtrasthaśekharagrantena ca padāntarasamabhivyāhāra eva prakṛtipadasya kāraṇaviśeṣaparatvāvagamāt | prakṛte ca tathāvidhasamabhivyāhārabhāvena tathāvaktumaśakyamiti |
`pratyākhyāne' ti dvitīyo'pi hetuḥ pareṣāṃ nārthasādhakaḥ, tadbhāṣyatastadabhimatārtalābhāt | tathā hi --`janikartuḥ' iti sūtre hi `ayamapi yogaḥ śakyo vaktuṃ kataṃ gomayādvṛściko jāyate golomāvilomabhyo dūrvā jāyante' iti `apakrāmanti tāstebhyaḥ, yadyapakrāmanti kiṃ nātyantāyāpakramanti? santatatvāt | atha vā anyāśca anyāśca pradurbavanti' ityuktaṃ bhāṣye | `lookaprasiddimāśritya taducyate--loke hi yadyasmājjāyateityādinā kāramātkāryasya nirgame `vakṣajanyaphalasyeva' iti dṛṣṭāntaṃ darśayitvā `vaiśe,ikasāṃkyamatayornepādānakāraṇātkāryasya nirtama' ityāha kaiyaṭaḥ | atra bhāṣyakaiyyaṭayoḥ sautraprakṛtipadamupādānakāraṇaparamityatra na kiṃcidapi gamakamupalabhāyate | `nanu gomayādvṛściko jāyate, gosomabhyo dūrvā jāyanta' ityādāharaṇameva gamakamīti cet, na | `putrātpramodo jāyate' ityādīnāmapi anenopalakṣaṇāt | `na codāharaṇamādaraṇīyam' iti nyāyācca | ata eva tatra tatra udāhakaṇabhāṣyaṃ na parigaṇanaparaṃ, kiṃ cu udāharaṇapradarśanamātraparamiti vadanti | nanu tarhi tadeva bhāṣyakṛtoo dāhartavyamiti cet, na | svatantrāṇāmācāryāṇāṃ tathā paryanuyogāyogāt | itrodāharaṇe bodhanīyaviśeṣāṃśayadbhāvenaitadudāharaṇacca putre kāraṇe pramodasyādarśanena pitrādāveva taddarśanena ca tatra boddhasyāpakramaṇasya spaṣṭamupasambhena, dūrvāṇāṃ saṃtatatvāt viśloṣasadbhāve'pi kāraṇānnirgamasya spaṣṭamanupalambhena `dhruvam' ityanena kataṃ golomāderapādānatā syāditi saṃbhavantaṃ saṃśayaṃ nivartayituṃ `gosomabhya' ityādyudāharaṇam | nanu tarhi `gomayāt' ityādyudharaṇaṃ vyarthamiti cet, na | `khadvendromālendra' ityādibhāṣyavimarśane dvayordvayorevādoharaṇayoḥ pradarśanaṃ bhāṣyakṛccailīsiddhamityavagamāt | etena--`gomayādvṛściko jāyate' ityatra vṛścikasarīropādānabhūto yāvān gomayabhāgaḥ sa kāryarūpeṇa pariṇataḥ san kāramabhūtamahāgomayarāśerapakrānta iti apakramasya tatra spaṣṭamupalambhāt tadudāharaṇaṃ vyarthamiti parāstam . yathā gaumayarāsareḥ vṛścikaśarīkarūpeṇa pariṇato gomayabhāgo'pakrāntaḥ, na tathā gosomakāryadūrvāṇāṃ tebhāyo'pakrama iti vyatirekadṛṣṭāntapradarsanāya tadudāharaṇāt | ata eva `apakrāmanti tāktebhya nātyantāya apakrāmanti' ityādinā dūrvāvi,ya eva apakramaṇamupapāditam | astyatrāpakramaḥ, athāpi vṛścakaśarīravat na spaṣṭaṃ pṛthagupalabhyate kāramasaṃbandhasyāpi sadbhāvāt, kāraṇasaṃbandhe kāraṇaṃ tu aviccedaḥ, yathā bisānnissakato dīrghabhogabhogino bileśyupalabdhau aviccedo heturiti bhāvaḥ | nanu `tāstebhya' iti apakramaṇopapādanamubhayasādāraṇamiti cet, na tathā sati `tyadāditaḥ se,e puṃnapuṃsakato liṅgavatanāni' iti puṃliṅganirdeśasya bhāvyatvāt | tataśca, atratyabhāṣyakaiyaṭābhyāṃ sautraṃ prakṛtipadamupādānakāraṇaparamiti na sukaraṃ nirdhāraṇam .|
kiṃ ca---`pratyākhyānaparabhāṣyasaṃmatam' ityāktyā sūtrakṛto nedaṃ saṃmatamityuktaṃ bhavati | evaṃ ca sūtrakṛnmate prakṛtipadasya kāramamātrakaratvaṃ `putrātpramodo jāyate, gomayādvṛsciko jāyate, daṇḍāddaṭo jāyata' ityādi sarvaṃ sūtrodāharamameva | na ca phalabhede pratyāśyānāyogaḥ, prāpambhe prakṛtipadaṃ kāraṇasāmāīyaparaṃ pratyākhyāne upādānakāraṇaparamiti `putrātpramodo jayāta' ityatra pañcamīpravṛttyapravṛttibhyāṃ phalabhedalya lpaṣṭatvāditi vācyam, `na bahuvīhau, na kvādeḥ, ityādīnāṃ phalabhede'pi pratyākyānavat tatsaṃbhavati | vaktutaḥ uktadisā bhāṣyataḥ prakṛtipadamupādānakāraṇaparamityalābhāt bhāṣyamate'ṣi kāramasāmānyaparameva prakṛtipadamiti na phalabedaḥ |
nanu `putratpramodo jāyata' ityatra `vibhāṣā guṇe'striyām' (pā.sū.2.3.25) ityatra vibhāṣeti yogavibhāgātad putrapadottaraṃ hetvarthe pañcamīti cet na | yogavibhāgasya bhāṣyo'darsanāt | `yato vā' ityādāvapi yogavibhāgonaina hetau pa़camīti nimittakāraṇatā vādināpi vaktaṃ śakyam | kārakavibhaktiprayuktakāryaviśeṣastu ubhayatra mṛgyaḥ | pariṇāmikāramastale viśleṣābhāvaṃ manyamānaṃ prati visleṣā'stīti bodhanāya bhāṣyaṃ pravṛttam | tataśca --pratyākyānabhāṣyeṇa prakṛtipadamupādānākāramaparamiti kalpanaṃ duḥśakam | tadvināpi bhāṣyasyopapadyamānatvāt | yat yena vivā'nupapannaṃ taddhi tasya kalpakamiti |
kiṃ cetyādinoktaskṛtīyo'pi heturna yuktaḥ, tatā hi--kāśikāvṛttau kārakādhikārapaṭhite,u dvātriṃśatisūtreṣu kvāpi va `kāraka' iti saptamyantapāṭhasya `nirdhāraṇasaptamī' ti vākyasya vopalambhaḥ, pratyuta `kraka' iti sūtre `kāraka iti viseṣaṇamapādānādisaṃjñāviṣayamadhikriyate' iti vākyamasti, dravādisaṃjñāvidhāyakapraghaṭṭake pratisūtraṃ `yat druvaṃ tatkārakamapādānasaṃjñam, tatkārakaṃ saṃpradānasaṃjñamaṃ, tatkārakaṃ karmasaṃjñam, tatkārakaṃ kartṛsaṃjñam' ityādi tattatsūtrārthapradarsakavkye `kārakam' iti viseṣaṇapadaṃ pratamāntaṃ copalabhyate | etena kāraka iti saptamyantaṃ padaṃ pratamāntatayā vipariṇamitavyam, sūtre tu prathamārte saptamīti tadabhiprāyo'dhyavasīyate | ato ratnāktaṃ na yuktam |
nanu tadvyākhyāyāṃ padamañjaryāṃ haradattena pratamāntapāṭhapakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā `kāraka' iti virdhāraṇasaptamā sāmānyāpekṣaṃ caikavacanaṃ viśeṣaṇādhikāre tu kārakeṣu madhye yadapāye dhruvaṃ kārakamutyarthe labhyate' iti gkanthena viśe,ṇādhikārapa7e nirdāraṇasaptamyantatāyāṃ mūlatātparyamavadhāritam, tadavalambya ratnakṛtā `vṛttimate nirdhāraṇa saptamyantasya' ityādyuktamiti cet, acyate--asti kārakaśabdo hetuvācako rūḍhoo yaugikasceti dvividhaḥ | tatra dvitīyaḥ,ta karoti kriyāmiti vyutpattyā kartariṇvulantaḥ kriyāyāṃ svatantrakartetyarthakaḥ, dhruvādīnāṃ pradhānakriyāyāṃ svātantryābhāvena tatra kārakaṃ nimittam' iti cānartāntasam | tataścajātra kārakaśabdoo nimittaparyāyo visoṣaṇabodhakaḥ | nanvevaṃ-- `māṇavakasya pitaraṃ panthānaṃ pṛcchati' ityādau māṇanakasya pitṛnirūpitānimuttatvasattavena karmasaṃjñā syāditi cet, na--`kasya hetoḥ kriyāyā' iti praśanottarābhyāṃ kriyāyā nimittameva vivakṣyate | kriyā tu prayogasamavetaiva grājhetyāsayāt | prakagṛte māṇavakasya pṛcchikriyānirūpitatvābhāvena na karmatvam . `dhruvam' ityādau kāraka iti viśeṣaṇabalāt kriyālābhaḥ, sā sāmānyā, tatrāpāyādiḥ kriyā viseṣaḥ apāyarūpāāyāḥ tasyāḥ kriyāyāmabhedenānvayaḥ, sāmānyaviśeṣayorabhedasya klṛptatvāt iti kṛtvaiva `apāye yatdruvaṃ tatkārakamapādānasaṃjñamā' ityādyucyate vṛttikṛtā | apāya rūukriyāyāṃ yannimittaṃ druvaṃ tadapādānamiti tadartha iti mañjarīcātparyam | evaṃ ca--vṛttikṛdādṛtapratamāntapāṭhapakṣasyopapatticintārthaṃ mañjarī prāvartata, na tu nirdāraṇasaptamyantapāṭhe vṛttikṛtaḥ tātparyakalpanāya | ata eva--`kiṃ doṣapratividhānavyasanena nimittaparyāyasyaiva grahaṇamastvati manyamāna āha' --iti `kārakaśabdaśca' iti mūlamavatrita tena | etena ---nirdāraṇasaptamyantapāṭhe asti doṣasaṃbhavaḥ, tatparuhārāya ca prayatitavyam, tato varaṃ prathamāntapāṭāśrayaṇamiti dhvanyate | `bhītārthānāṃ bhayahetuḥ' (pā.sū. 1.4.25) `janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ' (pā.sū. 1.4.20) iti sūtrayoḥ hetupadasya, kāramaparyāyaprakṛtipadasya `bhayahetuḥ, atpattikāraṇam'ityartena tayorviseṣatvāt, anuvṛttapratamāntakārakapadārthasya kriyāheturityasya sāmānyatvāt. `dravyaṃ ghaṭa' ityādāviva sāmānādhikaraṇyasya saṃbhavenabhayarūpakriyāhetuḥ, canirūpakriyāheturityarthapartavasānena na kācidanupapattiḥ | na caivaṃ hetvādipadaprayogavaiyarthtaṃ śakyam' kārakamiti saṃjñāvidhipakṣajñāpakatvena cāritārththat | nirdharaṇasaptamyantakārakapadādhikārapakṣe'pi tasya kriyājanakatvamityarthena sāmānyatvāt, bayajanihetutvasya viśeṣatvāt, `dravyaṣu ghaṭa' ityādi vyavahāravat kriyākāraṇeṣu bhayahetuḥ janikāramamiti vyavahāko'pi uuunna eveti n'nvayāsaṃbhavadoṣaprasaṃgaḥ | ata ova hi---nīrdhāraṇasaptamīpakṣe nirdhāraṇasyānerāśryatvāt, nirdhāraṇasaptamyānupadoktārthalābhāt' iti abhidadānau kaiyaṭanāgeśāvapi virdesānupapattikeva doṣa iti emaṃsāsātm, nānvayāsaṃbhavadoṣamavocatām | aktarītyā sāmānyaviseṣasadbhāvena anvayāsaṃbhavaḥ suparihaka iti tayorbhavaḥ |
