kiṃ ca---`itarathā hi `kārakoṣviti brayāt' iti `kāraka' iti sūtrabhā,yaparyālocanāyāṃ sūtrabhāṣyakārayo rnirdāraṇasaptamyantatvamanabhimatamiti pratīteḥ `kāraka iti nirdhāraṇasaptamī sāmānyāpekṣaṃ caikavacanaṃ, viśeṣaṇādhikārapakṣe kārake,u madya' ityādima़jarīgranthaḥ sūtrabhāṣyakārābhiprāyānabhijñasya pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ kalyācidukteranuvāda iti bhāti |
kiṃ ca--`kāraka' iti sūtrabhā,yataḥ kārakavadaṃ karoti kriyāmiti vyutpattyā anvarthasaṃjñāparamā, kartṛkarmādivyapadesaṃ karootīti vyutpattyā kriyāparamiti pakṣadvayameva sabhyate, na tu nirdāraṇasaptamīti kṛtīyaḥ phakṣaḥ, sa tu kasyacidautprekṣika eva | ta eva dīkṣitādayaḥ bhāṣyasiddapakṣadvayamāśratya `dhruvam' ityādi sūtrāṇi dvedā yojayanti | tatra kārarapadasya kriyāparatve `kāraka ddattaśrutayorāśiṣi' ityādauau kārakapadasya kārakādhikāravihitāpādāvanādi saṃjñāparatvaṃ bodyam | evaṃ ca-bhāṣyāsaṃmate, granthakṛtāpyanukte pakṣe kenacit tadīyatvena ātmanaivotprekṣite, doṣodbhāvanaṃ sarvathā'yuktam |
yattu prakṛtipadasya kāraṇamātraparatve, nimittaparyāyakārakapadānuvṛttau ubhayorekārthakatvena ghaṭaḥ kalaśaḥ ghaṭo gaṭa ityādāviva śābdabuddirna syāditi, tanna | ubhayoḥ sāmānyaviśṣabhāvasya anupadameva nirūpitātvāt | śabdasya prakāratayā bhānt bheda iti tu apeśalam | na ca kaustubhādau śābde śabdabhānaṃ vyavasthāpitamiti vācyam, tatra dṛḍhataramānābhāvāt |
kārakapadasya niruktavyutpattyā kriyāparatvamāśritya viṣayasaptamītvaṃ cāṅgīkṛtya janakatvaṃ ca viṣayatvaṃ kriyāyāḥ sāmānyatvāt janerviśeṣatvāt janirūpā yā kriyā dad‌deturapādānamityarke'nupapattigandhābhāvena prakṛtipadasya kāramamātraparatve'pi bādakābāvaḥ . kārakamiti saṃjñāpakṣe'pi ṇanvarthasaṃjñābalāt labdhasya kriyājanakatvasya janikāraṇe uparañjakatayā saṃbandha iti na doṣa iti | evaṃ ca --`mṛtpiṇḍād ghaṭojāyate, daṇḍād ghayo jāyate, putrātpramodo jāyata' ityādi sarvamasyai bodāharaṇamiti sarveṣṭasiddiḥ | etna -`putrātpramodaḥ, daṇḍādghaṭa' ityādau anupādāne upādānatvasya, aguṇe guṇatvasya vā''ropeṇa pañcamīṃ sādhayanto nāgośādayo niraṅkaśā eva |
atha vā---śrutau sūtre ca `yata' ityātra tṛtīyāntāttasiḥ, yona brajhaṇā imāni bhūtāni jāyanta iti śrutiyojanā | yena asya jagato janmādi bhavati tadvrajheti sūtrayojanā, `daṇḍena ghayo jāyate' ityādāviva yeneti hetau tṛtīyā | ata eva bhagavatā---
māyā'dhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sa carācaram |
hetunā'nena kaunteya jagadviparuvartate || (gī.9.10)
iti gītāyāṃ sarvādhyakṣasya sarvāmino hareḥ kaṭākṣavīṇena praritā prakṛītaḥ carācarajaganti sṛjatīti vadatā īśvarasya prerakatvena nimittakāraṇataiva vyavasthāpitā | ślokārthastu---adhyakṣeṇa sarvasvāminā mayā īkṣaṇe napreritā prakṛtiḥ pradhānaṃ, sacarā'caraṃ jagat sūyate janayati, he kaunteya! jagat, anena hetunā tattatprāṇikarmānuguṇenekṣaṇena hetunā, viparivartate punaḥ punaḥ jāyate, vividhavasturūpeṇa pariṇamate iti | eyaṃ bhāvaḥ ---lore yathā rājādhīno'mātyādiḥ kadācit prabhorīkṣaṇamātreṇeṅgitena māmayamasmin karmaṇi pravartayatīti jānan tatkarahma kurute tathā iyamapi prakṛtirbhagavadadhīnā sarvasvāminoharerīkṣaṇamātreṇa prabhoīrerabhiprāyaṃ jānatī sṛjati carācarātmakaṃ jagaditi | na ca ---acetanasya pradhānasyakathamidṛśaṃ jñānami tivācyam, tadabhīmānidevatāvivakṣayā tathokteḥ |
gadādhalo'pi valyitpattivāde pañcamīkārakanirūpaṇāvasare | `mṛtpiṇḍāt ghaṭo jayāta' ityādau `janikarturīri apādānatvam', tatraprakṛtitvaṃ na vikāritvaṃ prakṛtivikṛtibhāvā'bhāve'pi `prakkekayīto bharataḥ, tatobhūtad, vāyorjāta' ityādau pañācamīdarśanāt......tasmātkaraṇatvameva prakṛtitvaṃ `daṇjād gaṭo jāyata' ityādiprayogā iṣyanta eva ityādigkanthena `janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ' iti sūtre prakṛtipadaṃ karaṇaparamityabhiprayan dravyādyacetanopādānakāraṇatvaṃ brajhaṇo na saṃbhavati iti `yatoo dravayaṃ guṇāḥ karma ityādau pañcamyupapattiḥ prakṛtadipadasya kāraṇamātraparatva eva bhavatītyabhidatte |
idaṃ bodhyam---brajha jagadupādānamiti vadantaḥ tricaturā eva, tatra kecidvaiyākaraṇāḥ, sūtrabhāṣyakṛdanye | śabdabrajha jagato vivṛttopā dānaṃ na tu pariṇāmyupādānaṃ yato brajha nirvikāram |
anādinidanaṃ brajha sabdatattvaṃ yadakṣaram |
vivartate'rthabhāvena prakriyā jagato yataḥ ||
iti haryukteśceti vadanti | śabdabrajhaṇi sarvamidaṃ jagat āropitam iti toṣāmāśayaḥ | atra mate `yato vā' iti śrutau, yata iti saptamyāntātsārvavibhaktikastasiḥ yasminnityarthakamiti yuktam, na tu paṃcamyantātsiriti | na ca---`jāyanta' iti 8vaṇātpañcamītyucyata iti vācyam | `tatra jāta' (pā.sū.4.3.25) iti saptamīsamarthāt jāta ityarte aṇādipratyayavidhānena, `saptamyāṃ janerḍaḥ' (pā.sū 3.2.98) iti saptamyante upapade janerḍapratyayavidānena, mandurāyāṃ jātaiti vigkahavākyaprayogeṇa ca, janidhātusamabhivyāhāre saptamyā api yuktatvāt, śabdabrajhaṇa āropā'dhiṣṭhānatāvādinā vaiyākaraṇena tatra aviṣṭhānatvamastīti bodhanāya saptamyantatvasyaivāśrayaṇīyatvāt, vivakṣitārthabodhakavibhaktyantaśabdaprayogasyaiva nyāyyatvācca | anyayā lakṣaṇādikamāśrayaṇīyaṃ bhavet | yadyapi atra mate kiṃ parāyā vācaḥ brajhatvam, uta madhyamāyā vāca iti dvaitam, tathāpi mañjūṣtaḥ parākhyavāca eva tatvamiti niścetavyam |
anye tu -- sabdātiriktaṃ brajha jagadupādānakāraṇaṃ manyante | śiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvamatavat | atrāpi pañcamīnirdesaścintyaprayojanaḥ | saptamīnirdeso nyāyya iti bhāti | asminmatadvaye jagadupādānamāyā'vidyā dhiṣṭhānatvāt brajhaṇoo jagadupādānatvam |
pare tu -- vikārātmakaprakṛtiśarīrakatayā, prakṛtiniyāmakatvena vā brajha jagadupādānamiti bravate | atra-svaśarīrapradhānopādānakatvāt svāyattapradhānopādānakatvādvā jagataḥ brajhopādānakatvam | śrutisūtrasthasya `yata' ityasya bajhādhiṣṭhitāt pradhānāt, pradhānameva puruṣasaṃnidhānena jagadātmanā pariṇamate iti vadanti | apare tuīśvarapreritaṃ pradhānaṃ jagadātmanā pariṇamate atastadevopādānakāraṇamiti āhuḥ | kecittu-paramāṇavaḥ dvyaṇukādirūpeṇa jagatkāraṇamiti manyante |
yetu manyante brajha jagata upādānakāraṇamiti te sarve brajhaṇaḥ pariṇāmaṃ nāṅgīkurvanti; atasteṣāṃ mate brajhaṇa upādānatvookteḥ kiṃ prayojanamiti vimarśanīyam | etena-brajhātirekeṇābhāvāt mithyātveṃ `tadananyatvam' iti sūtrāt | tataśca vyāsasyāpi jaganmithyātve tātparyamiti yaduktaṃ mañjūṣāyāṃ prathamapariccedeee tatparāstam | kāraṇā'nanyatvabuddeḥ pariṇāmikāraṇaniṣaya eva saṃbhavāt | brajhaṇastu apariṇā mitnāt | vāsudevasya sarvasmāduttamatvaṃ svīkurvatā śrīvyāsapāṇinipatañjalīnāṃ jagato brajhopādānakāramakatvasyā'nabhimatatvena bpajhānavyatvamapi jagatoo na samatamityavagamāt | `tadananyatvam' iti sūtratātparyaṃ tu tadadhīnatvādinā tadananyatvoktiriti upariṣṭāt nirūpayiṣyāmaḥ, nirūpitaṃ ca kiṃcitpūrvamapi |
evaṃ ca - jagato brajhopādānakāraṇakatvaṃ bhāktameveti |
sṛṣṭivākyāni jagato nimittaṃ kāraṇaṃ hariḥ |
iti prāhusātayostamānnaiva sidhyedananyatā ||
nanu yaduktaṃ vāsudevo jagato nimittakāraṇaṃ nopādānam atastasya sarvasmādbheda iti, na tadyuktam;
mamaivāṃ'śo jīvaloke jīvabhūtaḥ sanātanaḥ |
manaḥ ṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇi prakṛtisthāni karṣati || (gī.15.8)
iti jīvasya tadaṃśatvoktivirodhāt | aṃśāṃśinorabhedasyaiva darśanāt | saki hi tayorbhede aṃśatvamanupapannamiti, ucyate-aṃśo hi dvividhaḥ bhinno'bhinnaśceti | tatra bhinnāṃśaḥ svāṃśasadṛsaḥ, sa ca jīvaḥ, abhinnāṃśastu antaryāmirūpaṃ rāmakṛṣṇādyavatārarūpaṃ caja | tataśca-mamaiṃvāśaḥ madaṃsasadṛsaḥ, na tu matsvarūpabhūtaḥ, śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭamiti | sādṛśyaṃ ca svāśasadṛśaḥ, na tu matsvarūpabhūtaḥ, śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭamiti | sādṛśyaṃ ca svāyattatvena, yathā svāṃśaḥ svāyattaḥ, tathā jīvaḥ svāyat iti bhāvaḥ | atha vā mamaivāśaṃḥ yaḥ, tadadhīnaḥ na svatantra ityarthaḥ | yo yadadhīnaḥ sa tacchabdena vyāpadiśyate `yatā rājā rāṣṭram' iti bhāvaḥ | eta eva sarvāśāstrāryasārasa,ṃgrahaṃ gītāyāṃ pañcadase'dhyāye ---
uttamaḥ puruṣastvanyaḥ paramātmetyudāhṛtaḥ |
yo laukatrayamāviśya bibhartyavyayamośvaraḥ || (gī.15.18)
iti kṣarākṣarapadavācyāt sarvasmāt prapañcādvāsudevasyottamatvādyabhidhānaṃ saṃgacchate | yadi jīvādiśvarasya na bhedaḥ tarhi tasyoktaṃ sarvettamatvaṃ kathamupapadyeta | atoṃ'śatvoktirna bheduvidhinī | etna gītāyāmupakramopasehākayornirūpito bhedo na vāstavaḥ, api tu aupādhika iti kasyaciduktiḥ parāstā | sarvasāstrārthrasaṃgrahe pañcadaśe adhyāye bhedokteḥ | na ca ayamapi bheda aupādhika iti vācyam śāstrartasārasaṃgrahe vāstavārthakatanasyaiva evaṃkalpanāyogāt |
anye tu--jīvaśabdasya taccarīrakaparamātmārthakatveva viśiṣṭai kadesatvena jīvasya paramātmāṃśatvavyavahāraḥ, athāpi svarūpasyabhāvabhedāt jīvaḥ paramapuruṣādbhinna eveti vadanti | mañjūṣāka़to mate īśvarānyacetanasyābhāvena ītvarasya ca niraṃśatvena upādhisaṃbandhasya vavatumasakyatayā śāstreṣu jīvasyoktamaṃśatvamanupapannameva syāt | atra `sanātana' iti viseṣaṇena jīvasya tattvamanādi iti śrīvyāsamaharṣerāsayo'vagamyate | pāṇininā tu jīvānāditvaṃ na niṣiddamiti tasyāpi tadiṣṭameva . tataśca---tadubhayāsaṃmatamupādhisaṃbandhasya jīvatvavyavahārahetutvanirūpaṇamiti |
idamatrānadheyam--na kenāpi jīvasyeśvaramukhyāṃśatvaṃ śakyanirūpaṇam | īsvarānyacetanaṃ na, īśvaro niraṃśa iti vādī tu īśvarasyo pādhisaṃbandenāṃśaṃ parikalpāya, tasya jīvatvaṃ, tasyeśvarāṃśatvaṃ ca nirūpayicu na prabhavati | evaṃ yuktyamahaprameyakalpaniāpekṣayā svarūpate bhinnasya jīvasya īśvarādhīnatvādinā tadaṃśatvakalpanaṃ varamiti |
jīvasyāśatvavacanaṃ nātmābhedasya sādhakam |
ataḥ śrīvāsudevasya sarvetkarṣe na bdhakam ||
gītāvacanavirodhoddhāraḥ
nanu vāsudevasya sarvettamatvaṃ bhedādhīnam sa ca bhedo gotāyāṃ pratiṣiddhaḥ, yataḥ--`sa yogī brajhanirvāṇaṃ brajhabhūto'dhigacchati (gī.5.24) upaiti śāntarajasaṃ brajhabhūtamakalpaṣam (gī.6.37) brajhabhūtaḥ prasannātmā na śocati na kāṅkṣati' (gī.18.54) ityatra`brajabhūta' iti, `sa guṇānaṃ samadītyaitān brajhabhūyāya kalpate (gā.18.53) ityatra `brajhabhūyāya' iti, `yaḥ prayāti sa madbhāvaṃ yāti nāstyatrasaṃśayaḥ (gī.8.5) madbhakta etadvijñāya madbhāvāyopapadyate (gī.14).19) ityatra madbhāvamiti ca praha bhacavān teṣāṃ ca brajhatvaṃ prāptaḥ, brajhatvāya, matsvarūpamityarthena brajhaprāptaḥ, brajhabhūyāyetyanayoḥ brajhaprāptimān, brajhaprāptaye, tatra sthityai ityarthaḥ . dbhāvam, madbhāvāyetyasyamayi sattām, yī sattāyai ityarthaḥ . madbhāvam, madbhāvāyetyasya mayi sattām, mayi sattāyai ityarthaḥ | dbhāvamadhigacchatītyetat mokṣaprāptiriti---
guṇānetānatītya trīn dehī dehasamudbhavān |
janmamṛtyujarāduḥkairvimukto'mṛtamasanute || (gī. 14.20)
ityattaraślokena bhagavataiva vyatkhyātam, ataḥ -eteṣāṃ svarūpābhede tātparyakalpavamayuktam |
brajhaṇi sthitiḥ brajhaiva sarvamiti niścayavāt, brajhaprāptaḥ, brajhabhavanāya (mokṣāya) madbhāvāya ityasya mokṣāyeti (śaṃ.bhaṅā) brajhabhūtaḥ asaṃkucitajñānādisvasvarūpeṇa sthitiḥ, brajhasāmyāya asaṃsāritvāya, evameva madbhāvapadasya, ekatra tu ---`jananādiduḥkhamuktatvam' iti (rā.bhā) `brajhabūta ityasya sarvatra brajhaṇi sthitamanaskaḥ, prītiviṣayatvāya, tanmanaskatvāya, nirduḥkhaniratiśayānandātmikāṃ sthitim, manmanaskatvam' iti (pū.bhā.)vyākhyātam | ato naiṣāṃ vacanā nāmabhede tātparyam | evaṃ ca---vāsudevasya sarvettamatve na gītāvacanavirodhaḥ | bhagavadupāsanāyāḥ tanmanaskatvaṃ ca pradhānamaṅramiti `manmanābhava madbhaktaḥ', (gī.9.34,18.65) `ananyāścantayanto mām' (gī.9.22) ityādinā vāraṃ vā3raṃ śikṣamāt bodhyam | ta eṣāṃ gītāvacanānāṃ na bhavaduktārthe tātparyam, gītācāryasya vāsudevasya sarvettamatvamabhiprayataḥ pāṇineśceti |
nanu saṃsāriṇe jīvasya vibhinnadharmavattvena bhede'pi muktānāṃ samastapuṇyapāpavidhuratayā śuddhatvāt viruddhadharmanattvābhāvena abhedo yujyata iti cet ucyate--
paraṃ bhūyaḥ pravakṣyāmi jñānānāṃ jñānamuttamam |
yajjñātvā munayaḥ sarve parāṃ siddimito gatāḥ ||
idaṃ jñānamupāśritya mama sādharmyamāgatāḥ |
rarge'pi nopajāyante pralaye na vyathanti ca || (gī.14.1,2)
iti dvābhyā `mama yonirmahadbrajha' (gī.14.3) ityādinā vakṣyamāṇa5ānaprasaṃsāpūrvaṃ tasya phalaṃ sadṛṣṭāntamabhidhāya, tatra muktānāṃ paramātmanā aikyabhramavāraṇāya teṣāṃ bhagavatsādharmyameva praha bhagavān . sādharmyaṃ ca samatā, sā ca jananamaraṇādido,rāhityena, jagatkāramatvādyatirittāpahatapāpmatvādinā, nityasukānubhavitṛtvena ca bodhyā . brajhamīmāṃsāyāṃ ca muktānāṃ `jagadvyāpāra' varjam ' (bra.sū. 4.4.1) iti jagatkāramatvaṃ na, `bhogamātre' ti sūtreṇa nityasukhānubhavamātramasti, `muktopasṛpye' (bra.sū.1.3.2) tyanena bhagavatprāptimattvamastītyāha sūtrakṛt | astyatrārthe ---

iti śrutyanugraho'pi | paśyaḥ draṣṭā (upāsakaḥ) nirañjanaḥ suddhaḥ, sāmyam upāsyena paramātmanā sāmyam | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | tataśca--- muktāvapi bheda eveti śrutisūtrasmṛtisiddhaṃ tattvam |
brajhabhūtetyādi gītāvākyādaikyaṃ pratīyate |
ityākṣepo nirākāri gītāśrutivacobalāt ||
manmanā bhava madbhakto madyājī māṃ namaskuru |
māmevaiṣyasi satyaṃ te pratijāne priyo'si me || (gī.18.65)
iti upāsanayā bhagavatprāptiriti |
śaṃkarabhāṣye---tattvamiti padaṃ vibhajya saḥ `sadeva' ityādivākyapratipāditaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ, ātmā, tattvam vāstavaṃ vastviti yojanāntaraṃ darśitam |
kecittu---śvetaketorekasya rāmakṛṣṇādivadavatārarūpatvaṃ jñātvā abhedoktiriti vaktuṃ śakyamiti nānena sarveṣāṃ jīvānāṃ paramātmaikyaṃ sidhyatīti vadanti |
kiṃ ca--tatra navakṛtva upaduṣṭeṣu śrutānāṃ śakunisūtrādidṛṣṭāntavākyānāṃ bhedaparatvena dārṣṭāntika `tattvamasī' ti vākyasya bhedaparatvena yojanaivociteti jīva īśvasatvabuddhirnaṃ kāryeti vibhāvyam | dṛṣṭāntavākyānāṃ bhedaparatvamanyatra nirūpitam |
raṅgarāmānujabhāṣye tu `tattvamasi' ityāsyāyamarthaḥ -tvaṃ padaṃ mukhyārthānupapattyā antaryāmiparam, tatpadaṃ sacchabditātmuryanasānavṛttyā antaryāmiparattvam | nanu tvaṃ padasya sarvatra saṃbodhyamānārthaparāmarśakarvasyaiva nyāyyatvena prakṛte śvetaketoḥ saṃbodhyatvāt tatparāmarśaṃ vinā antaryāmiparatvakalpanaṃ na yuktamiti cet, devadattaṃ saṃbodhya tatputrābhiprāyeṇa `devadat! tvamevaṃ kṛtavānasi' itivat upapattiriti | iyānviśeṣaḥ --`dṛṣṭānte mukhyārtānupapattyā devadattapadarya tatputre lakṣaṇā tvaṃpadasyāntaryāmiṇi lakṣaṇā' iti śrutireṣā vyāsyātā |
etena--`tattvamasyādivākyasya akhaṇḍārthena śuddenāpi brajhamā''dhyāsikaṃ tādātmyam' (mū.4.25) iti maṃjūṣoktaṃ parāstam, tena bhagavatsvarūpayorevaikyabooodhanāt tadaikyasya vāstavatvena ādhyāsikatvakathanasyāyuktatvāt, saṃbodhyamānaśvetaketuparatve'pi tadadhīnatvatatsādṛsyādireva śvedaketau bodhyata iti tādātmyasyaivāprasakteḥ | vāsudevasya sarvettamatvādikamabhiprayataḥ pāṇinerācāryasya śvetaketūpalakṣitajīveṣu bhagavattādātmyasyānabhimanatvācca |
(2) nanu `gatāḥkasāḥpañcadaśapratiṣṭhā devāśca sarve pratidevatāsu | karmāṇi vijñānamayaśca ātmā pare'vyaye sarva ekībhavanti' (mu.6.7)
yathā nadyaḥ syandamānāḥ samudre'staṃ gacchanti nāmarūpe vihāya |
tathā vidvannāmarūpādvimuktaḥ parātparaṃ puruṣamupaiti divyam || (mu.6.8)
sa yo ha vai tatparame brajha veda brajhaiva bhavati |
nā'syā'brajhavitkule bhavati || (mu.6.9)
ityātharmaṇe upasaṃhāre brajhavido brajhaṇā saha ekībhāvasya, brajhabhavanasya ca pratipādanāt, muktau jīvabrajhaṇoraikyameva | atra pratipādita ekībhāvo brajhabhāvaśca brajhatvameva | anyatā--abrajhaṇo brajhaṇo brajhabhāvāyogāt asaṃbhāvitārthapratipādikeyaṃ śrutirapramāṇaṃ bhavet | etena---mukteḥ pūrvamapi asarti jīvasya brajhatvaṃ, da0oṣavaśāttu anabhivyaktaṃ, tattvajñānena doṣanivṛttāvabhivyajyata iti tādātmyaṃ svato vartate, ato bhedastannibandhanavyavahāraścaupādhika iti yuktam | evaṃ ca--- gītāyāṃ nirūpito bhedo'vāstavaḥ, `vāsudevaḥ sarvam' ityakto'bhedastu vāstava iti |
atra vadanti---`dvāsuparṇā, yadā paśyaḥ' ityādi śrutīnāṃ bhedaparaṇāṃ bahūnāmupalambhāt gītāyāṃ nirūpito bhedā sakyam | kiṃ ca --`vedāntavijñānasuniścitārthāḥ saṃnyāsayogā yatayaḥ śuddhasattvāḥ | te brajhalokeṣu parāntakāle parāmṛtātparimucyanti sarveṃ' (mu.6.6) iti pūrvamantre---adhikāriṇo brajhamīmāṃsāśāstrābhāyāsāt, vedatadanusārisarvasāstrāścayavantaḥ sarvāṇi karmāṇi bhagavate samarpayantaḥ, satyalokaṃ gatvā tatra cirakālamuṣitvā tallokādhipateḥ caturmukhādupadeśaṃ prapya, viśeṣeṇa bhagavatsvarūpaṃ vijñāya, tadāyuṣo'nte muktā bhavantīti śrutam |
saptamena anena mantreṇa tatatprakāraḥ pradṛśyate | ,ṣoḍaśakalaḥ puruṣaḥ kalānāṃ pañcadasānāṃ nivṛttau mukto bhavati, tatra kalānāma jīvasya śarīradhāraṇahetuḥ prāṇādayaḥ ṣoḍaśapadārthāḥ te ca--`saprāṇamasṛjata prāṇācchraddhāṃ khaṃ vāyurjyotirāpaḥ pṛthivī indriyaṃ mano'nnamannādvīryaṃ tapo mantrāḥ karma lokā lokeṣu nāma caṭha (ṣa.pra.6.2) iti ṣaṭpraśnoktā jñeyāḥ | tatra---praṇāḥ śvāsocchvāsaḥ, śraddhā āstikyabuddhiḥ, annaṃ khādyaṃ vastu, vīryaṃ śarīrotpattihaṃtubhūtaṃ vastu, tapaḥ jñānasādhanaṃ vratam, mantrāḥ ṛgādiḥ, karma puṇyāpuṇyarūpaṃ, lokaḥ golakam indriyāśrayaḥ, nāma devadattādi, pratiṣṭāḥ śarīradhārikāḥ, kalāḥ prāṇādayaḥ, tadabhimānidevāśca, karmāṇi puṇyāni, tadabhimānī ca gatāḥ, sarve devāḥ kalābhimānibhya itare sarve, pratibimbabhūtāsu prajāsu (jīveṣu) sthitāḥ, vijñānamaya ātmā jīvaḥ sarve ca devāśca (kalābhimānino|ṛnye ca) avyeye akṣare, pare paramātmani ekībhavanti yathā--`atra karmaṇi sarvemātyādaya ekībhavanti' ityādau ekamanaskā bhavantītyarthaḥ, tathā paramātmavi,ye ekamanaskā bhavanti, na vipratipadyante ityartaḥ | (paramātmā adhyakṣaḥ, vayaṃ sarve taddāsā iti dṛḍhamanaskā bhavanti) paramātmanā nirdiṣṭaṃ karma ekamanuskāḥ santaḥ kurvantītyarte vā, paramātmanā saha ekasthānastitā bhavantītyartho vā, evamartha eva cvipratyaya upapadyate | abhūtatadbhāvee hi `kṛbhvastitayoge saṃpadāyakartari cviḥ' (pā.sū.5.4.50) iti cvirvidhīyate, tatra `abhūtatadbhāva' iti kātyāyanavacanam | yadvastu yena dharmeṇa pūrvamanutpannam, tasya vastunaḥ tena dharmeṇotpattau vivakṣitāyāmiti tadartaḥ | tataśca sāmānādhikaraṇyasaṃbandhena tadbhavatīti, ekaśabdasyāvidyamānaikamanaskatvādirūpadarmaviśiṣṭārthavācakatvena cvirbhavatīti śakyate vaktuṃ bhedavādibhiḥ pāṇinīyaiḥ | ekatvavādināṃ pāṇonīyatvenātmānaṃ manyamānānāṃ tu naivaṃ vaktuṃ śakyam | teṣāṃ mate ekyasya brajhatvasya ca sad vidyamānatvena tasya tadabhāvasya ca niruktasāmānādhikaraṇyasya durnirūpatvāt | na ca---ekatvādeḥsadātanatve'pi tadabhivyakteḥ pūrvamabhāvena tāmādāya ca ekaśabdasya abhīvyaktitvenābhivyaktibodhakatvābhāvena cvervaktumaśakyatvāt |
na ca--`brajhībhavatītyatra mañjūṣodyotayośchāndasatvāt cviḥ' ityukteḥ tatrevātrāpi chāndasaḥ svārthikaḥ cviriti vātyam --uktadilā sūtreṇaivopapattau chāndasatvādikalpanāyogāt | brajhībhavatīti `kṛbhvasti' iti sūtrakaumudipratikamādya `atra bhavanakartṛtvaṃ prakṛtibhūtasyā'brajhaṇa eva vikārābhedena bodhyam' ityuktaṃ śekhare, mañjūṣādau svārthe cviriti | atera kaḥ siddhānta iti cintyam | na ca---kaumudyāśayavarṇanaparaḥ śekharaḥ, mañjūṣāti tu svasīddāntāvedakamiti vācyam--śekhare `vastutaḥ, pare tu vā' ityāpakramya chāndasatvāt cvirityanyatra nirūpitam' ityādyanukteḥ tathākalpane mānābhāvāt | avirodhasaṃpādanāya tatā kalpyata ati cet vinigamanāviraheṇa ṇañjīṣādyuktireva parāśayavarṇanapareti vaktuṃ śakyat---kiṃ ca--anāropitā'tathābhūte āropitatadbhāvasaṃpattāveva cviḥ ata eva īśvaraprasādādinā'mahati mukhyamahattvotpattau na cviḥ' iti śekharoktadīśā atrāpyaikyasyāropo vātyaḥ, āropitasya ca tanmate mithyātvena vāstavamaiṃkyaṃ na sidhyet | kiṃ ca--svārte cvipratyayāṅgīkāre'pi brajhaṇo brajhatvamastītyarthasyaiva lābhena jīvasya brajhabhāvapraptiritīṣṭāsiddhiḥ |
aṣṭame mantre `puruṣamupaiti divyam' iti divyapuruṣaprāpterevoktatvena `ekībhavanti' ityasya svarūpaikyaparatayā vyākyānaṃ mandam | navame tadittaramantre `brajhaiva bhavati' iti vākyenāpi `brajhaprāptirevotyate' na ca jīvasya brajhabhāva iti tadartha iti vācyam | tatra bhavatītyasya prānpotītyartaṃ iti tatraiva śāṅkarabhāṣyokteḥ | evaṃ ca--pūrvamantre puruṣapadaṃ brajhaparamiti bodhayitvā pūrvamantrārtha eva navamena mantreṇa vivṛta iti navamamantrāntimavākyoktamamṛtatvena bhavanamapi atraivārthe'nukīlam | mukto bhavatīti tadarthaḥ | ata eva ca gītāyām |
māmupetya punarjanma duḥkhālayamaśāśvatam |
nāpnuvanti mahātmānaḥ saṃsiddhiṃ paramā gatāḥ ||
ābrajhabhuvanāllokāḥ punārānartino'rjuna |
māmupetya tu kaunteya punarjanma na vidyate || (8.15,16)
`māmevaiṣyasi' (gī.9.34;15.65) iti bhagavadṛpāsakānāṃ yogināṃ paramaphalatayā bagavatprāptirevokto, na tu tattādātmayaṃ, prāptistu na tādātmyamiti tadbhāvaḥ |
(3) nanu---`brajhaiva san brajhāpyeti' (bṛ.7.6.4.6) iti vājasaneyake brajhaṇa eva brajhaprāpterabhidānāt brajhabhāvo mukteḥ pūrvamapyastītyavagamena muktau na bheda iti cet, atra vadanti-brajhaśabdasya jīvavācitvena brajhaiva san jīvatvāvaccinna eva san (jīvabhāvamivihāyaiveti yāvat) brajha paramātmānam apyeti pranpoti, iti śrutyarthaḥ . muktāvapi na svatantro jīvaḥ api tu, paramātmasvāmikatvena tadadhīna eva vartate, idameva muktau jīvabhāvā'hānamiti tātparyam | na ca---brajhaśabdo na jīvavacīti saṅkyam | jījñāsādhikaraṇe `ata eva brajhaśabdasya jātyādyartāntaraṃ mā śaṅratavyam' iti śāṅkarabhāṣyākhyāne `ādiśabdena jīvakamalāsanaśabdarāśī grahaṇam' iti ānandagirīye ukte jīvavācitve'vivādāt |
(4) nanu `sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brajha' iti śrutyā brajhajagatoraikyokterabhedo yukta iti cet na | śruterabhede tātparyābhāvasya nirūpitatvat |
(5) `ahaṃ brajhāsmi' (bṛṃ.3.4.10) iti vākyamapi jīvabrajhaṇoraikyaṃ vaktīti nu śakyate vaktum | tasya--`brajha vā idamagra āsīttadātmānamevā'vet', `ahaṃ brajhavākyatvena jīvabrajhaikyaparatvābhāvāt | śrutyarthastu--jagato'gre pūrvam, brajhaivābhūt, tat brajha, ātmānam, ahaṃ brajhāsmīti avet ajānāt, tarmāt tathā jñānāddhetoḥ, sarvamabhavat pūrṇamabhavaditi | sṛṣṭeḥ bhāvaḥ | brajhaṇaḥ `ahaṃ brajhāsmi' iti jñānamudabhūdityarthakaṃ vākyamidaṃ jīveśābhede kathaṃ pramāṇaṃ syāt |
nanu ---`yo yo devānām' ityādyuttaravākyena devādīnāṃ madhyeyo'dhikārī, tat brajha veda, vāmadevopi tadetat brajha paśyan, `tadidamapyetarhi' iti `ya idaṃ veda ahaṃ brajhāsmīti sa idaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati' iti upāsakajñānānuvādena upāsakasya sarvatvasidderabhidhānām jīveśaikyasīddhiriti cet, na | tatra---`ahaṃ brajhāsmī' iti nopāsapajñānānuvādaḥ, kiṃtu `tadātmānam' ityādivākyaikadeśānuvādaḥ | tena `ahaṃ brajhasmi' at vākyaghaṭita `brajha vā' ityādivākyamupalakṣyate, tādṛśa jñānavat, nityapūrṇatvādimacca yat prasiddaṃ brajha `brajha vā idamagra' iti pūrvavākye nirūpitaṃ tat, etarjhapi (idānīmapi) yo vedetyarthasyaiva tataḥ pratīteḥ | tataśca nedamātmaikyaṃ pratipādayituṃ prabhavati | upāsakajñānānuvāda iti abhūmāne'pi ahaṃ brajhasadṛśo'smītyarthanarṇanasaṃbhavāt naikye tātparyamasya | atra--avet, abhavadityādau bhūtatatvaṃ na vivaśitaṃ brajhaṇaḥ svasvarūpavedanasya pūrṇatvādeśca sadātanatvāt |
(6) nanu---cāndogye caturte (11.khaṃ) upakosalavidyāyāṃ gāīpatyānvāhāryāhavanīyāstrayo'gnaya upakosalāya `ya āditye puraṣaḥ, yaścandramasi puruṣaḥ, yo vidyuti puruṣaḥ', `sohamasmi sa evāhamasmi' iti ādityādisthapuruṣamya ātmanaśca ekatvopadeśaśravaṇāt ātmaikye śrautamiti cet, na | triṣvapi vākyeṣu so'hamasmītyādeḥ tadadhīnaḥ, tatsadṛśoo vā'hamasmītyarthāt | yadyatrātmaikyamabhimataṃ, tarhi triṣvapyupadesavākyeṣu `ya āditye puriṣa' ityeva vaktavye candrādisthānabheśavākyeṣu `ya āditye puruṣa, ityeva vaktavye candrādisthānabhedakathanaṃ vyartaṃ syāt | na ca ādityādi,u vidyamānaḥ puruṣa eka eveti jñāpanāya stānabhedoktiriti vācyam, tajjñāpanasyā'prasaktaḥ | na ca--dvaitināṃ matepi candrādisthānabhedakathanaṃ vyartam, ādityasthaṣuādhinatvādikathanenaiva ātmādhīnatvādinā bhedasīddheriti vātyam | sarvāntaryāmiṇaḥ svarūpato bhedābhāvaḥ, adhiṣṭhānānāṃ niyamyānāmanekatvaṃ ceti bodhanāya stānabhedokteḥ | aikyavādinā tu naivaṃ vaktuṃ śakyam, śuddenaivaikyasamayābhimatatvena tasyaiva pratipādanīyatvāt | kiṃ ca aikyavādī kiṃ bhavān ādityapuruṣayooraikyamaṅgīkaroti, na veti praṣṭavyaḥ | yadyaṅgīkaromīti brūte tadā `ādityaḥ puruṣa' iti nirdeśena bhāvyaṃ, na tu---`āditye puruṣa' iti bhedena | yadi neti brate, tadadā anyeṣāmapi puruṣeṇaikyaṃ neti suvacaṃ bhedavādibhiriti vaktavyam |
(7) taittirīye tu `brajhavidānpotiparam' (tai.u.2.1) iti brajhavidāṃ parabrajhaprāptiṃ saṃkṣepeṇa prastutya tatra `satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brajha' iti mantravarṇasaṃvādaṃ darśayitvā `tasmādvā etasmādātmana ākśaḥ saṃbhūta' (tai.u.2.1.7) ityādinā brajhaṇavākyena jñeyatayā prāpyatayā ca prakṛtasya brajhaṇo jagatkartutvādilakṣaṇam, ānande tāratamyaṃ ca dvitīyavalyāṃ nirūpya, dvitīyavalyuktameva jagatkāraṇatvarūpabrajalakṣaṇaṃ bhṛguvalyāṃ pitāputrasaṃvādena pratipādya, upāsanāprakāraṃ ca darśayitvā `so'snute sarvān' iti mantravarṇagataphalavākyārthaṃ `asmāllokātpretya' (tai.u. 3.10.5) ityādinā spaṣṭayanaṃ saṃkṣepavistārābhyāṃ jīvaparayorbhedamevā''ha vedapuruṣaḥ |
tatra -- `asmāllokātpretya, etamannamayamātmānamupasaṃkramya, prāṇamayam o manomayam o vijñānamayam o ānandamayamātmānamupasaṃkramya, imān lakān kāmānnī kāmarūpī anusaṃcaram, etatsāmagāyannāste, (tai.u.3.10) ityādi śruyate | asya `sa yaścāyaṃ puruṣe, yaścāsāvāditye, sa ekaḥ, sa ya evaṃ vit' (tai.u.3.102) tṛptiriti vṛṣṭau' (tai.u.3.102) ityādinā adhyātmamadidaivaṃ ca bhagavadupāsanāṃ vidhāya upāsanāviṣayatayā nirdiṣṭasya brajhaṇo bhedaśaṅkānivṛttaye `yaśca' iti vākyadvaye prasaktaṃ brajha anadya `sa eka' iti vākyaṃ puruṣāpalakṣitamarvamānuṣāntaryāmiṇaḥ, ādityopalakṣitasarvadevāntaryāmiṇaśca ekatvaṃ (abhedaṃ) vidadhat sarvānryāmibhagavatsvarūpāṇāṃ bhedābhāvaṃ bodhayati | pūrvoktopāsanāyā antaryāmirūpatadbhedābhāvajñānasya ca phalamāha-sa evaṃ vidityādi | ayamarthaḥ--yaḥ adhikārī, evam uktaprakāreṇa, pavit bhagavantamupāsya, antaryāmiṇo'bhedaṃ jānāti, saḥ jñānī, asmāt lookāt etasmāt śarīrāt, pretya ūrdhva gatvā, annamayamityādeḥ tattatkośagatatvena tattatpadavācyamityarthaḥ | ātmānam paramātmanam, upasaṃkramya prāpya, (mukto bhūtveti yāvat kāmānnī kāmyamānaviṣayabhogavān‌, annaśabdo viṣayasāmānyaparaḥ, kāmaśabdaḥ kāmyamānaparaḥ karmadhārayānmatvartoya iniḥ, kāmarūpīḥ, kāmaśabdaḥ kāmyamānaparaḥ, karmadārayānmatnarthiya iniḥ, kāmarūpī abhimatarūpavān, atrāpi pūrvavadiniḥ, imān lokān bhūrādīn lokān, anusaṃcaran gacchan, etat vakṣyamāṇam, sāma `hā huva' ityādi sāmaviseṣam, yadvā-etat śāstreṣu pratipāditam, sāmabrajha, gāyan gānaṃ kurvan, stuvan, āste yatra ruciḥ tatrāsce | sāma darśayati-hā huveti | ayaṃ paramānandānāptijanītānandaviśeṣadyotakaḥ ato nā'syārthaviśeṣacintā | ahamannam, svāvaraṇāmahamupajīvyo'smī | ahamannādaḥ viṣayabhogaśīlaḥ, `jakṣan krīḍan ramamāṇa' (chā.5.12.3) ityādyukteḥ ahaṃ ślokakṛt hareḥ kīrtiṃ sarvatra prakāśamānāṃ kurvan, sarvatra hareranugrahānmamedṛsaṃ bhāgyamiti bhāvaḥ | sarvamidaṃ muktasya caturmukhasyetyāha-hamasmi prathamajā iti | ṛtasya jñānarūpasya satyasya vā bhagavato hareḥ, ahaṃ prathamajo'smi | kasmātprathamatvamityata āha---pūrvaṃ saṃbabhūva' (mu.1.1.1) `yo brajhāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvaṃ yo vai vedāṃśca prahiṇoti tasmai | taṃ ha devamātmabuddiprakāsaṃ mumukṣarve śaraṇamahaṃ praradye (śve.6.18) `hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavartatā'gre bhūtasya jātaḥ patireka āsīt | sa dadhāra pṛthivī dyāmutemāṃ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema' (ṛ.saṃ.8.7.3) ityādau caturmukhasyaprathamotpannatvaśravaṇāt | kiṃ ca, amṛtasya muktavargasya, nābhiḥ āśrayaḥ `arā iva rathanābhau' ityādau nābhipadasya āśryārthakatvaṃ prasiddham | `kāryaṃ bdarirasya gatyupapatteḥ (bra.sū.4.3.10) brajhaṇā saha te sarve saṃprāpte pratisacare | parasyānte parātmānaḥ praviśanti paraṃ padam' ityādau kāryabrajha (caturmukha) pratapteḥ, tena saha paramapadapraveśasya cābhidhānāt tasya muktāśrayatvam, parasyānte tena saha paramapadapraveśakathanāt parabrajha prāpayitṛtvaṃ ceti jñāyate | sa enān brajhagamayati' (chā.4.15) ityāsya, saḥ amānavaḥ puru,ḥ, enān muktān, brajha kāryabrajha, paraṃ brajha ca, nayati prāpayatītyartaḥ | tatra mānavaḥ purūṣaḥ, vāyuḥ caturmukhaśca | vāyuḥ kāryabrajha nayati, caturmukhaḥ paraṃbrajheti vivekaḥ | `amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ' (mu.2.1.5) iti śrutistu hareḥ sarvamuktāśrayatvamāha | evaṃ śārtrābhyāsapravacanābhyāṃ prāptaṃ svavībhavaṃ prakāsya, anyasyāpi upadeṣṭustattadyogyasukaṃ bhaviṣyatītyāha-- ya iti | yaḥ adhikārī, māḥ pramāṇāni, viṣṇūtkarṣapratipādakāni śāstrāṇi, dadāti ṇanyebhye yogyebhya upadiśati | saḥ adhikārī, it eva, evameva, svayogyaṃ yāvatsukamanubhavati | (pratamajatvaṃ tu na bhavati) dadātītyupalakṣaṇaṃ svasvayogyaśravaṇāderapi | mā mām, avāḥ pratapnuyāt, chāndasatvāt vyatyayena madhyamapuruṣaḥ | `ava rakṣaṇe'--- tyādidaṇḍake `dīptyavāptyāliṅigana' iti pāṭhāt prāptyarthakarvaṃ dhātoḥ |
adhyeṣyate ca ya imaṃ dharmyaṃ saṃvādamāvayoḥ |
jñānayajñena tenāhamiṣṭaḥ syāmiti me matiḥ ||
śraddhāvānanasūyaśca śrṛṇuyādapi yo naraḥ |
so'pi muktaḥ śubhān lokān prāpnuyātpuṇyakarmaṇām ||
(gī.15.70.71)
iti uide,yuḥ śrotuśca śubhalokāvāpteḥ phalatvena bhagavatokteḥ | ślooke sa iti upade,ṭṛparāmarśakam, apiḥ śrotṛsamuccāyakaḥ . ahamannam sarveṣāmahamupajīvyaḥ, annam harerannam, atra nnatvaṃ nśyatvam, adantam karmaphalaboktāram, ajhi upasaṃhakāmi, harerājhayā sarvāmupasaṃhākāmi | sarveṣāmīśvarādhīnātvena tadādiṣṭakāryakāritvaṃ dharma iti bhāvaḥ | aham viśaṃ bhuvanam sarvaṃ jagat abhi abhavam guṇaiḥ tiraskurvāṇo'bhavam, guṇaiḥ śreṣṭho'hamasmīti yāvat | tarhi kiṃ svatantro bhavānityata āha--suvarṇajyotiriti | suvarṇaḥ paramātmā jyotiḥ prerakaḥ yasya saḥ, edamiti vartate | ahaṃ nirduṣṭaparamānandaparamātmapereritaḥ tatparatantra iti yāvat | ya evamiti . yo viriñcaḥ, evaṃ veda paratantro'haṃ hareranugrahāt pūrvanirūpitabhāgyavānabhavamiti jānāti saḥ `sāmāgāyannāsta' iti pūrveṇa saṃbandaḥ |
caturmukhasya mukhyādhikāritvena tadanubhavaṃ nirūpayan vedapuruṣaḥ etaddṛṣṭāntena sarve'pi muktāḥ svasvayogyatānusāreṇa muktau sukhamanubhavitumarhantīti bodhayatīti |
atra brajhaprāptitatstutisāmagānādernirūpaṇāt muktau bheda eveti śrutitātparyam | `tadabrajhetyupāsīta brajhavān bhavati' (tai.u.3.10) iti vākyabodhitabrajhatvopāstiphalaṃ brajhavattvamapi brajhaguṇavattameva na tu tattādātmyam | itthameva abhiyuktaiḥ saṃkarācāryaprabhṛtibhiridaṃ vākyaṃ vyākhyātam | nanu---śrauto'pyayaṃ mauktiko bhedaḥ kālpanika iti cet, na | muktau māyānuvṛtterabhāvena anyasya ca tatkalpakasyābhāvena tatra tasya tattve mā3nābhāvāt | nanu---asti muktau gauṇamukhyabhedaḥ phalaśruteśca gauṇamuktiviṣayakatvamityādi svetpreśritaṃ vaiyākaraṇairādaṇīyamityatra na kaścinnirbandhaḥ | `ta ime satyāḥ kāmā anṛtāpidānā' (chā.8.3.1) ityādinā saṃsāre'jñānādinā''vṛtaṃ satyakāmādikamajñānanivattau muktāvāvirbhavatīti pratipādanāt muktau kāmādīnā satyātvameva na tu māyikatvam |
nanu--asyāmevopaniṣadi `so'kāmayat bahusyāṃ prajāyeya' (tai.u.2.6) iti brajhaṇa eva baṅubhāvookteḥ `tadevānuprāviśat' (tai.u.2.6) iti tasyaiva sarvatra praveśokteśca, tadatiriktātmano'bhāvena abheda eva yukta iti cet, phacyate --bhagavata eva niyāmakabahurūpeṇa sarvatra praveśa iti tadarthāt, jīvaniyāmakatayā jīvāntaryāmitvasya vājasaneye antaryāmibrakājhaṇaṃ (5) ukteśca bhagavadrūpāṇāmabheda iti tātparyeṇa bhagavadatiriktātmā'bhāve mānābhāvāt jīvaparayorabhedāsiddhirityādi anyatra vistareṇa nirūpitāmiti |
(8) nanu---bṛhadāramyake caturthasya turye, ṣaṣṭhasya pañcame ca `yatra dvaitamiva bhavati' `taditara itaraṃ jighrati, paśyati' (bṛ.4.4.64,6.5.15) ityādinā bhedaṃ pratiṣudhya, `yatra vā ātmaivābhūt tatkena kaṃ jigret paśyet' iti muktau ātmamātrāśeṣabodhakena vākyena ātmātiriktavastusāmānyābhāvaṃ bodhayitvā, `na pretya saṃjñā'sti' iti muktānāṃ jñānābhāvaḥ pratipāditaḥ, sa ca viṣayābhāvaprayojya eva, ato jñāyate muktau aātmaikatvameveti cet, brūmaḥ--udāhṛtena tena śrutivākyena, na muktau bhedābhāvaḥ, svarūpamātrāvaśeṣaḥ jñānābhāvasca bodhyate | yadi tena jñānābhāvaḥ pratipādyate, tarhi ātmābhāva eva bodhito bhavet, jñānasvarūpatvādātmanaḥ | tataśca ātmajñānasya ātmajñānasya ātmanāśa eva phalamiti jānan tatsādhanaśravaṇādau na ko'pi pre7āvān pravarteta | apravṛttau ca tadvidheḥ vaiyartyamāpadyeteti sarvaṃ śārtramapārthakaṃ bhavet | saṃjñānāstītyasya tu ayamarthaḥ-saṃjñāpadam `ātaścopasarga' (pā.sū.3.3.106) iti karmaṇi aṅantaṃ jñānaviṣaya atyartakam | pretya sthitaḥ puruṣaḥ, muktaḥ sana, saṃjñā amuktaiḥ jñeyo na bavatīti | amukto muktajīvaṃ na paśyatīti bhāvaḥ |
`yatra hi dvaitamiva' ityāsya cāyamartaḥ --dvaitapadaṃ dvābhyāṃ prakārābhyāmiti dvītaṃ prakāradvayaviśiṣṭaṃ dvitvaviśiṣṭamiti vā (bhedavaditi yāvat) tataḥ tasya bhāva ityarye `hāyanāntayuvādibhyoṇa' (pā.sū.5.1.13) ityanena yuvāditvādami bheda ityarthakam, yatra yadadā, dvaitaṃ bheda;, bhavati asti, tadā itaraḥ itaraṃ jighrati, pasyatīti | evaṃ ca--ghrāmadarśanādikaṃ bhedavyāptamityuktaṃ bhavati | atroktaṃ ghrāṇādikaṃ `jakṣan krīḍan ramamāṇa' (cā.8.123) ityādāvuktajakṣaṇāderapyupalakṣaṇam | hi śabdaḥ pramāṇaprasiddhiṃ bodhayati | nanu siddāyāṃ vyāptau kiṃ sādyamityata āha--yatra vā ātmeti | muktau jñānānaṅīkāre'tiprasaṃgamāha anena veda puruṣaḥ | akṣarārthastu--yatra yadā, ātmaivābhūt, tat tadā kena kaṃ jighret, paśyet na kamapi, jigrati, paśyati ca, `dakṣatkrīḍanramamāṇa' ityādyukteḥ | tataśca-muktā-vātmamātrā'vaśeṣe tatra jakṣaṇādibodhakaśrutirapramāṇaṃ bhavediti | etena `saṃjñānāstī' tyasya 5ānā'viṣaya ityevārtho nyāyyaḥ | yathā mudgarāgauau prakṣipto mudgaḥ samānābhihārāt mayākṣipta'yaṃ mudga iti vili,ya grahītu na śakyate, tathā muktasaṃghe praviṣṭo muktaḥ prākṛtanāmarūparahitatvāt muktajanairayaṃ mukta iti viśiṣya na gṛjhata iti bhāvaḥ | na ca --`brajha taṃ parādt yo'nyatrā''tmano brajha veda' (6.4.6) iti ṣaṣṭhe khaṇḍe brajhādīnāṃ `ātmano'nyatra' (hareranyatra) avastānaṃ viduṣaḥ deśānniṣkāsanaṃ vidhāya `idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā' iti tatkhaṇḍīyāntimanākyena braḍhadīnāṃ sarveṣāmātmābheda upadiśyate, tena ātmaikyasiddhiḥ, aikyādeva ātmano'nyatra sthitirnetyuktaṃ bhavati; evaṃ ca ṇanyatrastitiṃ jānato vihitaṃ niṣkāsanaṃ bhedajñānina eveti phalatīti vācyam | anyatrasthitipratiṣedhena kiṃ brajhādayo nirāśrayā iti śaṅkāyāṃ tadvāraṇāya pravṛttasya `yadayamātmā' iti vākyasya, idaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvektaṃ brajhādikam, yat yatra vartate, ayam sarvādhāraḥ, ātmā paramātamā, ityarthena abedabodhakatvābhāvāt | brajhādayo na nīrāśrayāḥ kiṃ tu paramātmānamāśritya vartante---
rūdraṃ samāśritā devā rudro brajhāṇamāśritaḥ |
brajhā māmāśrito nityaṃ nāhaṃ kaṃcidupāśritaḥ ||
iti bhagavadvacanāt `matstāni sarvabhūtāni' (9.4) `mayisarvamidaṃ protaṃ sūtre maṇigaṇā iva' (7.7) iti gītokteśca | yaditi tu `supāṃṃ suluk' (pā.sū.7.1.21) iti luptasaptamyantam avyayaṃ vā | na ca ---anyatra sthitijñāsyāparādhavidhāyakena `yo'nyatra brajha veda' iti vākyenaiva ātmani stitirami siddeti `yadayam' at vākyaṃ vyarthamiti vācyam | nirāśrayā brajhādaya ityapi tena pratītyā ātmāśrayatvasya spaṣyamalābhāt | ayaṃ cātmā `ātmā vā are draṣṭavya' ityādiśravaṇādividhiviṣayatayopadiṣṭaḥ paramātmaiva |
(9) nanu `yasmin sarvāṇibhūtāni ātmaivā'bhūdvijānataḥ | tatra ko mohaḥ kaḥ śoka ekatvamanupaśyata' iti īśāvāsyagato mantro jīvaparayoraikye pramāṇaṃ, tatraikatvajñānino mohaśokābhāvarūpaphalokteriti cet, atra vadanti -- ayaṃ mantraḥ `yasmin' ityādi `tatra' ityatena | `yastu sarvāṇi bhūtāni' ityādi ṣaṣṭhamantroktamīśvarasya sarvāndhāratvaṃ sarvāntaryāmitvaṃ cānuvadan antaryāmirūpasyaikatvaṃ paśyato mohaśokābhāvaḥ phalāntaraṃ, anuvādastu samuccayārthaḥ | sarvādāratvāsarvāntaryāmitvajñāninaḥ, ekatvajñāninaś‌coktaṃ phalamiti tātparyam | akṣarayojanā tu -- yasmīn harau sarvabhūtāni ti,ṭhanti, tatra bhūteṣu, ātmaiva harīreva, abhūt vartate bhūtakālo na vivakṣyate, śiṣṭaṃ sugamamiti | jīvaparayoraikyaparatayā yojane tu saptamīnirdeśānupapattirityanyatra vistaraḥ | kiṃ bahunā `amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ (mu.2.2.5) etatsāmagāyannāste (tai.u.3.904) muktānāṃ paramā gatiḥ, (sahastra) muktopasṛpyavyapadeśāt' (bra.sū. 1.3.2) ityādibhīḥ muktāśrayatvatatstyutva-tatsevyatvādeḥ paramātmano vyapadeśāt muktāvapi bheda eva muktaparamātmanoḥ, ata eva `etasyaivānandasyānyāni mātrāmupajīvanti' (bṛ.6.3.32) iti śrutirmuktānāṃ bhagavadānandaikadeśānandasadṛśānubhava ityāha | `ānando brajhati vyajānāt' (tai.u.2.4.3) iti bhagavadānandasya bhagavatsvarūpatvokteḥ anyena kenāpi āptumaśavyatvāt, anyasyā''nando'nyenānubhaktiṃ duḥsaka iti sarvānubhavasiddhatvācca, uktameva ānandaleśaṃ tatsadṛśānandam, anyāni bhūtāni muktajīvāḥ upajīvanti anubhavanti, dhātūvāmanekāऱthatvāt jīvateranubhave vṛtiriti |

`yo'sāvasau puruṣaḥ so'hamasmi' iti īśāvāsyamantrasyāpi yaḥ pūṣādiṣu vartate, asau prāṇe'pi vartate, ahaṃ, saḥ tadadhīnaḥ tatsadṛśo vā'smi ityarthāt nābhede tātparyamiti kṛtaṃ bahunā |
`anāntā vai vedā' (kā. 1-pra.36) `anantaśāstraṃ bahu veditavyam' ityādi śrutismṛtiṣu vedādisāstrāṇāmanantatvokteḥ puruṣāyuṣā sarvaṃ śāstraṃ veditumasakyamiti bhagavān gītācāryo jīveśabheda eva tātparyaṃ śāstrāṇāmiti sarvasāktrārtasaṃgrahe gītāpañcadase'rjunāyopadisati sarvāstrārthabhūtaṃ bhedameva, ataḥ sa eva sarvasāstrārtha ati sudhībhirākalanīyam | tatra hi bhagavānāha---
dvāvimau puraṣau loke kṣaraścākṣara eva ca |
kṣaraḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni kūṭasto'kṣara ucyate ||
utmaḥ puruṣastvanyaḥ paramātmetyudāhṛtaḥ |
yo lokatrayamāvisya bibhartyavyaya īśvaraḥ ||
yalmātkṣaramatīto'hamakṣarādapi cottamaḥ |
ato'smiṃloke vede ca prathitaḥ puruṣottamaḥ ||
yo māmevamasaṃmūḍho jānāti puruṣottamam |
sa sarvavidbhajati māṃ sarvabhāvena bhārata ||
iti gujhatamaṃ sāstramidamuktaṃ mayā'nagha |
etadbudhvā buddhimān syātkṛtyasca bhārata ||
(gī.15.13-20)
iti | ślokārthastu---`7raḥ, akṣaraḥ, īśvara' iti trayaḥ padārtāḥ, tatra kṣarapadena `kṣaraḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni' iti atraivokteḥ kṣaraṇadharmacetanācetanapadārthasāmānye grājham, akṣarapadena 7raṇadharmaśūnyaṃ nityaṃ vastu `kūṭasta' ityakteḥ | kūṭamthapadasya `kūṭasthairavicālibhiḥ' iti paspaśāhrikamabhāṣyadiśā nityārthakatvaṃ saṃpratipannam | tādṛsaṃ vasti lakṣmīḥ, cetanasya kṣaraṇadharmatvaṃ tu prakṛtikāryadehādidvārā puruṣapadametadubhayārtakam | tataśca --- lakṣmītaditaracetanācetanavargābhyām; anyaḥ puruṣaḥ yaḥ sa uttamaḥ, saḥ ka iti cedāha---paramātmeti | yaḥ sāstre tathā udāhṛtaḥ sa ityarthaḥ | tasyāsādāraṇadharmamāha---ya iti yaḥ paramātmā, lokatrayadhāraṇapoṣaṇādikartā īśvaraḥ sarvasvāmī avyaya iti codāhṛtaḥ | purūṣoottamapadapravṛttinimittaṃ spa|ṭamāhayasmāditi | uktāśāstrārte.़saṃmūḍhaṃḥ vyāmoharahitaḥ san māṃ puruṣottamam evam uktaprakāreṇa jānāti, sa sarvavit sarvaśāstrārthavit, sarvaśāstre evamevookteḥ, sarvabhāvena pūrvoktasarvaprakāreṇa (puruṣottamatvādina) bhadati sevate (evaṃ bhajanīyamiti yāvat) idamatirahasyaṃ śāstram avaśyakartavyadharmapratipādakaṃ śāstraṃ, śāstraśiṣṭo dharmo vā | etat upadiṣṭaṃ śāstraṃ, buddhvā jñātvā, buddhimāna kartavyeṣu pratibhāvān, ata eva kṛtakṛtyaḥ kartavyaṃ kṛtavān sacchāstraśravaṇaṃ tadarthānuṣṭhānaṃ ca kṛtavān syāditi saṃbandaḥ, spaṣṭamanyat | carācaraprapañcajātāt bhinnaḥ puruṣottamapadavācyaḥ paramātmā śāstrapratipādyaḥ, taṃ tatā jānato bhadatasca janma saphalamiti bhāvaḥ | sarvabhāveneti karmadārayaḥ bhāvaśabda uktapuruṣoottamatvādiprakāravācī . ata eva pīrvaṃ bhagavataḥ sarvottamatvādinirūpaṇaṃ sārthakam | yadyevaṃ na bhajate mumukṣuḥ śāstre tannirūpaṇaṃ nirarthakaṃ, guṇanirūpaṇasya jñānopāsanaphalakatvāt | atha vā--sarvabhāvena pitṛtvādinā `pitā,़hamasya jagata' (gī.9.17) ityukteḥ, saṃpūrṇapramṇā paramabhaktyeti yāvaditi vārthaḥ | yattu sarvasya bhāva iti ṣaṣṭīsamāsaḥ sarvarūpeṇa bhajatīti tadarthaḥ sarvarūpeṇa bhajatīti vākyārtha iti nāgeśīyapakṣapātināṃ vyākhyānaṃ tat nādartavyam | tathā sati pūrmaṃ sarvarūpatvameva nirūpaṇūyaṃ na tu puruṣottamatvādi . evaṃ ca---jīveśayorbhedaḥ sarvaśāstrārtha iti gītācāryāṇāmāśyaḥ | pāṇinerapi ayamevāsaya iti pūrvaṃ nirupitam |
rāmānujīye---kṣarākṣarapede muktāmuktavargapar iti vyākhyātam |
śāṃkare---kṣarapadaṃ vikārajātaparam, akṣarapadaṃ tu kṣarapadavācyasya puruṣasya utpattibījaparamiti |
evamanyānyapi abedaparatayā pratīyamānāni śrutismṛtivacanāni bhedaparatayā yojyāni, yojitāni ca bahupramāṇopanyāsena, bhejaparatayā brajhavādībiranyatra | kiṃ ca prativedāntaṃ pratividyaṃ sṛṣṭyādinirūpaṇaṃ, brajhaḍīvayorupāsyopāsakabhāvādinirūpaṇe ca vāsudevasyotkar,ādāyakaṃ brajhajīvabhedatātparyakaṃ ceti prapañcitaṃ vistareṇa taireva |
etena ---`prativedāntaṃ sṛṣṭyādinirūpaṇamātmaikatvavyutpādanāyeti mañjū,oktam, `tattavamasyādivākyamakhaṇḍārtena śuddenāpi brajhaṇā ādhyāsikaṃ tādātmyam' iti tatroktam, `catvāri śrṛṅgā' (ṛ.3.8.10) iti ṛgarthanirūpaṇaparabhāṣyakaiyaṭavyākhayāvasaraṃ `śabdaḥsvābhedamāviṣkṛtavān, `saktumiva' iti śrutitātparyanirūpaṇāvasare samānakhyātayaḥ bhedagrahasya nivṛttatvāt sarvapramāṇaviruddam, vyāsapāṇinipatañjalīnāmasaṃmate ca, sṛṣṭyādinirūuṇasya harāvupāsyatvaprayojakotkarṣavyutpādakatvādityuktatvāt . atata eva bhagavān vyāsaḥ `janmādyasya yataḥ' (brajha.sū.1.1.2) iti naye jijñāsyasya brajhaṇaḥ sṛṣṭyādikartṛtvaṃ lakṣaṇamabhidāya tata eva utkarṣasadbhāvāt, brajha cetanācetanajagadvilakṣaṇamityasūcayat | anena sūcitameva vailakṣaṇyaṃ prāyeṇa pratyadhikaraṇaṃ jīvāvṛttiguṇavattvabodhaka `bhedavyapadesāt' ityādinā spaṣṭīkaroti | `mahatā devena naḥ sāmyaṃ yathā syādityadhyeyaṃ vyākaraṇam' ityadhyetṛṇāṃ bhagavatsāmyameva phalamiti kaṇṭhato bhāṣyakāraiḥ `catvāri' iti śruterabhiprāyavarṇanāt, sakhāyaḥ sakhayānītyanayoḥ tyaktabedabhāvanāḥ, sāyujyānīti bhā,yakārairavyākhyātatvena kaiyaṭādauṃ tatāvyākyānaṃ cintyam | sāyujyamityarte'pi nātmaikyalābhaḥ atisūkṣmadarśibhirapi, vibhāgena darśanāyogyasaṃyogaviśeṣasyaiva sāyujyapadārthatvāt . evaṃ ca --`sarvamekam' iti kaiyaṭaḥ, `ātmānā saṅa tyaktabhedabhāvanā' iti nāgeśaśca cintyaḥ | taduktārtasyakṣaramaryādayālābhāt, bhāṣyānukteśca | bhāṣyākṣarārthastu-atreti viṣayasaptamī, ṣaṣṭhyarthe vā sā | atra brajhaṇami viṣaye, brajhaṇa iti vārthaḥ, sakhāyaḥ mitrāṇi, brajhasakhyasya phalamāha-sakhyānīti | `sakhyuryaḥ' (pā.sū.5.1.126) iti bhāve karmaṇi vā yapratyayāntaṃ sakhyapadam | vāsudevakānāmiti śeṣaḥ | vāsidevabhaktānāṃ maitrāṇi, jānate prāpnuvanti | vāsudevabhaktānāmapi mitrāṇi bhavantīti yāvat | atra padaṃ vyākhyāti ya eṣa iti || durgaḥ gurukulavāsaśāstrābhyāsādinā gamya iti durgaḥ, mārgaḥ anveṣaṇīyaḥ, `so'nveṣṭavya' iti śruteḥ, `mārga anveṣaṇa' iti caurādikātkarmaṇi ghañ | ekagamyaḥ mukhyatvena gamyaḥ, vāgviṣayaḥ śrutipratipādyaḥ, praśanaprativacavābhyāmādhikāraṇamāha---ke ityādi | teṣāmadhikāritve hetuṃ pṛcchati-kuta iti |. hetumevāha---bhadrati || eṣām vaiyākaraṇānām adhivāci vāgindraye, bhadrā mokṣalakṣaṇaśāśvatikasaṃpatkarī, lakṣamīḥ mokṣasādhanavāsudevottamatvajñānasādhana prakṛtipratyayādivibhāgapūrvakaśrutyarthasaṃvitpūrvakapravaktṛtvarūpā saṃpat, nihitā sthāpitā | yadvā--vāsudevottamatvajñānaṃ mokṣasādhanamiti yā lakṣamīḥ, adhivāci śrutyādau, nihitā, sā lakṣmīḥ eṣāṃ bhavatītiṃ |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- vedādau yaduktaṃ vāsudevasarvetamatvajñānaṃ ṇokṣasādanamiti tajjñānaṃ tādṛśajñānotpādakaśāstrapravacanaveduṣyaṃ vaiyākaraṇānāmeva, ta eva hi vedādiśāstrajanyajñānasaṃpādane śāstrapravacatakaraṇe ca prabhavanti, yata ete tadupakaraṇaśabdasāstre'dhītinaḥ, ete,veva bhagavato bhāgavatānāṃ ca prītirutpadyate, eteṣāṃ sacchāstrādhyayanādhyāpanādau mukhyadhikāritvāditi | sakhyānīti bahuvacanaṃ tu nirūpakabhedāt | pāṇunestu neṣṭaṃ jīvaparamātmaikyamiti pūrvameva nirūpitam |
viruddavatpratīyanta āgamā yatra vai mithaḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭānusāreṇa taṣāmartho'nvavekṣyata ||
iti vacanāt, `dvā suparṇā' ityādibhedāgamairviruddhavadavabhāsamānānāṃ tattvamasyādivākyānāṃ tatsādṛśyādiparatayā bheda eva tātparyāvadāraṇena pratīyamānavirodhaḥ pariharaṇīyaḥ | dṛṣṭaṃ pratyakṣam | pratyakṣānukūlāgamāvirodhena pratīyamānavirodhāḥ tattvamasyādyāgamā netavyāityakṣarārthaḥ | pratyakṣānukūlā āgamāstu bhedapara `dvā suparṇā sayujā' ityādayo jñātavyāḥ | ittameva śutivākyānāṃ yojanā | vāsudevasya sarvottamatvamabhiprayataḥ pāṇunerapyācāryasya saṃmateti apare phāṇinīyāḥ | `sukhadaḥkhajñānaniṣpattyaviśeṣāt aikātmyam' (vai.3.2.12) `vyavastāto nānā' (20) śastrasāmarthyācca (21) iti trisūtryā ātmaikatvamāśaṅkya ātmanānātvaṃ siddāntitaṃ kaṇādena | atrādyena ekatvamityākṣepaḥ, dvābhyāmuttarābhyāṃ nānātvamiti siddhānta iti upaskāravṛttivitikārau | bhāṣyakṛccandrakāntastu āmmaikakvameva siddāntaḥ nānātvaṃ tu aupādhikamiti trisūtrīṃ vyācaṣṭe | paraṃ, śaṃkarabhāṣyādiṣu kaṇādasya bhedavāditvena parigaṇanāt candrakāntavyākhyānaṃ mūlaviruddham |
tattvamasyādivākyānāṃ nābhedaparatā tataḥ |
tadvirodho nāsti bhede vyāsapāṇinisaṃmate ||
śabdārthayorakhaṇḍatvanirāsaḥ
nanu padapadārthau vākyātadarthau cā'khaṇḍau, akhaṇḍameva vākyaṃ bodhakamaऱṇḍārthasya, `akhyatadarthau śuddena brajhaṇa' iti śaktivicāre mañjūṣāyām, `śuddhacittasya tūbhayamapi bodhakamakhaṇḍaviṣayasya, evaṃ sarvabodhakeṣu brajhatvabuddau jātāyāṃ tenaiva dṛṣṭāntena sarvapadārteṣu brajhatvabuddhirvaiyākaraṇānāma,' iti `saktumiva' iti śrutyarthavicāraparabhāṣyavyākhyāvasare paspaśāyāmudyote coktatvāt brajhaṇo'khaṇḍatvena, sarvatra brajhatvabuddhyudayena ca sarvaṃ brajhaiva, brajhātiriktaṃ vastu kiṃcanana nāstyeveti sidhyatīti `tattvamasi' ityādivākyānāmabhedaparatvena yojanameva yuktamiti cet, atra vadanti---naitadyuktaṃ sūtrabhāṣyakārābhyāṃ prakṛtaśrutestathā'vyākhyānāt, tasyārthasya prakṛtaśāstrapratipādyaviṣayāsaṃbaddhatvenānupayogāt, viṣayākhaṇḍatvasya vede pāṇinīyaṃ tantre brajhamīmāṃsāyāṃ ca siddhāntā'karaṇāt, vaiyākaraṇānāṃ sarvatra brajhatvabudderabhāvāt prakṛte tannirūpaṇasyāprasakteśca | etena akhaṇḍārthastu mānalabodhaviṣayaḥ, `neha nānā' - iti niṣedhavākyasahakāreṇa dharmopalakṣite śaktigrahāt `tadupalakṣitākhaṇḍārthasyaiva bodha' iti śaktivicāre (ma. 4.25) tatā vṛttivicāre `yatā vedāntināṃ mañjūṣāyāmuktam, samarthasitrodyote `tasmādvedāntimate tattvamasyādivākyādāvakhaṇḍārthabodha eva vṛttau' (pu.33) ityuktiśca cintyam | `neha nānā' iti vākyasya brajhātiriktapadārthasāmānyaniṣedhakatvābhāvasyopari vakṣyamāṇatvena brajhavādibhiranyatra tatā siddhāntitatvena ca tatsahakāreṇa brajhaṇo'khaṇḍatvā'siddhoḥ, dvitīyamānasabodhasya sarvatra sarveṣāmanudayāt, tathābodhasya puruṣaviśeṣaniṣṭhatvena `tattvamasi' ityādau sarveṣāṃ tādṛśabodhājananāt, bhagavato vyāsasya, pāṇineḥ, patañjaleśca vākyaviṣayavyavasthā'yogāt | tathā hi -- `yatā vedāntinām' iti dṛṣṭāntastāvadanupapannaḥ, tanmate śabdatadabhidheyayorakaṇḍatvasya durvacatvāt, brajhātiriktastunmate māyikatvābhāvāt | vedāntipadena bhagavān bādarāyaṇa eva mukhyato vyapadeśamarhati na tvanye bhāṣyakārā tavṛttikārā vā sarvasaṃpratipannatvābhāvāt sarvasaṃmatapuriṣavacanaṃ hi dṛṣṭāntatāṃ bhajeta |
tairvijñāpitakāryastu bhagavān puruṣotmaḥ |
avatīrṇo mahāyogī satyavatyāṃ parāśarāt ||
utsannān bhagavān vedānujjahāra hariḥ svayam |
caturdhā vyabhajattāśca caturviśatidhā punaḥ ||
śatādhā caikadā caiva tataiva ca sahastradhā |
kṛṣṇo dvādaśadā caiva punastasyārthavittaye ||
cakāra brajhasūtrāṇi yeṣāṃ sūtratvamañjasā |
ityabhiyuktodāhṛtapramāṇaiḥ, `vādāntakṛdvedavideva cāham' (gī.15.15) iti bhagavadvacanena ca vyāsasyaiva vedāntakṛttvena sarvairnirdāritatvāt | gītā vāsudevasya tattvaṃ vakti, na vyāsasteti mā śaṅkanīyam | ubhayorbhagavato nārāyaṇasyaiva avatāratvāt | kṛṣṇāsya nārāyaṇānatāratvena kaścidvipratipadyeta, `ādikartā bhagavānnārāyaṇākhyo viṣṇuḥ bhaumasya brajhaṇo brājhamatvasya ca rakṣaṇārthaṃ devakyāṃ vasudevādaṃśena kṛṣṇāḥ kila saṃbabhūva' iti gītābhāṣyopakrame śrīśaṅkarācāryekteḥ, anyairapyācāryaiḥ anyatra ca tathokteḥ vvāsasya tu `tairvijñāpita' it.yādi pūrvodāhṛtapramāṇaiḥ `ko jhanyaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣānmahābhāratakṛdbhavam' ityādi pramāṇaiśca nārāyaṇāvatāratāvagamāt | ata evoktaṃ śāṅkaramatānuyāyinā vipaścitā ---
śaṃkaraḥ śaṃkaraḥ sākṣādvyāse nārāyaṇe hariḥ |
tayorvivāde saṃprāpte kiṃ kurmaḥ kiṃkarā vayam ||
iti śrīdhariye śrīmadbhāgavate `mahāmunikṛta' iti padyekadeśavyākhyānaṃ `nārāyaṇāvatāreṇa vyāsena muninā' ityuktiśca vyāsasya nārāyaṇāvatāratvamupodbalayati | sa ca vyāso gītāyāmanyatra ca brajhamīmāṃsāyāṃ ca bhagavatoo nārāyaṇasya jagatkartṛtvādyanekaguṇanatvaṃ kathamaṅgīkuryāt? yadi tattvaṃ tadabhimataṃ tadā `vikāraśabdānneti cenna prācuryāt ' (bra.sū.1.1.13) ityādinā vyātpattipradarśanapūrvaṃ tattacchabdaboodhitadharmavadarthapratyāyanaṃ na segaccheta |
kiṃ ca ---
yāni nāmāni gauṇani vikhyātni mahātmanaḥ |
ṛṣibhiḥ parigītāni tāni vakṣyāmi bhūtaye ||
iti mahābhārate bhagavannāmnāṃ maharṣipradarśitanirūktibalāt pratīyamānaguṇaviśiṣṭabhagavannāmnāṃ kathanapratijñānaṃ, viṣṇupurāṇe ca---
śuddhe mahāvibhūtyākhye pare brajhaṇi śabdyate |
maitreya bhagavacchabdaḥ sarvakāraṇakāraṇe ||
saṃbharteti tathā bhartā bhakāro'rthadvayānvitaḥ |
netā gamayitā straṣṭā gakārārthastathā mune ||
aiśvaryasya samagrasya vīryasya yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ |
jñānavijñānayoścaiva ṣaṇṇaṃ bhaga itīraṇā ||
vasanti tatra bhūtāni bhūtātmanyakhilātmani |
sa ca bhūteṣvaśeṣeṣu vakārārthastathāvyayaḥ ||
evameba mahāśabdo maitreya bhagavāniti |
paramabrajhabhūtasya vāsudevasya nānyagaḥ ||
atra pūjyapadārthoktiḥ paribhāṣāsamanvitaḥ |
śabdo'yaṃ nopacāreṇa jhanyatra jhupacārataḥ ||
(vi.pu.6.5.72-77)
iti bhagavacchabdaṃ nirucchabdaṃ nirucya tadbalena pratīyamānastraṣṭṛtvādiguṇaviśiṣṭabhagavato bhagavacchabdavācyatvābhidhānaṃ ca na saṃgacchaceta | tataśca neṣṭaṃ śabtārthākhaṇḍatvaṃ tasya bhagavato vyāsasyeti `vedāntinām' iti dṛṣṭāntābhidānaṃ na yuktam | śrutau ca--`sa vā eṣa ātmā hṛdi, tasyaitadeva nirūktaṃ hṛdyayamiti, tasmāddhṛdayamaharaharvā evaṃvitsvargaṃlookameti' (chā.8.3.3) `tasya ha vā etalya brajhaṇo nāma satyamiti (chā.8.34) iti `dāni ha vā trīṇyakṣarāṇi sītayamiti tadyatsattadamṛtatha yatti tanmatryamatha yadyaṃ tenobhe yacchati yadanenobhe yacchati tasmādyamaharaharvā evaṃvitsvargaṃ lokameti' (chā.8.35) iti daharavidyāyāṃ hṛtpuṇḍarīkākāśagatasya bhagavato nāmnoḥ hṛdayasatyaśabdayoḥ avayavārthamuktvā, tadviśiṣṭabhagavajjñāninaḥ phalasya ca nirūpaṇāt, `tadetatryakṣaraṃ hṛdayam' (bṛ.7.3.1) iti hṛdayanāmnastryakṣaratvamabhidāya tadakṣaratrayopāsanasya vājasaneye phalanirūpaṇācca, padādīnāmakhaṇḍatvavyutpādanaṃ śrutiviruddham |
maharṣiḥ pāṇirniṃrapi padapadārthānāṃ vākyavākyārthānāṃ cākhaṇḍatvaṃ necchati, yato'yamarthaviśeṣe śāsti kāryaṇi | tathā hi -- `antaraṃ bahiryogopasaṃvyānayoḥ, mṛṣastitikṣāyām, prakāśanastheyākyayośca, parājerasoḍhacha, dhvāṅkṣeṇa kṣepe, apavarge tṛtīyā, yeṣāṃ ca virodhaḥ śāśvatikaḥ, otāvaśyake, lakṣaṇahetvoḥ kriyāyāḥ, hetuhetumatorliṅ, jānapadakuṇḍagoṇa - indravaruṇa - tumunṇvulau kriyāyaṃ kriyārtāyām | dhātorekāco halādeḥ, kriyāsamabhivyāhāre yaṅ, rājaśvaśurādyat, kaumārāpūrvavacane, deyamṛṇe, bhūtapūrve caraṭ, tatra kuśalaḥ pathaḥ, praśaṃsāyāṃ rūpap, kṛbhvasti, kṣayyajayyai śakyārthe, saṃparibhyāṃ karotau bhūṣaṇe, nivāce vātatrāṇe, putre'nyatarasyām, nāñceḥ pūjāyām, tujhostātaṅṅāśiṣyanyatarasyām, añceḥ pūjāyām, varṣasyabhaviṣyati, bhiyo hetubhye ṣuk, aśanāyodanyadhanāyā bubhukṣāpipāsāgardheṣu, nityavīpsayoḥ, rājanvān saurājye, praṣṭho'gragāmini, vāhanamāhitāt' ityetairarthaviśeṣe sarvanāmasaṃjñādikāryāṇi uttarapadasaṃbandikāryāṇi ca prayoge boddhaṇāmetaiḥ śabdairarthaviśeṣopasthitirbhūyādityabhiprāyeṇa vidadhāti | śabdārthatattvakovidāḥ tamācāryābhiprāyaṃ jānanto vipaścitaḥ tattacchabdaprayogasthale'rthaviseṣa buddhavā, pravacanādisamaye parānapi bodhayanta ātmānamanyāṃścānandayanti | kātyāyano'pi `arthāccā'sannihita' ityādivacanairarthaviśeṣe pratyayotpattiṃ niyamayati, `upadeśe'janunāsika it' (pā.si.1.3.2) iti sūtre upareśapadaṃ karaṇaghañantaṃ vivakṣitārthaviśeṣabodhakaṃ bhavati ataḥ `upadeśane'c' iti kātyāyanavacanaṃ nārambhaṇīyam, sarvanāmasaṃjñāyā anvarthatvāt `saṃjñopasarjanībhūtā' iti vacanaṃ nāraṃbhaṇīyam, karoti kartṛkarmavyapadeśāniti yogena kriyetyasya lābhaḥ, upapadamiti mahāsaṃjñākaraṇāt `karmaṇyaṇ' (pā.sū.3.2.1) ityādi vidherapi padavidhitvasaṃbhavena `upapadavidau samarthavacanaṃ na vaktavyam' ityādi vadati bhāṣyakāraḥ | ato jñāyate padavākyayorakhaṇḍatvaṃ, padato vākyato vā'khaṇḍārtopasthitirbhavatītyetat sunitrayāsaṃmatamiti |

yadi śabdenākhaṇḍasyaivārthasyopastitiḥ na tatra śāstrīyakāryopādhibhūtārthaviśeṣānugatirītyucyate, tarhi--arthaviśeṣopādhinā kāryānuśāsanam, anvarthasaṃjñākaraṇam, saṃjñāyā anvarthatvavivaraṇaṃ ca nirarthakameva syāt | śabdasvabhāvasāmarthyamajñātvā śāstramakarot ācārya iti ajñatā ca ācāryasya kalpitā syāt | pravacanādau pāṇinīyaṃ śāstramanusṛtya arthaviśeṣaṃ vadanto'śabdamarthaṃ vadantīti nindanīyā bhaveyuḥ | kiṃ ca--rājapurūṣādipadairapi `tattvamasyā' dinā vākyena nirviśeṣākhaṇḍabrajhabodhavat bhedasesargānavaccinnapuruṣavyaktimātrabode vivakṣitārtāpratītyā vyavahārocchedaḥ |
nanu sarvamidaṃmāyikamiti vadatāṃ na kācidanupapattirasmākamiti vātyam, tatāvāde munitrayavacanābhāvāt | śabdānāṃ sāvayavatvaṃ samarthasūtrodyote `kecidityādinā prāhuḥ' ityantena gkantena samartitaṃ nāgeśena, tatra kimidaṃ svamatam, utānyadīyamiti vicāro'nyaḥ |
śabdārtayorakhaṇḍatvaṃ śrutyādīnāṃ na saṃmatam |
ityāhuḥ kovidāstatra śabdānāṃ yogadarśanāt ||
nānārthopādhinā kāryaṃ vidadhānasya pāṇineḥ |
kurvato'nvarthasaṃjñāṃ ca tayornākhaṇḍatā matā ||
vivṛṇvāno'nvarthasaṃjñāṃ yogādeva patañjaliḥ |
darśayan vārtikārthasya lābhaṃ necchatyakhaṇḍatām ||
bhedanirūpaṇam
nanu `yadyapi bhedo nirūpayutumaśakyaḥ, tathā hi ghaṭaḥ paṭādbhinna iti bhedajñānaṃ bhinnatvena gṛhītadharmipratiyogijñānapūrvakaṃ, tatra sa bhedo'yameva cet ātmāśryaḥ, bhedāntaraṃ cet tadapi bhinnayoreva dharmipratiyoginorjñātavyam, sa cet pūrvabheda eva tadā anyonyāśrayaḥ, tṛtīyo bhedo'nyaścet cakrakāpattiḥ, bhedaparaṃparāsvīkāro.़navasthā, antimabhedā'siddyā mūlabhūtabhedaparyantamasiddhermūlakṣayāt | ata eva-- bhedasyāsatsāmānyaśabdena kaiyaṭādau vyavahāraḥ | asattvamuktarītyā, sāmānyatvaṃ ca kevalānvayitvāt, tatāpi avicāritaramaṇīyenaiva māyikena bhedena vyavahāra iti vyāvahārikasyaiva sattvenākṣatiḥ | ghaṭādyarthasya ghaṭādipadavācyatvavat | anyathā--`ghaṭatvāśrayatvena ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ, ghaṭavṛttitvena ca idaṃ ghaṭatvam' ityanyonyāśrayatvāt . padārtamātrasyāvicāritaramaṇīyasya anirvacanīyasyaiva vyavahāraprayojakatvāt | ata evokitama śrīharṣeṇa---
`samastalokaśāstraikamatyamāśritya nṛtyatoḥ |
kā tavāstu gatistarkairvastudhīvyavahārayoḥ ||
anirvaktavyatāvādapasevāgatistayoḥ |
yathā sato'janirnaivamasato'pi janirna ca ||
iti |
janyatvameva janyasyā'nirvācyatvasamarpakam |
bhadasatyaparastarko bhedamāyāparo'paraḥ ||
bhedasatyaparastarko jhasan vedavirodataḥ |
eka vijñānanigamānneha nāneti ca śruteḥ ||
ityalam' iti grantena | enirvacaniyasarvasvākhyaśrīharṣakṛtakhaṇḍanakhādyagranthasaṃmatipradarśanapūrvaṃ padārthasāmā4nyaṃ tatrāpi bhedasvarūpamanirvacanīyaṃ (māyikaṃ, mithyā) iti nañryavāde mañjūṣayāṃ samarthitatvena, bhedapadārthasyaivābhāvena bhedaparatayā śrutyādīnāṃ yojanā na yukteti jīvesabhedāsiddhariti cet ---
atra vadanti--satyamuktamitthaṃ nāgesena, athāpi taduktiṃ nādriyante pramāṇaśaraṇāḥ padārthatattvavicāracaturā manīṣiṇaḥ | tathā hi -- śrīharṣo nāgesaśca `samastalokaśāstraikamatyamāśritya nṛtyatoḥ' ityādi vadan anuvadan sattvena sarvapramāṇasiddhasya paridṛśyamānasamastasya prapañjasya anirvacanīyatvaṃ samartayate | tataś‌ca brajhātiriktaṃ nikhilaṃ jagat asaditi tayorāntayobhiprāya iti tadīyagranthataḥ pratipadyate | tataśca tadvacanaireva tatpraṇītayorgranthayoḥ, tatra sādhitāyāḥ sarvānirvacanīyatāyāśca prathamataḥ anirvacanīyatā prānpoti tadgranthatatpratipāditasarvānirvacatayoḥ sarvapadārtā'ntaḥpātitvāt, sarvānirvacanīyatāyā anirvacanīyatāsiddhau ca sarveṣāṃ padārthānāṃ bhedasya ca satyatvaṃ sidhyatīti pareṣāmiṣṭāsiddhiḥ, asmākamabhimatasiddhiśca bhavati |
kiṃ ca--bhedapadārta eva neti vadan vādī praṣṭavyaḥ, kiṃ bhavān ghaṭapaṭādīnāṃ padārthānāṃ sarveṣāmekatvāt (svato bhedābhāvāto) bhedo neti brūte, atha-na dāne'haṃ vastūnāṃ bhedam, ataḥ sa neti |
ādye-- `vaicitrayeṇa padārthānām', iti san sūtre, `padārthānāṃ vicitraśaktiyogāt' iti sarūpasūtre ca, kaiyaṭāt, `pādartānāṃ vicitraśaktitvāt', ittanyeṣāmabhiyuktānāṃ vyavahārāt, svābhāvikametaditi `hayavaraṭ' iti sītrīyamahābhāṣyācya pratipadārtaṃ tattatpadārthamātraviśrāntasvasvātiriktasarvavyāvartakaśaktisvabhāvādipadavācyadharmaviseṣo vidyata iti pratīteḥ pratipadārthaṃ bhedaḥ svata eva vartata iti siddhyā sarveṣāmekatvabudderbhramatvāt | na dvitīyaḥ - satyatvena sarvapramāṇasiddhavastumātrasyānirvacanīyat sādhanāya pravṛttasya bhavato, bhavataḥ pramāṇabhūtasya ca pratītyapratītī, vastusattātadabhāvaniyāmake na bavata iti bhavadādyapratīteraprayojakatvāt | bhavān sa ca naipathyāntaramāsthāya pracchāditaprakṛtiḥ sarvāsattvasādhanāya svairaṃ prāvartata iti bhavadīyagranthapariśīlināṃ spaṣṭaṃ viditaṃ bhavati | tava tvatsakhasya ceyān viśeṣaḥ --sphoyākhyaṃ brajhaikaṃ vastu vāstavam, anye sarve'nirvacanīyāḥ iti saṃgarite iti | yadi bhavadīyaṃ matamāśrayante lokāḥ, tarhi ayaṃ brājhaṇo'yamabrājhaṇaḥ, iyaṃ mātā, iyaṃ dayitā, eyaṃ prajāvatī, iyaṃ jāyā, idaṃ payaḥ, iyaṃ surā, iyaṃ patitaḥ, idaṃ muvarṇaṃ idaṃ na surṇam, iyaṃ gamyā, iyaṃ na gamyā, idaṃ bhojyaṃ, idaṃ na bhojyam' ityādi vividhalokavyavahārasiddhabhedavadvastuvuvekaśūnyāḥ ---
vihitasyānanuṣṭhānānninditasya ca sevanāt |
angrahāccendriyāṇāṃ naraḥ patanamṛcchati || (yā.smṛ.3.219)
ityādyār,vacaneṣu, `satyaṃ vada, dharmaṃ cara, na surāṃ pibet' ityādi śrautavacaneṣu ca prāmāṇyabuddihīnāḥ kartavāyākartavyavivekaśūnyahṛdayābrajhahananādyakāryaṃ kurvāṇā mahāntamanarthamāpnuyuḥ | tataśca-śrotasmārtavidhiniṣedhaparāṇāṃ śāstrāṇām, tadanusāritathāvidhavividhaprāyaścittavidhīnāṃ ca viṣayābhāvaprasaṃgena śaśaviṣāṇamastīti vākyasyeva avidyamānārthabodhakatvarūpamaprāmāṇyaṃ syāt | (aprāmāṇyabudhdyā teṣāmanuṣṭhānābhāvena, ananuṣṭhānarūpāprāmāṇyaṃ ca syāt |) nanu sarvānirva canīya.tāvādināmasmākaṃ sarvamidamiṣṭameveti cet, na | evaṃvadatāṃ mate sphoṭabrajhātiriktapadārthasāmānyasya mithyātvena śrutismṛtītihāsādyagamasyāpyanirvacanīyatvāt (māyikatvāt, asatyatvāt) | sphoṭabrajhaṇoḥ sādakapramāṇābhāvena sphoṭasvarūpasya, brajhaṇaśca (īśvarasya ca) asiddhyāpatteḥ | tataśca sphoṭākhyaṃ śabdabrajhaiva jagadātmanā vivartate, svataḥsiddhe cidātmani brajhatattve kevale bhāramavalaṃbya' (ma.23.21) ityādisvasvasakhokterasaṃgatīḥ | na ca `svataḥsiddha' ityuktatvena pramāṇasāpekṣatā tatsiddhau vāstīti vācyam | kā nāma tatra svataḥsiddhateti anuyoge sarvānirvacanīyatāvādinā sā nirvaktumaśakyeti | tasyāḥ tattvāsiddhiḥ yadi nirucyace tadā sarvānirvacanīyatāsiddāntabhaṅgena apasiddhāntaḥ, svavyāhatiḥ, brajhasvarūpasyaivāsiddyā śūnyavādimatādavailakṣaṇyaṃ ca | na ca jagati āropitaṃ sattvaṃ brajhacāṅgīkurmo vayam, te tu taddvayaṃ nāṅgīkurvantīti asti mahadvailakṣaṇyamiti vācyam | sattvasyārope tannāstītyevoktaṃ bhavati pramāṇasāmānyasya tava mate'sattvena brajhasattābodhakapramāṇābhāvena brajhāpi nāstītyevoktaṃ bhavatīti vaisakṣaṇyasya durupapādakatvāt | pramāṇapramitaṃ sattvaṃ pratiṣidhya āropitaṃ sattvaṃ vadan viseṣataḥ ślāghanīyaḥ | agatikā hi gatīrāropaḥ | nanu mithyābūtenāpi śrutyādipramāmena īśvarasattvaṃ bodhyata ityabhyupagaccāma iti cet, acyate---śaśaviṣāṇāderasato'sattvamarthakriyākāritvābhāveneti hi vadanti | brajhasattvasyāryarūpatvena tatkāriṇaḥ śrutyāderasattvāṅgīkāre asattvalakṣaṇasya vedādāvasattvenā'vyāptiprasaṅgaḥ, bhramaprakāratvaṃ mithyātvamiti vadato'pi tasyāsattvaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyam, asattvasyoktarītyaiva vaktavyatayā uktadoṣasyaiva prasaṅgāt | kiṃ ca mithyābūtenāpi pramāṇena brajhasatyatā bodhyata ityaṅgīkāre aviśeṣāt sarveṣāmapi padārthānāṃ tena satyatā vaktuṃ śakyā | kiṃ ca sarvamithyātvabodhakapramāṇasya mithyātvasādhakapramāṇābhāvāt sphoṭādyatiriktasya `neha nānā' iti vākyasya sattvena sphoṭāderadvitīyatvāsiddhiḥ . na ca `nahe nānā' iti śrutiḥ anyeṣāṃ mithyātvaṃ bodhayantī svasyāpi tattvaṃ bodhayatīti vācyam | `pūrvatrā'siddham' (pā.sū. 8.2.1) iti sūtre `pūrvatrā'sidhdam' iti śāstraṃ svasyā'sddatvaṃ pratipādayet yadi, tarhi sarveṣāṃ siddhatvātsūtrāsambha evā'narthakaḥ syāt' iti kaiyaṭoktarītyā sarvamityātvabodhakasya svasya mithyātvabodhane sarveṣāmitareṣāṃ mithyātvāsiddheḥ `neha nānā' iti vākyasya vaiyarthyāpātāt | nanu asti vyāvahāriko bhedaḥ sarvatreti cet, atra vaktavyam - kiṃ vyāvahārikatvamāropitatvam, uta puruṣavyāpāridhīnaṃ dharmāntaram? nobhayā - `svābhāvasiddhametat' iti pūrvodāhṛtamahābhāṣyādinā bedasya svābhāvikasyaiva sarvatra astitāsiddeḥ ārepitatvapuruṣavyāpārādhīnatvayostatrābhāva āt | nanu tena bhāṣṣeṇa kathaṃ bhedasiddhiriti cet, ucyate tatra bhāṣye---`samānamīhamānānāṃ cādhīyānānāṃ ca kecidarthairyujyante, apare na......tatra kimasmāmiḥ kartu śakyam...svābhāvikamecac iti uktam | phalānukūle prayatne'dhyayane ca samāne'pi labhate kaś‌catphalam, kaścinna labhate iti loke sarveṣāmanubhavasiddham tatra prāptāvaprāptau ca sādhakaṃ pratibandhakaṃ vā na kaścidīṣṭe kartum | kiṃ tu kāraṇamiti tadbhāṣyārthaḥ | tadeva vailakṣaṇyaṃ vyaktyantarāt vyāvartakaṃ bhedaśabdena vyavahriyata iti padārtānāṃ paralparaṃ bhedasiddiriti | na ca---bhedena lokavyavahārāttsiddhiriti vyāvahārikatvaṃ tasyeti vācyam | vicitravyavahāramūlabhūtabhede, asya vyavahārasya hetutve, vyavahārasattve bhedasiddhiḥ, bhedasattve vyavahārasiddhirityanyonyāśrayāt | vyavahārāntaraṃ pūrvabhede kāraṇaṃ cet, bhedavyavahāraparaṃparākalpanāpatyā'navasthā . tataśca -- bhedaḥ padārthasvabhāva eveti siddham | idaṃ sarvamabhipretyaivoktamabhiyuktaiḥ --`bhinnāśca bhinnadharmāsca padārtānikhilā api' (anu.2.2.670) iti | nikhilā api padārthāḥ, bhinnadharmāḥ --svata eva parasparavyāvartakalvarūpabhūtadharmavantaḥ, ata eva bhinnāḥ -parasparapratiyogikabhedavanta iti advaitaparibhāṣāvyākhyātāro'pi | evaṃ ca na ātmāśrayādidoṣāvakāśaḥ kiṃ ca bhede vyāvahārikatvakalpanāya tasyābhyupagantavyatayā tannirāsaprayāso viphalaḥ | kiṃ ca bhedo nāma kascitpadārthoo na bhavet tarhi sarveṣā tīrthakaraṇāṃ svapakṣaparapakṣasādhanadūṣaṇayoḥ pravṛttirva syāt; na syācca bodhanīyapuruṣāṇāṃ svaparapakṣayornirdoṣatvasadoṣatvahetukaprābalyadaurbalyajñānādhīnagrājhatvāgrājhatvapratyayaḥ, na bhavetāṃ ca tatra pravṛttyapravṛttī, nirdoṣatvāderbhedanibandhanatvāt |
kiṃ ca bhedarūpapadārthānaṅgīkāre svaparapakṣasādhananirasanayorāgrahaprayāsau viphalau | ayaṃ tu bhedo neti bahubhirācāryairbahudā nirākṛto vistareṇa sāstreṣu |
kiṃ ca bagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ ānandamayādyadhikaraṇeṣu ānandamayatvādikaṃ jijñāsyasya brajhaṇo'stīti siddāntayan jīvabrajhaṇoorabedāt jīvasyāpi asti ānandamayatvādikamiti codyaṃ `bhedavyapadeśāt'(1.3.5) bhedavyapadesāccānyaḥ (1.1.21) śarīraścobhaye'pi hi bhedenainamadhiyate (1.2.20) viśeṣaṇabhedavyapadeśābhyāṃ ca netarau (1.2.22) bhedavyapadesāt (1.3.5) suṣuptyutkrāntyorbhedena (1.342) ityādibhirubhayorvibhinnadharmavattvena bhedāddhātoḥ parākurvan bhedanāma kaścitpadārtho'stīti abhipraiti |
kiṃ ca `tulyāsyaprayatnaṃ sarvarṇam (pā.sū.1.1.9) stānivadādesaḥ (pā.sū.1.1.56) pañcamī vibhakte' (pā.sū.2.3.42) iti, ānyabhāvyaṃ tu deśakālavyavāyāt, yugupaddeśapṛthaktvāt | ākṛtigkahaṇāsiddham, rūpasāmānyādvā' (pra.2.ā.) iti, tatra tatra `svābhāvikametat' iti, abhidadhānāḥ pāṇinikātyāyanapatañjalayaḥ bhedasvarūpamaṅgīkurvantīti pratīyate | ato bhedasvarūpanirākaraṇaṃ munitrayavacanaviruddham | tatra sādṛsyamatideśaśceti dvayaṃ bheda nibandhanamiti suvyaktam | vibhakta ityasya vibhāgo bheda iti vyākhyānam | ānyabhāvyam -- bhedaḥ vyaktīnāmānantyena, bedena ca, sarvāsu vyaktiṣu śaktigrahāsaṃbhavaparihākāya jātipakṣaḥ sādṛsyaṃ ceti dvayamāśritam | ākṛtiḥ jātiḥ, rūpasāmānyaṃ sādṛśyam | vistarastubhāṣyādau bodhyaḥ | kiṃ ca bhedasvarūpameva nāstīti bravāṇaḥ tatra māyikatvādikaṃ kalpayituṃ kathamīṣṭe ? tasya bhedarūpatvāt | māyiko'yamiti tu dhruvāgītiḥ |
nanu `nañivayuktamanyasadṛśādhikaraṇe jharthagatiḥ' iti nyāye anyaśabdaprayogo'bhede|pi sādṛśyaṃ gamayati | anyathā beda eva sādṛśyasaṃbhavena `sadṛśādhikaraṇa' ityuktyaiva bhedalābhāt nyāye'nyagrahaṇaṃ vyarthaṃ syāt | kiṃ ca `akartari ca' iti sūtrabhāṣyeṇā'bhedepi sādṛśyamiti rūcitam' iti nañarthavādamañajūṣokteḥ sādṛśyoktirna bhedasya jñāpiketi cet, na `abhede'pi sādṛsyam' ityuktyā sādṛśyaṃ sarvatra bhedamūlakameva | yatra kvāpi vyaktyabhede sādṛśyaṃ prāmāṇikairucyate tatra kathaṃcittaduktiḥ saṃghayanīyeti pratīyate | saṃghayanā ca ekasyaiva padārthasyā''vṛttyā anekatvaṃ saṃpādya sādhāraṇadharmasyānekavṛttitvena sādṛśyaṃ yujyata iti | tataśca sādṛśyaṃ bedanibandhanamiti sādṛśyasyaṃ bhedajñāpakatvākṣateḥ | na caivamanyagkahaṇavaiyarthyamiti vācyam | abhede'pi sādṛśyaṃ yujyeteti kasyacidvacanaṃ śrutvā bhrāmyeyuḥ janā iti tadbhramavāraṇāya bhede satyeva sādṛsyaṃ saṃpannaṃ bhavatīti bodhanāya tasyāvaśakatvāt | yadyabhede'pi sādṛsyaṃ sughaṭaṃ, tarhi ivārthanirūpaṇe `anekavṛttidharmasyaiva sādhāraṇadharmatvena tatrā''vṛttyā ekasyāpyanekatvāt' iti ma़jūṣāgranthapravṛttirviphalaiva | `akartari ca kāraka' ityādi tu nana yuktaṃ, tatratyabhāṣyeṇa tatā'sūcanāt, tatra bhāṣyo'sya vicārasyaivākaraṇāt |
etena sarvāsattvasya (māyikatvasya) durvacatvanirūpaṇena-- "anena sarvasya janakatvāderavabhāsa eva na vastu kiṃcidastīti spaṣṭamevoktam, anādivāsanāvaśāt, bhramaviṣayameva tat, `yato vā' ityādi śrutayo'pi anuvadanti..... `satyaṃ jñānam, ānandaṃ brajha' iti śrutayo'pi śuddhacitaṃ prati `nahe nānāsti' ityādi śrutisahakāreṇa dharmatyāgena advaitaṃ śuddaṃ brajha bodhayanti" (1.pa.30.31) iti grantena, prathamaparicchede jagadanirvacanīyatāṃ prasādhya, mañjūṣākṛtkṛtaḥ prapañcāsatvaniṣkarṣa-, udāhṛtamañjūṣāgranthamupanyasya nañarthavāde---pāyuguṇḍīvaidyanāthakṛtaprapañcāsatyatvaniṣkarṣaśca durāgrahamūlakaḥ svāvālanāmātrakalpitaḥ | padavācyatvasya avicāritaraṇīyatāprayojakatve upadiṣṭārthamātrasāyāsattvāpatyā sarvairapi vāceyamaireva bhavitivyaṃ, vyavahārasyāprayojakatvāditi sarvavyavahārocchedaprasaṅgaḥ | `ghaṭatvāśrayatvena' itiyādi dūṣaṇamapi hetumātreṇa padārthān sādhayato hetukān pratyeva vaktavyaṃ, na tu śrutyādipramāṇasiddārthānāśritya vyavaharataḥ pramāṇaśaraṇan prati, etaiḥ (śabdapramāṇakaiḥ) kevalena hetunā padārthasyāsādhanāt, ghaṭādiriti jñānavyavahārāvityadoṣācca | yadatra bhedasyāsattve kaiyaṭo'pyanukūla iti `bhedasyāsatsāmānyaśabdena' ityādi tadīyavyavahārapanyasanaṃ, taccintyam, tasya kaiyaṭasya etadabhimatārthasamānārthakatvābhāvāt | tathā hi -- `yadā'satsāmānyavṛttirnañ brājhaṇadibhirviseṣyate brājhaṇatvenā'san anyathadhāsan arthaḥ kṣatriyādiḥ' iti tadīyo grantaḥ | tatra asatpadārthasya `brājhaṇatvenāsan' iti tenaiva vivṛtatvāt bhedasyāsatvaṃ na svarūpataḥ, kiṃ tu svapratiyogitāvacchedakabrājhaṇatvādeḥ svānuyogini kṣatriyādau avidyamānatāsamarpatveneti tadāśayo'vagamyate | atra svaṃ bhedaḥ | kiṃ ca bhedasya svarūpato'sattve kevalānvayitvānupapattiḥ, asyātyantābhāvā.़pratiyogitvarūpatvena asattvasya ca tatpratiyogisvarūpatvena parasparavirodāt | tataśca asya kathamanukūlaḥ kaiyaṭa iti vibhāvayantu sūraya iti | śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇānāṃ sarve,āmapi pramāṇānāṃ saguṇabrajhabodhakatvena, kasminnapi brajhadarme kasyāpi bhramānudayot, brajhagatajagajjanakatve dharme bhramābhāvena `yato vā' ityādīnāṃ taṭasthalakṣaṇabodhakatvabhramaviṣayārthānuvādakatvābhāvāt, `neha nānā' iti śruterbrajha tadguṇayokapṛthaksthitirūpabhedābhāvapratipādakatvena svagatabhedarāhityārtakatvena ca brajhātiriktapadarthāsattvasya, brajhaṇī dharmābhāvasya cā'bodhakatvāt, `yato vā' ityādīnāṃ śrutīnāṃ mañjūṣākṛdabhimatārthaparatayā sūtrakṛdbhyāṃ śrīvyāsapāṇinibhyāmavyākhyātatvācya sarvasyāsya nirmūlatvāt, ato darśitā 8utiyojanā yiktā | evaṃ ca ghaṭapaṭādipadārthānāṃ, bhedasya ca pramāṇasiddhatvena māyikatve mānābhāvāt `avicāritaramaṇīyena māyikena bhedena vyavahāraḥ, avicāritaramaṇīyasyaiva padārtamātrasya vyavahāraprayojakatvam' ityāktireva āpātaramaṇīyā pṛthakjanamohiketi kṛtaṃ vistareṇa |
padārtānāṃ māyikatve pramāṇaṃ nāsti kiṃcana |
bhede padārte mānaṃ tu sūtraṃ bhāṣyaṃ ca darśitam ||
tasmāgbhedapadārtaśca sūtrakārādisaṃmataḥ |
tasmādbhedaparattvena śrutīnāṃ yojanā varā ||
asattve nyāyasūtrasaṃmatinirāsaḥ
yacca mañjūṣayāṃ tṛtīyaparicchede---`gautamo'pi svapnaviṣayā bhmānavadayaṃ pramāṇaprameyābhimānaḥ' (nyā.sū.4.2.21) `māyā gandharvanāgaramṛgatṛṣṇāvadvā' (vyā.sū.4.2.32) `hetvabhāvādasiddhiḥ' (nyā.sū.4.2.33) ityāhetyādinā, `anyatra vistasa' ityantena granthena prapañcā'satyatve gautamasaṃmatipradarśanam | atrāhuḥ --`pratyavatiṣṭhate vijñānavādī' iti vācaspatinā.़vataraṇāt ādyasūtradvayaṃ pūrvapakṣaprāpakam, tṛtadīyaṃ tu siddāntasūtram | yathā svānpo'bhimānaḥ (jñānaṃ) nirviṣayaḥ, tatā jāgkati pramāṇaprameyābhimāno.़pi nirviṣayaḥ | svānpādivastuvat jāgkajjaśāyāmapi ghaṭapaṭādivastu māyikameveti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ prāpte siddhāntamāhahetviti | prabodādāvanupalambharūpahetusattvena svānpādīnāmasattve'pi jāgrati pramāṇādīnāmupalambhena anupalambharūpahetvabhāvāt, asiddhiḥ- asattvāsijjhiḥ, asatvasatvayoranupalambhopalambhau hetū, tatra bodhābādhāviti bhāvaḥ | svānpāderuktamasattvamabhyipetya siddhāntasūtrārtho varṇitaḥ, vastitastu anupalambhahetvabhāvāt asiddhiḥ - jāgrati pratīyamānavastunaḥ asatvā'siddhiḥ, svānpasyāpi upalambhāt ṇanupalambharūpahetvabhāvāt, asiddhiḥ-asattvā'siddhiḥ, svānpasyaivā'sattvābhāvena, hetvabhāvat -- dṛṣṭāntābhāvāt, asiddhiḥ -jāgkajvastusattvasādhakānumānāsiddhiḥ, evaṃ ca svapne, bhrame, jāgrati ca vastūnāmasattvaṃ neti gautamamatimiti nyāyabhāṣyādito lābhāt nānirvacanīyatāvādo gotamasaṃmata ityavaseyam | evamakṣarārthe sati `jāgradavasthāyāṃ samīpa evopasabhyamānānāṃ yāvadvyavahramabādhyamānadātīyānāṃ samipa evopalabhyamānānāmaṃ tattve (māyikatve) na heturiti bhāṣyavārtikādau siddāntatvena vyākhyātam | evaṃ ca--anirvacanīyatāvādasya sūtrasaṃmatatvamuktaprāyam' iti anirvacanīyatve gautamasya tātparyakalpanamayuktam | tadbhāṣyādau `yāvadvyavāhāramabādhyamānajātīyānāma' iti vākyasya, evamarthakavākyāntarasya vā'darśanāt, tatrā'dṛṣyavākyādhyāhāreṇa sūtraṃ vyākhyāya `bhā,yādau vyākhyātam' ityābhidhānaṃ bodhdhṛṣu pradveṣaṃ sūcayati; viruddhārthapratipattyā vimujhantu ime iti | vātrike ca--`sarva eva mithyā pratyāya bhaviṣyantīti bruvāṇāḥ pradhānamanuyoktavyaḥ, na cā'niṣpradhānaṃ paśyāma' ityuktam | anena - yasya kasyacitpratyayasya sadviṣayakatvena samīcīnapratyayatvamabhyupem, enyathā abhimatapratyayasya mithyātvāsiddhirityuktaṃ bhavati | kiṃ ca `smṛtīsaṃkalpavacca svanpaviṣayābhimānaḥ' (34) `mithyopalabdhervināśāstattvajñānāt svapnaviṣayābhimānapraṇāśavatpratibodhe' (35) ityattarasūtrābhyāṃ smṛtisaṃralpasvapnabhrameṣu viṣayāṇāṃ satyatvamevoktaṃ gautamena tatra mithyopalabdhisvapnābhimānayornāṣa evoktaḥ, na tu viṣayasyāsattvam | sūtrarthastu bhāṣyādito jñātavyaḥ | ata eva kusumāñjalau pañcame stabake granthopasaṃhāke svāpnajñānasya smṛtitvaṃ nirasya `tasmādanubhavo'sau svīkartavyaḥ, asti ca svapnānubhavasyāpi kasyacitsatyatvaṃ saṃbādāt' ityādinā uktaṃ svāpnasya satyatvam |
yadapyuktam ---`tasya ca śrutmūsatvena hetvabhāvādasiddhiḥ' ityanena `śaṇḍanāsaṃbhavāccha' iti śrutimūlatvamanirvacanīyatvasya, tadapi cintyam, prapañcānirvacanīyaraśrutepabhāvena tasya tatsiddhatvābhāvāt | bhagavatā maharṣiṇā vyāsena dvitīye samayapāde bājhārthānāmabhāvavādimatasya `nābhāva upalabhdheḥ' (2.2.27) ityādipañcasūtryā pratyākhyātatvena as‌ya vādasya śrautatvābhāvāvagamācca | yadyayaṃ vādaḥ śrautaḥ syāt tarhi śrutyarthanirṇayāya pravṛtto sa maharṣiḥ śrautaṃ tamarthaṃ kathaṃ pratyācakṣīta? tatrāpi samayapāde nirāsāt vedapramāṇyamanabhyupayataḥ kasyacinmatamudamiti spaṣṭam | `neha nānā' (4-11) iti kāṭhakavākyasya, vājasaneyavākyasya ca jagadanirvacanīyatāyāṃ na tātparyaṃ, sūtrakārābhyāṃ tathā'vyākhyātatvādityuktam | kiṃ ca akṣaramaryādayāpi na tādṛśārthalābhaḥ | tathā hi---
nānāśabdasya bhinnamityarte ihetyasya paramātmanītyarthe ca, ihabrajhaṇi, kiṃcana bhinnaṃ vastu nāstīti vākyārtho vācyaḥ, evaṃ ca sati pāऱthakyena jagataḥ sattā neti labhyeta na tu prapañco neti | tataśca brajhāśritameva jagatiti bhāvaḥ | etaccoktaṃ bhagavatā---`mayi sarvamidaṃ protaṃ sūtre maṇigaṇa iva' (gī.7.7) iti | etena --pratipannopādau niṣedhāt jagato'bhāva eva siddha iti parāstam | niṣedhenoktārthālābhāt | aseya niyamasya anyamatasiddatvena śābdikapūrvācāryaiḥ pāṇinipramukhairīdṛśārtānaṅgīkārāt `bhramaprakāratvaṃ mithyātvam', ityeva nāgeśokteḥ niṣedhena jagataḥ bhramaprakāratā'lābhāt | nehetyādiśrutīnāṃ pāṇinipatañjalibhyāmavyākyātatvena nāgeśābhimata evārthaḥ śruteriti rājājñāderabhāvena śrutikanyathāpi yojayituṃ śakyā | tathā hi---śrutau nānāśabdo bhāvapradhāno bhedaparaḥ, `loke ca nānālabdasya pṛthagbhāvābhidhāyitvāt' iti `vinañbhyāṃ nānāñauau na saha' (pā.sū.5.2.27) iti sūtre kaiyaṭookteḥ bhedaparatve na vipratipattiḥ | `manasaivedamāptavyam' iti kāṭhake pūrvavākyam | idaṃ bodhyam---`yataścodeti' iti navamena mantreṇa prāṇapadavācyasya paramātmanaḥ sarvadevādhāratā'bhihitā, `yadeveha' iti daśamena mantreṇa sarvadevādārasyaiva hareḥ atrāmutra ca loke sarvādhāratvaṃ sarvaniyāmakatvaṃ ca bodhayatā, lokadvayagatabhagavadrūpamabhinnameva tatra bhedajñānino mahadaniṣṭamiti bodhitam | ayamekādaśo mantraḥ abhinnaṃ brajhasvarūpaṃ jñātavyaṃ tatla brajhaṇi aṇumātraṃ bhedo neti pūrvamantrasūcitameva kaṇṭhato vadan bhedajñānina uktamevā'niṣṭaṃ phalaṃ draḍhayati | pūrvamantroktamabhinna brajhasvarūpamidamāparāmṛśyate | idam - abhinnaṃ brajhasvarūpaṃ, manasāśuddena cetasā, āptavyam- cintanīyam, iha - atrāmutra ca vidyamāne brajhaṇī, kiṃcana nānā-bhedaleśo'pi neti yojanā | bhagavatsvarūpāṇāṃ bhedo neti tātparyam | bhedajñāninaḥ phalamāha---mṛtyoiti | yaḥ adhikārī, iha - ubhayatra vartamāne paramātmasvarūpe, nāneva paśyati-bhedo'stīva paśyati, saḥ, mṛtyormṛtyum - mṛtyorapi mṛtyupradāṃ kaṣyatarāṃ daśāṃ prānpatītyarthaḥ? | vājasaneye tu manasaivānudraṣṭavyaṃ neha (6.4.19) ityādi paṭhyate | tatra---`te nicikyurbrajhapūrāṇamagyram' iti pūrvavākyāt brajhetyanuvṛttaṃ, prathamāntatayā vipariṇamayitavyam | `yasmin pañcapadhāratayoktaṃ brajha, manasaiva - gurūpadeśapūrvaṃ śāstramabhyasya brajhasvarūpe na bhedaleśa iti jñānarūpasaṃskārasaṃskṛtena cetasā, anudraṣṭavyaṃcintanīyam, iha-draṣṭavye brajhaṇi | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | evaṃ ca - `neha nānā' iti vākyaṃ brajharūpayorbhedābhāvajñāpakaṃ, na tu brajhātiriktavastvabhāvabodhanadvārā ḍagato'sattvabodhakamiti mantavyam |
yadvā---`vinañbhāyāṃ nānāñau nasaha' iti sūtreṇa nasahārthe vinānānāśabdayorvyutpādanena vināparyāyo nānāśabdaḥ | nasahārthaśca sāhityabhāvaḥ, `nānātvaṃ, sahatvaṃ yaugapadyamiītyādau nānāśabdo'sahabhūte sahaśabdaṃ sahabhūte, vṛttau vartate, ato'sahabhānādau pratyaya' iti `tasya bhāva' iti sūtrodyotokteḥ | iheti na brajhaparaṃ kiṃ tu prapañcaparam | `manasaivedamāptavyam' iti pūrvavākyādidamityanuṣajyate | tacceha nānāśabdayogāt `pṛthagvinānānābhistṛtīyānyatarasyām' (pā.sū.2.3.32) iti sūtreṇa dvitīyāntam | iha-prapañce, kiṃcana vastu, idaṃ nānā-paramātmanā vinābhūtaṃ na, kiṃ tu brajhā'vinābhūtameveti kāṭhakavākyārthaḥ | vājasaneye tu--`te nicikyurbrajhapurāṇamagyram' (13) iti vākyāt brajhapadamanuṣajyate tacca nānāśabdayogāt dvitīyāntam | yotanā kāṭhakavākyavat | atra yoojanāyāmavinābhāvaḥ śābdaḥ, pūrvayojanāyāṃ tvārthaḥ sarvapadārthānāṃ bhagavadavinābhūtatayā vartanaṃ `na tadasti vinā yatsyānmayā bhūtaṃ carācaram' (bha.gī.10.39) iti bhagavadvacanasiddham | mṛtyorityuttaravākye'pi ihapadaṃ prapañcaparameva | iha-prapañce, nāneva bhagavadvinābhāvo'stīva yaḥ pasyatītyādirarthaḥ | prapañce bhagavadavinābhāvābhāvaṃ paśyato mahadaniṣṭamāpatatīti bhāvaḥ | ata eva śrutau ihapadaṃ, nānāpadaṃ ca sārthakam | anyathā `nedamasti kiṃcana, atha vā--nāsti kiṃcana' ityetāvataiva brajhaṇo'nyannāstītyarthalābhāt kiṃ tābhyām |
atra nagośapakṣapātī kaśicidāha--nānāśabdasya `vinañbhyām' iti sūtreṇa naño nā़pratyaye niṣpannatayā tatsūtrabhā,ye tasya pratyayasya svārthi-katvavyavasthāpavāt, bhinnasaṃbando'rthaḥ, tatra saṃbandhasya samabhivyāhṛtapadabalāllābhena bhedāśa eva cacchakya iti na vināśabdaparyāyatvaṃ tasya, ato `neha nānā' ityasya vinābhūtaṃ netyarthavarṇanaṃ na yuktamiti | nāgeśānuyāyibhyo'nye vaiyākaraṇāstu `vinañ‌bhyāmasahavācibhyāṃ nānā़au bhavataḥ svārthe' iti tatsūtrabhāṣyāt vinānānāśabdayorasahārthe paryāyatvaṃ sūtrabhāṣyakṛtsaṃmatamiti `neha nānā' iti śrutervinābhūtaṃ neti vyākhyānaṃ yuktaṃ pāṇinipatañjalisaṃmataṃ ca iti kathatanti | `nānāviṣṇurmokṣado nāsti deva' ityādau bhinnārthe'vyutpanno'nekārtha iveti śakyaṃ vaktum | `bhedāṃśa eva śakyo bhāṣye svārthikatvokteḥ' iti vadatā bhedārthakār naño nāñpratyaya ityuktaṃ bhavati | taccintyam | sūtre `na' ityeva vaktavyaṃ na saheti śabdaprayogeṇa, bhāṣye bhedārthāditi avivṛtya `asahavācibhyām' iti tadarthavivaraṇena ca, naño'nekārthakatvaṃ tatra asahavṛtternaño nāñapratyaya ityarthasya lābhena tadiktārtālābāt | anena bhāṣyeṇa sūtropāttārthavṛttibhya iti niyamalābhena sūtrānupāttabhedārthavṛtternañaḥ pratyayotpattau tadbhāṣyasyāsādhakatvāt | svārthikaprakaraṇe kvacit `anukampāyām' ityadisūtraviṣaye sūtropātto'nukampādirūpo'rtho viseṣaṇatayā prakṛtibodhyo bhavati, kvacittu--svātantreṇa (viśeṣyatvena) prakṛtibodhyaḥ yathā--- prakṛtasūtraviṣaye |
yacca--tatraiva `agra' ityādinā apavarge'pi naiyāyikamate cittātiriktasadvastvabhāvanirūpaṇam, tadapi mandam| nyāyamate jīvānāṃ nānātvena muktau muktajīvāntaraṇāṃ, brajhaṇaśca satvena teṣāṃ ca satyatvena, tadā tadabhāvanirūpaṇāyogāt | `vāstavau bhedasāmānyau (5.ā.) bhinānāstāvadātmānaḥ, ātmajñānamapavargasājhanam, muktau svarūpeṇa muktānāmavasthitiḥ' iti nyāyamañjaryāṃ (9.ā) cayantabhaṭṭokteḥ | `tadabhāvaścāpakarge' (gau.nyā.4.2.45) iti sūtreṇa muktānāṃ śarīrendriyāṇāmabhāvāt bājhārthānāmindriyasaṃyogābhāvena buddhyutpattyabhāva ityarthakena na muktau muktānāṃ jñānābhāvamātrasiddhyā cittātiriktapadārthābhāvāsiddheḥ | bhāṣyādāvidaṃ sūtramitthameva vyākhyātam | sūtrākṣarārthastu na prasaṃga iti vartate, yata iti śeṣaḥ, prakṛtatvāt muktānāmiti labhyate | apavarge-mokṣe muktā nām, yataḥ tadabhāvaḥ -jñānakāraṇaśarīrendriyābhāvaḥ, tasmt, na prasaṃgaḥ -baddyutpādaprasaṃgo neti | kiṃ ca bhāṣyavārtikatātparyaṭākāprabhṛtiprauḍhaprācīnanyāyaprabandhān samāloḍya paṇḍitaprakāṇḍabhaṭṭajayantapraṇītanyāyamañjaryāṃ nyāyasūtratātparyavivatau pañcamasaptamāṣṭamanavameṣvāhnikeṣu vijñānavādādinākuvādānnirasya vijñānādyatiriktabājhasatpadārthasya sapramāṇaṃ sādhitatavena `anirvacanīyatāvādo na gautamasaṃmataḥ' | kiṃ ca---`so'śnute sarvān' (tai.u.2) ityādi śrutbhiḥ, `saṃpadya' ityādi brajhamimāṃsāturyādhyāyaturīyapādena ca viruddhamidam | tataśca jāgradādau vastūnāmasattvamiti gautamīyagranthato'nyagkanthato'pi nirṇetuṃ na śakyam |
nanu `yasmātparaṃ nāparamasti kiṃcidyasmānnāṇīyo na jyāya'sti kaścit | vṛkṣa iva stabdho diki tiṣṭhatyeko yenedaṃ pūrṇaṃpuruṣeṇa sarvam' (tai.u.4.28, śve.5) iti mantreṇa pūrṇapuruṣātparāparavastumātrapratiṣedhena brajhaikameva vastu sat, tasmādanyatsarvamasaditi sidhyatīti cet. avadhīyatāṃ mantrārtham | ayamartho mantrasya---yasmāt - pūrṇātpuruṣāt, paraṃ - uttamaṃ na, aparaṃ kiṃcit - avaraṃ vasti, asti-vartate, vartamānavaraṃ vastu kiṃparimāṇenā'ṇīyaḥ, uta jyāya iti śaṅkāyāmha - yasmānnāṇīya ityādi . yasmāt aṇiyo na, jyāyaśca kaścinna `aṇoraṇīyānmahato mahīyān' (tai.u.4.26.1,śve.3.20) ityuktaṃḥ | kaściditi liṅkavyatyayaśchāndasaḥ | evaṃ ca pūrvārdhe vākyacatuṣṭayam | na ca dvitīye'pi vākye naño|cha़nuṣaṅgeṇa paramaparaṃ ca vastu netyeva mantratātparyamiti vācyam, `vibhāṣā madhye ca ye vidhayaste nityā bhavanti' iti `paṅaḥ katvā ca' (pā.sū.1.2.10) iti sūtramahābhāṣyoktanyāyena nañdayamadhyapaṭhite dvitīyavākye nañanaṣaṅgo neti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | anuvṛttau tu saṃbandhasyāvicchinnatayā uttaratrāpi etadanuvṛttyaiva niṣedhalābhena nañdvayopādānaṃ vyartaṃ syāt | etna vākyena brajhātiriktavastumātrābhāvaṃ bodhite vastano'bhāvena tatra aṇīyastvādiśaṅkāyā anudayena `yasmānnāṇīya' ityādi niṣedhavākyānuttatiprasaṃgaḥ, purūṣātiriktavastvantarasattābodhaka `aṇoraṇīyān' iti mantravirodaprasaṃgaśca | etena `yasmātparamaparaṃ ca na' ityekameva vākyamiti parāstam . uttānupapatterduṣpariharatvāt . nanu pūrvamantreṣu rudraśrateḥ śivaparo'yaṃ mantra iti cet, āha-nātra tathā mantavyam | `rujaṃ drāvayate yasmādrudrastasmāñjanārdana' iti nirūktyā rudrapadaṃ śivaparamiti vācyam | `hiraṇyagarbhaṃ paryata jāyamānam' atyuttarārdhe etanmantrapratipādhyasya viriñcaputrakatvaliṅgaśravaṇāt tasya rudre'saṃbhavena, vāsudeva eva saṃbhavena ca, niravakāśaviṣṇuliṅgatvāt tadbalena `yogādrūḍheḥ' iti nyāyānavatārāt, śruteśca vāsudeve sāvakāśatvāt | divi-svastāne vaikuṇṭhe, ekaḥ-samābhyadhikaśūnyaḥ, stabdhoo vṛkṣa iva, tiṣṭhati-āste | evaṃ ca-kulālādiriva na jagatsṛṣṭyādau vyāpāravāt, kiṃ tu icchāmātreṇa sṛjatīti bhāvaḥ | yeneti kvacit pāṭhaḥ yena puruṣeṇedaṃ pūrṇaṃ sat tiṣṭhatīti yojanā | idaṃ bodhyam -- prathamapādena parāparavastumātrābhāvabodhane `yenedam' ityādivākyamanarthakaṃ, vyāpyavastuno'bhāvena vyāptyādikathanāyogāditi . etena `akhaṇḍārtena śuddhena brajhaṇā ādhyāsikaṃ tādātmyaṃ, tadgrahaśca `neha nānā' iti niṣedhavākyamanane satiḥ; `satyaṃ jñānamanantam' ityādiśrutayo'pi śuddhacittaṃ prati `neha nānā' ityādi 8utisahakāreṇa dharmatyāgena advaitaṃ suddaṃ brajha bodhayati' (ma.1.pra) ityādi mañjūṣādyaktivat |
bhedasatyaparastarko bhedamāyāparo'paraḥ |
bhedasatyaparastarko jhasan vedavirodhataḥ ||
ekavijñānanugamānneṅa nāneti ca śruteḥ |
ityādi śrīharṣoktirapi parāstā | ekavijñānanigamasya sādṛśyenopapattervaktuṃ śakyatvāt, `neha nānā' ityādi śrutīnāṃ berajhaṇo nirtharmakatvaṃ, jagadasatyatvādau ca na tātparyamityuktatvācca | kiṃ ca vaiśeṣikādayo vastutattvamapalpya bhāṣiṇa iti nāgeśasiddhāntāt vastuniṣṭhaṃ sattvamapalapya anirvacanīyatvamāha gautam iti śakyaṃ vaktum |
`tasya bhāva' (pā.sū.5.1.119) iti sūtre prakṛtipratyayārthayorbheda eva bhāvārthapratyayāḥ śiṣyanta ācāryeriti, anvarthaṃ khalvapi nirvajanaṃ guṇasaṃdrāvo dravyamiti' ityantena bhāṣyeṇa tayorbheda eveti nirdāritam, asattvaṃ ca tasya tatra nābhāṣi ato'sau sanneveti munitrayasyāśayaḥ | ataḥ pāṇinīyaiḥ bhedasyātyatvamanyairukataṃ nābhyupeyaṃ bhāṣyādāvanukteḥ | `bhedasyāsattvamapi tadbhāṣyādau spaṣṭam' iti nañarthavādastakalā tu `tāttvirabhedābhedacintāṃ parihāya lokaprasiddhaṃ bhedamāśritya' iti kaiyaṭam, vāstavabedastu nāstyeveti tātparyam ityudyotaṃ cānusṛtya pravṛtteti tayoreva nirmūlatvāt cintyā | kiṃ ca svasvāṅgamaṅgalipañcakavalokyamānaṃ vibhaktenaiva rūueṇā'vagamyata iti sarvapratyakṣasiddhaṃ bhedamapahnotuṃ ko vā śaknuyāt, bādhādihetvadarśanena asanniti vaktuṃ kasya vā jihvā pravarteta ? ato'sau nāgeśo bhagavatā sūtrakṛtā `nābhāva upalabdheḥ' 9bra.sū.2.2.27) ityādisūtraiḥ pratikṣiptaṃ vedaviruddhaṃ vijñānavādipakṣamāśritya, śrutyādau maharṣivacane ca pardveṣaṃ prakāśayatīti bhāti | ayamatra saṃgrahaḥ ---
anirvaktavyatāvādapādasevāgatistayoḥ |
bhegasatyaparastarko jhasanvedavirodhataḥ ||
ityevaṃvādino vastubhedasatyatavavādibhiḥ |
evaṃ tu prativaktavyā yenā''tmā saṃprasīdati ||
ekavijñānanigamo neha nāneti ca śrutiḥ |
ātmanyabhāvaṃ dharmaṇāṃ na bodhayati tatvataḥ ||
na vakttyasattvaṃ bhāvānāṃ kiṃtu satyatvameva hi |
brūte'khilaṃ vedavākyamiti vidvidvinirṇayaḥ ||
ato'nirvacanīyatvavādapādaniṣevaṇam |
na no gatīḥ, kiṃtvaduṣṭaṃ gatiḥ śrutivaco'khilam ||
