śrutisiddhaṃ vastusattvaṃ bhavataḥ pratiṣedhataḥ |
vijñānavādināṃ pādasevanaṃ śaraṇaṃ viduḥ ||
ataḥsatyaṃ sarvamiti mataṃ yuktaṃ satā matam |
nābhāvādhikṛtau sūtrakṛtā vyāsamaharṣiṇā ||
pratikṣiptaṃ bhāṣyakṛtāṃ pāṇinerapyasaṃmatam |
śrutismṛtiviruddhaṃ ca mataṃ vijñānavādinaḥ ||
āśritya nṛtyati jheṣa sakhā śrīharṣadhīmataḥ |
ataḥ-bhedasatyaparastarko jhasanniti vadan pumān ||
asanniti hi vaktavyo brajhāsattvaṃ vidan yathā |
iti prāhurgautamīyāstathaiva brajhavādinaḥ ||
taṭasthākṣepaḥ
atra kecitpratyavatiṣṭhante --mañjūṣāyāṃ `vaiyākaraṇasiddhāntamañjūṣaiṣā viracyate' iti pratijñāya jaganmithyātavādeḥ ātmaikatvasya ca nirūpaṇāt, tasya ca pramāṇikatvavyutpādanāya śrutismṛtipurāṇānām, anyeṣāṃ tīrthakarāṇāṃ vākyānāṃ ca pramāṇatayopanyāsāt, tatroktaṃ sarvaṃ pṇinyādi maharṣisaṃmataṃ prāmāṇikaṃ ceti nirṇayāmaḥ | na ca atra jīveśabhedaparaiḥ prapañcasatyatvaparaiśca tantrāntaraiḥ virodhaḥ śaṅkyaḥ | teṣāṃ sarveṣāmātmaikatvādipratipādakādhyātmaśāstrāṅgatvamiti dvitīyaparicchede granthakṛtā siddhāntayiṣyamāṇatvāt aṅgāṅginorvirodhasya kvāpyadṛṣṭeḥ, śāstrakṛdbhīstayoravirodhasyaivāṅgīkārat | na ca pratijñāvākyagatavaiyākaraṇapadena pāṇinyādireva vivakṣaṇīya iti ko nirbandhaḥ, anyaḥ kaścidindrādireva vaiyākaraṇastatpadena vivakṣito'stu iti vācyam | etaireva bhaṭṭācāryaiḥ śekhare saṃjñānasaṃprapseyā tapasyataḥ sanakādīnuddiśya brajhavijñānārtaṃ maheśvareṇopadiṣṭānām, `aiuṇā' dīnāṃ caturdaśasūtrāṇāṃ `śrotavyaḥ śrutivkyebhye mantavyaścopapattibheḥ' iti śrautena smātaina vā pramāṇena śrutyaivana brajhaṇaḥ śrotavyatvadhānācchrutitvaṃ vyavastāpya, tanmūlakatvātpāṇiniyasya śrutimūlakatvena, vyākaraṇamapyadhītaṃ mayetyarthakaṃ `vedānāṃ vedaḥ' (chā.7) iti chāndogyavkyamapi etadviṣayakameveti śrutyanugṛhītatvoktyā, atra granthe --`adyatve sādhutvabodhakaṃ vyārakaṇaṃ pāṇiniyameveti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣye' iti bhāṣyakṛtā pataṃjalinā''dṛtatvoktyā ca niratiśayaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ prasādya, śāstreṇa śabdāpaśabdavivecanapūrva śabdaprayogeṇā'bhyudayamabhilaṣatāṃ pāṇinīyameva vyākaraṇamadyeyamiti vyavasthāpitātvena tatsiddhāntaniṣkarṣa eva sarvairapekṣitaḥ syāt iti tatsiddhāntavyutpādanasya nyāyyatayā tatra tatra mahābhāṣyodāharaṇeva ca pratijñāvākyagatavaiyākaraṇapadena pāṇinyādaya evā'ṣa़cārya vivakṣitā bhaveyuḥ | idaṃ sarvamabhipretyaiva `vaiyākaraṇānāṃ - pāṇinyādīnām' iti kalāyāṃ vāyakhayātamiti |
taṭasthasamādhānam
atra vajanti -- nāgestāvadanyamukhena svābhimatamadvaitasiddhāntaṃ pratipipādayiṣuḥ vākyapadīye bhartṛhariṇā pradarśitena pathā sphoyasvarūpaṃ nirūpayan --
anādinidhanaṃ brajha śabdatattvaṃ yadakṣaram |
vivartate'rthabhāvena prakriyā jagato yataḥ ||
iti ādyapadyoktasphoṭalakṣaṇaghaṭakabrajhatva -- nāmarūpātmakaprapañcavivartopādānakāraṇatvayoḥ samarthanaṃ duṣkaraṃ tasyā'dvitīyatvasādhanena vinā, tacca prapañcasya māyikatvasamarthanamantarā duḥśakamiti nirdhārya ātmaikatvasiddhāntasthāpakaramārthasārābhidhe śāstratvena tadabhimate grante kṛtabhūripariśramaḥ tatra dṛḍhatarasaṃskāraḥ ātmaikatvameva śāstreṣu sarvatra pratipādyata iti anyathā jānan bhedaparāṇāmanyeṣāṃ tantrāṇāmadvaitapratipādakādhyātmaśāstrāṅgatvam, sāṃkhyavaiśeṣikagautamādīnāmapi advaita eva tātāparyam, ato na tadvirodho'dvāntasya, śrīśaṅkarācāryaprabhṛtastu sāṃkyogavaiśeṣikādīnāṃ maduktaṃ gūḍhamabhisaṃdhimajānānā eva sāṃkhyagranthādau pratīyamāne dvaita tātparyaṃ teṣāmiti kṛtvā tatkhaṇḍane pravṛttāḥ | evameva teṣāmanyeṣāṃ ca sphoṭavādakhaṇḍane'pi pravṛttiriti bodhayituṃ tatparākṛtasya sphoṭasya svarūpanirūpaṇamagrataḥkṛtvā svābhimatātmaikatvādi pāṇinerapyabhimatamiti bālānāṃ mithyāpratyayaṃ janayituṃ `vaiyākaraṇasiddhānta' iti pratijñāṃ kṛtvā advaitatātparyakaṃ sphoṭavādāparanāmānaṃ mañjūṣābhidhānaṃ granthamataniṣṭa |
tatra prathamaparicchede śaktilakṣaṇāvyañjanākhyavṛttitrayanirūpaṇātparam, `nanu ko'sau śaktyāśrayaḥ śabda' ityādinā `jāterapyasaṃbhavācceti cet' ityantena granthena varṇānāmutpattirabhivyaktiritipakṣadvaye'pi varṇānāmabhivyaktīnāṃ cotpattivināśasālitvāt kramaniśiṣṭavarṇasamudāyarūpapadasya, tatsamudāyarūpavākyasya ca durnirūpatvena padādipratya7ābhāvāt vācakaśabdajñānābhāvena arthapratyayānupapattiriti codyamudbhāvya, `asti kaścitsphoṭābhidhaḥ śabdo'rthavān tajjñānenārthadhīrupajāyata' iti samādhāsyan sphoṭasvarūpaṃ nirūpayituṃ tantrāntaroktaṃ pralayaṃ sṛṣṭikramaṃ ca `atrocyata' ityādinā atisaṃkucitenagrantena grantena nirūpya, pralaye ctana īśvare līnāy līnasarvajagatkāyā māyāyā bhagavata īśvarāt tasya sisṛkṣābhāve'pi prāṇikarmavaśāt puru,eṇa saha prādurbhāvaḥ, tasyāśca parameśvarasya sisṛkṣātmanā pariṇāmaḥ, tatastatsakāśāt sattvādiguṇatrayātmakaśaktitattvākhyabindurūpasya vastunaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ, tatra cit, acit tadubhayamiśraṃ cetyastyaṃśatrayam, tatra acidaṃśaḥ śabdārthobhayasaṃskārarūpo'vidyetyucyate, cidacinmiśro nādaḥ, cidaṃso binduḥ, cidaṃśādbindurūpāt varṇaviśeṣarahitaṃ jñānapradhānaṃ sṛṣṭyupayogyavasthāviśeṣepetaṃ cetanamiśra jagapādānakāraṇaṃ śabdabrajhāparanāmadheyaṃ nādamātraṃ jāyate, tat jñātamarthaṃ vivakṣoḥ purūṣasya iccājanyaprayatnasaṃyogakūpasaṃskāravaśāt nābheradhodese sthitacakrākāramūlādhāredeśe jātapavanacalanena, tatra tataḥ kramāt nābhihṛdayakaṇṭhadeśān gatena tena marutā, tattaddeśeṣu abhivyañjitaṃ sat krameṇa parā, paśyantī, madhyamā, vaikharī iti nāmāni bhajate, tasya parābhidhānasya nādasya sarvāsvavasthāsvanuvartane'pi madhyamāgato nādo vaikharīnādābhivyaṅgyaḥ sphoṭābhidhāṃ dhatte . sa tu --"ākāśadeśaḥ śabda iti `aiuṇ' iti sūtramahābhāṣyāt bājhākāśagataḥ sarvaprāṇihṛdayākāsadeśaśca, sa evāyaṃ gakāraḥ tadevedaṃ padaṃ vākyamiti dhīviṣayatvāt tattaddeśagatatvena jñānaviṣayatvācca nitya eko vibhuśca | vyañjakadhvanigatavarṇasaṃskārakramamanurudhyaiva lokasiddhavyañjakadhvanigatagātvādidharmarūṣito nānātvamāpanna iva dhvanigatāntyavarṇābhivyakta eva arthadhiyaṃ janayati |
anādinidhanaṃ brajha śabdatattvaṃ yadakṣaram |
vivartata'rthabhāvena prakriyā jagato yataḥ ||
kriyāśaktipradhānāyāḥ śabdaśabdārthakāraṇam |
prakṛte bindurūpiṇyāḥ śabdabrajhā'bhavatparā ||
ityādi bhartṛharivacanāt parābhidhaḥ śabda eva nāmarūpātmakaprapañcarūpeṇa vivartate, `ekasyaiva sphoṭasya sarvaśabdatadarthobhayopādānatvena ubhayarūpatayā ubhayorapi tatkāryayorubhayarūpatā, śrotragrājhavaikharīsaṃskṛtāntaḥkaraṇagrājo bhavatīti āntarasphoṭa iti vyavahāraviṣayaśca | etena tatttprāṇihṛdayākāsthasphoṭasya śrotragrājhatvātkathaṃ tadupalabdiriti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ . evaṃ ca bauddha eva śabdo'rthasya pratyāyakaḥ, na tu bojho vakhairīnāmakaḥ sarvakṣotragrājha' iti vyaktisphoṭasvarūpaṃ niraṇaiṣīt |
"jātisphoṭo'tiniṣkarṣa iti paramasiddāntānusāreṇa tatsvarūpaṃ pratipipādayiṣuḥ, `jātirnāma anugatākārapratītijanakaḥ śabde'rthe ca vidyamāno dharmaḥ, sa ca śabdaniṣṭho vācakaḥ, artaniṣṭhastu vācyaḥ | tatra--śabdaniṣṭhaniruktadharmarūpajāterekatvaṃ nityatvaṃ vibhutvaṃ ca sahatukaṃ vyavasthāpya, tasyā dhvanigatavarṇoccāraṇakramānusārisaṃskāreṇa vyañjakānekagatagatvakatvādinānārūparūṣitatayā nānātvena bhāsamānāyā nānājātyavacchinnaghaṭadināpadārthepasthāpakatvena, yugapadanekārthopasthiteḥ, saro rasa ityanayoraviśeṣasya ca prasaṃgo na, tattadarthopasthāpakatattaddharmarūṣaṇarūpābhivyañjanasāmagyayabhāvāt | `anekavyaktyabhivyaṅgyā jātiḥ sphoṭa iti smṛtā' iti harivacanāt" atyādinā savistaraṃ tatsvarūpaṃ prapañcya, sa ca sphoṭa āntaro'rthavācakaḥ, vācyo'pyartho buddhisattāsamāviṣṭiḥ (bauddhaḥ) anyathā `śaśaśrṛṅgaṃ nāsti, ghaṭa ityanenaiva tatsattāvagamena ghaṭo'stīti gatārthatvāt astīti prayogānāpattiśca | bauddhasya padārthasyāgīkāre tu buddhasato bājhasattā tadabhāvabodhanāya asti nāsitīti prayoga upapadyate | ataḥ sarve padārthā bauddhā eva, bājhasattvaṃ saṃvādamātramiti padārthasāmānyasya buddhasattāsamarthanapraghaṭṭake `bhrame jñānākārasyaiva viṣaye āropa' ityādinā, bhramaprakārarajatāderasattvamuktvā `prapañco'pyevameva' ityādinā `alaṃ prasaktānuprasaktyā' ityantena prabandena ātmaikatvaṃ, brajhaikameva yat tadatiriktaṃ samastaṃ jagat māyikaṃ, jagato brajha vivartopādānakāraṇam, tattvaṃ ca bhramādhiṣṭhānatvam, jagato māyikatvaṃ ca bhramaprakāratvamityādi prathamapiriccede, ittaratra paricchedatraye'pi tatra tatra ātmaikatvādi ca nyarūrupat | tatra māyāsvarūpavicāraprasaṅge śrīśaṅkarabhāṣṭavyākyānadūṣaṇaṃ tanmatasiddhānādbhāvarūpā'vidyākhaṇḍanaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ kṛtam | tatra prasaktistu cintyā | prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ |
tatra brajha satyamityatra vinā cārvākādibhyo nā'nyaḥ kaścidvipratipadyate | vastusāmānyasya māyikatvoktiṃ tu na kṣamante lokāḥ | prapañcitaṃ cedaṃ saprapañcaṃ parāparatattvavicārāya pravṛttairācāryaiḥ svasvaprabandheṣu | nanu bhavatu nāma idamanyeṣāmācāryaṇāmanabhimataṃ, pāṇinyādīnāṃ tu abhīmatamidamiti vadāmo nāgesena ma़jūṣāyāṃ `vaiyākaraṇe' tyādi pratijñāpūrvaṃ pratipādanāt iti cet, atrāpyāhu --ācāryaḥ pāṇinirātmaikatvasiddhāntavyavasthāpanāya na śāstramidaṃ prārabhateti jānīmaḥ, yato'yamādau śabdasaṃskāśāstropayogi saṃjñākāṇḍamevārabhat, bhagavān bhāṣyakāro'pi `athaśabdānuśāsanam. keṣāṃ śabdānām' ityādinā'syaśāstrasya viṣayaḥ śabdāḥ, tajjñānaṃ ca prayojanamityabhāṣata | etena `athātaḥ śabdān vyākariṣyāma' iti pratijñāṃ manasikṛtyaiva śāstramārabhata ācārya iti śekharayorvadannāgeśo'pi śabdā evāsyaśāstrasya viṣaya iti manyate | yadyevaṃ tarhi vāsudevaṃ sarvottamaṃ manyate bhagavān pāṇiniriti kathaṃ paraḥ praheti tātparyavanta iti kecit manvīran | mābhūttādṛśo|ḍabhimānaḥ | ācāryāṇāṃ tu na tathā tātparyaṃ, hetvabhāvāt | ātmabheda eva teṣāṃ tātparyaṃ hetudarśanāt iti samarthayituṃ pārayanti pare'pi paṇḍitā iti bodhanāya paravṛtteḥ | mañjūṣādau pratipāditātmaikatvādau idamatra pramāṇaṃ bhavatīti pāṇinyādivacanānidāhakaṇameva tasya tatsaṃmatatvābhāve prabalaṃ vacanamiti aupaniṣadaḥ, nāgesādayo yadbhāṣyaṃ svoktārthe pramāṇatayopanyasyanti tadeva bhāṣyamātmaikatvasiddhāntavyavastāpakamiti tu manorathamātram, tasya bhāṣyasya nāgeśādyukta evārtha iti rājaśāsanādyabhāvena śrutyādyanurodena anyathā yojayituṃ śakyatvāt | sekharādau svābhimataprakriyāsamarthanāya pramāṇatayā udāhṛtaṃ mahābhāṣyamanyathā yojayitvā śekharādyuktaniṣkarṣaṃ nirākurvanti nipuṇamatayaḥ prāñco'rvācīnāśca paṇḍitā iti pravacanaparāyaṇā vaiyokaraṇā jānantyeva | evaṃ sthite paramāstikaśiromaṇeḥ patañjalervākyasya nāgeśādikṛtāṃ śrutyādipramāṇaviruddhāṃ yojanāmadhyātmavidyaviśāradāḥ kathaṃ sādhvīṃ manyeran |
<2>
kiṃ ca `sarūpa-' sūtre (1.2.64) `striyāma' (4.1.3) iti sūtre ca `tasmānna vaiyākaraṇaiḥ śakyaṃ laukikaṃ liṅgamāsthātum, avaśyaṃ kaścitsvakṛtānta āstheyaḥ, ko'sau svakṛtāntaḥ ? saṃstyānaprasavau liṅgam' iti liṅgasvarūpanirṇāyakaṃ bhā,yam | `tatra siddhāntavdī yuktaṃ pakṣamāśrayati--laukikamiti' --iti sarūpasūtre kaiyaṭaḥ, bhāṣyasthakṛtāntapadasya siddhānta' ityartha atyuddyotaḥ | striyāmiti sūtre tu --`svakṛtānta' iti padamādāya `svasīddānta' iti vyācakhyau kaiyaṭaḥ | `sāṃkhyamatāvaṃṣṭambhenāha - svakṛtānta iti' ityevaṃ kaiyaṭaḥ | `sāṃkhyamatāvaṃṣṭambhenāha - svakṛtānta iti' ityevaṃ kaiyaṭamavatārya, `etena sāṃkhyasāstrānusāritvaṃ vyākaraṇasyeti sūcayati `iti nigamayati nāgeśaḥ | sa eva tatraiva sūtre `sāṃkhyānāṃ siddhānta' iti kaiyaṭaṃ `seśvarasāṃkyānāmācāryasya patañjalerityartha' iti vyākurute | tena idamavadhāryate yat bhāṣyapradīpakārau pāṇinerācāryasya seśvarasāṃkhyamatānusāritāṃ manyete iti |
nirīśvaraṃ seśvaraṃ ceti sāṃkhyaṃ tu dvividam | tatrebhayatra puraṣabhedaḥ, jagato māyikatvābhāvaśca pratipādyata iti vyaktaṃ viduṣām | eva ca pāṇinerapi bheda evābhimata iti suvacam | dvividhā api sāṃkhyā bhedavādina iti subodhāya saṃkṣipya kiṃcinnirūpyate |
tatrādye -- prakṛtiḥ purūṣaśceti dve tattve, puruṣāśca bahavaḥ, jananamaraviṇakāraṇānāṃ pratiniyamādayugapatpavṛtteśca | `puruṣabahutvaṃ siddhaṃ traiguṇyaviparyayāccaiva' (18) iti sāṃkhyakārikokteḥ | prakṛtistu puruṣopabhogabhogyaviṣayarūpeṇa pariṇamati, purūṣāstu nānā | evameva kārīkāmimāṃ vyākhyāya puruṣanānātvaṃ vyavastāpitaṃ sāṃkhyamate gauḍapādācāryaprabhṛtibhiḥ |
dvitīye -- prakṛtiḥ puruṣa īśvaraiti trīṇi tattvāni, acetanāpi prakṛtirīśvarapravartitā puruṣapadavācyajīvopabhegayogyaviṣayarūpeṇa pariṇatā puruṣasya bhooogamapargaṃ ca sādhayati | evaṃ ca -- prakṛtiḥ bhogādisādhanam, puruṣo bhoktā, īsvaraḥ prerakaḥ, īsvarapraṇidhānātpuruṣasya mokṣaḥ, iti seśvarasāṃkhyācāryamaharṣipatañjalipraṇītayogasūtrato'dhyavasīyate | tathā hi -- `prakāśakriyāsthitisīlapraṇītayogasūtrato'dhyavasīyate | tathā hi--`prakāśakriyāsthitiśīlabhūtendriyātmakaṃ bhogāpavargārthaṃ dṛśyam (yo.sū.2.18) īśvarapraṇidhānāt (1.23) klośakarmavipākāśayaiparāmṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa īśvaraḥ (1.24) tasya niratiśayaṃ sārvajñabījam (1.25) pūrveṣāmapi guruḥ kālenānāvaccedāt (yo.sū.1.26) tasya vācakaḥ praṇavaḥ (1.27) tajjapastadarthabhāvanam' (1.25) iti saptasūtryā tattvatrayaṃ pratipādya `īśvarapraṇidhānāt' ityādisūtraṣaṭkena bhagavadupāsanam, tallakṣaṇam, tasya sarvajñatvam, tasya brajhādigurutvam, tasya praṇavāvātyatvam, tadvācakapraṇavajapaḥ, tadvācyeśvaracntanaṃ ca kaivalyarūpamuktisādhanayogasiddhyaṅgamiti nirūpayatā upāsyopāsakabhāvena puruṣeśvarayorbhedasya, `kṛtārthaṃ prati naṣṭamapyanaṣṭaṃ tadanyasādhāraṇatvāt' (yo.2.22) iti sūtreṇa puruṣanānātvasya ca pratipāditatvena, `acetanāpi prakṛtiḥ puruṣasya bhogāpavargalakṣaṇamarthaṃ sādhayituṃ tattadadṛṣṭādisahakāreṇa īśvareṇa pravartyata' iti `striyām' (4.1.3) iti sūtrodyotoktyā ca, sāṃkhyānāṃ mate jagato mithyātvānuktyā ca, astī jagataḥ satyatvam, asti ca ātmabhedaḥ sāṃkhyānāṃ mate ityavagamyate |
tena ca tadanuyāyibhiḥ vaiyākaraṇorapi jagatsatyaṃ bhinnāścā''tmānaḥ, īśvarastu teṣāṃ niyāmakaḥ, upāsyaśca teṣām, ata evottama ityabhyupeyamiti sūtrakṛtaḥ pāṇinermate ātmaikatvādikalpanaṃ na yuktamiti asmākaṃ niṣkarṣa iti | imaṃ śaṅkāsamādhāne tṛsthapuruṣasya tu | jijñāsūnāṃ subodhāya dve ete anvavādiṣam |
bauddhasattānirākaraṇam
nanu mañjūṣāyāṃ padārtānāṃ bauddhasattāsamarthanāvasare pramāṇatayā `upadeśe'janunāsika it (1.2.2) hetumati ca (3.1.16) paṅktiviṃśati-(5.1.59) tadasyāstyasmin' (5.2.94) iti catuḥsūtrībhāṣyodāharaṇāt, mṛgatṛṣṇodakādidṛṣṭāntaḥ śāstre uktaḥ, prapañcasyetyukteśca satāmasatāṃ ca padārthānāṃ bauddhīsattā bhāṣyābhimatā | ataḥ buddisattāsamāviṣṭaśaśavi,āṇāderiva tathāvidhajagato'pyasatvaṃ ca yuktamiti cet, sattyam | evaṃvadanā idamādau samālocanīyamāyuṣmatā, yadi vayaṃ bauddhasattāmātreṇa śaśaśrṛṅgadṛṣṭāntena ca padārthānāmasattvaṃ vadāmaḥ, tadā bhavaduktadiśā śabdajapratītiviṣayapadārthatvāviśe,āt brajhaṇaḥ sphoṭasya ca buddhisattāsamāviṣṭatvena śaśaviṣāṇadṛṣṭāntena tayorapi gaganakusumasodaryabhāvaḥ syāditi parānuyogaṃ kathaṃ samādhāsyāmaḥ, kathaṃ vā `brajhasphoṭayoḥ satyatvaṃ taditarasyāsatyatvam' ityasmadvyavasthopapadyeteti | kiṃ ca--vyavahrasya vastusattānibandhanatve bauddhasattaiva tatra heturiti nānuśāsanamācāryāṇāmiti bahiḥsattāmādāyāpi sukaroo vyavahāraḥ |
nanu bahiḥsattā tāvat na vyavahāretiḥ, ghaṭa ityanena sattāyā apyavagamāt, tatra vidhivaiyarthyaṃ jñātasya niṣedhāyogaśceti `ghaṭo'sti, ghaṭo nāsti' iti vidhiniṣedhāyogāt | bauddasattābhyupagame tu bauddho ghaṭe bahirasti, sa bahirnāstīti vidhiniṣedhau yutyeyātāmiti cet, na | ghaṭa ityukte yathā ghaṭatvātiriktaghaṭavṛttidravyatvaprameyatvayorna bhānaṃ tatā tadvṛttisattāyā apīti ajñātatvātsattāyā uktadoṣānavakāśāt | sattāpi śabdavācyeti tu dhaṭaśabdaśravaṇamātreṇa sattāviśiṣṭaghaṭabodhasyānanubhavāt na yuktam | kiṃ ca--vyavahraṃ prati sattāyāḥ kāramatvaniyame'pi antaḥkaraṇe śaśavi,āṇādisaṃktārasya sattve tadviṣayakaśābdabodha eva mānamiti nāgeseneva śaśaviṣāṇaṃ nāstīti śakyaṃ vaktum | kiṃ ca--yasya nāsti śaśaviṣāṇādermukhyā bājhā sattā tasya paraṃ bauddhasattāsvīkāra ucitḥ yasya tu ghaṭāderasti nukyā bājha sattā tasya kutaḥ sā'ṅgīkāryā | bājhasattāyā mukhyatvaṃ matupsūtrodyote spaṣyamabhihitam | kecittu--sattāyā eva vyavahrakāramatvaṃ na, eta eva śaśaviṣaṇādervyavahro dṛśyate ityāhuḥ | astu vā manoratho bhavatāṃ bhāṣyametadbau2ddhasattasādhakamiti, athāpi naitadbhāṣyaṃ ghaṭapaṭādipadārthanāṃ mukyāṃ bjhasattāṃ pratiṣedhati, nāpi arthakriyākāritvaṃ svābhāvikadharmapracyāvane samartho bhavet | ato buddhisattāmātreṇāsatvaṃ jagataḥ śaśaviṣāṇasāmyāditi vādī svagoṣṭhyāṃ mānyaḥ | atrodāhṛtaṃ sarvaṃ bhāṣyaṃ bahiḥ sadviṣayakameva | tatra-`ihasthaḥ pāṭasiputrasthaṃ devadattamupadiśati aṅgadīkuṇḍalīvṛttabāhuḥ' iti `upadeśe'c' iti sūtrabhāṣyaṃ hi bahiḥ sattāviśiṣṭadevadattādiviṣayakameva `pāṭaliputrastam' ityadyukteḥ | asti buddherdeśakālavyavahitavastuviṣayīkaraṇasāmarthyamiti desāntarastho'pi padārtho buddhiviṣayo bhavatīti bhāṣyāśayaḥ |
`hetumati ca' iti sūtrasthaṃ `sato buddhiviṣayān' iti bhāṣyamapi pūrvakālāvacchinnasattāviśiṣṭāneva svabuddhiviṣayānāha ityarthakaṃ bahuḥsattāviśiṣṭārthaviṣayakameva | ata eva `sata' iti pūrvaṃ prāyuṅkta, anyathā `buddhiviṣayān sata' iti prayokṣyat |
`paṅiktaviṃśati-' iti sūtrasthaṃ `samudāye viṃśatyādayo bhaviṣyanti, saṃghaḥ, samūhaḥ, samudāya ityanarthāntaram' ityādi bhāṣyāṃ ca na bauddhasatātāsamarpakamarthānām | avayavāvayavinoḥ guṇaguṇinośca bedajñāpakameva | `guṇas‌ya prādānye paṭasya śuklaḥ, guṇinaḥ pradhānye śuklaḥ paṭaḥ iti vyavahāra' ityādi tatsūtrabhāṣyamapi na baddhisattājñāpakaṃ, nāpi bahiḥsattāpratiṣedhakam | ata eva matupsūtre `guṇacanebhyo luk, iti lukaṃ vidadhāno vararuciḥ `avyatirekātsiddha iti cet dṛṣṭo vyatireka' iti guṇasya guṇyavyabhicāreṇa vartanāt abhedādevaśuklaṃḥ paṭa iti prayogasiddau lugvacavanārambho vyartha iti śaṅkāṃ ghaṭasya śukla iti bhedena vyavahāradarśanena bheda evobhayoriti vacanamārabdhavyamiti siddāntitavān | tatra bhāṣyakṛdapi avyatirekādeva sddhamiti kṛtvā lugvacanaṃ na pratyācaṣṭe, etena guṇaguṇinorvyatirekastadubhayasaṃmata iti pratiyanti | vacanārambhapratyākhyānayostu nā'styadhikāro'smādṛśanām, atisūkṣamamapi vastu yogināṃ pratyakṣagamyam, ato nāsmatprayakṣagaucara iti tannāstīti vaktaṃ na pryate, upadeśaparaṃparayā kāryadarśanena ca samasti bolā iva dajhamānā na lakṣyate vikṛtiḥ sannipāte | astīti tāṃ vedānte tribhāvāḥ sūkṣmo hi bhāvo'nimitena gamya' iti `vārtamāne laṭ' itisūtre'bhyadhāyi |
asyārthaḥ --visadāhena tadantargatatantavo dajhamānā api tattvena yathā na lakṣyante tathā ekaikakṣaṇaḥ, sannipāte--kārakalamāvese vikṛtiḥ kṣaṇavṛttikriyāviseṣaśca, atisūkṣmatvāt na dṛśyate | asmaddarśānā'viṣayatvamātreṇa kṣaṇādikaṃ nāstīti na vācyamityāha -- astīti | tribhāvāḥ - triṣu kāleṣu bhāvaḥ jñānaṃ yeṣāṃ te tribhāvā yoginaḥ sthūlasūkṣmakālatrayatādṛśakālavṛttivastuviṣayakajñānavantaḥ, tāṃ-vikṛtiṃ kriyopalakṣitasūkṣmakṣaṇādikaṃ, vedayante-jānanti, svārtheṇic | kāryanyathā'nipapattimūlakānumānenu upadeśaparaṃparayā ca sūkṣmo'pyartho gamyaḥ jñātavyo'smābhiriti | evaṃ ca asmadādipratyakṣāgaucare padārtho yogipratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamiti bodhayan tādṛśapadārtho nāstiti vādoo padārthe yogipratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamiti bodhayan tādṛśapadārtho nāstīti vādo nādaraṇīyaḥ pramāṇikairiti upadiśati bagavān bhāṣyakṛditi veditavyam | atra--`īdṛśe sthale vikalpākhyajñānamasmākam' iti mañjūṣā tu cintyā | yogipratyakṣasya pāramārthikasadviṣayatvena pramāṇajñānatvāt vikalpākhyajñānasya vastuśūnyatvena bdhakāle jāyamānatvena ca pramāṇānyavṛttitvamiti yogasūtrabhāṣyakārādibhirnirṇotvena pramāṇavṛttisthale tadviruddhavikalpavṛtteraṅgīkārāyogāt |
`goviṃśatirānīyatāmiti guṇasya codane' ityādinā guṇacodane guṇino dravyasya codanāviṣayatānirupaṇabhāṣyaṃ tu parasparaparihāreṇa avidyamānatvarūpasahacaritatvasadbhāvenetyarthakam natvabhedeneti | `avyatirekā' diti tatsūtrabhāṣyamapi aktā'vyabhicaritatvārtha kameva | evaṃ ca -- avayavāvayavinoḥ guṇaguṇivoṣca svarūpato bhedo'tisīkṣma iti bhāṣyatātparyamiti bhāti | ata eva --`tasya phūraṇe ḍaṭ' (5.2.48) iti sūtre `anyatvādumayornyāyyāvākṣīśakhānidarśanam' at bhāṣye'vayavāvayavinorbhedena vārkṣītivat pañcama ityādau prakṛtipratyayārthayorbhedāt vṛttirnyāyyetyāha bhāṣyakāraḥ | `vṛkṣasya avayava' ityarthe `anudāttādeśca' (4.3.140) ityavayo'rthe'ñ‌pratyayaḥ atrāpi bhāṣye bauddhasattāsadbhāve bhedasyā'vāstavatve ca gamakaṃ nopalabhāmahe |
`na sattāṃ padārtho vyabhicarati' iti matupsūtrabhāṣyaṃ tu arthakriyākārivastusattāṃ na vyabhicaratītyarthakamiti bājhasattāviśiṣṭavastuviṣayakameva, bājhasata eva arthakriyākāritvāt, ato na vyabhicāraḥ, śaśaśrṛṅgādau arthakriyākāritvābhāvātsya | etatsūtracatuṣṭayapradīpoddyotagranthastu bālamānasarañjakaḥ | etena--`buddhivyavasthāpitārthanibandhanāśca śabdāḥ buddhimevā'rthākārāmupajanayanto'satyapi vāstave bhede tamavagamayanti ' iti `paṅakti-' iti sūtre kaiyaṭaḥ, `baddhiparikalpitārthābhidhāyina eva śabdā na bājhapadārthābhāvasya, śabdānāṃ bājhapadārthavācakatvābhāvasya cānukteḥ, ityudadyotaḥ evamarthakamañjūṣāgranthaśca bhāṣyaviruddhaḥ | bhāṣye bājhapadārthābhāvasya, śabdānāṃ bājhapadārthavācakatvābhāvasya cānukteḥ, `ghaṭamānaya' ityādiśabdavyavahāravilayaprasaṃgāt | śāstramātrasiddhayoḥ brajhasphoṭayorapi śaśaviṣāṇasāmyāpātāt | tasmātpuruṣabuddhimātraparikalpito'rthaḥ pramāṇapramitāṃ vastusattāṃ na pratibadhnīyāt | bauddhasattāsvūkāreṇa vyavahāropapattyabhyupayogī | kīṃ ca asatāmapi bauddhasattāsvīkāreṇa vyavahāropapattyabhyupagame vyapadeśivadbhāvāśrayaṇam, `ādyantavadekasmin' iti sītraṃ ca vyarthaṃ syāt, ādyantatvayoḥ, vyapadeśivadbhāvalabhyadhātvavayavatvādeśca bauddhasattāsamāviṣyatvena āditvādinimittakakāryāṇāṃ susaṃpādatvāt | kiṃ ca sarvasādāraṇyena bauddhasattāhgīkāre asatpadārthasyaivābhāvena sadarthakatve śabdasya pramāṇyam, asadarthakatve'prāmāṇyamiti vyavasthā'nupapattiḥ | īdṛśe viṣaye vikalpākhyavṛttyāśrayaṇa- ca nopapadyate | nanu bauddhasattāyāḥ `śaśaviṣāṇāṃ nāsti' iti vyavahāramātraprayojakatvānnaiṣa do, iti cet, tarhi bauddhasatau mṛgatṛṣṇodakādeḥ dṛṣyāntatvamapi na saṃbhavatīti taddṛṣṭāntena jagato'sattvasya duḥsādhatvena jagataḥ satyatvasiddhirityavehi | kiṃ ca ghaṭādivastunoo bahiḥ sattā neti na kvāpyavādīdābhāṣyakṛditi mṛgatṛṣṇodakādidṛṣṭāntoktiśca cintyā | `nedaṃ rajatam' itivat `nāyaṃ ghaṭaḥ' ityādi bādhajñānānudayācca nā'sattvam | `āgamāpāyino'nityāḥ' (gī.2.14) iti viṣayāṇāmanityatvokteḥ, `nityānityavastuvivekaḥ' ityabhiyuktokteśca anityaṃ jagaditi pramāṇikaḥ panthāḥ |
nanu
buddhau kṛtvā sarvāśceṣṭāḥ kartā dhīrastanvannītiḥ |
śabdenārtānvācyāndṛṣṭvā buddau kuryātpaurvāparyam ||
iti `paraḥsaṃnikarṣaḥ saṃhitā' (1.4.1.9) iti sūtrabhāṣye bauddhārthasyaiva jñānaviṣayatvamuktamiti mañjū,āyāṃ prathamaparicchedokterarthānāṃ bauddhatvaṃ, tasyaiva jñānaviṣayatvaṃ ca bhāṣyasaṃmatamiti yuktamiti cet, atrāhaḥ --tatra bhāṣye spa,ṭamevamanuktestadubhayaṃ na bhāṣyasaṃmatam | tatra hi bhā,ye'rthānāṃ jñānaviṣayatvamātramabhihitaṃ, na tāvatā bhavadabhimatasddhaḥ, nāpi kāciddhāniḥ pareṣām | tatra vāgīndriyasya yugapadvarṇadvayoccāraṇasāmarthyābhāvāt, pūrvarṇanāśettarameva uttaravarṇotpattesca naṣṭavartamānayoḥ paurvāparyasaṃbhavena saṃnikarṣābhāvātsūtramanupapannamiti `na hi varṇānāṃ paurvāparyamasti' ityādinā varṇā'nityatvavādinā pūrvapakṣite, śabdānāṃ nityatvapakṣe na doṣa iti samādhatte buddhāvityādinā | ślokārthamāha bhāṣyakṛt ---`buddhiviṣayameva śabdānāṃ paurvāparyam' iti | idamādyapādārthavivaraṇam | kṛtvāśāstraṃ pravartitamiti śeṣaḥ | ke te śabdā ityata āha-iha ca ya eva manuṣyaḥ prekṣāpūrvakārī bhavati sa paśyati asminnarte'yaṃ śabdaḥ prayoktavya iti | idaṃ bhāṣyaṃ dvitīyatṛtūyapādārthavivaraṇaparam | dhīraḥ tanvannītirityanayorartaḥ -- prekṣāpūrvakārīti | samastaviṣayavyāpanaśīlabuddhimānpaṇḍita iti tadarthaḥ | bhavatītyasya ślokasthakartetyanenānvayaḥ | kartā -- prayoktā bhavatītyartaṃḥ | `sa paśyati' iti tṛtīyapādārthavivaraṇam | akṣarārthaḥ spa,ṭaḥ | anena vācyān -- upadeṣṭavyān arthān, śabdena-etaiḥ śabdaiḥ bodhayāmi iti, dṛṣṭvā-niścitya, yadvā śabdena vācyānartān etacchabdavācyo'yamartha iti niścityetyakṣarārthaḥ sūcitaḥ | etena, śabdena sahitānarthāniti vyāśyānamapāstam | `asminnarte'yaṃ śabdaḥ prayoktavya iti pasyati' iti tu tātparyakathanam | `artaṃ budhvā śabdaracanā' iti nyāyāt ayaṃ śabdo bodhanīyārthavācakaḥ, ayaṃ śabdo'trārte prayoktavya iti dhīro niścinotīti bhāvaḥ | uktaṃ paurvāparyasya buddhiviṣayatvamupapādayati -- asmīn tākat śabde'ye tāvadvarṇastato'yaṃ tato'yamitīti | asti krama iti śeṣaḥ | asya `iti paurvāparyaṃ buddau kuryāt' iti ślokasthena saṃbandhaḥ | karteti kartṛpadamadyāhāryam | saṃbhāvanāyāṃ liṅ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ --- śabdāstavannityāḥ kramaviśiṣṭāśca prayoktavyatvena buddhiviṣayā iti tadgatakramo'pi nāntarīyakatayā vaktṛbuddiviṣayo bhavatyeva | tataśca paurvāparyamapi tadbuddiviṣaya iti tadeva paurvāparyaṃ sāstrapravṛttiprayojakamiti niścatya pravartitamācāryaiḥ śāstramiti |
nanu sphoṭa eva nityaḥ śabdaḥ tatra tu varṇakramau na tasyā'khaṇḍatvādityuktam, tatkathamevamucyata iti cet, na | rekhāgavayanyāyena śāstramanuśāsakaṃ śabdānāmiti siddāntena rekhāsthānīye vyañjake kramasadbhāvena ghaṭaśabdena paṭopasthītivāpaṇāya vyaṅgye sphoṭe vyañjakagadharmāropa āvaśyaka iti bhavataivoktatvāt tatrāpi varṇakramasadbhāvena vyaṅgye vyañjake ca varṇakramayoḥ sattvāt | anena buddhiviṣayatvamevārtānāmuktaṃ na tu tadatiriktabauddhasatteti nirmatsarā nipuṇadhiṣaṇāḥ pratipadyeranniti |
stānivatsūtre ca bhāṣyakāraḥ padārthānāṃ bauddhatvaṃ kaṇṭhato nooo brūte | tatra hi--- `pracīnaṃ grāmādāmrā' iti upadeśe grāmaprācīdeśe vidyamānavṛkṣasāmānye prasajantī āmratvabuddiḥ, tatratyeṣu nyāgrodheṣvapi prasattā | sā `ye kṣīriṇo'varohavantaḥ pṛthuparṇāste nyagrodā' iti viseṣopadesena nivartate | tata upadiṣṭaḥ puru,ḥ nyagrodhe āmratvabaraddhiṃ na karotīti uktam | etadbhāṣyeṇāpi padārtho buddhiviṣayo bhavatītyeva sidhyati na tu buddhistha iti | bhāṣye apakṛṣyamāṇānityasya nivartyamānānityarthaḥ, na tu cittasthānapakṛṣyeti | buddhigatanivṛttyutpattyorvastuni āropa ityuktamuddyote, na tu buddhistavastugate apakarṣopadhāne iti |
athocyeta -- bauddārtā'naṅgīkāre `sa bhūriti vyāharat sa būmimasṛjata' (tai.btā.2.2.4.2)
sarveṣāṃ tu sanāmāni karmāṇi ca pṛthakpṛthak |
vedaśabdebhya evādau pṛthaksaṃsthāśca virmame || (manu.9.21)
iti śrutismṛtibhyāṃ bhūḥśabdādibhyastadarthajñānapūrvamuktā bhūmyādisṛṣṭirnopapadyeta, sṛṣṭoḥ pūrvaṃ bājhāyā bhuvo'bhāvena bhūḥśabdādibhyaḥ sisṛkṣitabhūmyādipadārthaśaktigrahabodhayoranudayāt | bauddhārthāṅgīkāre tu hiraṇyagarbhasya svabuddhisthabhūmyādipadrthe śaktigrahastadviṣayakabodhaśca bhavatīti sṛjyamānapadārthajñānasadbhāvena śrutyādyuktasṛṣṭirupapadyata iti śakyate vaktumiti satyam | pūrvakalpasthitān bhūmyādīn bhūḥśabdādibhyo vijñāya asṛjaditi śrutismṛtitātparyeṇa sṛṣṭeḥ pūrvaṃ bājhārthābhāve'pi na sṛṣṭikathanānupapattiḥ | `satyapi sarvavyavahārocchedini mahāpralaye parameśvarānugrahāt hiraṇyagarbhādīnāṃ kalpāntaravyavahārānusaṃdānopapatteḥ' iti `samānanāma-' iti sūtre śrīśaṅkarācāryādibhiruktatvena, `yasya pūrvakalpe yānyāsan ādau sṛṣṭyādau vedāśabdebhya evāvagamya nirmama' iti manuvacanavyākhyānena ca kalpāntarasthitapadārthaśaktigrahabodhābhyāṃ śrutyādyuktasṛṣṭīrupapanneti nedamarthasya bauddhatve pramāṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | patañjaliridaṃ śrutyādi nāgeśoktadiśā kvāpi bhāṣye na vyācaṣṭe | `śabda iti cenna' (bra.sū.1.3.17) `samānanāma-' iti sūtradvayaśaṅkarabhā,yaṃ, manusmṛtivyākhyānaṃ cānavalokya, tatroktārtho'saṃgata iti matvā vā evamabhihitavānnāgeśa iti bhāti | paṇḍitā eva mañjūṣāyāṃ pradarśitaprakārāntareṣu pratividhātumīśata iti na tatra pravarte vistarabhiyā |
kecitu -- śaśaviṣāṇaāditucchavastuviṣayakavikalpākhyavṛttiḥ svaviṣayavastuno na padavṛttiviṣayatāmapekṣate, deśakālasaṃbandhavata eva padavṛttiviṣayatvaṃ, tacchasya tu tadabhāvanna tattvam, spaṣṭaṃ cedamadvaitasiddhitadvyākhyānayoravidyānirvācyatvanirūpaṇaprakaraṇe | tatra hi -- nirupākhyatvamasattvam, nirūpākyatvasya padavṛttyaviṣayatvarūpatvāt | deśakālasaṃbandhābhāvena tucchasya na padavṛttiviṣayatvaṃ, śaśaviṣaṇādipadajanyo vikalpastu na padavṛttisāpekṣaḥ, śabdapramāviparyayoreva tattvāditibhāvaḥ' (vyā.candrikā) ityuktam | śaśaviṣāṇādiśabdaiḥ śaktigrahādyapekṣāmantareṇāpi bodho jāyate śabdaśaktimahimneti `śabdajñāna' iti sūtratātparyamiti tayorāśayaḥ | evaṃ ca ṇasatāṃ bauddhasattāmantarāpi nirvāhāt teṣāṃ tatkalpanaṃ mudhaiva | udāhṛtamahā bhāṣyārthā'paryālocanayā advaitasiddhyādau tatoktiriti tu na saheran vipaścitaḥ | na ca, buddhyatiriktadeśasaṃbandhābhāva iti tattātparyamiti vācyam, saṃkoce kāraṇābhāvāt, evaṃ kalpanāpekṣayā tatpathāśrayaṇasya varatvāt | na ca atra vṛttijñānamantarā śābdabodha ityaṅgīkāre tatpadajanyaśābdabuddhiṃ prati tatpadanirūpitavṛttijñānaṃ heturiti vyāptepādikatvānāpattiriti vācyam | anuraṇādibodhātiriktabodhaviṣayakatvakalpanenādauṣa ityāśayāt | na caivamapi vṛttyabhāve prātipādikatvānāpattiriti vācyam | anukaraṇasthale sādṛśyākhyasaṃbandhena anukāryabodhakatvena arthavattvātprātipadikatvavat śabdajñānottarabhāvivikalpākhyavṛttiviṣayatāsaṃbandhena asadarthabodhakatvāt arthavattvena prātipadikatvopapatteḥ |
kiṃ ca, matupsūtrakaiyaṭoktamevārtham `evaṃ śakyo'pyartha' ityā dinā nirūpya, `sattāpi śabdavācyeti tadbhāva' ityuktatvena sarvamidaṃ kaiyaṭābimatameva na tu svābhimatamityapi vaktaṃ śakyam | na ca `buddhistapadārthāṅgīkartraśaya' iti kalāyāṃ vyākhyānāt nāgeśasyāpi iṣṭametaditi jānīma iti kalāyāṃ vyākhyānāt nāgeśasyāpi iṣṭametaditi jānīma iti vācyam, tadvyākyānasya `tadbhāva' iti akṣarānuguṇatvāt | yadi idaṃ svasyeṣaṭaṃ syāt tarhi `sattāpi śabdavācyā' ityetāvadevāvakṣyat | sarvo'pi padārtānāṃ bauddhatvanirūpaṇaparo mañjūṣāgranthaḥ kaiyaṭoktasyaivopodbalakaḥ | astu vā kaloktadisā nāgeśasyāpi saṃmatametaditi, tāvatāpi na kiṃciddiyate pramāṇapramitaprapañcasatyatāvādinām | eteni `buddhivyavasthāpita-' ityādi pradīpamupamupajīvya vastūnāmuktaṃ buddhyākāratvam, `pratibhāsamātraśaraurasya mithyāvastuna' iti ma़jūṣoktaṃ pratibhāsamātraśarīratvaṃ ca na yuktamiti bodhyam, sūtrabhāṣyakārābhyāṃ vastuno buddhyākāratvādyanukteḥ | kiṃ ca, evamaṅgīkāre vijñānavādimatapraveśāpattiḥ | ayaṃ tu vādo nirākṛto vyāsena dvitīyasya dvitīye caraṇe brajhamīmāṃsāyām | pāṇinipatañjalyora pi na saṃmato'yaṃ vāda iti vidāṃkurvantu sūrayaḥ |
padārthānāṃ bauddhasattāṃ vakti na kvāpi bhāṣyakṛt |
bahiḥsattā neti tasmātsatyaṃ ghaṭādikam ||
ato'sattvaṃ ghaṭādestu vaktaṃ nārhanti śābdikāḥ |
āhuḥ kaiyaṭabhaṭṭojināgeśādyastu śābdikāḥ ||
āśritya matamanyaṣāṃ naitadbhāṣyakṛto matam |
vimṛśya bhāṣyamevaṃ hi saṃgirante manīṣiṇaḥ ||
sarvatantrāṇāmadvaitāṅgatvanirākaraṇam
nanu ma़ñjīṣāyāṃ `sthūlasūkṣmaśarīrātiriktātmasādhanadvārā adhyātmaśāstrāhgamityāsayaḥ, gurvādibhirvāde tattvanirṇayārthaṃ caitadityapi tatraivoktam | anena adhyātmapratipādakādvaitaśāstrātiriktasarvanantrāṇāmevaṃvidhatvaṃ sūcitam' iti ākhyātārthavāde vartamānatvanirvacanāvasaraṃ uktatvena, advaitasyaiva pārāmārtikatvām, tadatiriktasāṃkhyādisāstroktātmanānātvāderamārtikatvaṃ sidhyati, ataḥ sāṃśyoktooo bhedo māyika eveti tadanuyāyināṃ vaiyākaraṇānāmapi mate tasya tathātvameva yuktamiti cet, na | vedatadanusāripramāṇaśaraṇanām `ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ rathameva ca' 9kā.1.3.3) evameva `athāyamātmā idaṃ śarīraṃ nihatyā'vidyāṃ gamayitvā'nyamākramamākramya,' evameva....`an‌yannavataraṃ kalyāṇataraṃ rūpaṃ kurutaṃ (bṛ.4.4.3) `na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre'(gī.2.20, kā.u.2.18) tathā `śarīrāṇi vihāya jīrṇānyanyāni saṃyāti navāni dehī' (gī.2.22) `antavanta ame dehā nityasyoktāḥ śarīriṇaḥ' (gī.2.18) `tadantarapratipattau raṃhati saṃpariṣvaktaḥ praśnanirūpaṇābhyām' (bra.sū.3.1.1) iti śrutyādibhireva dehātiriktātmavivekasaṃbavena tatra sāṃkhyāditantrāpekṣābhāvāt tadarthaṃ teṣāmaṅgatvakalpanāyā āvaśyakatvābhāvāt | kiṃ ca, aṅgāṅgibhāvakalpanāyā mārjārakṣīrapānanyāyena kvāpi kṣoṇīkoṇe sthitvā netere nimīlya svavāsanāmātrasāhāyakena kalpitatvāt | yadi mañjīṣokto'ṅkāṅkibhāvo vāstavo bhavet, tadā gautamādibhiīrapi svasvaśāstramadyātmabodhakādvaitaśāstrāṅkamityanusaṃdhānapūrvaṃ praṇītaṃ syāt, tathātve ca teṣāṃ tathāvidhatvaṃ śaṅkarācāryaprabhṛtibhirapi vijñātameva bhavet, sati tu teṣu teṣāṃ tādṛśe vijñāne `ikṣaternāśabdam (bra.1.1.5) īkṣatikarmavyapadeśātsaḥ (1.3.13) sāmṛtyanavakāśadauṣaprasaṃga iti cennā'nyasmṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṃgāt (2.1.1) racanānupapatteśca nānumānām (2.2.1) etena yogaḥ pratyuktaḥ (2.1.3) ānumānikamapyekeṣāmiti cenna śarīrarūpakavinyastagṛhīterdarśayati ca' (1.4.1) ityādīnāṃ sāṃkhyayogādisamayadūṣaṇaparatayā śaṃkarācāryakṛtavyākhyānasya, tathā `ābhāsa eva ca' (2.3.40) iti sūtraṃ ātmabahutvavādināṃ sāṃkhyakāraṇādādīnāṃ mate sarveṣāmātmanāṃ sānnidhyāviseṣāt ekenātmanā manaḥ saṃyogasya nāntarayīkatayā ekasya sukhitve duḥkhitve ca sarveṣāṃ sukhitvaduḥkhitve syātāmiti doṣakathanasya, tathā `adṛṣyā'nyamāt' (bra. 2.3.41) ityādisūtratrayasya ātmabahutvavāde anupapattinirūpaṇaparatayā śaṃkarācāryakṛtavyākhyānasya, tathā śaṃkaracāryakṛtabhāṣyarītyā pradhānakāraṇatāvadanirasatātparyakāṇāṃ bahūnāṃ sūtrāṇām, tattatsūtraśāṃkarabhāṣyasya ca, tathā `etena yogaḥ pratyuktaḥ' (bra.sū. 2.1.3) iti yogasāstrapratyākhyānapare sūtre `ātmabhedajñānaṃ muktisādhamiti sāṃkhyayogamatam, ākmaikatvavijñānaṃ muktisādhanamiti brajhavādisiddhāntaviruddhamiti | tātparyakavaidikātmavijñānādanyat na niḥśreyasasādhanam `tameva' iti śruteḥ, dvaitino hite sāṃkhyā yogāśca nātmaikatvadarśina iti śāṃkarabhāṣyasāya, tathā -- tatraiva sūtre `tatkāraṇaṃ sāṃśyayogābhipannam' (śvaṃ.3.7) iti tu pratyākhyātayogaviṣayaṃ na, kiṃ tu vaidikajñānadhyānaparam' iti tayoḥ śrautatvābhāvakathanasya, tathā tatraiva sūtre `sarvāṇi tarkaparāṇi prativaktavyāni' iti `vaiśeṣikādīnyapi tantrāṇi ātmabhedaparatvāt brajhavitsiddhāntaviruddhatvena tānyupekṣyāṇi' iti sūtrakṛdāśayavarṇanaparaśāṃkarabhāṣyasya, tathā tatraiva sūtre `tānyapi tarkopapattibhyāṃ tattvajñānāyopakurvantīti śaṅkāyām, upakurvantu nāma tatvajñānaṃ tadu vedāntavākyebhya eva' iti tarkaparāṇāṃ tattvajñānasādhanatvā bhāvenopekṣyāṇyeveti vedāntaśāstrātiriktaśāstrāṇāṃ nindāparaśāṃkarabhāṣyasya, tathā vājasaneye maitreyībrājhaṇe (4.15) aho anumānakauśalaṃ darśitamapucchaśṛṃgaistārkikabalīvardaiḥ' iti saṃkarācāryakṛtabhedavāditārkikanindasya, tatā taittirīyabhṛguvalyāṃ (10.4) śāṃkarabhāṣye `kapilakāṇādādivirodha' iti ātmaitvasya sāṃkyādivirodhamāśaṃkya `teṣāṃ mūsābhāve vedavirodhe ca bhrāntatvopapatteḥ iti teṣāmajñānitvakathanasya ca, tattattantrakṛdabhiprāyānabhijñānavijṛṃbhitatvaṃ syāt, yadi sāṃkhyādīnāmadvaitāṅgatvaṃ tarhi aṅkatvātteṣāṃ na tadvirodha ityevāvakṣyat tathaiva vaktaveyaṃ ca |
kiṃ ca atroktarītyā sarvaśastrāṇāmadvaitaśāstrāṅkacve prathamapariccheda eva prapañcasya māyikatvanirūpaṇāvasare pratīyamānabhedaparāṇāṃ kāṇādādiśāstrāṇāmapi adhyātmaśāstrāṅgatayā na tadvirodha ityuktyaiveṣṭasiddhau `kaṇādādayo'pi māyikatvena brajhabhūtatvena ca jñātasya prapañcasya bodhanīyaśiṣyādhikārānurodhena māyikatvādyapalapya brajhatadvena jñātaparamāṇvādibhyaḥ sṛṣṭim' ityādinā teṣāṃ māyikatvādyapalāpakatvoktirvyarthā | na ca evamuktirmāyikatvasādhikā teṣāmapalāpakatvaṃ vinā prapañcasya tattvāsiddheriti vācyam | yadi māyikatvaṃ prapañce pramāṇapramitamabhaviṣyat tadeyamuktiḥ tatsādhirā'bhavi,yat | `asatyamapratiṣṭhaṃ ta' (gī.15) iti jagato māyikatvaniṣedhātsyaivāpramāṇikatvena tadapalāpena anyathāvāditvoktiḥ teṣāṃ ni dāparyavasāyinyeva bhavet |
kiṃ ca yadi kaṇādādīnāṃ prapañcamāyikatvādijñānaṃ tadā tairasmadīyamidaṃ śāstramadhyātmapratipādakādvaitaśāstrasyāṅgam, idaṃ sarvamātmasvarūpabhūtameveti vāstava evārtha upadeṣṭavyaḥ, tathāvādītva eva te āptā ācāryapadabhūṣitāśca bhaveyuḥ | yathārthavādina eva hi āptā ācāryāśca bhavanti | `āptastu yathārthavaktā' iti gautamīyokteḥ | `aāpto nāma-anubhavena vastitattvasya tārtsnyena niscayavān rāgādivaśādananyathāvādī (1.pra.1.1.4) āptatvaṃ ca bhramādidauṣarāhitye sati tattatkriyāviṣaye sveṣṭapratibandhā'niṣṭotpattyubhayānukūlayatnābhāvavattvam' (95.15) iti mañjūṣokteśca | vastutattvamapalapya bhāṣāmāṇānāṃ yathārthavaktṛtvābhāvena anyathāvādtvena ca sveṣṭapratibaddhādyanukūlayatnavanta eva te ityadhyavasāyāt anāptā evati aviśvasanīyavacanā bhaveyuḥ |
ācinoti hi śāstrārthamācāre sthāpayatyapi |
svayamācarate yastu tamācāryaṃ pracakṣate ||
iti smaraṇāt, yathārthato'nubhūtaśāstrārthopadesena parānapi sadācāre vartayatāmevā vāryapadārhatvena anubhūtārthavirūddhārthopadeṣṭāraḥ kathamācāryapadabhājo bhaveyuḥ ?
kiṃ ca kaṇādādayaḥ sarvatrātmatvabuddhimantastāṃ buddhimapalapya paramāṇukāraṇatāṃ śiṣyebhya upādiśanniti vadan praṣṭavyaḥ, kiṃ bhavānitthaṃ svapne'badhyata, kaṇādādaya eva bhavadantikametya tathā'vadan svānubhavo'nyaḥ upādiṣṭo'rthaścānya iti | nātrā''dyaḥ kalpaḥ śakya āsthāṃtuṃ, bhavanmate svāpnaghaṭārbodhottaramarśanenā'sattvavat asyāpyasattvāt | dvitīyo'pi kalpo na tarāṃ ghaṭate | kīrtimātraseṣāṇāṃ kaṇādādīnāmadyatve bhavadantikāgamanāsaṃbhavāti prathamaparicchedoktaṃ ca cintyam | tataśca, sāṃkyayogakaṇādaśāstrāṇāṃ nādvaitasāstrāṅgatā | tathāvādastu svābhimatātvaitasya sarvatantrānukūlyasaṃpādanāgrahamūlakaḥ |
ataste,āṃ svātantryameva | teṣu siddhāntitamātmanānātvādikamapi pāramārthikameva na tu māyikam, sāṃkhyādau tasya tathātvakalpakavacanānupalammāt | pāṇinistu bhedavādīti āveditaṃ pūrvameva | etena `sāṃkhyaśāstrānusāritvaṃ vaiyākaraṇasyeti sūcayati' iti striyāmiti sūtroddyote vadatā nāgeśenāpi pāṇinerbhedavāditā'bhyupagataprayā, `sāṃkhyānāṃma bhedavāditvena tadanusāriṇo vaiyākaraṇasyāpi tatātvasya nyāyaprāptatvāt | sāṃkhyānāṃ bhedavāditvaṃ tu pūrvodāhṛtaśaṅkagabāṣyāt, `kapilo hi nāma sarvātmadarśanaṃ nānumanyate ātmabhedābhyupagamāt' ityādismṛtyadhikaraṇīyaśāṅkarabhāṣyāditaḥ sāṃkhyādigranthataścā'dhyavasīyate |
nanu `tattvādhyavasāya' iti sūtramupanyasya, idaṃ śāstraṃ vyarthamataāhetyavatārya, tatsūtrabhāṣyamupanyasya `tasyāmavasāthāyāmityādinā tadāśaya' ityantena saugatamatakhaṇḍanatātparyakamidaṃ śāstramadhyātmāṅgaṃ bhavati, `anena' ityādinā `sarvatantrāṇāmevaṃdhatvaṃ sūcatam' iti ca mañjīṣāyāmupapādanāt sarvatantrāṇāmadvaitāṅgacā gautadamasūtrabhāṣyasaṃmateti cet, atrāhuḥ -- sūtrakāro bhagavān gautamaḥ pramāṇādīnāṃ `tattvajñānāpadavarga' iti prathamasūtreṇābhidhāya tatrāpyātmajñānaṃ pradhānaṃ taccātmasaṃskāramapekṣate, tasya sādhanāni yamaniyamādīni tāni yogaśāstrāt jñeyanīti, `yamaniyamābhyāmātmasaṃskāro, yogāccādhyātmavidhyupāyaiḥ' (4.2.46) iti sūtreṇa sādhanāntaramapyāha | tatra, tadartham-apargārtham, yogāt yogaśāstrāt, anyatspaṣṭam, iti prathamasūtrārthaḥ | jñānamātmavidya, tadvidyaiḥ ātmavidyāvicakṣaṇaiḥ, anyatspaṣṭam, iti dvitīyasūtrārthaḥ | nanvevaṃpradhānaṃ ātmatattvajñāne vādakathamasūtrārthaḥ | nanvevaṃpradhāne ātmatattvajñāne vādakathayaiva siddhe, jalpavitaṇḍābhyāṃ kiṃkṛtyamityākṣepe, `tattvādhyavasāyasaṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jalpavitaṇḍābhyāṃ kiṃkṛtyamityākṣepe, `tattvādhyavasāyasaṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jalpavitaṇḍe bījaprarohasaṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ kaṇṭakaśākhāvaraṇavat' (50) iti sūtritavāt | idaṃ ca bhāṣye -- `svapakṣarāgeṇa caike nyāyamativartante, tatra -- (tattveti) anutpannatatvajñānāmaprahīṇadovāṇāṃ tadarthaṃ dhaṭamānānāmetat' iti vyākyātam | nyāyaṃ - sadvicāraniyamam tadarthaṃ - tattavajñānārtham | etat-jalpo vitaṇḍaśceti dvayam | anyat sugamam | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- vāde tattvavicārasabhāyāṃ vā, matāntare kṛtādaraiḥ prayuktakutarkanirasanena svapakṣasaṃrakṣaṇaṃ jalpavitaṇḍayoḥ phalamiti | anena sūtrabhāṣyeṇa `gautamīyasyādvaitāṅgateti, sarvatantrāṇāmevaṃvidhatvam (advaitāhgatvam) sūcitam' iti cārthalābhaḥ kathamiti vimṛśantu sudhiyaḥ | na ca advaitaśāstravidbhiḥ svasamayaparipālanāya jalpavitaṇḍayorāśrayaṇena asya advaitāṅgatvamiti tātparyamiti vācyam | sarvatantrajñairapi svasvasamayarakṣaṇāya jalpanitaṇḍayorāśrayaṭaṇātsarvatantrāṅgatvasidadhyā nāgeśamanorathā'siddheḥ | ayaṃ hi tasya manorathaḥ advaitaśāstramekameva pradhānam, itaratsarvaṃ tadaṅgatayā'pradhānamiti | aṃśena upakāre visiṣṭasya śāstrasya sarveṣāṃ ca tantrāṇāmaṅgatākalpanaṃ kathaṃ yuktaṃ bhavet, upakāramātreṇa aprādhānyaṃ kathaṃ sidhyet ṣa| na hi loke upakurvati purūṣe aprādhānyaṃ kalpayanti prājñāḥ | aśenopakaramātreṇāṅgatāsiddhau advaitaśāstramapi aṃśenā'nyoopakārakaṃ bhavatīti śakyaṃ vaktumiti tadapyan‌yasyāṅgaṃ bhavediti tadiṣiṭaṭāsiddhiḥ |
kiṃ ca, `nanvevaṃ tena siddhamidaṃ śāstraṃ vyartham' iti `tattvādhyavasya' iti sūtravataraṇam, tanmatakhaṇḍanatātparyakamidaṃ śāstram' iti sāstraprayojanakathanaṃ cāyuktam | `tattvādyavasāya' iti sūtrasya śāstraprayojanakathanaṃ cāyuktam | `tattvādhyavasāya' iti sūtrasya śāstrayojanakathanaṃ cāyuktam | `tattvādhyavasāya' iti sūtrasya śāstraprayojananirūpaṇāyā'pravṛtteḥ | bhāṣyakṛdādibhiḥ tatā'vyākhyānāt, pratyuta prathamasūtrabhāṣye ca `tadavasāno tattvajñānaphalo vādaḥ, tatpālavanārthaṃ jalpavitaṇḍe, tadviśeṣau jalpavitaṇḍe tattvādhyavasāyasaṃrakṣaṇārthamityuktam' iti jalpavitaṇḍāprayojanaparatayaiva vyākhyātatvācca |
kiṃ ca, `tadartham' iti sūtre yogapadaṃ vedāntānāmapyupalakṣaṇamiti na suktam, vedāntatvena sarvābhimatāyāṃ vaiyāsikyāṃ caturlakṣaṇyāṃ yamaniyamasvarūpānurūpaṇāt | yadyapi mañjūṣātaḥ nāgeśasyābhimatamadvaitavedāntaśāstraṃ paramārthasārākhyamiti sidhyati, tathāpi tatrāpi na yamanipamādisvarūpaṇaṃ pasyāmaḥ | ato'phalamupalakṣaṇaṃ kathanam | nanu, ātmajñānaṃ vedāntādeva bhavatīti tadapyadhyeyamiti, bodhanamupalakṣaṇaphalamiti cet, ucyate--yamaniyamādivat ātmavidyādhyayanamapi tattvādhyavasāyāhgamiti sūtrakṛdeva `jñānagrahaṇa-' iti sūtreṇāha | tatra, jñānapadasyātmavidyetyarthaḥ | evaṃ ca, tadupalakṣaṇatvokterna prayojanam | ayamatra naiyāyikasamayaḥ -- ānvikṣikyeva ātmavidyā' iti bhāṣyokteḥ, ātmavidyetipratīkamādāya ānvīkṣikīvidyaivātmavidyeti vyākhyānāt | `pramāṇaprameya-' iti pratadamasūtre `ihādhyātmavidyāyāmātmajñānaṃ tattvajñānam' iti vātsyāyanena iheti bhāṣitatvāt, sūtrakṛtā `tattvajñānānniḥśreyasādhigamaḥ' iti prathamasūtraśeṣeṇa svīyatantrasya ātmatattvajñānatatsādhyamokṣobhayaphalatvābhidhānācca | tasyā eva adhyayanadhāraṇasatatacintanāni kāryāṇi taiśca, anyaiścoktāṅgaiḥ ātmādhyavasāyo bhavati tena cāpavargaḥ | evaṃ ca, pradhānameva gautamīya tantraṃ mokṣasādhanaṃ nānyasyāṅgaṃ bhavitumarhatīti | evameva sarvatantrakṛtsiddhānto'pīti na sarveṣāmadvaitāṅkatā |
etena, `upaniṣadeva mukhyādhyātmavidyā mukhyaṃ tattvajñānamadhyā tmavidyopaniṣaduktameva anyattu tadupāya iti spaṣṭamevoktam' iti uparitano'pi ma़jūṣāgranthaḥ cintyaḥ | īśvarasattve tasya jagatkartṛtve ca prasiddhāṃ `sadeva somya' (chā.6) `yato vā' (tai.u.3) ityādiśrutīḥ parityajya anumānasyaiva naiyāyikaiḥ śaraṇīkaraṇena teṣāmānvīkṣikyāmeva adhyātmavidyātvādyavasāyo, nopaniṣadi, ityavagamena, tajjhanye ca jñāne teṣāṃ mokṣasādhanatvaniścayābhāvānumānāt | aupaniṣadadarśanaṃ taistyaktamityabhiyuktoktesca | uktaṃ ca śāṅkare gītābhāṣye - `aupaniṣadaṃ darśanaṃ tyaktaṃ vaiśeṣikaiḥ, sāṃkhyārhatabauddhaiśca' iti sūtrabhāṣye ca samudāyādhikaraṇe (2.2.4) vaiśeṣikarāddhānto duryuktiyogāt vedavirūddatvāt śiṣṭā'parigrahācca nāpekṣitavya ityuktam, so'rdhavaināśika iti vaināsikatvasāmyāt sarvavaināśikarāddhānto natarāmapekṣitavya' iti | atra vaiśeṣikapadaṃ gautamasyāpyupalakṣaṇamubhayoratraviṣaye ekāmiprāyāt | ata eva `mahaddīrghādhikaraṇaṃ' (2.2.2) vaiśeṣikanaiyāyikamataṃ nirākareti--`maha' dityavatārya sureśvarācārya ubhayasamayanirākaraṇaparatayā yojayati sma |
kiṃ ca,
pradīpaḥ sarvavidyānāmupāyaḥ sarvakarmaṇām |
āśrayaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ vidyoddeśe prakīrtitaḥ ||
iti pramāṇopanyāsena sarvavidyopakārakatvamuktaṃ gautamīyanyāyabhāṣye, `ānvīkṣikī trayī vārtā daṇḍanītiścaturthikā' iti pramāṇodāharaṇena trayyāṃ sarvavidya antarbhāvya trayyādisarvavidyopakārakatvamānvīkṣikyā vyākyātṛbhirvyavasthāpitam | ata adhyātmavidyāmātropakārakatvāsiddhiriti `tadupāya' iti cintyam, tasyaivopāya iti hi tadāśayaḥ | kiṃ ca, pramāṇe ānvīkṣikyeva prakṛteti anyaditi sāmānyoktiścintyā, ānvīkṣikyatiriktasya pramāṇe'śravaṇāt nyāyabhāṣyādāvanikteśca | nanu, nyāyamañjaryāṃ mokṣasvarūpanirūpaṇāvasare `ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ' (bṛ.u) iti vedāntoktadiśā ātmopāsanena svarūpāvasthānamokṣasiddhirityukternyāyatantraṃ vedāntāṅgamiti siddhyā svatantramidamiti kathamupapadyata iti cet, satyam -- `vaiśeṣikairopaniṣadaṃ darśanaṃ tyaktam' iti śāṅkarabhāṣyoktyā prāptamavaidikatvadūṣaṇadūrūkaraṇāya tathoktiḥ jayantasya | astu vā upaniṣadaṅgatvam, tāvatā nādvaitaśāstrāṅgatvasiddhiḥ, upaniṣadāmadvaitaparatvābhāvāt | jayantasyāpi sarvaṃ śāstraṃ dvaitaparameveti abhiprāyāt advaitāṅgatāyāmeva vivādaḥ | kiṃ ca, yeṣu phalaśravaṇaṃ teṣāṃ sarvatantrāṇāṃ svātantryeṇa mokṣaphalakatvameveti tattantrakṛtāmabhiprāyaḥ | ata eva śaṅkarācāryādināṃ tattanmatakhaṇḍane pravṛttiḥ saphalā bhavatīti dhyeyam |
kiṃ ca, nāgesāmatadvaitaśāstraṃ brajhopādānakāramam, ātmano nānātvābhāvaṃ bājhanāmarthānāmasattvaṃ ca pratipādayati | gautamīyādikaṃ tu tantraṃ īśvaraḥ nimittakāraṇam, ātmano bhedaṃ bājho'rthaḥ sanniti hodhayatīti paramaprameye parasparamasti mahān visaṃvādaḥ | `tattavamasi, brajhavit brajhaiva bhavati' ityādyaikyaparaśrutivākyānāṃ sāmye paryavasānamiti tadanuyāyino muktāvalīkārādayaḥ | tataśca atyantaṃ virūddhayoḥ śāstrayoraṅgabhāvatvaṃ kaḥ prekṣāvān manyeta ? ko vā naiyāyikaḥ samarthayeta ? ataḥ gautamiyāditantrasya advaitaśāstrāhghatvaṃ tadbhāṣyata eva siddhamityetat upekṣyameva | sarveṣāṃ tantrāṇāmadvaitāṅgatvaṃ vayaṃ manmaha iti yadi vadet tadāyaṃ dhīmān ślāghanīyaprajño bhavediti saṃkṣapaḥ |
nāgeśābhimatādvaitaśāstrasyā''ste virodhanam |
gautamīyāditantraistu tasmānnaiṣāṃ tadaṅgatā ||
ātmaikye bhāṣyasaṃmatinirāsaḥ
nanu `kataṃ dvāvātmānau antasātmā śarīrātmā' iti `ṇeraṇo yatkarma ṇau cetsakartā'nādhyāne (pā.sū.1.3.67) karmavatkarmaṇā tulyakriyaḥ' (pā.sū.3.1.57) iti sūtradvayabhāṣye'ntaḥkaraṇādyupādhibhedena ātmadvaividhyaṃ vadatoo bhagavataḥ patañjalestadaikyamanumatamiti nirṇiyata iti cet, na tatsūtradvayabhāṣyasya ātmaikatvapratipādanāyāpravṛtteḥ | tathā hi -- tatrādye `hantyātmānam' ityaṇyantāvasthāyāṃ karmabhūtasyātmanaḥ, `ghātayatyātmā' iti ṇyantāvastāyāṃ kartṛtvāt | anenātmanepadaṃ syāditi codye `ātmanaḥ karmatve pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ' iti vacanaṃ pravṛttam | aṇau karmaṇa ātmano ṇaukartṛtve'pi nānenātmanepadamiti tadarthaḥ | nedaṃ niṣedhavacanaṃ kartavyaṃ prathamakakṣyāyāṃ karmaṇo dvitīyakakṣyāyāṃ kartṛtvābhāvādityabhipretyāha--`na vā ṇyantasya kartṛtvāt' iti | tadarthamāha -- anyadatrā'ṇyante karma anyo ṇyantasya kartā, katham ? `dvāvātmānau' ityādi | bhāṣyārthaḥ spaṣṭaḥ | ayaṃbhāvaḥ--antarātmā īśvaro'ntaryāmi, śarīrātmā jīvaḥ | patañjasimate īśvaraḥ puraṣa iti cetanadvayamastīti yogasūtrato lābhāt | idamantarātmanaḥ karma yat purūṣasya (jāvasya) tattatpuṇyapāpānusāreṇa tattaducitavyāpāre pravartanam | tena svakarmānusārātpuruṣaḥ sukhaduḥkhe anubhavati, yadā puṇya karma karoti jīvaḥ, tadā antarātmā tadanuguṇaphalapradānāyajāgarti | tadeva tasya sukham | yadāt pāpakarma karoti jīvaḥ, tadā antarātmā tasmai drujhati | tadeva tasya duḥthamiti | "antarātmā antaḥkaraṇam | śarīrātmā tu śarīrameva `ātmā yatno dhṛtirbuddhiḥ svabhāvo brajhavarṣma ca' iti kosāt" iti vyākhyānaṃ tu śarīrāntaḥkaraṇayorubhayorapi jaḍatvāt bhoktṛtvāyogena, `sukhaduḥkhe anubhavati' iti bhaṣyānanuguṇam |
kiṃ ca, bhāṣye śarīramiti vaktavye ātmeti padavaiyarthyaṃ ca | sāṃkhyamate'pi `purūṣo'sti bhoktṛbhāvātkaivalyārtaṃ pravṛttesca' (sāṃ.kā.17) iti purūṣasyaiva bhoktṛtvābhyupagamāt |
kāryakāraṇakartṛtve hetuḥ prakṛtirūcyat |
purūṣaḥ sukhaduḥkhānāṃ bhoktṛtve heturucyate || (gī.13.20)
iti gītāyāṃ puruṣasyaiva bhoktṛtvādhānāt | `sukhādibhyaḥ kartṛvedanāyāṃ' (pā.sū.3.1.18) iti sūtreṇa svāśrayasukhādiviṣayakavedanāyām (anubhave) sukhādibhyaḥ kyaṅ vidadhatā pāṇininā susānubhavayoorātmaniṣṭhatvabodhanācca | padavedanā anubhava iti spaṣṭaṃ mañjaryām | sukhayate svasamavetaṃ sukaṃ svayamanubhavatītyarthaḥ | etena aṅgasauṣṭhavaṃ tadvaikalyaṃ ca śarīrātmanaḥ sukhaduḥkhānubhava iti pratyakṣasiddhatvāt, tayoranubhavasya cetanadharmatvena śarīrasya tadayogācca | `hanti ātmānam' ityasya purūṣaḥ anyāyācaraṇena ātmānaṃ nāśayati ityartaḥ ghātayatyātmetyasya antarātmā īśvaraḥ ātmānaṃ puruṣaṃ ghātayati, tadīyaduṣkarmamā ado nayatītyarthaḥ | `karmavatkarmaṇā' iti sūtrasthabhāṣyasyāpi evamevārtha iti nātamaikatve tātparyam | bhāṣyasya ekasyatmana aupādhikabhedatātparyakalpanāmātreṇa jīvaparamātmanorbhedo nāstīti anena bhāṣyeṇa kathaṃ labhyeta iti vibhāvyatam |
yadapi ma़ñjaṣāyāṃ " sanvidhisūtrabhāṣye sarvasya cetanāvattvaṃ `śṛṇota grāvaṇa' ityathavādabalenoktam, īśvaro'pi śrutyādinā jagatkāraṇatayā antaryāmitayā ca siddha iti bodānteṣu prasiddhaṃ cetanasyaikyameva lāghavāt" ityādinā vṛttiparicchede ātmaikatve bhāṣyāditātparyanirūpaṇam, tadapi tatsūtrasthabhāṣyavārtikatātparyamācchādyaivajñānam, aktākāraṇatvādisaṃbandena īśvarasya sarvatra tattvena nimittena sarvasya cetanāvattvajñānam, nāgeśasyaiva maharṣerapi kātyāyanasya patañjaleścāstīti vaktuṃ śakyam | yataste'pi vedāntaśāstrārthaniśrayavantaḥ, bahajñāḥ, sākṣātkṛtaparāparā, avatārapurūṣāḥ | tatra vararucinā yadacetaneṣbityuktaṃ tat jñāpayati `īśvaracetanena sarvasya cetanāvattvavyavahāra' iti | etena śāstre'pi tena sarvasya tathā vyavahāro netyevetyadhyavasātavyam | evaṃ ca, īśvarātiriktacetanasattva eva cetanāvattvavyavahāraḥ kūlādiṣu ca īsvarānyacetanābhāvāt icchāyā abhāvena sano'prāptau savarthaṃ taduktiḥ sārthikā | evaṃ ca cetanaikyaṃ na tatsaṃmatam | bhāṣkārasya tu īśvarādanyā asti kācidamimānidevatā nāma cetanā | tāmādāya kūlādau cetanāvattvamiti icchāyāṃ sūtreṇaiva sanniti tātparyam |
idaṃ tu bodhyam--bhāṣyamidaṃ brajhavidāṃ matamāsthāya pravṛttam, vārtikaṃ tu brajhavitsiddhāntasya sarvairaparijñānāt tadanāśritya | idaṃ ta teṣāṃ matam `āpo'bravan, mṛdabravīt, āpa aikṣanta' ityādiśratirapramāṇam acetanānāmavādīnāṃ vacanādikartṛtvāyogāt | loke'cetanaghaṭapaṭādīnāṃ vacanādyanupalambhāditi śahkāyām, naivaṃ mantavyam | asti tāvatsarvatra kācanā'bhimānidevatā | tāmādāya vedapuruṣa evaṃ vyavaharati | śrutau āpa ityādīnāṃ tadabhimānidevatā ityarthaḥ | siddhāntitaṃ caivameva bhagavatā vyosena `abhimānivyapadeśastu viśeṣānugatibhyām' (bra.sū.2.1.5) iti sūtreṇa | `mṛdabravīdityādau mṛdādyabhimānidevatānāṃ vacanādikartṛvyavahāraḥ, sarvatrābhimānidevatāsadbhāvasya ca mantrārthavādetihāsādibhiḥ siddhatvāditi sūtrārthaḥ | īśvaracetanena sarvasya cetanatāyāṃ nedamupapadyeta | evaṃ ca īśvarādanyā amimānidevatānāma kācanāstīti īśvaraikacetanavādo brajhavitsiddhāntaviruddhaḥ | sansūtre ca bhāṣye kaṇṭhata īśvaracetanena cetanāvattvādityanuktyā abhimānidevatayā cetanāttvādityevārthaḥ sanasūtrabhāṣyasya, brajhavitsiddhāntatyāge kāraṇabhāvāt | `dāmahāyanāntācca' (pā.sū.4.1.1.7) iti sūtre utkramaṇaṃ vā apakramaṇaṃ vā, dāma ca acetanam, acetaneṣvapi cetanavadupacāro dṛśyate, strastānyasya bavdhanāni, strasyante cāsya bandanāni' iti gacchatīti lokavyavahākānusāreṇeti bodhyam | abādhitalokavyavahārasya śāstre'pi āśrayaṇamaviruddhamiti sūcanāya lokavyavahārānusāreṇaiva tatropapattiḥ kṛtā | tatra bhāṣye pūrvapakṣavākyagatacetanāvatpadaṃ karmārabdhadehāvacchinnaprāṇiparam | nāgeśastu brajhavitsiddāntaviruddhabhāṣī vijñānavādapakṣapātīti purastānnirūpitam | atastaduktiraviśvasanīyā | kiṃ ca, asya cetanaikye tātparyavarṇanaṃ `mahatā devena naḥ sāmyaṃ yathā syādityadhyeyaṃ vyākaramam' iti `catvāri śṛṅgā' iti mantratātparyavarṇanaparapaspaśāhrikabhāṣyavirūddham, tatra sāmyasyaiva phalatvenokteḥ |
`devatādvandve ca' (pā.sū.6.3.26) iteyadhikāre `divo dyāvā', `divasaśca pṛthivyām', `uṣāsoṣasaḥ' (pā.sū.6.3.29,30,31))' iti trisūtrīṃ kurvan pāṇiniḥ dyulokādyabhimānidevatā'stīti abhipraiti | na ca, trisūtryāṃ `devatādvandva' ityasya nānuvṛttiriti vācyam, sūtratraye'pi kaumudyāṃ deva-tādvandva ityukteḥ,ādyasūtrodāharaṇe dyāvākṣāme ityatra `devatādvandve ca' (pā.sū.6.2.141) iti pūrvottarapadayoḥ prakṛtisvara iti vedabhāṣyokteḥ, `devatādvandve ca' ityataḥ pūrvaṃmasyā akaraṇācca tatra tadanuvṛttisiddheḥ | `nottarapadeṣa़nudāttādāvapṛthivīrudrapūṣamantiṣu' (pā.sū.6.2.142) iti sūtre pṛthivīparyudāsaḥ, `dyāvāpṛthivī janayan' iti udāharaṇaṃ ca uktārthe mānam | yadyabhimānidevatā na syāt tadā `devatādvandve ca' iti pūrvottarapadaprakṛtisvarasyāprasaktyā niṣedhāpravṛtteḥ paryudāse pṛthivīgrahaṇavaiyarthyam | udāharaṇe dyāvādeśāpravṛttiśca | yadi īśvaracetanena devatādvandvatvamiti āśayaḥ tarbi `īśvaradvandva' ityeva vadet | janayanniti ca abhimanyamānavastvapekṣayeti mantavyam | etena vityatvāt jananāsaṃbhavena evamuktirayukteti pratyuktam |
kiṃ ca, chāndogye ṣaṣṭhe pitāputrasaṃvāde sṛṣṭinirūpaṇaprakaraṇe `tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti tattejo'sṛjata, tatteja aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajayeya iti tadaposṛjata, tā āpa aikṣanta bahvyaḥ syāma prajāyemahīti tā annamasṛjanta' (chā.6.2) iti, tejaḥprabhṛtīnāmīkṣāpūrvakartṛtvavyapadeśaḥ, `hantāhamimāstistro devatā anena jīvenātmanā'nupravisya' (chā.6.3) iti teṣāṃ devatātvena vyavahāraḥ, `yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ taṣṭhan,ka ya ātmani tiṣṭhan' (bṛ.5) ityādinā pṛthivyādīnāṃ bhagavanniyamyatvoktiḥ, `yaṃ pṛthivī na veda' ityādinā teṣāmantaryāmibhagavatsvarūpasya avedanoktiśca tejapadāvācyāstatadabhimānidevatā īśvarādanyā|santīti bodhayantī, cetananānātvaṃ ca gamayati | tejaḥ prabhṛtīnāṃ jaḍānāmīkṣaṇādivyāpārādyasaṃbhava iti sarvalokaviditam |
`pṛthivyādyabhimāndevatā niyamayati nārāyaṇaḥ' iti sāṅkaravājasaneyabhāṣyam | jyotirādiviṣayā api ādityādayo devatāvacanāḥ śabdāḥ cetanāvantamaiśvaryādyupetaṃ taṃ devatātmānaṃ samarpayanti, mantrārthavādādiṣu vathāvāyavahārāt | mṛdādiṣvapi cetanā adhiṣṭhātāro'bhyupagamyante | `mṛdabravīt' iti `bhāvaṃ tu' (1.3.33) iti sūtraśāṃkarabhāṣyaṃ cātrarthe'nukūlam |
nanu `ātmānamātmanā hanti' ityādau parayā kartṛsajñayā karmasaṃjñāyā bādhamāśahvya `ahaṃkārādiviśiṣṭātmabhedamāśritya samāhitaṃ bhāṣye' iti `sādhakatamaṃ kataṇam' (pā.sū.1.4.42) iti sūtrīyamanoramagranthena evaṃjātīyakasubarthaprakaramasthamañjūṣāgranthena ca ātmokatvaṃ bhāṣyasaṃmatamityavagamyata iti cet, ucyate--ekasya devadattāderātmana ekasyāṃ kriyāyāṃ yugapatkartṛtvakarmatvavyavahārānupapattiḥ sukalpeti bhāṣyāśayaḥ | ata eva ratnavyākhyāne bhairave `sati prāmāṇike prayoge sādhutvārthamiyaṃ kalpanāna tu evaṃ kalpanayā yatheṣṭaṃ prayoktuṃ śakyam' ityuktam | tataśca nedaṃ bhāṣyaṃ jīvātmaparamātmaikyasādhakaṃ, nāpi śāstrasiddhajīvaparabhedapratiṣedhakam | kiṃ ca evamānupūrvīkaṃ bhāṣyaṃ gaveṣaṇīyam |
kiṃ ca `manyakarmaṇyanādare vibhāṣā'prāṇiṣu (pā.sū.2.3.17) anuviparyābinbhyaḥ syandateraprāṇiṣu (pā.sū.8.3.72) aprāṇijāteścārajāvādīnām-' (vā) ityādau prāṇiparyudāsaḥ, `acittahastidhenoṣṭhak (pā.sū.4.1.47) cittavati ntyam' (pā.sū.5.1.89) ityādāvacittetyādikathanaṃ ca īśvaratattadabhimānidevatetarakarmārabdhadehavatāṃ jīvānāṃ sadbhāvaṃ, jīvanānātvaṃ ca bodhayati | īsvareṇa, amimānidevatayā vā sarvasya cetanatve tu sarveṣāmapi prāṇitvena cittavattvena ca vyāvartyābāvena paryudāsādervaiyarthyaṃ spaṣṭam | ata eva ca--`stāne'ntaratamaḥ' 9pā.sū.1.1.50) iti sūtre, bhāṣye loṣṭhādīnāṃ, varṇānāṃ cā'cetanatvoktiḥ saṃgacchate ātmanānātvaṃ munitrayasaṃmatamiti jñeyam |
`dvā suparṇā' (ā.u.) `sthityadanābhyāṃ ca' 9bra.sū.1.3.7) iti śrutisutrābhyāmekasmin śarīre jīveśayoḥ sahāvasthānoktiśca cetananānātve mānam | na ca aupādhikabhedamādāya tatra tathoktamiti vācyam, śrutisūtrayoraupādhikabhedaparatve gamakādarśanāt | tasyā'gatikagatitvāt, śuddhasya brajhaṇa upādhisaṃbandhasya durvacatvācca | tataśca īśvareṇa cetanena sarvasya cetanāvattvamityarthamupavarṇya ātmaikatve bhāṣyatātparyavarṇanaṃ sarvapramāṇaviruddham, abādarāyaṇīyam, apāṇinīyaṃ ceti tattvamiti |
ṇeraṇau karmavatsūtrabhāṣyānna brajhajīvayoḥ |
abhidāsidhyatītyetat sapramāṇaṃ prapañcitam ||
sarvasya cetanāvattvaṃ na bhavedīśvareṇa tu |
iti śrutyādimānena niscinvanti vipaścitaḥ ||
sṛṣṭericcāpūrvakatvanirūpaṇam
yaduktaṃ--`(īśvarasya) sisṛkṣābhāve'pi bhagavato māyāpuṣau prādurbhavataḥ ' iti sphoṭavādārambhe, tadapi na yuktam, udāhṛtachāndogye `tanmano'kuruta ātmanvīsyām (bṛ.3.2.1) so'kāmayata dvitīyo me ātmā jāyeta (bṛ.3.2.4) taparā cīyate brajha tato'nnamabhijāyate (ā.1.8) sa īkṣāṃcakre kasminnvahamutkrānte utkrānto bhaviṣyāmi kasminvā pratiṣṭhite pratiṣṭhāsyāmi sa prāṇamasṛjata tasmai sa hāvāca prajākāmo vai prajāpatiḥ sa sato'tapyata sa tapastaptvā sa mithunamudapādayate (ṣa.pra.1.4) so'kāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti' (tai.u.2) ityādau ca sṛṣṭerīkṣāpūrvakatvaśravaṇāt |
māyāsṛṣṭīḥ sisṛkṣāyā abhāve'piti bhāṣaṇam |
cintyaṃ sṛṣṭeḥ śrutau dṛṣṭipūrvatvaśravaṇādvibhoḥ ||
carakādisaṃmatinirāsaḥ
yadatra mañjūṣāyāṃ caturthaparicchedi taddhitavṛttinirūpaṇe ātmanānātvābhāve `dehī sarvagato jhātmā vibhutvamata evalāsya, yasmātsarvagato mahāna, nityānubavdhamanasā dehakarmānpātinā | sarvayonigataṃ nidyādekayonāvapi sthitam' iti pātaṃjalacarakavacanopanyasanaṃ tadapi tasya na sādhakam | tatra ādyavākyaṃ jagatkāraṇasyāntaryāmiṇaḥ `antarbahiśca tatsarvaṃ vyāpya nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ' (tai.ā.4) ityādi śrutyuktadiśā sarvavyāpitvapratipādakam | dvitīyaṃ tu -- ekasminneva śarīre jīvo vartata iti na, kiṃ tu anekadehāraṃbhakakarmānusāriṇā manasā aneraśarīrasaṃbaddho bhavatītyarthakam |
uddharedātmanātmānaṃ nātmānamavasādayet |
ātmaiva jhātmano bandhurātmaiva ripirātmanaḥ || (gī.6.5)
mana eva manuṣyāṇāṃ karaṇaṃ bandhamokṣayoḥ |
uddharenmanasā jīvaṃ na jīvamasādayet ||
ityukteḥ karmaśāt jīvo nānāyoniṣu jāyata iti tātparyam | atastenāpi jīvaparayorabhedāsiddhyā na tasyeṣṭasiddhiḥ | ekayonau sthitamapi karmavaśāt sarvayonigataṃ vidyāditi yojanā | atrārtha eva vacanasya tātparyaṃ `dehakarmānupātinā' iti vākyasvārasyāt, na tu ātmaikatve, īśvarasya ca dehārambhakakarmābhāvāt |
yoge patañjalinā `kṛtārthapratinaṣṭam' (yo.sū.2.22) ityādinā ātmānānātvasya jīvaparayośca bhedasya ca siddhāntitatvena asyāpi patañjalivākyasya tadavirodhenāryavarṇanasya yuktatvāt | na ca carakānurodhena yogasyā'rthavarṇanaṃ yuktamiti vācyam, yogasya sākṣāt mokṣasādhanopāyabodhakatvena, mukyatvena carakasya aihikasukhasādhanaparatvena, amukhyatvena, amukhyasyaiva mukhyānurodhena yojanāyāyuktatvāt | uktadiśā carakasyā''tmaikatve tātparyābhāvāt | yogasya ca śrīśaṅkarācāryaprabhṛtibhiḥ ātadmaikatvābhāve tātparyasya sūtrabhāṣye nirdhāritatvāt |
yadapi paramārthasāre'pi patañjalirāha --`yadvaddinakara eko vibhāti salilāśayeṣu sarveṣu | tadvatsakalopādhiṣvavasthito bhāti paramātmā' iti caturthe vṛttivicāre ātmaikatve pramāṇodāharaṇaṃ, tadapi antaryāmipatayā netuṃ śakyam | kiṃ ca ātmanānātvapara `kṛtārtham' iti yogasūtraviruddham | sūtrārthastu anupadameva vakṣyate |
kiṃ ca `dvā suparṇā sayutā sakhāyau samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte | tayoranyaḥ pippasaṃ svādvatti anaśnannanyo'bhicākaśīti' (mu.3.1.1)
dvāvimau puruṣau loke kṣaraścākṣara eva ca |
kṣaraḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni kūṭastho'kṣara utyate || (gī.15.8)
ityādi paraḥsahastraśrutismṛtiviruddhamātmaikatvam | śruterayamarthaḥ - dvaudvitvaviśiṣṭau bhinnāviti yāvat; suparṇau - suparṇasarūpau, suparṇo garuḍaḥ, sayujā - saṃyuktau, sakhāyau -- upakāryopakartṛbhāvena sakhitvamubhayoḥ, upakāryo jīvaḥ, upakartā hariḥ, samānaṃ vṛkṣam - dehākhyamekaṃ vṛkṣam, pariṣasvajāte - āliliṅgatuḥ tatra vartete iti yāvat; pippalam - dehākhyāśvatthajanyaphalaṃ karmaphalaṃiti yāvat; anyaḥ-jīvaḥ, anyaḥ - īśaḥ, anaśnan - abhuñjānaḥ, neśvarasya karmaphalabhojanamiti bhāvaḥ, abhi - sarvataḥ, cākaśīti - prakāśate | anyatsugamam | śaṅkarācāryāstu -- suparṇau - suśobhanapatanau, pakṣisāmānyādvā suparṇau, sakhāyau - samānakhyānau samānābhivyaktikāraṇau | raṅgaramānujamunistu-yujyata iti yuk guṇaḥ, apahatapāpmatvādiguṇaiḥ, samānaguṇau, saśāyau - sahacarau, śiṣṭaṃ samānam, iti | śrutireṣā bhedaparaiveti sarvairvyākhyātā | gītārthastu pūrvamukta- | na ca paramārtasāravacanavirodāt śrutigītādi upekṣyamiti vācyam, pauruṣeyavacanamāśritya śrutyādyupekṣāyā ayogāt |śrutismṛtiviruddhārthakapauruṣeyavacanā'nādaraṇameva vaidikānāṃ bhūṣaṇam | sarvamidaṃ carakaparamārthasārayoḥ patañjalikṛtatvābhiprāyamanurudhyoktam |
vastutastu tāvubhāvapi granthau na mahābhāṣyakṛtā maharṣipatañjalinā praṇītau, ataḥ `carake patañjaliḥ' ityādi tatpadollekhena carakaparamārthasāravacanopanyasanamayuktam | `pātañjalamahābhāṣyacatakapratisaṃskṛtaiḥ' ityādinā cakradattādibhiḥ carakavyākyānārambhe carakākhyavaidyatagkanthasya carakaṛṣisaṃskṛtatvokteḥ, vaidyakagranteṣu kvāpi kenacidapi patañjalikagṛtatvasya vā, tatsaṃskṛtatvasya vā'nukteḥ, āyurvedavidāṃ gauṣṭhīṣu patañjalikṛtatvavyavahārasyaivābhāvāt; bhāvaprakāśakṛtā vaidyasārvabhaumena āyurvedavidācāryaparaṃparāṃ nirūpayatā tatra patañjaleraparigaṇanāt, patañjalicarite rāmabhadrīye `sūtrāṇi yogaśāstre vaidyakaśālastre ca vārtikāni tataḥ' iti vaidyake vāktikākhyasya kasyacidgranthasyaiva patañjalikṛtatvokteḥ,
yogena citasya padena vācāṃ malaṃ śarīrasya ca vaidyakena |
yo'pākarottaṃ pravaraṃ munināṃ patañjaliṃ prāñjalirānato'smi ||
iti padye granthanāmno'nirdeśācca carakādanyaḥ kaścana vaidyako granthaḥ patañjalinā kṛtaḥ, paramārtasārastu cakradattādibhiḥ, dhārānagarādhipatinā bhojabhūpatinavā ca parigaṇiteṣu ahipatipraṇīteṣu prabandeṣu, patañjalicarite `yogena' iti sāṃpradāyike ca padye, parigaṇitapata़jalipraṇītagrantheṣu ca aparigaṇitatvena ahipatinā patañjalinā vā na kṛta iti vimarśakā vadanti |
nanu caraka iti, śeṣanāga iti ca, patañjalereva nāmāntaraṃ tataḥ tāvubhāvapi granthau patañjalikṛtāveveti nirdhārayāma iti cet, ucyate -- yadyevaṃ tarhi maharṣipatañjalermahābhāṣyakṛtaḥ śrīśaṅkarācāryaprabhṛtibhyaḥ pratnatayā, rāmakṛṣṇakṛta sāṃkhyakārikagāvat paramārthasāravatacanamapi patañjalikṛtatvena ārṣatvādatyupādeyatābuddhyā svasamīhitā'dvaitopaṣṭambhakatvena anuvadeyuḥ | ubhayathāpi śāṅkaragkanthādiṣūdāharaṇādarśanena śeṣanāgaḥ kaścidarvācīno vipaścit nāgeśabhaṭṭātprācīnaḥ na tu nāmāntaraṃ patañjaleḥ, na tatkṛtatvaṃ carakasārayoriti manyante iti |
syādetat, gītāyāṃ bhagavān vāsudeva `udārāḥ sarva evaite jñānā tvātmainava me matam' (7.18) ityatra ātmaiveti sāvadhāraṇamātmatvabodhanāt paramātmatādātmyameva śā3strasaṃmatamiti yuktamiti, ucyate -- adhikāriṇo jīvasya brajhatādātmyasya śrutiviruddhatvāt, gītopakramopasaṃhāraviruddhatvāt, bpajhamīmāṃsāvirūddhatvāt gītāyāḥ sādṛśyāvadhāraṇatātparyakatvameva yuktam | yadvā `nipātānāmanekārthatvam' ityabhiyuktokteḥ, `tato bhūya iva te' (ī.9.12) iti mantrārthavarṇanāvasare śrīśaṅkarācāryaiḥ `bhūya iva' ityasya `bhūya eva' ityarthavarṇanāt ivaivaśabdayoḥ paryāyatvāvagamena atra evaśabda īvārthaka iti naikye gītāyāstātparyam, adhikaṃ `vāsudeva' iti ślokārthanirūpaṇānalare nirūpitamiti |
phaṇipatirakaroccarakaṃ granthaṃ paramārthasāranāmānam |
iti na brūte kaścit vaidyakavidvacchrutijñagoṣṭhīṣu ||
atastadanusāreṇa kṛtaṃ śāstrārthanirṇayam |
nādriyante vido vedasmṛtimārgānuyāyinaḥ ||
brajhaṇa āvidyakavanānātvanirāsaḥ
yadapi mañjūṣāyāṃ subarthavicāre kartṛtvanipaṇaprakaraṇe `yadvā brajhaṇa eva avidyāvaśādupacaritanānātvabhāvābhāvarūpatvatat tadrūpajanmanāśavyavahāro'pi avicāritaramaṇīya iti bodhyam' ityanena brajhaṇa upacaritanānātvādirathanam, tadapi āpātaramaṇīyāṃ kasyaciduktimavalambyoktam, tadapi āpatarahamaṇīyāṃ kasyaciduktimavalambyoktam, śāstre kvāpi bhagavato'vidyāvaśatvasya tannibandhananānātvasya cānukteḥ | ata ucchāstramidaṃ svotprekṣitamunmattavacanamivāviśvasanīyam | etena mañjūṣāyāṃ prathamaparicchede `tattadākādivisiṣṭabrajhaṇa eva ghaṭāditvānukteḥ, tathātve vikāritvaprasakteśca | nana ghaṭādiviśiṣṭatvena tattvavyavahāraḥ na tu tadrūpatveneti cet, evaṃ cet brajhātiriktaprapañcābhāvasādhanāya pravṛttena tena kiṃsādhitaṃ ? na kimapi | tādṛśo gauṇavyavahāraḥ sarveṣāmiṣṭa eveti jitaṃ brajhātiriktaprapañcasatvaṃ tayorbhedaṃ ca vadadbhiḥ | rāmakṛṣṇādirūpeṇa śāstreṣu vāraṃ vāramupadeṣṭaṃ bhagavaco nānātvaṃ ca vāstavameva | ata upacaritatvaṃ na kadāpi mantavyaṃ gamakābhāvāt |
idamatra bodhyam--yathā nāgesaḥ `mahābhāṣyavirodāt vedabhāṣyamupekṣyam; vede sarvadā ānupūrvo ekaiva na kenāpi kṛteti navyamīmāṃsakaśraddhājaḍyaṃ tu tadarthakasya tadīyārṣavacayo'nupalambhāt, mahābhāṣyakārādivacanavirodhāccānupādeyam' iti vedabhāṣyakārādīnāṃ vacanaṃ na visvasanīyam ityāha | tathā-pāṇinīyā api san sūtre ātmādvaitadarśanena iti kaiyaṭoktam, `avidyāvaśādbrajhaṇa upacaritanānātvam' iti mañjūṣoktam, advaitaparamañjūṣāvākyavyākhyāvasaraṃ `paramasiddhāntamāha' ityādiīpāyiguṇḍe vaidyanāthoktam, evaṃjātīyakaṃ dīkṣitādivaiyākaraṇektaṃ ca, `īsvareṇa cetanena sarvaṃ cetanāvat, avidyāvaśāt īśvarasya nānātvam' iti bādarāyaṇena sūtrakṛtā, tadīyasūtrabhāṣyakārairbahubhirācāryaiḥ, patañjalinā ca `śṛṇota gravāṇa' ityādau siddhāntākaraṇāt tādṛśarthakapāṇinīyavacanānupalambhācca anupādeyamiti prativaktumarhanti nāgeśaṃ prati |
nanu vṛttipariccede--
sarvabhūteṣu yenaikaṃ bhāvamavyayamīkṣate |
avibhaktaṃ vibhakteṣu tajjñānaṃ viddhi sattvikam ||
pṛthaktvena tu yajjñānaṃ nānābhānān pṛthagvidhān |
vetti sarveṣu bhūteṣu tajjñānaṃ viddhi rājamam ||
yattu kṛtsnavadekasmin kārye saktamahaitukam |
atatvārthavadalpaṃ ca tattāmasamudāhṛtam || (gī.18.20-22)
iti gītāvacanānāṃ nāgeśabhaṭṭācāryaiḥ, `ātmādvaitaviṣayakaṃ jñānaṃ sāttvikamiti prathamavacanārthaḥ, etadevottaralsoradvayaṃ dpaḍha.yatīti ātmaikatvaparatayā vyākhyānāttasya nānātvamupacaritameveti vaktavyamiti cet, atra vadanti--yadyabhede tātparyamādyavacanasya tadā `sarvabhūteṣu' ityādivyadhikaraṇanirdeśāsaṃgatiḥ, `sarvabhūtāni yenaikaṃ jñānamavyayamīkṣate | avibhaktaṃ-bhedarahitam, ekaṃ bhāvaṃ-paramātmānamavyayamīkṣate, yena jñāneneti yojanayā vibhinneṣu bhūtābhidheṣu jīveṣu antaryāmitayā vartamānaṃ vastu ekameva, tacca vāsudevābhidhaṃ brajhavetyarthaḥ etena jīvānāṃ bhedaḥ, teṣu antaryāmī bhagavān eka eva antaryāmirūpāṇāṃ na bhedaḥ, jīvebhyastu bhedo'ntaryāmiṇa iti tattātparyam | īśvarasya antaryāmitvaṃ `sarvasya cāhaṃ hṛdi saṃniviṣṭaḥ', (gī.15.15) `iśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ hṛddeśe'rjuna tiṣṭhati' (gī.18.61)ityābhyāmuktam | tatratyākṣarasvārasyāllabhyamarthamapalāpya netre nimilya yathecchaṃ varmito'rthaḥ kathaṃ tattvavidāṃ hṛdayaṃgamaḥ syāditi vibhāvyatām | dvitīyasya tu--sarvabhūteṣu pṛthaktvena sthitaṃ bhagavantaṃ yat jñānaṃ tathā na vuṣayīkaroti, .yadvā sarvabhūṣu antaryāmirūpaṃ pṛthaktvena viṣayīkaroti, antaryāmirūpaṃ bhinnabhinnaṃ viṣayīkarotīti yāvat, nānābhāvān sarvapadārtān pṛthagvidhān vetti - viṣayīkaroti, tat jñānaṃ rājasamityarthaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-viṣṇvanyacetanaviṣayakajñānasya sāttvikatve'pi paramātmaviṣayajīvapratiyogikabhedajñānasya, antaryāmirūpāṇāṃ bhedajñānasya ca, sattve rājasatvameveti | tṛtīyasya tu jīve viṣṇutvajñānaṃ, samastajagatvajñānaṃ ca tāmasamityarthaḥ |
anye tu --brajhakṣatrādiṣu ātmatvajātirasti iti jñānaṃ sāttvikam | brajhaṇādiṣu tattadākārabhedena ātmākyabhāvānapi bhedana paśyatīti yat tajjñānaṃ rājasam, atyalpaphalaṃ pretabhūtādyārādhanarapmaṇi saktaṃ jñānaṃ tāmasamiti ślokatrayatātparyamāhuḥ ato naitāni ātmaikatve pramāṇāni bhavitumarhanti |
ekaseya berajhaṇo'vidyāvasānnānātvamiṣyate |
iti nāgeśavacanaṃ na yuktamiti cintitam ||
yato'vidyāvaśādbrajha bahubhāvaṃ samaśnute |
ityevaṃ pāṇinirnāha phaṇīrāḍapi nābravīt ||
pratibhāsamātraśarīratvanirāsaḥ
yaccoktaṃ `vijñānāntaryāmī' ityādiparamārthasāravacanamupanyasya `pratibhāsamātraviṣayaḥ māyāvāsanā'vidyāpadābhidheyabhramasaṃskāramūlakaḥ prapañca' (ma.1) iti tadapi cintyam | tasya prāmāṇikaiḥ pramāṇatvenā'parigaṇanāt, patañjalikṛtatve ca na niścaya iti pūrvamevopapāditatvāt, vyāsapāṇinipatañjalibhirevamanukteśca | śāstrake kvacijjagatkāraṇatvenoktā māyā tu pratṛtirīśvareccā ceti braṅavidaḥ prāṅuḥ | prāgudāhṛtacchāndogyaśrutyādau--
mama yonirmahadabrajha tasmin garbhaṃ dadhāmyaham |
saṃbhavaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ tato bhavati bhārata || (gī.14.2)
mahābhūtānyahaṅkāro buddhiravyaktameva ca |
indriyāṇi daśaikaṃ ca pañca cendriyagocarāḥ || (gī.13.1)
so'bhidhyāya śarīrātsvātsisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamavāsṛjat ||
tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahastrāśusamaprabham |
tasminjajñe svayaṃ brajhā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ || (ma.smṛ.1.8.9)
iti | `antastaddharmopadeśāt, saṃjñāmūrto' ti sūtrabhā,yodāhṛtasmṛtiṣu ca vāsanāparaparyāyamāyāriktapadārthaureva prapañjasṛṣṭerabhidānāt | sūtrakṛtā ca `saṃjñāmūrto' tadi sūtreṇa trivṛtkṛtateja ādīnāmeva jagatkāraṇatvokteśca | pratibhāsamātrasarīratve tatāvidhaśiktirajatāderiva prapañcasyārthakriyākāritvābhāvaprasaṃgācca | paramārtasāre'pi ghaṭapaṭādiprapañcasya pratibhāsamātraviṣayatbaṃ nābhyadhāyi |
<3>
na ca śuktirajatadṛṣṭāntadānena tasya tathātvaṃ tadabhimatamiti vācyam, taddṛṣṭāntapradarśanasya naśvaratvaṃ tātparyāt | `bhrānta' iti sārokterapi naśvaratva eva tātparyam | ata eva--`prakṛteridaṃ kṛtaṃ yena' iti prapañcasya kṛtiviṣayatvoktiḥ saṃgacchate sāre, na hi pratibhāsaśarīraṃ vastu kṛtiviṣayo bhavati, śuktirūpyādāvadarśanāt | naśvaratvaṃ tu dṛśyate kulālādikṛtiviṣaye ghaṭe | etaddṛṣṭānta eva nopapadyate ityanyatra vistaraḥ | tatra prakṛterityasya saṃskārātiriktaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ vastu ādāyetyarthaḥ | kṛtamityasya kṛtiviṣaya ityarthaḥ | svavibhūtyetyasya svīyaiśvaryayogena svasāmarthyeneti vārthaḥ | tena uktasaṃskārātiriktameva vastu jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ, jagacca na pratibhāsamātraviṣaya ityuktaṃ bhavati | bhramaviṣayo hi na kṛtiviṣaya iti sarvalokasākṣisiddham | śuktirajatādernirmāturadarśanāt | nāgośakṛtatadvyākhyānaṃ tu na tatratyākṣarānuguṇam, sāre prayuktamāyāpadasya ādyapadyoktaprakṛtiparatvamīśvarecchādiparatvaṃ vā | bhramasaṃskārarūpāvidyāyā āśrayajijñāsāyāṃ `paramātmaivāśrayastavātiriktāvidyāyā iva' iti pratibandyuttaradānena, sūtrabhāṣyakṛtorbhramasaṃskārakūpaivā'vidyeti mataṃ sūcitam iti nirūpaṇena ca anādibhāvarūpāvidyāṃ vyavasthāpayatāmadvaitācāryāṇāṃ śāṅkarasūtrabhāṣyādyabhiprāṃyā'nabhijñatāṃ dyotayati | nāgeśokteḥ miśrādyuktyavirodhasaṃpādanaṃ tu duḥśakam | na ca `iha devatādhikaraṇa' ityādimañjūṣokterna virodha iti vācyam, tathā saṃkoce kāraṇabhāvāt, anyatra miśrādibhirnāgeśoktarītyā māyāyā anupapādanāt | kiṃ ca prapañco yadi pratibhāsaśarīraḥ, tarhi jñānarūpaḥ kṣaṇikaśca syāditi hānopādānā'saṃbhavena sarvasokavoyavahārocchedaḥ | bhagavatā vyāsena ca vijñānavādaḥ, kṣaṇikavādaśca nirākṛto `nābhāva upalabdheḥ, kṣaṇikatvācca' (bra.sū.2.2.28.31) ityābhyām | ata eva abhiyuktā nānumanyante idaṃ vādadvayam |
bhāgavate ca--
sā tatra dadṛśe viśvaṃ jagatsthāstu ca śaṃ diśaḥ |
sādridvīpā'bdhibhūgolaṃ savāyvagrīndutārakam ||
jyotiścakraṃ jalaṃ nabhaḥ svarviyadaṃva ca |
vaikārikāṇīndriyaṇi mano mātrā guṇāstrayaḥ ||
etadvicitraṃ sahabījakālasvabhāvakarmāśayaliṅgabhedam |
sūnostanau vīkṣya vidāritāsye vrajaiḥ sahā''tmānamavāpa śaṅkām ||
(8.37-39)
iti daśame bhagavadudare carācarasamastaprapañco vartata ityukterna māyikaṃ jagaditi vadanti | na ca--uttaraśloke
kiṃ svanpa etaduta devamāyā kiṃ vā madīyo bata buddhibhedaḥ | (40)
iti yaśodāśaṅkānukāraṇāt bhrama eva tajjñānamiti vācyam |
atho amuṣyaiva mamārbhakasya
yaḥkaścanautpattika ātmayogaḥ || (41)
atho yathāvanna vitarkagocaraṃ
ceto manaḥ karmacobhirañjasā |
yadāśrayaṃ yena yataḥ pratīyate
sudurvibhāvyaṃ praṇato'smi tatpadam || (42)
ityadhyardhaślokena nāyaṃbhramaḥ, kiṃ tu mama putrasya sahajo'yamaisvaryayogaḥ, idamaiśvaryaṃ na manuṣyamātrasādhāraṇaṃ, kiṃ tu vāṅmanasādyaviṣayavicitratarajagadādhāras‌ya bhagavata eva saṃbhavet, ato bhagavāneva mama bhāgyavaśātputro'bhūditi niścitya bhagavadbuddhyā tamanamadityuktatvena tajjñānasya bhramatvāyogāt | ślokākṣarārthastu bhāgavatavyākhyābhiravaseyaḥ |
purā kalpāpāye svakṛtamudarīkṛtya vikṛtaṃ
tvamevā''dyastasminsalila uragendrādhiśayane |
pumān śete siddhai hṛdi vimṛśitādhyātmapadaviḥ
sa evā'dyākṣṇoryaḥ pathi carasi bhṛtyānavasi naḥ ||
(bhāga.4.7.42)
iti bhagavadudare sarvaṃ jagadastītyanyatrāpi prakaraṇe vyāsokteḥ | `udbhavaḥ kṣobhaṇo devaḥ śrīgarbhaḥ parameśvaraḥ' iti sahastranāmaśāṅkarabhāṣye śrīgarbha iti nāmnaḥ `śrīḥ vibhūtiḥ yasyodare jagadrūpā sthita' iti nirvacanāt `anena nāmnā udare samastaloravatvaguṇo bhagavata ukta iti ṇanyeṣāṃ tadavyākhyānāt, kasyacitkaviśekharasya `antarviṣṇostrilokī nivasati' iti padyasya viṣṇorūdare lokatrayamastīti vyākhyānācca na jaganmāyikam |
paṇḍitakavisārvabhaumo bhaṭṭamurārirbhagavadguṇanirūpaṇānasare--
viramati mahākalpe nābhīpathaikaniketana--
stribhuvanapuraḥ śilpī pratikṣaṇamātmabhūḥ |
kimadhikaraṇaṃ kīdṛt kasya vyavasthiti--
rityasāvudaramaviśaddraṣṭuṃ tasmai jagannidhaye namaḥ || (2)
ityāha | asyāyamarthaḥ -- viramati - mahāpralaye samāpte, mahākalpe - anyabrajhakalpe prāpte, sṛṣṭikāle sati, paraḥśilpī - ādau nirmātā śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | caturmukhasya prathamaśilpitvena kathaṃcidapi padārthānāmādhārādheyabhāvasya, avayavasaṃniveśasya cā'parijñānāt tatparijñānāya bhagavadudarapraveśakathanena bhagavadudare sarvaprakāropetaṃ vastu sūkṣmarūpeṇa vartata iti śāstrasiddho'rthaḥ sūcitaḥ | māyopādānakānāṃ, pratibhāsamātraviṣayāṇāṃ padārthānāṃ cirakālavartitvāyogena teṣāṃ bhagavadudare vṛttyasaṃbhavāt taddarśanāya tatpraveśoktirasaṃgatā syāt | tena jñāyate nāgeśoktaṃ tadubhayamayuktamiti |
nanu kavivacanamitprekṣārūpaṃ kathaṃ śāstrārthanirṇayāya kalpata iti cet, ucyate--nāyaṃ kaviḥ kevalaṃ svamanīṣikayā amumarthamutprekṣācakre, kiṃ tu ārṣaṃ vacanaṃ pramāṇamāsthāya abhidadhātisma | kiṃ tarhi tat,
śrīpaterudaraṃ bhūyaḥ praviveśa pitāmahaḥ |
tānevalokān garbhasthānanvāsyan pajhasaṃbhavaḥ ||
iti laiṅgavacanam | sarvaṃ jagat bhagavadudare sūkṣmarūpeṇa vartata iti vacanārthaḥ | anvāsyannityasya pasyannityarthaḥ | etena hiraṇyagarbho bauddhānpadārtān jñātvā sṛjatīti nāgesoktaṃ cintyam | na caivaṃ pūrvakalpasthitānanusaṃdhāya sṛjatīti śaṃkarācāryādivyākhyānaṃ kathamupapadyate, ucyate -- ubhayatra pramāṇasadbhāvāt pūrvakalpasthānanusaṃdhāya udarasthān dṛṣṭvā padārthasvarūpaṃ samyak niścitya sarṛjatīti tātparyāt | nanu (maṃ. 29.7) devatādhikaraṇe)
jñānameva paraṃ brajha jñānaṃ bandhāya ceṣyate |
jñānācmakamidaṃ viśvaṃ na jñānādvidyate param ||
vidyā'vidye ca maitreya jñānamevopadhāraya |
iti viṣṇupurāṇavacanasiddhaṃ sarvasya jñānasvarūpabrajhātmakatvamiti cet, ucyate -- tatra `satyaṃ jñānam' iti śruteḥ brajhaṇo jñānatvoktirūcitaina, `bandhako bhavapāśna bhavapāsācca mocaka' ityukteḥ tasya bandhakatvamapīṣṭameva | atrāyaṃ vivekaḥ -- ya tu sakāmā vihitaṃ karmānutiṣṭhanti teṣāṃ tattatkāmyapradānena sāṃsārikasukhānubhāvayitṛtvāt jñānātmano harerbandhakatvam | ye tu niṣkāmāḥ vihitaṃ karma bhagavadārādhanarūpaṃ kartavyamiti dhiyā'nutiṣṭhanti teṣāṃ muktidānena mocakatvamiti | idaṃ viśvaṃ - samastaṃ dagat jñānātmakam - jñānarūpaṃ braṅa ādānādikartā yasya tat, tatsvāmika tanniyamyamiti yāvat | jñāvarūpabrajhaṇo'nyat paraṃ uttamaṃ na vidyate, vidyā'vidye cajñānā'jñāne api, jñānameva jñānādhīne eva, tadadhīnatvāt rājā rāṣṭramitivat sāmānādhikaraṇyavyapadeśaḥ | atra jñānaṃ hareḥ puruṣottamatvajñānam, ajñānaṃ tadavyatiriktaṃ dvandvamohādijanyam | niruktamidaṃ gītāyāṃ saptamādau `kāmaistaistaihṛtajñānā' (20) ityādinā, iti vacanatātparyāt uktacodyaṃ nāvakāśaṃ dhatte | etena (ma.1.pa) idaṃ vacanamupanyasya `nirūpādhi brajhaiva vidyā, sopādhi tadevā'vidyeti tadartha' iti upādhiviśiṣṭaṃ hrajhaiva jagaditi sarvasya brajhātmakatvābhidhānamayuktaṃ śabdamaryādayā taduktārthālābhāt | ata eva `svāśayamāha--nirūpeti' | iti kalāyāmavatāritamidaṃ vākyam | tena vacanārtho'nyo'sti iti jñāyate | śuddhasya upādhisaṃbandho durnirūpaḥ tasya nirdharmakatvātsamādhau yogigamyatvamapi tatā, ato nirūpādhisopādhivibhāga evānucitaḥ |
saṃjñāmūrtīti sūtreṇa trivṛtkṛtamabādikam |
jagataḥkāraṇamiti śrutyarthaṃ prāha sūtrakṛt ||
vaidikāgresarasyedaṃ bhavatīṣṭaṃ ca pāṇineḥ |
abādhitamidaṃ tasmāt syādeveṣṭaṃ patañjaleḥ ||
tasmātsaṃskārajanyatvaṃ pratibhāsaśarīratā |
iti nāgeśavacanaṃ na munitrayasaṃmatam |
akṛtetiyogasūtrasya bhedamāyikatvaparatvanirāsaḥ
yadatra mañjūṣayāṃ (88.14) svābhimatātmaikatve yogasūtramapi anurūlamiti bodhanāya antaḥkaraṇānāṃ vibhutvanirūpaṇāvasaro adṛṣṭavāsanādīnāṃ sādhāraṇyāt `sarveṣāṃ yugapatsukhaduḥkhaprasaṃgaḥ' ityāśaṅkya `sarvasya māyikatvena jalanalinīpatravadadoṣāt kṛtārthaṃ prati naṣṭamapyanaṣṭaṃ tadanyasādhāraṇatvāt (yo.sū.2.22) iti sūtreṇa tathābodhanāt' iti samādhāya `kathamekamanaṣṭaṃ naṣṭaṃ cetyupapadyata' ityādinā ātmanānātvaṃ māyikamityāha | tannirmūlam | tathā hi--upanyaste sūtre āvidyakārthakatvabodhakapadāśravaṇāt, pūrvottarasūtrayośca tathārthakapadāśravaṇena anuvṛtyapakarṣayoścāsaṃbhavāt, etatsūtraghaṭitapādasya sādhanapādatvena tatra kasyacidapi sādhanasyā''vidyakatvānirūpaṇāt sūtrākṣaramaryādayā prakaraṇena ca tathārthālābhāt, sātreṇa tatābodhanāditi nāgesoktamayuktaṃ patañjalimate kasyacidapi varatupo māyikatvābhāvācca |
na ca ekasya vastuna esaṃ prati naṣṭatvānaṣṭatvayorayogāt agatyā'nyatara,ya māyikatvaṃ kalpanīyamiti vācyam, purūnānātve sūtratātparyeṇa naṣṭatvānaṣṭatvayoradhikāribhedenopapatteḥ | vyākhyātamidamevameva mśrādibhiḥ | kiṃ ca `na ca pardhānavadekaḥ puruṣaḥ', tannānātvas‌ya janmamaraṇasukhaduḥkhabhogamuktadisaṃsāravyavasthāya liddheḥ, prakṛtyekatvapurūṣanānātvayośca śrutyaiva sākṣātpratipādanāt | `ajāmekām' iti śruterartha evā'nena sūtreṇā'nudita' iti yogasūtratātparyaṃ śrutitagatparyaṃ ca jīvanānātva evetyarthakamiśragranthavirūddham | tataścāyaṃ sūtrārthaḥ -- kiṃ puruṣaḥ prakṛtivadekaḥ, atha nānā, iti saṃśaye siddhāntamāha--kṛtārthamiti | tat - pradhānam | kṛtārthaṃ prati - asaṃbuddhiviṣayīkṛtaviṣayendriyasukhaduḥkhādisaṃvidrūpabhogaṃ purūṣaṃ prati, naṣṭamapi - vuratavyāpāramapi (tatpurūṣīyopabhogopayogiviṣayādinā apariṇāmyapi) anaṣṭam - vyāpāravat (viṣayādinā pariṇāmi), kān prati ? akṛtārthān prati,anyasādhāramatvāt - kṛtrthapuruṣavyatiriktapuruṣasādhāraṇatvāt iti | ayamāśayaḥ -- sarvasādhāraṇamapi pradhānaṃ viśayaviraktasya viṣayādirūpeṇa pariṇataṃ nopakārāya bhavati, avivekinaḥ (rāgavataḥ puruṣasya) bhogyavasturūpeṇa pariṇataṃ sat upakārāya kalpate, ekasyaiva pradhānasya viraktarāgipurūṣabhedena naṣṭatvamanaṣṭatvaṃ copapadyata iti puruṣanānātvasiddhariti |
sāṃkhyayogau bhedavādināvityetat sārvalaukikama | sāṃkhyānāṃ mate ca jagato māyikatvaṃ neti ṣaṣṭitaṃtre paṃcaśiśācāryaiḥ --
guṇānāṃ paramaṃ rūpaṃ na dṛṣṭipathamṛcchati |
yattu dṛṣṭipathaṃ prāptaṃ tanmāyeva sutucchakam ||
ityanena nirūpitam | asyāyamarthaḥ --- triguṇātmakapradhānakāryabhūtaṃ (jagat) vastujātaṃ, māyeva - māyānirmitavastviva, yathā māyāvinā nirmitaṃ vastu ihnāya naśyati, tatā pradānakāryam āśu vinasyati iti | evaṃ ca sāṃkhyānāṃ mate na jagat māyikaṃ, kiṃ tu satyam arthakriyākārītyato jagadantaḥpāti, bhedo'pi tanmate vāstava eva | na ca tatsūtre ekatvaśrutīnāṃ ca pramāṇāntaravirodhāt kathaṃciddeśakālavibhāgābhāvena bhakttyāpyupapatteḥ iti miśrokteḥ, `vyavahāraparamārthabhedādaikātmyanānātvayorūpapatti vātyam | bhaktyetyuktyā ekatvaṃ gauṇaṃ na tu mukhyam tatrāpi kathaṃcidityuktyā ca deśakālavibhāgasya sadātanatvena tadabhāvasyaivābhāvena tena tathākalpavaṃ klaṣṭamiti miśrāśayāt | bhikṣigranthe yathāsaṃkhyānvayena aikātmyaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ mukhacandrayoriva, paramārthato nānātvaṃ ghaṭapaṭayoriveti bhikṣvāśayāt | ata eva vijñānabhikṣuṇā `kleśakarmavipākāśayairaparāmṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa īśvara' (yo. 1.24) itīśvarasakṣaṇasūtre īśvarasya sarvavailakṣaṇyaṃ vadataḥ sātrakṛto'bhiprāyanirūpaṇāvasare sāṃkhyayogayorātmanānātva eva tātparyamiti nipuṇaṃ nirūpitam | ayamatra saṃkṣepaḥ -- patañjalistāvatsāṃkhyācāryeṣvanyatama iti suprasiddham, ataḥ sāṃkhyamata iva etanmate'pi jagat ātmanānātvaṃ ca vāstavameveti etatsūtraṃ vāstavabhedatātparyakamiti varṇanaṃ nyāyyam | vyākhyātṛṣu bhāṣyakārādiṣu kaścidapi etatsūtrabodhito bhedo māyika iti nābravīt | `dvaitino hi te sāṃkhyā yogāśca' iti śrīśaṅkarācāryā āhuḥ, sūtramidaṃ māyikabhedaparamiti na kaścidapi vipascit vyācaṣṭe | ato nāgeśavyākhyānaṃ na vidvajjanā ādriyante | na ca `dvaitino hi te' iti śāṅkarabhāṣyasya vyāvahārikadvaitavādina ityartha iti vācyam, tathāsati tanmatakhaṇḍanāya toṣāṃ pravṛttyayogāt | ayamapi yogavṛttau miśrāṇāṃ mārganusṛtya etatsūtraṃ, vyākyat | evaṃ ca mañjūṣāyāṃ mayikaṃ bhedaṃ bodhayati sūtramiti bhāṣaṇaṃ yogasūtrīyabahuvyākhyānaviruddam, kiṃ ca śrūśaṅkārācāryādīnāṃ yogatantrābhiprāyānabhijñatāṃ sūcayati |
akṛtārthetisūtreṇa māyiko bheda ucyate |
iti vyākyā yogasūtrabahavyākhyātra saṃmatā ||
na saṃmatā yogasūtrakṛkaścāpi patañjaleḥ |
sāṃkhyayogamate kaścitpadārtho māyiko na hi ||
janmadyasyeti ślokārthaḥ
nanu `janmādyasya yato'nvayāt' (bhāga.1.1.1) iti bhāgavatādyapadye `yatra trisargo mṛṣā' iti bhagavatkartṛkasṛṣṭeḥ, tatsṛṣṭasya vā mṛṣātvamabhidhīyate bhagavatā vāyāseneti nāśaṅkyam | yato'tra bādarāyaṇaḥ parasamin brajhaṇi triguṇakāryaṃ mṛṣetyabhipraitīti tattātparyam | akṣarārthastu---yatrati dārṣṭāntike saptamīśravaṇāt `mṛtā' miti ṣaṣṭhī vaiṣayikādhārasya saṃbandhitvavivakṣayā | yathā keśoṇḍhrakādau tejastvādiviṣayako vinimayaḥ (bhramaḥ) tathā, yatra - yasmin bhagavapato rāmakṛṣṇādyavatārarūpeṣvapi, trisargaḥ - guṇatrayakāryasaṃbandhaḥ (tatkāryam) mṛṣā - mithyā, sa nāsti, `kevalo nirguṇaśca' (śvetā.) `sarve nirguṇam' (go.13.14) ityukteriti | yadvā-amṛṣeticchedaḥ | vinimayaḥ kāryam | yatreti saṃvandhino'dhikaraṇatvavivakṣayā saptamī tatastral | tripadaṃ guṇatrayātmakaprakṛtiparam | ayrtaḥ --- yachā tejaḥprabhṛtīnāṃ kāryam, amṛṣā - satyam, arthakriyārāritvāt tathā yatsaṃbandhitriguṇātmakaprakṛtikāryaṃ mahadādi, amṛṣā-satyam, mithyā neti | yadyatra brajhasṛṣṭasyaiva sithyātvamabhipraitīti brayāt, tarhi `nirastakuhakam' ityanena nirastamāyikatvoktirasaṃgatā syāt | satyajagatstraṣṭṭatve hi tadupapadyeta | anyathā aindrajālikasyeva kuhakatvaṃ māyikatvameva bhavediti tadasāṃgatyaṃ suspa,ṭam | aindrajālikena sṛṣṭamapi na mithyeti sāṃkhyamatam | kiṃ ca `asatyamapratiṣṭhaṃ te' iti bhagavadvacanaviruddhaḥ jagadasatyatvāvāda iti prāṅnirūpitaṃ na vismartavyam, nirūpayiṣyate caitat | vistarastu anyatra draṣṭavya iti |
etena prathamaparicchede sthaladvaye `vipra pṛthvyādi cittasthaṃ na bahiṣiṭaṃ kadācana | svanpabhramamadādyeṣu sarvairevānubhūyate' iti kaurmavacanamupanyasya sarvaṃ jagat cittasthāmeva na bahirāste iti yadābaha nāgeśaḥ, `atra kadācana' ityuktyā sarvaḥprapañco bahirasanneveti yaccāha kalāyāṃ vaidyanāthaḥ, tadubhayamasaṃgataṃ veditavyam | tathā hi---svanpādi,u anubhūyate vastu, bodhe tu na dṛsyate, tatkutratyamiti jijñāsāyāṃ jāgrati anubhūtaṃ saṃskārarūpeṇa cittasthameva vastu svanpādirūpodbodhakasāmāgyā anubhūyate na tu jāgrati bahirvidyamānaṃ, svanpasyānyairananubhūyamānatvāditi vacanatātparyaṃ, na tu bājhavastuno'sattve | yojanā tu---he vipra saṃskārūpeṇa cittastameva pṛthivyādi svanpādiṣu nānubhūyate asya svanpādyavasthānāpannasarvānubhavāyogyatvāditi | yatsarvānubhavayogyaṃ tadbahiṣṭham yatsvaptṛpuruṣamātrānubhavayogyaṃ tat saṃskārarūpeṇa cittasthamiti vivekaḥ evaṃ ca bahiḥ padārtho'sannevetyartho nānena labyata iti |
janmādiviprapṛthvītipadyayornaiva māyike |
tātparyamiti saṃkṣepāduktamanyatra vistaraḥ ||
īśvarasya sarvadevatārūpatvanirāsaḥ
