mañcūṣāyāṃ taddhitārthavicāre `aindraṃ haviḥ, aindro mantraḥ' ityādau taddhitāratho devatā `sā ca śabdātiriktā tiryagadhikaraṇe na karmasu devatā adhikriyanta' ityarthakena `na devātāntarābhāvāt' iti sūtreṇa `jaiminināpyaṅkīkārācca' iti gkanthena jaiminmate śabdātiriktadevatāsadbhāvaṃ prasādhya, `devatātvaṃ ca mantrakaraṇakahavistyāgajanyasvargātiriktaphalāśrayatvenoddeśyatvaṃ, mantrastutyatvaṃ ca' iti devatātvaṃ nirūcya, svābhimatātmaikatnasthemne `uddeśyatā ca mantroktaśabdaśarīrobhayāvacchanneśvaracaitanyasya' tadeva ca mantroktamindrādipadavācyam, `indraṃ mitram' ityādimantravarṇāt | `indaḥ sahastrākṣo gotrabhidvajrahāhu' rityarthavādabalāt tatpradarśitavigrahahavirbhojanaiśvaryatṛptiphalapradatvānāṃ `na bādha' iti granthena tattacchabdabodhyavigrahaviśiṣṭeśvaracaitanyasyaiva devatātvaṃ samarthayate nāgeśaḥ | atra vadanta---`jaiminyaṅgīkārāccha' ityuktistāvanna yujyate navame devatādhikaraṇe bhāṭṭapikāyām--`jaiminimataniṣkarṣastu śabda eva devatā prātipadikānurodhena tadarthaścetano'cetano vā bhavatu, tasyā vigrahastu nāstyeva atastasyāḥ phalapradatvaṃ na, kiṃ tu apūrvameva karmajanyaphalapradam, mama tvevaṃ vadato'pi vāṇī duṣyati harismaraṇameva śaraṇam' iti khaṇḍadevakṛtajaiminimataniṣkarṣasya, tadbodhitatadanuvādamātrajanitadoṣaprāyaścuttasya ca paryālocanāyāṃ śabdātiriktadevatāsadbhāva ityetat na jaiminisiddhānta ityavagamāt | ubhayoranayoḥ paṇḍitaprakāṇḍatvāviśeṣe'pi khaṇḍajevakṛtaniṣkarṣasyopadeśapararāsiddhatvena khaṇḍadevoktireva balavatīti saivādaraṇīyā | `devānāṃ tvātmoddeśyakatyāgāsaṃbhavāt, vigrahasyābhāvācca śakterabhāvānnādhikāra' ityāditiryagajhikaraṇīya (6.1) pārthasārathimiśrādyuktiḥ, evamarthakakumārilabhāṣaṇaṃ ca tathāvidhopadeśaparaṃparāsadbhāve mānam |
kiṃ ca devānāṃ karmaṇyadhikārābhāvaṃ bodhayatā `na devatāntarābhāvāt' ityanena kathamakṣaramaryādayā atiriktadevatālābha iti vibhāvyam |
kiṃ ca `sītreṇa' ityuktireva mañjūṣākṛto nāgeśasya pūrvatantreṣa़nyādṛśaṃ pāṇjityaṃ dyotayati | idaṃ vākyaṃ jaiminīyasūtrapāṭhe kvāpi na dṛśyate | nāpi vyāśyātaṃ pratisūtraṃ vyākurvatā bhāṣyakṛtā śabarasvāminā, vṛttikṛtā ca kāmamiśreṇa | uttarakamīmāṃsāyāṃ devatādhikaraṇaṃ devānāmadhikāro neti codyas‌ya saṃpoṣaṇāya vākyamidamudāharantaḥ śaṅaakarācāryā api sūtramidamiti nā'vocan | śābarabhāṣyastha vākya metaditi sāṃpradāyikāḥ | śaṅkarabhāṣyaṭippaṇyāmasya vākyasya sūtrasaṃkhyālekhanam `ati prathamasūtrārthaḥ' iti lekhanaṃ ca anavadhānanibandhanamiti jaiminimatānuyāyinaḥ |
kiṃ ca śāṃkarabhāṣye devatādhikaraṇe (bra.sū.1.3.8) devatāyā vigrahādirāhityavādijaiminimataṃ dūṣayitvā vigrahavattvasya sddhāntitatvena śabdātiriktadevatā nastītyeva jaiminimatamiti khaṇḍadevoktameva yuktam | yadi devatāyāḥ śabdātiriktatvaṃ jaiminerapi saṃmataṃ syāt tadā vigrahavattvamapi arthavādādihalena sidhyatīti śakyaṃ vaktum | tataśca śāṅkarīyajaiminimatakhaṇḍanamapi tadīyābhiprāyāparijñānamūlakameva syāt | mahāraviḥ śrīharṣo'pi śleṣeṇa prakṛtāprakṛtamarthaṃ varṇayan devanāṃ vigraho jaiminirmanyata ityāha---
viśvarūpakalanāpapannaṃ tasya jaiminimunitvamudīye |
vigrahaṃ makhabhujāmasahiṣṇurvyarthatāṃ madaśaniṃ sa nināya ||
(nai.5.39)
ityanena | viśvarūpakalanāt matsyakūrmādyanekāvatāradhāraṇāt, nānārthakasūtrakaraṇādityarthaḥ | sarvapadārthasākṣātkaraṇādityapyartho vyajyate | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam |
bhagavatā sūtrakṛtā bādarāyaṇena `phalamata upapatteḥ' (bra.sū.3.2.39) ityanena īśvaraḥ phalapradaḥ, dharmaḥ apūrvadvārā phalada iti pratipādya tataḥ `dharmaṃ jaiminirata eva' (bra.sū.3.4.41) iti jaiminimatamupanyasya dharmāpūrvayoracetanatvāt tayordātṛtvāsaṃbhavena uktadiśāsarvādhyakṣa īśvara eva phalaprada iti yuktaḥ pakṣaḥ iti siddhāntitam | kiṃ ca yadi śabdātiriktā devatā jaiminisaṃmatā tarhi saiva marmaṇā''rādhitā phalapradā bhavitumarhatītyāśayena `pīrmaṃ tu bādarāyaṇo hetuvyapadeśāt' (bra.sū.3.2.42) iti sūtreṇa tanmatanirākaraṇamsaṃgataṃ syāt |
kiṃ ca jaiminimate atiriktadevatādisadbhāvasādhane kālāntarabhānavisvargādiphalotpādakāpūrvāstitvādisamarthaparāṇi `codanā punarārambha' (2.1.5) ityādīni bahūnyadhikaraṇāni jaimininā'nārambhaṇīyāni bhaveyiḥ, vigrahavaddevatāprasādādeva phalotpattisaṃbhavāt |
yaccānena abhihitam īsvaracetana eka evoddeśyatvena devatā mantroktendrādipadavācyo dīyamānaṃ dravyamaśnāti `indraṃ mitraṃ varuṇamagnimāhiratho divyaḥ sa suparṇooo garutmān | ekaṃ sadviprā bahudhā vadanti agniṃ yamaṃ mātāriśvānamāhu_ṭa (ṛ.saṃ.2.3.22) tadyadidamāhuḥ, amuṃ yajāmuṃ yajetyekakaiṃ devaṃ, etasyaiva sā visṛṣṭiḥ eṣa ujheva sarve devā (bṛ.u.1.4.6) ityādibhistasyave sarvadevatātvena pratipādanāt' iti śabdātiriktāpi devatā īśvara eveti |
atredaṃ vaktavyam -- `śabdātiriktadevatā'sti, vigraho'pyasti tasyā' iti vacanaṃ tāvatsundaram, idaṃ ca sarvairmomāṃsakātiriktairāstikajanaiḥ saśiraḥkampaṃ saślāghamādaraṇīyam | bhagavatottaramimāṃsākṛtā `taduparyapi bādarāyaṇaḥ saṃbhavāt' (bra.sū.1.3.23) ityādinā bhagavadupāsanāyāṃ teṣāmadhikāro'stīti samarthitatvāt | yadi toṣāmatiriktatvābhāvaḥ, vigrahābhāvaśca tadā upānāyāṃ pravṛttyayogena tadadhikaraṇārambho vyarthaḥ syāt | paraṃ tu tasyāstanmate uktamīśvaratvam, indrādirūpeṇa havirbhojanam, śabdātiriktatvaṃ phaladatvaṃ cāyuktam, indrādirūpeṇa havirbhojanam, śabdātiriktatvaṃ phaladatvaṃ cāyuktam | jaimininā tathānukteḥ `jaiminināpyaṅgīkārācca' itiyuktiḥ tasyā īśvaratvādyuktiscāsaṃgatā, mīmāṃsakamata īśvarābhāvāt | ata eva bhūṣaṇasāre neśvarasaṃketaḥ śaktiḥ, mīmānasakānāmiśvarābhāvāt tatsaṃketagrahābhāvena arthopasthityāderabhāvaprasaṃgādityuktaṃ śaktivāde | `kasye' (4.2) diti sūtre kaiyaṭastu--`yāgasaṃpradānaṃ devatā, mantrastutyaścārtha ityeva devatāpadārthamāha | `agnirvai devānāmavamo viṣṇuḥ paramaḥ tadantareṇa anyāḥ sarvā devatāḥ, (ai.brā.1.) yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan, ya ātmani tiṣṭhan, īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānām ityādiṣu īsvarātiriktānāmakeṣāṃ devādīnāṃ sadbhāvasya harau taduttamatvādeśca jotanaikyāyogāt |
cetananānātvasya pūrvamadāhṛtabahupramāṇaliddhatvena ekasyaiveśvarasya indrādipadavācyatvaṃ tattadrūpeṇa tattaddevatātvamiti ca cintyam | chāncogye `atha ye cāsyeha jīvāḥ, evamevemāḥ sarvāḥ prajā aharahargacchantya etaṃ brajhalokaṃ na vindanti anṛtena hi pratyūḍhā' (8.3.2) ityādau jīvā ityādibahuvacanaśravaṇam, aṣṭame ṣaṣṭājiṣu khaṇḍeṣu `ekaśataṃ varṣāṇi brajhacaryaṃ vasata indrasya prajāpateḥ sakāsāt brajhavidyāgrahaṇaśravaṇaṃ bhagavato lakṣmīpateranyasya bhagapatpadavācyacatā neti pratipādanāvasaraṃ bhagavatā vyāsena varvettamatvanirūpaṇaṃ, bhāgavate tatratyaśrīdharīyaṃ ca cetanaikyābhāve mānam |
nanu
sattvaṃ rajastama iti prakṛterguṇāstai--
ryuktaḥ paraḥ puraṣa ekaihāsya dhatte |
sthityādaye hariviriñcikaretisaṃjñāḥ
śreyāṃsi tatra khalu sattvatanornṛṇāṃ syuḥ ||
(bhāga.1.2.24)
iti ekasyaiva hareḥ pālanādikarmabhedena nāmabheda iti vyāsokteḥ bariviriñciharāṇāmekatvaṃ tadabhimatamiti na vācyam, nānenābheda ucyate, kiṃ ti--
tatra tatra sthito viṣṇustattacchaktīḥ prabodhayan |
eka eva mahāviṣṇuḥ kurute sarvamañjasā ||
ityādyukteḥ viriñcādiṣvapi antaḥsthito hariḥ sṛṣṭyādikaṃ kurute tatra tatra sthitatvāt, mañcasthānāṃ mañcapadavācyatvavat viriñcādipadavācyatvamastīti bodhyate ityadoṣāta | na ca `nārāyaṇakalāḥ śāntā bhajanti jhanasūyavaḥ' (bhāga.1.2.27) ityādau nārāyaṇakalātvokterekatvamiti vācyam, nārāyaṇakalāsadṛśā ityarthaḥ na tu tatsvarūpabhūtā ityadoṣāt | evaṃcātīyakāḥ śrutismṛtyādaya evamena vyākyātavyāḥ | śāstradipirāyāmātmaikatvavādasya (1.1. pā) nirākṛtatvena mīmāṃsakamatarītyā nedaṃ yujyateti jñeyam |
`indraṃ mitram' ityādiśrutestu indrādiśabdairyogabalena pratīyamānaguṇasambandhena ādityasyāganervā stutariti vedabhāṣye mādhavācāryairvyākhyātatvena agnyādyanyataradevatāparatvaṃ, na tu nāgesoktadiśā iśvaraparatvam |
kiṃ ca cetanaikyaṃ vadato mate iśvarānyacetanābhāvemi īśvarasya ca śuddhasvabhāvatvena āvdiyakadharmasaṃbandhasya ca vaktumaśakyatayā aupādhikabhedasyāpi vaktumaśakyatvāt | karmakarturabhāvena yāgasyaivābhāvaprasaṃgena tajājanyaphalabhoktupabhāvena (svargādiphalatadanubhavitrorapyabhāvena) sarvaṃ vedādiramapramāṇaṃ syāt, unmattabhāṣitaṃ, bālabhāṣitaṃ vā syāditi tadabhimatacetanaikyaṃ niṣpramāṇameva bhavediti vibhāvyam | na ca upādhisaṃbandhena sarvamupapadyata iti vācyam, śuddhe vastuni tatsaṃbandhāyoga ityuktatvāt |
nanu `tasmāt-parameśvarāt, yajñāt-yajanīyāt (pūjyāt) sarvahutaḥ-sarvairhūyamānāt, iti ṛgvedabhāṣyopoddhātānurodhena maṃjūṣākṛdavocaditi cet, na | puruṣasūktabhāṣye tasmādyajñāt-parvoktamānasayāgāt, sarvahutaḥ-sarvātmakaḥ puruṣaḥ yasmin yajñe būyate so'yaṃ sarvahuta iti mādavācāryeṇa vyākhyāto'yaṃ mantraikadeśaḥ | bhaṭṭabhāskareṇāpitasmāt-mānasāt sarvātmā puruṣaḥ paśutvena hūyate yasminniti sarvahut, adhikaraṇe kvip iti vyākhyānāt, `indram' ityāderagnyādiparatayā vyākhyātatvācca upoddhātoktārthe prāmāṇyāniścayāt | īśvarasyendrādirūpatve mānābhāva iti pūrvamuktatvācca | etena `bhāṣyopoddātayorīśvarasya sarvātmakatvaṃ bhāṣitamiti na virodha' iti tu cintyaṃ, tasyārthasya pramāṇasiddhatvābhāvāt | `sarvahuta' ityasya tu -- sarvaḥ - pūrṇaḥ, vyāpto vā hūyata ityartaḥ sarvaśabdasya etadubhayārthakatve mānamuktaṃ `vāsudeva' iti ślokavyākhyāvasare | yadvā sarvairhūyata iti karmaṇi kvipi sarvairhūyamāna ityarthaḥ | vaiṣṇavayāgeṣu hakeḥ sarvairhūyamānatā'styeva |
kiṃ ca havane nāma prīṇanaṃ `prīṇanepīti bhāṣyam' iti kaumudyāmukteḥ, tacca svasvaviṣayakapritiprasādādipadābhidheyasvottamadevādisamavetecchānukūlo vyāpāraḥ, sā cecchā ayaṃ mama bhaktaḥ, īpsitamidaṃ phalaṃ prānpoti, māṃ vā prānpotu ityevaṃrupā | tādṛśavyāpāraḥ stutirūpaḥ, kṛtasya śrautasmārtakarmaṇo bhagavatu samarpaṇaṃ vā, tatra---
anādinidhanaṃ viṣṇuṃ sarvalokamaheśvaram |
lokādhyakṣaṃ stuvannityaṃ sarvaduḥkhātigo bhavet || (sahastranāma.6)
iti prītidvārā stavavasya phalopāyatvasmaraṇāt,
kāyena vācā manasendriyeṇa buddhyātmanā vā'nukṛtaḥ svabhāvāt |
karoti yadyatsakalaṃ parasmai nārāyaṇāyeti samarpayettat || (bhāga.11.11.37)
yatkaroṣi yadaśnāsi yajjuhoṣi dadāsi yat |
yattapasyasi kaunteya tatkusṣva madarpaṇam || (gī.9.27)
iti anuṣṭhitasya vihitasya karmaṇaḥ sarvasya samarpaṇasmaraṇāt,
śubhāśubhaphalairevaṃ mokṣyase karmabandhanaiḥ |
sanyāsayogayuktātmā vimukto māmupaiṣyasi || (gī.9.28)
iti arpaṇasya bhagavatā phalasmaraṇācca |
nanu `sarvaśca prajāpatiḥ' iti `kasyet' (pā.sū.4.2.25) iti sūtrabhāṣyaṃ sarvasya brajhātmatve mānam | tatra `kāryakāraṇayorabhedāditi bhāva' iti kaiyaṭo'vyākhyat | kāraṇabhūtabrajhaṇaḥ sarvātmatvena sarvarūpaprajāpativācakatvena kaśabdasya sarvanāmasaṃjñā bhaviṣyatīti bhāṣyasya prākaraṇikārthaḥ | tataśca anena bhāṣyeṇa sarvasya brajhātmatvaṃ tatsaṃmatamiti jānīma iti cet, ucyate--`idaṃ bhāṣyamuttarabhāṣyaṃ ceti dvayacaṃ, dvayorekadeśinooḥ prauḍhoktimātram' ityuddyote, śekhareco ktatvena na siddhāntisaṃmatam | kiṃ ca `sarvaśca prajāpatiḥ' iti bhāṣyamādāya `ata evoktaṃ `sarvaṃ samānpoṣi tato'si sarva' iti gītāyāmuktam' iti uddyotena brajhaṇaḥ sarvatve na sarvātmakatvaṃ hetuḥ, kiṃ tu sarvavyāpanamiti bhāṣyābhiprāyo varṇita iti brajhaparpañcayorbheda eva bhāṣyasaṃmata iti siddheḥ hrajhaṇaḥ sarvātmatve nedaṃ bhāṣyaṃ mānamiti |
`tadyadyadidam' (3.4.6) ityāderayamarthaḥ -- amuṃ yaja amuṃ yajeti yat ekaikaṃ devamuddiśya yat yajanavidhānaṃ, tadidaṃ etasyaiva sā visṛṣṭiḥ (preṣaḥ) bhagavaduddeśena vidhīyamānayajanavidhireva | tatra hetumāhuḥ - eṣa iti | sarvaṃ devā eṣa u eva-bhagavanniyamyatvena tadadhīnā eva, hrajhaśabdenocyante | hi- `yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣiṭhan (bṛ.4.7.3) ityādi śrutyādiṣu prasiddhamiti | `avidvanmatāntaranindopanyāso'saṃ sā visṛṣṭirdevabhedaḥ' iti śāṃkarabhāṣye | asya vākyasya nindāparatve gamakaṃ mṛgyam | tatra -- pṛthivī, ap, agri, antarikṣa, vāyu, div, ādtya, dik, caṃdra, tāraka, ākāśa, tamaḥ, teja sarvabhūta, prāṇa, vāk, cakṣuḥ, śrotra, manaḥ, tvak, vijñāna (ātmā), retaḥ, iti pṛthivyādiretontairekaviṃśatiśabdairekaviṃśatikhaṇḍikāgataiḥ tattadabhimānidevatā grājhā iti śaṅkarācāryairvyākhyātam | `yaṃ pṛthivī na veda yaḥ pṛthivīmantaro yamayati' iti śrutugataireva vākyairantaryāmyajñānabodhakaiḥ pṛthivyādipadaṃ tadabhimānidevatāparamiti spaṣṭaṃ bodhyate | anyathā pṛthivyādīnāmacetanatvena avedanasya svataḥsddheḥ taduktirvyarthā syāt | `eṣa u eva sarve devā' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyavyapadeśastu tadadhīnatvanyāyena rājārāṣṭramityādivat bodhyaḥ |
nanu niyamyānāṃ yajanaṃ bhagavadyajanaṃ bhavediti cet, ucyate--bhagavānniyamayati sarvāniti vedaprāmāṇyavādinaḥ sarve ācryā udāhṛtaśrukibalaṃna ekakaṇṭhā vadanti ato'tra na ko'pi pratyavasthātā | savaniyantuḥ sarvādhyakṣatvaṃ svataḥsiddham tena vinā sarvaniyamanāyoogāt | yajanaṃnāma prītyutpādako vyāpāraḥ | sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ cātmiyatvena teṣāṃ yajanavapi ātmayajanamiva ātmanaḥ prītikarameneti ātmā prasanno bhavati tadyājiṣu | loke'pi rājādaya ātmīyajanasaṃmānanena tatkartari prīto bhavatīti sarvānubhavasiddham | `labhate ca tataḥ kāmānmayaivavihitān hitān' (gī.7.22) iti anyayajane'piśvaka eva phalada iti gītācāryaḥ, `brajhadṛṣṭirutkarṣāt' (bra.sū.4.1.5) iti sūtre, `sa ya etamevaṃ vidvānādityaṃ brajhetyupāste' (chā.3.19.4) ityādinā ādityopāsanaṃ vidhīyate tanna kāryamaphaladatvāt, kiṃ tu brajhopāsanameva kāryaṃ phaladatvāditi prapte, ādityopāsanameva kāryaṃ tatrāpi phalaṃ brajhaiva dāsyati sarvādhyakṣatvāt `phalamata upapatteḥ' ityukteriti ātmīyopāsanena svayaṃ prītaḥ san phalaṃ pradadāti bhagavāniti śaṅkarācāryaḥ | atha vā indrādipadaṃ tattatpadavācyendrādidevatāntaryāmiparaṃ sat antaryāmiṇaṃ paramātmānamāheti śrutyarthaḥ | antaryāmiṇamiddiśya sarvāṇi karmāṇi purvanta antaryāmiṇaḥ svasvādhyakṣasya yajanena indrādayo'pi prasannā bhavanti, yathā loke rājādūnāṃ sabhājanena amātyādayaḥ prasannacittā bhavanti |
bhoktāraṃ yajñatapasāṃ sarvalokamaheśvaram |
suhṛdaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ jñātvā māṃ śāntimṛcchati || (gī.5.29)
ahaṃ hu sarvayajñānāṃ bhoktā ca prabhureva ca |
na tu māmabhijānanti tattvenātaścyavanti te || (gī.9.24)
iti sarvayajñabhoktṛtvaṃ harerbhagavānāha | idaṃ ca svasya svīyānāṃ ca yajaneneti jñeyam |
atra yudhiṣṭhisasya rājasūyayāge sarvasokamahesvarasya kṛṣṇasya agrapūjanena sarve rājāno maharṣyādayaśca prasannā abhūvan, arhaṇāṃ kurvantaṃ sahadevamabhituṣṭuvuḥ, devāśca prasannāḥ puṣpaṃ vavarṣuriti bhāratīyā sabhāparvagatā kathātrānusaṃdheyā |
nanu īśaavarasyaiva `bhoktāram' ityādipramāṇahalātsarvayāgeṣu bhojanamityaṅkīkāre nāgeśamatādaviśeṣa iti na mantavyam, nāgeśastāvat ekasyaiveśvaracetanasya upādhivaśāt indrādirūpatā tena ca rūpeṇa yāgeṣu bhojanamityāheti tanmate indrādidevāḥ na pṛthak santīti vastusthitiḥ | bhedavādināṃ mate tu `angirvai devānāmavamo viṣṇuḥ paramastadantareṇa sarvā anyā devatāḥ' ityādiśrutibalāt havurbhujo devāḥ vastutaḥ santi bahavaḥ, teṣāṃ viṣṇuruttamaḥ, sa ca sarvāntaryāmī san havirbuṅka iti sarvayāgeṣu devānāmantaryāmiṇo viṣṇośca bhojanamiti vastusthitiriti mahānasti viśeṣaḥ | ata eva --
devān devayajo yānti, pitṛn yānti pitṛvratāḥ, bhūtāni yānti bhūtejyāḥ, yanti madyājinopi māṃ | iti tattadārādhakānāṃ tattatpraptikathanaṃ saṃgacchate | indratvāderaupādhikatve upādhinivṛttau indrāderapi abhāvena prāpyasyābhāvattatprāptikathanāsaṃgatiḥ |
kiṃ ca `atiriktadevatāyāḥ jaiminināpyaṅgīkārācca' ityetat svoktivirūdvam | tathā hi -- liṅarthanirūpaṇaprakaraṇe `jaiminirbhāratokteṣu keṣucirteṣi sandihāna ekadā mārkaṇḍeyametya papraccha--bhagavan ! chindhi me saṃśayamiti, sa tu na me'vasakaḥ, vasanti vindhye catvāraḥ pakṣiṇaḥ tān pṛccheyamiti' iti mārkaṇḍeyādau, iti purāṇakathāmanūdya, (tasyāyamāśayaḥ -- vyāsaśiṣyo'pyayamaśuddhacitto vyāsoktaṃ na sādhu manyate, īśvaraḥ phalado na; kiṃ tu karnaiva phaladaṃ devatiryagādīnāṃ vyāsoktaṃ jñānādhikāraṃ yuktaṃ na manyate, atastiraścāṃ śiṣyo bhūtvā toṣāṃ jñānādhikāre jñāte sarvatra vāyasokte viśvāsī bhavet, tanmikhādeva devāsurasaṃgramakathāśravaṇena teṣāmavigrahatvābhimānaṃ ca tyakṣyati' iti) jaimineḥ `devānāṃ vigraho na' iti tātparyamāviṣkṛtavān | tathā tatraiva `parāśarasmṛtivyāśyāyāṃ mādhavācāryo jaiminimatānusāriṇo jahāsa īśasiddhibhayādvedaśāstrāprāmāṇyamīyuṣām | śāktrakṛjjaiminiminiḥ kena mānena sidhyati' iti | atra īśasiddhītyupalakṣaṇaṃ devatāvigrahāderapi | atra devatā ca vigrahaśca devatāvigrahau tāvādī yasyeti vigrahaḥ | ādinā tāsāṃ phaladatvādergrahaḥ | devatāpadena śabavadātiriktendrādidevatāyāśca grahaḥ | īśvarasya, devatāyāḥ, tadvigrahasya,tāsāṃ phaladatvādeśca siddhibhayāditi mādhavācāryanacanārthaḥ | anena īśvarādisādhakavedādirapramāṇaṃ teṣāṃ mate ityuktaṃ bhavati | tataśca tatra īsvaradevatāvigrahādyabhāve jaiminerāśāyamabhidadhatā atra `jaiminināpyaṅkīkārāccha' ityabhidhānaṃ svapūrvagranthaviruddam | nanu prāptopadeśasya jaiminerdevatāyā vigrahādikamabhimatamiti vṛttiparicchedama़jūṣātātparyam, aprāptopadeśajaiminitātparyavarṇanaparo liṅaparthaprakaraṇasthamañjīṣāgrantha iti na virodha iti cet, ucyate -- jaiminerupadeśāttarakālikīṃ manaso vṛttiṃ parijñātuṃ bhagavāneka eva sarvāntaryāmi prabhavati, na tu tadanye, tatrāpi asmadādayo'lpajñāḥ | tadīyatantreṇaiva hi tadīyamatasthitiravagantavyā na tūpāyāntareṇa | ata eva hi tattadācāryasiddhāntanirupakāstattadācāryāṇāṃ vākyameva pramāṇatayodāharanti | nāgeśo'pi jaiminīyasūtropanyāse naiva tanmataniṣkarṣamakārṣot | tadudāhṛtaṃ sūtramuttarāvastāyāṃ praṇītamiti kena mānena nirṇiyate tenā''yuṣmatā | liṅrthaprakaraṇasthatadīyamañjūṣāgranthato jaiminirupadeśātpūrvameva pūrvanantraṃ praṇītavāniti śakyate vaktum | anyathā tadabhiprāyaparijñānopāyābhāvena `ayamāśaya' ityādinā vigrabādyabhāve tasya tātparyaparṇanaṃ kathaṃ saṃgaccheta | tadvacanena hi tattātparyaṃ jñātvā tadāśayo varṇanīya ityalamativistareṇa |
sīrayaḥ khaṇḍadevādyāḥ pūrvatantrārthavedinaḥ |
pārthasārathimiśrādyaiḥ kṛtān granthānvimṛśya tu ||
devatāyā vigrahādi neti cakrurvinirṇayam |
nābravījjaiminimuniḥ kaṇṭhato vigrahādikam ||
tasmānna saṃmatastasya nāgeśakṛtanirṇayaḥ |
iti jānantu vidvadaṃso mātsaryādivivarjitāḥ ||
mayaḍarthavicāraḥ
yacca -- `tatprakṛtavacane mayaṭ' (pā.sū.5.4.21) iti sūtrārthavicāre brajhaṇo nirdharmakatvapratipipādayiṣayā `ānandamayo brajha, cinmayaṃ brajha' ityādau ayameva mayaṭ, tatra prācuryaṃ nirdharmakatvaṃ vāyavahārikamaparicchodyatvaṃ ca, anantatvaśruteḥ........prāṇamayādāvapi ayameva mayaṭ, vikārārthasyenopapattyabhāvāt | śuddhimantre'pi śuddhirāvaravaraṇanāśarūpeti na virodha iti `vṛttikṛta' ityantena arthataḥ śabdato vā vṛttigranthānuvādarūpeṇa granthena vallīdvaye, śuddhimaṃtre ca śrūyamāṇo mayaṭ prācuryārthaka iti pratyapādi nāgeśena mañjūṣāyām |
tatra vicāraḥ -- vikārārtasyāñjasyenopapattyabhāvādityuktyā `idaṃ tu vaktavyam' ityādinā vṛttikṛnmataṃ dūṣayitvā ānandamayaśabde vikāre mayaḍiti ānandamayādhikaraṇe vikāre mayahiti śaṅkarācāryakṛtasiddhānto na samīcinaḥ, vikāre mayaḍiti śāṅkarabhāṣyoktiscintyā `dvyacaśchandasi' (pā.sū.4.3.150) iti chandasi dvyavaca eva vikāre mayaḍvidānāt | ānandaśabdasya tryactvāttadaprapteriti kaiścit śābdikaiḥ kṛtacodyanirāsāya bhāṣyādyanuktasūtrapratyākhyānādyupāyapañcakanirūpaṇaparamanoramāgranthaḥ, etadarthako'nyo'pi gkantho'yukta iti spaṣṭamuktaṃ bhavati |
atra brajhaṇo nirdharmakatvamayuktaṃ tatra mānābhānāt | nanu
eko devaḥ sarvabhūteṣu gūḍhaḥ sarvavyāpī sarvabhūtāntarātmā |
karmādhyakṣaḥ sarvabhūtādhivāsaḥ sākṣī cetā kevalo nirguṇaśca ||
(śve.6.11)
iti śvetāsvataraśrutiḥ pramāṇaṃ, tatra nirguṇaścetyukteḥ nirguṇa ityasya dharmarahita ityarthāditi cet, na, guṇapadasya tatpadavācyasattvādiguṇa iti śābdamartaṃ parityacya aśābdadharmasāmānyārthakatvaṃ mānābhāvāt | na ca guṇapadaṃ vṛttimatparamiti dharmasāmān‌yavācakamiti vācyam, mukhyārthasyābādhena lakṣaṇāyāṃ mānābhāvāt |
sarvendriyaguṇābhāsaṃ sarvendriyavivarjitam |
asaktaṃ sarvabhṛccaiva nirgṛṇaṃ guṇabhoktṛ ca || (gī.13.14)
iti ślokavyākhyāne śīśaṅkarācāryaiḥ nirgṛṇapadasya sattvādiguṇarahitamiti vyākhyānāt atrāpi tasyaivārthasya nyāyyatvācca | yadi nirgṛṇapadaṃ nāgeśoktārthakaṃ, tarhi `eko deva' ityādinā anekaguṇavidhānamanarthakaṃ syāt | na ca ya ekatvādiguṇakaḥ sa nirgṛṇakaḥ sa nirgṛṇa iti uddeśyavidheyabhāvena anvayaḥ tataśca anuvādakatvātpūrveṣāṃ, na vaiyarthyamiti vācyam, evaṃ tarbi nurguṇatvānuvādena ekatvādikaṃ vidhīyate itiyapi vaktuṃ śakyatayā tadiṣṭāsiddheḥ | na ca uddeśyavacanaṃ pūrvaṃ vidheyasya tataḥ param iti nyāyena itthaṃ yojanāyā ayoga iti vātyam `apṛkta ekālpratyayaḥ (pā.sū.1.2.41) samarthaḥ padavidhiḥ' (pā.sū.2.1.1) iti sūtre vidheyasya pūrvanirdeśadarśanena tanniyamasyāsārvatrikatvāt |
kiṃ ca uddesyavideyabhāvenānvaya eva durghaṭaḥ samuccāyakacaśabdaśravaṇāt | ekatvādidharmatadabhāvayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyāyogāt asmatpradarśitayojanaiva jyāyasī | na ca --
namaḥ śāntāya ghorāya mūḍhāya gumakarmaṇe |
nirviśeṣāya sāmyāya namo jñānaghanāya ca || (bhāga.8.3.12)
iti bhāgavate nirviśeṣāyetyukternirdharmakatvaṃ bagavataḥ pramitamiti vācyam, tasya nirvākārāyetyarthena śamādipradānavāsudevādirūpavattva'pi jīvavat guṇakṛtavikāraśūnyatvaṃ tātparyāt | idaṃ hetugarbhaṃ viseṣaṇam, ata eva sāmyāyetyuktam | sarvatra sama iti tattātparyam | śāntāyetyuktireva nirviseṣāyetyasyoktārthe mānam | nirdharmakatvaṃ hi śamoktiḥ kathaṃ saṃgaccheta | evaṃjātīyāḥ śrutismṛtaya evameva vyākhyeyāḥ | śrīdharīye tu `guṇadharmiṇe' iti paṭhitvā sattvādidharmānukāriṇa iti vyākhyātam |

`vyāvahārikamaparuccedyatvam' iti tu na yiktam, śrutau tādṛśārthapadāśravaṇāt | yadyantareṇāpi śabdaṃ tatātvaṃ braṣe tarhi aśābdamarthaṃ kaḥ prekṣāvān śraddadhīta | kiṃ ca satyatvāderapi tacchratibodhitasya vyāvahārikatvāpatyā brajhaṇaḥsatyatvamapi neti śakyaṃ vaktum | ekavākyabodhiteṣu ekasya pāramārthikatvam aparasya vyāvahārikatvamiti kalpanaṃ prabalapramāṇamantarā na śakyam | prācuryasya nirdarmakatvaṃ vyāvahārikāpariccedyacive ṭa vastuto brajhaṇi dharmādi nāstītyevoktaṃ bhavatīti ānando'pi nāstītyuktaṃ bhavet | nāgeseneti tadāśrayaṇaṃ vyartham | prastute vastuni guṇātisayaṃ bodayituṃ hi pracurye mayaṭamāśrayanti dhīmantaḥ prayoktāraḥ | mayaṭaścāpyeṣa svabhāvaḥ yat svavyadhikaraṇe svaturanyasmin prakāsālpatvaṃ pratīyate | kvacitu svādhikaraṇe svavidātīyālpatvabodakatvam, yathā-prakāśamayaḥ savitā, ityādau savituranyasmin prakāśālpatvaṃ pratīyate . kvacittu svādhikaraṇe svavijātīyālpatvabodhakatvam, yathā annamayam, atra annaṃ bahu, taditarat alpamiti pratīyate | yathāpramāṇaṃ prayogeṣu vyavasthā | prakṛte ānandamaya ityatra ānandavijātīyaduḥkhāderbrajhaṇi satve pramāṇābhāvena brajhātirikte cetane ānandālpatvapratītiriti prācuryamayaṭaḥ prayojavaṃ vaktavyaṃ na tu brajhaṇo nirdharmakatvasiddiriti prāmāṇikāḥ |
atra vṛttikṛtpadena kiṃ prasiddho bodhāyanaḥ, utānyaḥ kaścit vivakṣita iti vicāraṇāyāṃ, kecidāhuḥ -- nātra sa bodāyano vivakṣito bhavet yato'tra brajhaṇo nirdharmakatvaṃ prācuryamityuktam | bodhāyanastu brajhaṇo nirdharmakatvam, `satyam' ityādiśrutibodhitāparucchedyatvasyāpāramārthikatvaṃ ca nābhyupaitīti `bhagavadbodāyanakṛtāṃ vistīrṇāṃ brajhasūtravṛttiṃ pūrvacāryāḥ saṃcikṣipuḥ, tadanusāreṇa sūtrākṣarāṇi vyākyāsyanta' iti śrīmadrāmānujamunivaryāṇāṃ vacanabalena niścinumaḥ | yadyapi `eka ātmanaḥ śarīre bhāvāt' (bra.sū.3.3.55) iti sūtraṃ `ata eva ca bhagavatopararṣeṇa pratame tantre ātmāstitvābhidānaprasaktau śārīrake vakṣyāma ityuddhāraḥ kṛta' iti śāghkarabhāṣyekteḥ, vṛttikāro bhagavānupavarṣonāmno bhrātā so'tra vivakṣito'stviti śakyaṃ vaktum, tathāpi tasya brajhaṇo nirdharmakatvavāditāyāṃ pramāṇābhāvaḥ | tarhu ko bhavedatra vṛttikṛditi cet etatsadṛkṣaḥ kaścidvipascit ayameva (nāgeśo) veti gṛhāṇa | spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ ca `gurucarairvyāsasūtravṛttau' iti devatātaddhitārthaprakaraṇasthakalāvyākhyānāt nāgośo brajhasūtrasyāpi vṛttimatānīditi pratīmaḥ |
`vikāre mayaṭ' iti śāṃkarabhāṣyaṃ tu `dvyacaśchandasi' iti niyamasya prāyikatvābhiprāyeṇa pravṛttam | manoramā tu svairaṃpravṛttā na ramayati viduṣāṃ manāṃsi | etena manoramānusāriṇo bodhinyādigranthāsca vyākhyātāḥ |
atra kalāyāṃ `vaktuta' ityādinā mayaḍantānāṃ prācuryāt ekajātīyaprayooganirvāhāya ayamapi atyantasvārthikamaḍhantaḥ prayukta iti sūtrārthaḥ `tatprakṛta' iti sūtre taditi yogavibhāgāt atyantasvārthikasyāpi vidhānamiti upuditaṃ gurucaraṇairvyāsasūtre induśekhare cetyuktam | atredaṃ vaktavyam -- ānandamayādikaraṇe ānandamayaḥ kiṃ paramātmā atha jīva iti brajhaśabdābhyāsāt paramātmeti ādyasūtreṇa siddāntayitvā ānandamayaśabdasya vikārārthakamayaḍantatvena na paramātmā vācya iti prāpte prācuryārthakamaḍantatvāttasya śabdasya paramātmaiva vācya iti `vikāraśabdāt' (bra.sū.1.1.13) ityanenāha sātrakāraḥ, sarve ācāryā api evameva varṇayanti sūtrārtham | evaṃ cātyantasvārthikamaḍataprayogasādhane sūtrakṛdādīnāṃ tātparyamiti na śabdamaryādayā labhyate | klṛptasādhyāṃśānuvṛttiṃ parityajya sādhyāṃśāntarādhyāhāre gauravam | kiṃ ca `tatprakṛta' iti sūtre taditi yogavibhāgo bhāṣye na dṛśyate | bhāṣyādṛṣṭayogavibhāgasyāprāmāṇikatvaṃ vadatā nāgesena tādṛśayogavibhāgamaśritya atyantasvārthikamayaḍvidhānopapādanam, tanmūlakedṛśavrajhasūtrārthavarṇanaṃ ca kathamupapadyeta | kiṃ ca svārthe mayaṭi tātparyakalpane prācuryāditi śabdasvārasyabhaṅgaḥ, tena hi ānandātiśayo brajhaṇi bodhyate kadatiśayaśca ānandavalyāṃ bṛhadāramyakādau ca spaṣṭaṃ pratipādita iti śrīśaṅkarācāryādibhirātāryairbhāṣyādau nipuṇaṃ nirūpitaṃ, lāghavātsvārtāditi vaktavye prācuryāditi vyarthaṃ syādityalam |
vallīdvaye śuddhimantre mayaṭ prācuryabodhakaḥ |
nirviśeṣaṃ paraṃ brajha pracuryaṃ dharmaśūnyatā ||
iti nāgeśavacanaṃ pramāṇavikalaṃ viduḥ |
asaṃmataṃ bhāṣyakṛto nādartavyaṃ sudhījanaiḥ ||
avidyāvicāraḥ
yaccoktaṃ nāgesena mañjīṣāyāṃ prathamaparicchede `prapañco'pyevameva' ityādinā `alaṃ prasaktānuprasaktyā' ityantena brajhādvaitapratipādanāvasare māyā nāma kaścitpadārtho brajhaṇaḥ prapañcasṛṣṭau sahakāriṇī sā ca saṃskārasūharūpā, ata eva jaganmāyikam |jaganmāyikam | avidyā tu tajjanyā, nānādiḥ, atraiva śrīśaṅkarācāryāṇāmapi, na hi brajhātiriktamajaṃ saṃbhavati `sadevasaumyedamagraāsīt' iti `sāmānyāttu' (bra.sū.3.2.32) iti sūtre brajhātiriktavastusāmānyasyā'nāditvaṃ pratiṣedhatāmavidyānāditvasyāsaṃmatatvāvagamāt | kiṃ ca sarvāsaṃmatā'vidyāviṣaye, adyāsabhāṣye praśnottarānupalambhena sā na bhāṣyasaṃmatā, kiṃ ca `anādyavidyāyāḥ kāraṇatve aprāmāṇikavistvabhyupagamaḥ, sarvasya brajhaikakāraṇakatvasiddhāntabhaṅgaḥ, advaitahāniśca' iti doṣānudbhāvya avidyāyā anādibhāvatvam, jagatastatkāraṇakatvaṃ cāyuktamiti |
atra -- `etadviṣayapraśnottarānupalambhāt na sā bhāṣyasaṃmatetyuktyā, anirvācyānāgyavidyāparikalpitaḥ iyamanādiriti nirūḍhā' ityādi bhāmatīkārāṇāṃ miśrāṇāṃ vacanam, avidyāsādhānānumāne svaprāgabhāva' ityādadi viśeṣaṇaṃ deyamiti pañcapādirāvivaraṇakārāṇāṃ vacanam, `prākkoṭirāhityamanāditvamadhyāsasya' iti vārtikakṛdvacanam, anādibhāvarūpatve sati jñānanivartyamavidyātvam' iti citsukhācāryavacanam, `jīva īśo viśuddhā cittathā jīveśayorbhidā avidyā taccito yogaḥ' iti śrīśaṅkaramatānuyāyivṛddhatamānāṃ vacanam, `anādyavidyāvilasitamidaṃ samastaṃ jagat' iti tadvargyāṇāṃ vacanam, tadīyānāmanādyavidyāsādhanodyamanam, `mūlakāraṇāvidyārūpabhāvapadārthanimittaka' iti `mithyājñānanimitta' iti adyāsabhāṣyīyānandagirīyādivyākhyānaṃ ca śrīśaṅkarabhāṣyabhāvāparijñānamūlakamiti sūcitaṃ bhavati | kiṃ ca `evamayamanādirananto naisargiko'dhyāsa' iti adhyāsabhāṣye'dhyāsasyānāditvoktiḥ `na hi brajhātiriktā'jaṃ saṃbhavati' iti sāmānāyāttviti sūtrabhāṣyeṇa viruddhā, tena brajhānyasyā'nāditvaniṣedhāt, `atasmin tadbuddhiradhyāsaḥ tamadhyāsaṃ paṇḍitā avidyeti manyanta' iti bhāṣyeṇa avidyādhyāsayorarthāntaratvābhāvābhidhānāt avidyāyā anāditve etadbhāṣyatātparyāt |
atra vadanti -- `prasaktānupraktyā' ityukteḥ, pāṇinīyavacanānudāhṛteḥ paramārthasāravacanodhṛteśca nirūpaṇamidaṃ na pāṇinimatāturodhena,, kiṃ tu paramārthasārakṛnmatānurodeneti pratīyate |. tatra ca brajhātiriktānādivastu neti noktam | kiṃ tu anādeḥ prakṛteḥparaḥ (uttamo bhinnaśca) viṣṇunārāyaṇavāsudevopendraharyādipadābhidheyo bhagavān paraṃ brajha, prakṛtimāyāvibhūtyādipadavācyasvabhāvabhūtasvaśaktyā vicitravividaprapañcaṃ nirmimīte | sā ca śaktiranādirviṣṇoradhīnā acetanā ata eva na svatantrā, jagatsṛṣṭyādau viṣṇoḥ sahakāriṇī, evaṃ vidānārāyamavijñānaṃ muktisādhanamiti nirūpitam | kiṃ ca `paraṃparasyāḥ prakṛteḥ' ityādyāpadyena, `svabhāvabhūtā' ityasya svato āryayā ca māyāyā anāditoktā, ` svātantryam' iti vyākyāne rāghavānandamunikṛte anādtvokteḥ mūlasyāpi tatraiva tātparyāt | prakṛterapi sadātanatvamarthachasddhamiti ārdyapadyatātparyam |
nanu aprāmāṇikamanādibhāvarūpāvidyākhyaṃ vastviti cet na, `anādimāyayā supta' ityādipramāṇasiddhatvāt | na ca anāditve sarvasya brajhaikakāraṇakatvasiddhān‌tabhaṅga iti vācyam, parādhīnasattākatvamātreṇa tatkāramakatvopapatteḥ | na ca advaitahāniḥ, prāgabhāvavat asyā nāśadarśanenā'nantatvābhāvāt anādyanantavasitusattva eva taddoṣāt | yadvā samānasattākavastvantarasatve taddoṣaprasaṅgaḥ, avidyāyā anāditve'pi brahasattāsamānasattākatvābhāvāt nāsya doṣasyāvakāśa iti siddhāntaleśasaṃgrahavyākyānādita'vagamyate | anādivāsanārūpā māyā triguṇā `ajāmekām' iti śrutipratipādyā, brajhaikakāramakaṃ jagaditi vadatā bhavatāpi evameva vaktavyam | anyathā māyāyāḥ sahakāriṇītve brajhaikakāraṇakatvabhaṅgaḥ | tasya janyatve ajāmiti śrutivirodhaḥ, tayā tasyā ajanyatvokteḥ | śrutyā cānāditvasiddhau advaitahāniriti doṣaḥ tavāpi mate āpatati | na ca asmin kalpe'janyamiti śratyarthena na doṣa iti vācyam, pratikalpamasyāḥ śruteḥ sattvena tatrāpi atra kalpe'janyamityarthavanarṇanasaṃbhavena anāditvasyaiva sddheḥ | māyāyāḥ sahakāriṇītvābhāve jagato māyikatvoktirasaṃgatā syāt |
`na hi brajhātiriktamajaṃ saṃbhavati'iti bhāṣyasya tu tadatiriktaṃ svatantramajaṃ nāsitītyarthenā'doṣa iti tatsaṃpradāyavidaḥ | ata eva śāṅkare gītābhāṣyeye `prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ caiva viddhyanādī ubhāvapi' (gī.13.19) iti ślokavyākhyāvasare īśvarasyeśvaratvamanupapannam' ityuktam | prakṛtipuruṣayoranāditve'pi īśvarādhīnasattākatvamasti īśvarasya sarvasvāmitvāt | tataśca tasyesvaratvamupapadyate iti tadbhāvaḥ | spaṣṭīkṛtametat gūḍhārthadīpikāyām, śrīdharīye ca tacchlokārthavarṇanāvasare |
advaitasiddhau ca dṛṣṭisṛṣṭivāde sarveṣāṃ sṛṣṭatve `jīva īśo viśūddhā cit' iti prācāṃ vacanavirodhaḥ | tatra jīvādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāmanā ditvoktayā teṣāmajanyatvādityāśaṅkya anādyatiriktasṛṣṭiviṣaya eva dṛṣṭisṛṣṭisvīkārāditi samāhitam, `jīvabrajhavibhāgasyā'nāditvena' iti tatraiva vāde spaṣṭam, tatraiva avidyāyāḥ cinmātrāśrayatvavicāre nāpi jīvasya sāditvāpattiḥ upādhibimbasaṃbandhānāditvenā'nāditvopapatteḥ' iti brajhātiriktasya saṃbandhasya, jīvasya cā'nāditvamuktam | ata eva `sa ca pratyagatmā'nādisiddhaḥ tasminnaisargikatvasyā'nāditvamarthasiddham' ityadhyāsabhāṣyapañcapādikāyāṃ pajhapādācāryairuktaṃ pratyagātmano'nāditvam |
prajāpatipatiṃ viṣṇumanantamaparājitam |
pradhānapuṃsorajayoḥ kāraṇaṃ kāryabhūtayoḥ ||
tasmādabyaktamutpannaṃ triguṇaṃ dvijasattama |
avyaktaṃ puruṣe brajhannirguṇa saṃpralīyate ||
ādirnāstyātmanaḥ kṣetrapāraṃparyamanādikam |
prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ caiva kṣetraṃ kṣetrajñameva ca ||
sthūlaṃ vilāpya karaṇe karaṇaṃ nidāne
tatkāraṇaṃ karaṇakāraṇarjite ca |
itthaṃ vilāpya yaminaḥ praviśanti yatra
taṃ tvāṃ hari vimasabodhaghanaṃ namāmaḥ ||
iti avidyāyā janyatve ma़ñjūṣoktapramāṇānāṃ na tatra tātparyam |
tathā hi --
atra--ādye ajayorityuktyā pradhānapuruṣayoranāditvamuktam | kāryabhūtayorityasya abhivyaktayorityarthaḥ māyāyā īśvarādvirbhāva iti nāgeśoktadiśā anayorapyāvirbhāva iti vaktaṃ śakyatvāt | dvitīye avyaktamutpannamityapi āvirbhūtamityarthakameva | tṛtīye turye ca kṣetrapadaṃ prakṛtikāryadehaparam | yathā māyāyā īśvare layaḥ saṃbandhaviśeṣa eveti nāgeśo'vadat, lathā harau prakṛtirūpakāraṇasya layasaṃbandhaviśeṣa eva iti anyepi prabhavanti | ataḥ etāni kathamuktārthe pramāṇāni syuriti vibhāvyatām | kiṃ ca anādyavidyāsahakāreṇa īśvaraḥ sṛjatītyaṅkīkāre'pi svatantravastvantarānapekṣatvamātreṇa prapañcasya ekakāraṇakatvasiddhaḥ | etatpraghaṭṭake `uktajātīyaśrutīnāṃ maduktavyākhyaivocitā' iti nāgeśoktyā ca anāditvavādināṃ śrutiyojanā anyādṛśīti bhāti |
kiṃ ca vaiṣamyanairghuṇyādhikaraṇa (bra.sū.2.1.12) śāṅkarabhāṣye brajhātiriktasṛṣṭihetvanādivastusattā pratipāditā | kiṃ ca `bhramātsaṃskārato'pyanyā maṇḍūkamadudāhṛteḥ | avidyā'nādibhāvātmā matā vācaspateriha' iti devatādhikaraṇīyakalpataruṇāpi anādibhāvarūpā'vidyā vācaspatisaṃmatetyuktam | tatrehetyasya śrīśāṅkarīyāṇāṃ mate ityarthaḥ, devatādhikaraṇa iti saṃkoce kāraṇabhāva iti pūrvaprakaraṇe nirūpitam | nanu parādhīnasattākatvaṃ janyatvamityaṅkīkāre viyadāderapi tathātvābhyupagamena janyatvaṃ suvacamiti viyadāderutpattisādhanaprayāso vyartha iti viyadadhikaraṇānutthitiprasaṃga iti cet, ucyate -- `ātmana ākāśa' ityādāvakāśasyotpattiśravaṇāt tadanutpattivādaḥ śrutiviruddha iti bodhanāya tatsamarthanamānaśyakam, avidyāyā utpatteḥ kvāpyaśravaṇena `anādi māyayā' ityanāditvaśravaṇena brajhaṇaḥ sarvaniyākatvaśravaṇena ca evamaṅkīkṛtamiti | adhikamadvaitagrantheṣu draṣṭavyam |
kiṃ ca `asti karmākyamanādivastu śubhā'śubhaṃ prāṇināṃ janmāntarakāraṇam' ityarthaka `na karmā'vibhāgāditi cennā'nāditvāt' (bra.sū.2.1.36) iti vyāsasūtravirūddhaṃ nāgeśavacavam | tena ca sūtreṇā'nādivastu kiṃcidasti' iti pratipādanāt |
avidyāyā anādibhāvatvakathanaṃ `yāvadavikāraṃ tu vibhāgo lokavat' (bra.sū.2.3.8) iti sūtreṇa kāryatvasya bhedavyāpyatvabodhakena ākāśasyevādiyāyā api atathātva (kāryatva) bodhanāt tadviruddhamiti mañjūṣoktaṃ dūṣaṇamapi na samīcīnam, ākāśādibhūtotpattiviṣaya eva vicārasya tatra `brajhaṇo'dharmakatvena kiṃcinnirūpitabhedo brajhaṇī na iti na tasya janyatnam' iti, taccintyam, brajhaṇo'dharmakatvaṃ vadacosya mate pratiyogitvasyāpi dharmasya brajhaṇī asaṃbhavena avidyāyāṃ śuddhabrajhabhedasya sattvāt, kāryatvamāyāti' iti svoktivirodhāt | tataśca brajhaṇo'pi kiṃcinnirūpitabhedavattā suvaceti tacasyāpi kāryatvāpātaḥ | kiṃ ca `yāvadvikāraṃ tu' iti sūtrabhāmatīkalpataruparimalādgranthānavalokanena vā, tatra taduktamasaṃgatamiti pratipipādayiṣayā vā, ayamatrā'vidyānāditvaṃ dūṣayatīti bhāti | tatra hi sūtre bhāmatyā brajhā (ātmā) nyatve sati vibhaktatvāditi heturuktaḥ, kalpatararau ca -- avidyādāvityādinā `ṣaḍasmākamanādaya' ityuktajīveśvarādyarthāntarasaṃgraha ityuktam | etena prabandhatrayeṇa avidyādyānāditvaṃ bhāṣyakṛtsaṃmatamiti pratipadyate | miśrādayo hi na kadācidbhāṣyakṛdanabhimatamarthaṃ pratipādayeyuḥ, bhāṣye'pyavidyādīnāmanāditvaṃ tatra tatrā'bhihitam | ato `na sā bhāṣyasaṃmatā' iti nāgeśoktiścintyaiva | etena `saṃbhṛṣṭādhyāsa' iti ānandagirūyādhyāsabhāṣyavyāśyānam, `mūlakāraṇāvidyārūpabhāvapadīrthanimittaka ityarthadha' iti `mithyā jñānanumitta' ityanyeṣāmadhyāsabhāṣyavyākyānaṃ cānūdya `tanna' ityādinā taddūṣaṇamapi cinnyam | avidyānāditvasya bhāṣyasaṃmatatvokteḥ | ayaṃ tāvanmahadbhirācāryairvirṛtārthāni sūtrāgyakṣarāṇi anyathā yojayatīti suvyaktaṃ mañjūṣādṛśām | atredamudāharaṇam--`pratijñāhāniravyatirekācchabdebhyaḥ' (bra.sū.2.3.7) iti sūtre akārapraśleṣamakṛtvā pratijñābhahkaḥ syāt iti vyākhyā pratijñāhānyāpādanaparatayā sūtrakhaṇḍaṃ yojayitvā ahāniriti akārapraśleṣeṇa pratijñāyā ahāniranuparodha iti śrīśaṅkarācāryakṛtasūtravyākhyānādalmadīyacaṃ vyāśyānaṃ varamiti sūcayati nāgeśaḥ | yadyapi akārapraśaleṣatadabhāvayoḥ phalāviśeṣaḥ, athāpi sautrākṣarāṇi yojayituṃ vayameva samartā iti vaiduṣyābhimānapradarśanameva prayojanamiti veditavyam | evamanena bahūnāṃ sūtrāṇāṃ svasamīhitārthaparatayā vilakṣaṇā yojanākṛtā | sā ca etatkṛtavyāsasūtrendunāmakagranthato'vaseyā | idaṃ jñeyamavidyāpadārthasvarūpanirṇayaviṣaye advaitcāryāṇāṃ pramāda eveti bodhanāyaiva brajhādvaitanirūpaṇamupakrāntaṃ mañjūṣāyāmiti akṣarārthamārmikā iti |
avidyābhramasaṃskārarūpā śaṅkarasaṃmatā |
na tvānādirbhāvarūpetyāha nāgeśapaṇḍitaḥ ||
taccā'dvaitamatācāryabahagranthavirodhataḥ |
na yuktamiti manyante śrīśaṅkaramatānugāḥ ||
anirvacanīyatvavādavicāraḥ
yacca mañjūṣāyāṃ dvividhaprapañcasya māyikatvaṃ prasādhya `mayāmātramiti mātrapadena anirvacanīyotpattivādamapi nirākaroti' ityādinā śuktirajatādyanirvacanīyapadārthasvīkartṛśrīśaṅkarācāryamatā'nuyāyināmadvaitācāryāṇāṃ matanirasanam, etacca pratividhāste | pratividhānaṃ ca matadvayaniṣkarṣasāpekṣamiti prathamaṃ sa saṃkṣityate -- tatra--adhyāsabhāṣyavyākyāyāṃ bhāmatyāṃ `mṛgatṛṣṇājalādi na sat, nā'sam, nāpi na sadasat (kiṃtu) nirvacanūyam' iti miśragranthas‌ya, evaṃjātīyakasyā'nyeṣāmadvaitācāryāṇāṃ granthasya, advaitasiddhau ajñānavāde'nirvacanīyatvalakṣaṇanirūpaṇaprakaraṇasthasya `asadvilakṣaṇatve sati sadvilakṣaṇatve ca sati sadasadilakṣaṇatvam' iti granthasya ca paryālocanāyāṃ, sat sat, anirvacanīyamiti vastu trividhaṃ, tatra sat-brajha, asat-śaśaviṣāṇādi, anirvacanīyaṃ tu śuktirūpyaṃ, jagacca | tatrādyayornetpattiḥ, tṛtīyaṃ tu utpattimat, sadasadvilakṣaṇatvamanirvacanīyatvamiti vastusthitiradvaitināṃ mate | nāgeśasya mate tu-sat, asaditi dvividhameva vastu, tatra brajha sat asattu brajhānyatsarvam, tacca-jāgratprapañcaḥ, svānpo'rthaḥ, śuktirajatādi, śaśaviṣāṇādi | anirvacaniyatvaṃ ca-sattvena nirvaktuṃ, asattvena ca nirvaktumaśakyaṃ yat tattvam | śrīśaṅkarādaitācāryāṇāmabhimatamanirvacanīyatvalakṣaṇaṃ tu na yuktam, na sūtrakṛdvyāsasaṃmatam | māyāmātreti mātretyukteriti sthitiḥ |

tatra `sadasadbhinnatvamanirvacanīyatvam' iti advaityabhimatasyā'nirvacanīyatvalakṣaṇasya sadbhinnatve'sattvaṃ prānpoti asadbhinnatve ca satvamiti sattvāsattvo'bhayānākrāntavastvasiddhiriti dūṣaṇamayuktam, asya doṣasya nāgeśamate'pi saṃbhavāt, `yaścobhayoḥ samo doṣaḥ parihāro'pi vā samaḥ' iti nyāyāt | tathā hi--jagadasaditi vadannāgeśaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ kiṃ bhavān śaśaviṣāṇādivat jagadasaditi manyate, uta satpadavācyabrajhānyaditi | nādyaḥ, jagato'ḍarthakriyākāritvena śaśaviṣāṇatulyatvābhāvāt | na dvitīyaḥ, brajhabhedasya jagati sarvairaṅkīkārāt, kasyāpyaniṣṭābhāvāt | evaṃ ca jagati bhavatā brajha śaśaviṣāṇabhedo'ṅ‌gīkṛto bhavatīti śrīśaṅkaramatānuyāyyadvaitācāryasaṃmatobhayabhedavailakṣaṇyābhāvena teṣāṃ mate bhavato'ddhāṭito doṣaḥ tava mate'pyāpatatīti | kiṃ ca jagato'sattve `asattvena nirvaktumaśakyam' iti bhaṅāṣaṇaṃ vyāhatam | asya tannirvacanarūpatvāt | kiṃ ca asattvena nirvaktumaśakyatve satvaṃ prānpotīti na jagato'sattvāsiddhiḥ | yaccoktaṃ tadananyatvasūtreṇa, māyāmātreti sūtreṇa ca māyikatvasyaiva siddheriti tadapi na | tābhyāṃ sūtrābhyāṃ tathā'lābha iti, mātrapadasya kevasārthakatvaṃ neti ca uttaraprakaraṇayornirūpayiṣyate | svotprekṣitameva śrīśaṅkarīcāryāṇāmapi saṃmatamiti bodhanāya `avidyāyā kalpitā' ityādibhāṣyamevameva vyākyeyam | `īśvarasyātmabhūte anirvacanīyā' ityādibhāṣyasyapyayamevārthaḥ | evajātīyakamanyadapi bhāṣyamevameva vyākhyeyam' ityādinā keṣāṃcidbhāṣyavākyānāṃ svasamīhitārthaparatayā'rthavarṇanana tatraivamevetyādikathanena ca prācāmācāryadeśīyānāṃ tadbhaṣyāvyākyānadūṣaṇam, tadapi na saheran ācāryacaraṇaśaraṇā advaitinaḥ | taduktārthasya bhāṣyakṛdādyācāryāsaṃmatatvāt, yuktiyuktatvābhāvācca | atra nāgeśavyākhyānasya prācāṃ vyākhyānasya ca yuktāyuktavicāro granthagauravabhiyā na kṛtaḥ | khyātibādhānyathānupapattyā ca anirvacanīyaṃ vastu aṃgīkartavyamiti advaitināṃ grantheṣu spaṣṭam | paramārtasāravyākyākṛdrāghavānandamunirapi jagataḥ sadasadanyatvameveti vadati | idaṃ tu bodhyam--śrīvyāsapāṇinipatañjalibhirjagato'sattvādyanukternedamasattvādi vyāsādyabhimatam, `asatyamapratiṣṭhaṃ te' (gī.) ityādyukteḥ | jagato'nirvācyatvavyavahārastu padārtānāṃ hānopādānahetubhūtāvāṅamanasagocaravilakṣamasvābhāvikadharmavattveneti |
jagato brajhavatsattvaṃ nā'sattvaṃ śaśaśṛṅgavat |
tasmāttadubhayānyattvaṃ yuktaṃ śāstrāvirodhi ca ||
ato'dvaitamatācāryaistathoktasya ca dūṣaṇam |
nāgeśapaṇḍitakṛtaṃ rocate na vidāṃ hṛde ||
jaganmāyikatve tadananyatvasūtrasaṃmatinirāsaḥ
yadapyuktaṃ mañjūṣāyāṃ "yadyapi jāgratprapañcasyāpi `tadananyatvamārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ' (bra.sū.2.1.14) iti sūtreṇa māyāmātratvamucyata" iti, atra vadanti -- na tena sūtreṇa jagato māyikatvamucyace, tatra tādṛśārthakapadābhāvāt, ananyatvoktistu `mukhaṃ candra' ityātvameva bodhayatīti śakyaṃ vaktum | `yenā'śrutam, vācārambhaṇam' (cā.6) ityādipratijñādṛṣṭāntavākyayokapi māyikārthakapadāśravaṇāttābhyāmapi na jagatastathātvāsiddhiḥ | na ca ananyatvoktireva prapañcasya vastutvābhāvaṃ bodhayantī māyikatvamapi gamayatīti vācyam, roke tathā'nubhavādikaṃ pratīyate iti vakti | kiṃ ca ghaṭapaṭādiprapa़ñcasyā'cetanatvena brajhaṇaścetanatvena cetanācetanayorananyatvāyogāt tathokteḥ kāryasya kāraṇā'vinābhāve tātparyamiti vaktavyam | avinābhāvaśca `tatsṛṣṭvā tadevānuprāviśat (tai.u.2) viṣṭabhyāhamidaṃ kṛtstramekāṃśena sthito jagat' (gī.10) iti śrutismṛtipramāṇapramitaḥ | kiṃ ca ghaṭaśarāvādayaḥ sarve vikārā naikamṛtpiṇḍaprabhavā iti sarvasākṣisiddham |
śrutyartastu ekena-vijñāveneścareṇa, avijñātama-ajñātamapi vastu, vijñātam--sādṛsyādvijñātaṃ bhavati | atra sādṛsyaprayojakadharmaḥ sattvam | vikāraḥ kāryajātam ghaṭaśarāvādi, vācāraṃbhaṇam - vāgindrayeṇa, bhinnabhinnaśabdena vyavahrayate, ghaṭādīnāṃ sarveṣāmapi mṛttiketyapi nāmadheyamasti, mṛdvikāratvāt satyam vāstavamiti | ayaṃ bāvaḥ - ghaṭaśarāvādīnāmākārabhedena bhinnaśabdena vyavahāre'pi kāramaguṇasya tadaṃśasya ca kārye'pi sattvena kāramavācakaśabdenāpi vyavahāraḥ saṃpratipanna iti | kāramaguṇasaṃparkaḥ kārye'pyastīti mahākaviḥ śrīharṣe'pyāha--
svargāpagā hemamṛṇālanīnāṃ nālāmṛṇalāgrabhujo bhajāmaḥ |
annānurūpāṃ tanurūpaṛddhi kāryaṃ nidānāddhi guṇānadhīte ||
(naiṣadhaṃ.3.17)
iti | ślokārthaḥ spaṣṭaḥ | yadvā aśrataṃ-śrutaphalaśūnyam, yena śrutenaśrutena paramātmanā, śrutaṃ-śrutaphalakaṃ bhavatītyartaḥ | kevalakarmakāṇḍādśravāṇādi na phalāya bhavati, paramātmāśravaṇādau tu phalavadbhavati | jagajjñānaṃ jñāte brajhaṇi jagadīśvaratvajñānakāraṇamiti īsvarajhānena jagajjñānaṃ saphalamiti tātparyam | mṛtkārye ghaṭādau mṛttvena jñāte tatra nikṣiptaṃ dadhyādi śuktādidoṣayuktaṃ na bhavatīti niścayāt tatra dadhyādi nikṣepe pravṛttirjāyate |
kiṃ ca anena pratijñādṛṣṭāntena īśvarādanyat jñātavyaṃ vastu astītyeva jñāyate, na tu anyannāstīti | yadyanyasya jñeyasyā'bhāve tātparyaṃ, tadā'vijñātāmityādipadasvāsyabhaṅkaḥ, yenā'śrutaṃ netyetāvātā taveṣṭasiddhiḥ `vācaiva kevalamārabhyate vāgalambanamātraṃ na vastvasti kiṃcit' iti nākṣa ramaryādayā labhyate | kiṃ ca kāraṇānanyatva kāryasya pariṇāmikāṇasthala eva na tu nimittakāraṇasthalaṃ | īśvarastu jagato nimittakāraṇāmiti pūrvameva avocāma | ato'nanyatvokterasmadukta evārthe tātpartya yuktam | etena `tatttprāṇikarmavaśādvibhramarūpāṃ sarṛṣṭiṃ sa karoti' ityapi cntyam, jagato vibhramarūpatve mānābhāvāt | sṛṣṭeḥ pūrvaṃ praṇinām (īsvarātiriktajīvānāmḥ abhāvena `prāṇikarmavaśāt' iti ca na yuktam | kiṃ ca vāsanārūpabhramasya dharmatvena āśrayamantarā sthitirdurnirūpā | na ca īśvara āśrayaḥ tasya nirdharmakatvena yathārthajñānavattvena ca bhramāyogāt | ātmaikatvavādinastasya mate īsvarānyajīvasya sṛṣṭeḥ pūrvabhāvena sabramāśraya ityapi na | tataśca āśrayābhāvena vāsanā'stitvaṃ durghaṭam | uktaṃ ca śrīśaṅkarācāryaiḥ `na bhāvo'nupalabdheḥ' (bra.sū.2.2.30) iti sūtre `api ca vāsanā nāma saṃskāsaviśeṣaḥ | saṃskāraśca nā''śrayamantareṇā'vakalpate, evaṃ loke dṛṣṭatvāt' iti | ataḥ kasyacidapi vastuno māyikatvaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyam | na ca ādhyāsirasaṃbandena brajhaiva vāsanārūpāvidyāśraya iti vācyam | adyāsasāyā''roparūpatvena adhyastasya ca mithyātvena brajhaṇi bhramo nāstyevetyuktaṃ bhavatīti tasya kartṛtvāyogāt | ādisṛṣṭau vāsanāsitatvasya durvacatvācca | na ca tadā janmāntarīyā sā gṛjhata iti vācyam | tathā sati tasyā anāditvāpatteḥ | brajhā'nyānādivastu nirākurvatā evaṃ vaktumaśakyatvāt | kiṃ ca jagato brajhānanyatvaṃ satyatvaṃ, cetanatvaṃ ca syāt | mṛtpiṇḍadṛṣṭānto'pi mṛtkāryasya ghaṭādermṛttvena jñānamātre tātparyavān vijñātamiti tadaśravaṇāt | śābdasyārthasya parityāge aśābdasyārtasya parigrahe kāraṇābhāvāt | etena bhogyavastujātasya `bhoktā bhogya śrutyupanyāsena brajhatvoktirapi cintyā | śrutau brajhatvavyapadeśastu tadadhīnatvānyāyena rājārāṣṭramitivat | anyatā bhogyajaḍavastuno'pi cetanatvāpattiḥ | kiṃ ca ---
asatyamapratiṣṭaṃ te jagadāhuranīśvaram |
aparasparasaṃbhūtaṃ kimanyatkāmahaitukam ||
etāṃ dṛṣṭimavaṣṭabhya naṣṭātmano'lpabuddhayaḥ |
prabhāvantyugrakarmāṇaḥ kṣayāya jagato'hitāḥ || (gū.16.8,9)
iti gītāvacanavirodhaḥ | asurasvabhāvā eva jagadasatyamityāhuḥ etada eva te jagataḥ ahitāḥ śatrava ityarthaḥ gītāyāḥ | teṣāṃ svarūpamāha--
pravṛttiṃ ca nivṛttiṃ ca janā na vidurāsurāḥ |
na śautaṃ nāpi tātāro na satyaṃ teṣu vidyate || (gī.16.7)
ityanena jagadasatyatvavādino janasya śaucācārasatyavacanā'bhyudayaniḥ- śreyasasādhanapravṛttinivṛttidharmajñānāvidhuratā svarūpamiti bhagavadāśayaḥ | `ye jagadasatyaṃ vadanti te'nṛtavādino māyinaḥ' iti śrīśahkarācāryā gītārthamāhuḥ | śrutirapyeṣā bhagavatī `kavirmanīṣī paribhyaḥ' (ī.8) iti yathārthān padārthān sṛjati bhagavāniti spaṣṭaṃ vadati | kiṃ ca asya sūtrasya jagato māyikatve tātparyavarṇanaṃ `tadantarapratipattau raṃhatisaṃpariṣvaktaśnanirūpaṇābhyam' (bra.sū.3.1.1) iti tārtiyīkaprathamapādīyabahvadhikaraṇaviruddham | tatra hi--jagato māyātiriktā'vādipadārthamapādārthopādānakatvamuktam | vettha yadito'dhi prajāḥ prayanti, vettha yathā punarāvartante, vettha pathordevayānasya pitṛyāṇasya ca vyāvartanā, vettha yathā pañcamyāmāhutau puruṣavacaso bhavanti' (chā.5.2) iti chandogānāmāmnāye abodibhūtānyeva śarīrotpaktau kāraṇamiti abhihitam | evaṃ vājasaneye aṣṭame dvitīyasmīnbrājhaṇe'pi śrūyate | paramārthasāravyākyāne rāghavānandamuninā prapañcasya brajhānanyatvaṃ tu ` parasya prapañcāvasthitihetutvāt' iti ādyapadyavyākhyānāvasare pratipāditam |
nanu
bhāvādvaitaṃ kriyādvaitaṃ dravyādvaitaṃ tathātmanaḥ |
vartayantsvānubhūtyeha trīn svanpāndhunute muniḥ || (bhāgā.7.15)
iti bhagavatā bādarāyaṇena sarvātamucyata iti jagato māyikatvasiddhiriti na bhramitavyam | bhāgavatasya kāryakāraṇayorvidyamānabhagavadrūpāṇāṃ bhedābhāvarūpabhāvādvaitam, itaradevatārādhanaṃ na kāryamiti kriyādvaitam, ātmādīnāmisātiriktānadinatvarūpaṃ dravyādvaitaṃ ca cintayet | evaṃ bhāvayan trīn svanpān jāgradādyavastaṃ na prānpoti mukto bhavatītyartāt . evaṃ ca svarūpaikyaparatayā vyākhyānaṃ cintyam | asya ślokasyārthavivaraṇarāṇāmuktaraślokānāmayamevābhiprāyaḥ, na tu kāryakāraṇayoraikye tātparyam | `tatsṛṣṭvā tadevānuprāviśat' iti śrutiḥ kāryeṣu bhagavadastitvabodhikā | bhagavān sūtrakāro'pi kāraṇatvena cākāsādiṣu yathāvyapadiṣṭokteḥ (bra.sū.1.4.15) iti sūtreṇa ākāśādiṣvapi tatkāryāṇāṃ vāyvādīnāṃ brajhakāraṇakatvasiddhaye brajhāstitvamāha | ata evoktamabhiyuktaiḥ `bhūtaṃ bhūtābhimānī ca taddeho'ntarniyāmaka' iti | nanu anyaistīrthakarairevaṃ sūtraśayo na varṇita iti cet, bramaḥ -- sarvaiṃrācāryaiḥ sarveṣā brajhakāramakatvaśrautasiddāntaparipālanāya ayamarto'ṅgīkāryaḥ ayaṃ ca śrautasiddhāntaḥ | `yato vā imāni bhūtāni, ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ, tattejo'sṛjat' ityādayaḥ śrutayo hi ākāsada sarvaṃ brajhakāraṇakaṃ vadanti | tatsṛṣṭvā tadevā'nuprāviśat ityādayastu hrajhaṇo'nuprayeśaṃ sṛjyamāneṣu sarveṣu bodhayanti | bagavān sūtrakāraśca `janmādyasya yata' sūtreṇa dṛsyamānaṃ sarvaṃ hrajhakāraṇakamiti siddhāntayati | `kāraṇatvena' iti sūtre śrīśaṅkarācārya api `kvacidākāśasya, kvacittejasaḥ, kvacidapāṃ prathamaṃ sṛṣṭiḥ śrūyate, ataḥ sṛjyamānānāṃ krameṃ vigānepi na straṣṭari vigānaṃ sarvatreṣvarādeva sṛṣṭyukteḥ' iti sarvasya brajhakāramakatvameveti nirṇayanti | evaṃ ca sarvasya brajhakāraṇakatve śrutitātparyam | yadi sṛjyamāneṣvākāśādiṣu na bhagavadrūpamasti, tadā agnyāderbhagavatsṛṣṭatvaṃ kathaṃ syāt ? kathaṃ ca s‌raṣṭari vigānaṃ na syāt ? kathaṃ ca śrautasiddhāntaḥ sūtrakṛtsiddhāntaśca saṃgaccheteti vibhāvyatām |
tadananyatvasūtreṇa kathyate sāyikaṃ jagat |
iti nāgeśaviduṣo bhāṣaṇaṃ naiva yujyate ||
śrutau sūtre tādṛśārthapadaṃ yasmānna vartate |
tasmādasābdamarthaṃ taṃ ślāghante na hi paṇḍitāḥ ||
māyāmātretsūtravicāraḥ
yadatra svānpasya māyikatve `māyāmātram' (3.2.3) iti sūtropanyasanaṃ, tadapi cintyam, tatsūtrasya tatra tātparyābhāvāt | tathāhi--tatra māyāpadamiśvarīyavicitraśaktiparam,
ekastvameva bhagavannidamātmamāyayā
māyākyayoruguṇayā mahadādyaśeṣam |
sṛṣṭvā'nuviśya puruṣastadasadguṇeṣu
nāneva dārūṣu vibhāvasuvadvibhāsi (bhāga.4.9.7)
ityatra vicitresvaraśaktermāyeti abhidhetyuktatvāt paramārtasārasyākyāne vyākhyānātya mātrapadaṃ mādhātoḥ `hayāmāśrubhasibhyastran' ityuṇādisūtreṇa karmaṇi trani nirmitamityarthakam | `na tatra rathāḥ, atha rathāḥ' (bṛ.4.3.10) ityādiviṣayavākyabalena rathādikamiti śeṣaḥ sandhye nirmātāraṃ ceti vaktate | nirmātretitṛtiyāntatayā vipariṇamyate | ayamarthaḥ - nirmātrā-parameṣvareṇa svānparathādikaṃ, māyāmātram-tadīyayā vicitraśaktyā nirmitam, kātyarnyenānabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt,-jāgratpadārthavat sāmastyena acitadeśakālavṛttitvena anabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt bhāsamānasvarūpatvāditi | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - svanpadraṣṭṛpurūṣūyatāktālikānubhavamātrayogyaṃ svāpnaṃ vastu sṛjati īsvaraḥ, tadīyaśaktervaicitryaṃ ca tathāvidhavastūtpādatvamiti | atha vā-- māyāpadamīsvarīyecchāparam,
mahāmāyetyavidyeti nimatirmohanīti ca |
prakṛtirvāsanetyeva tavecchā'nanta kathyate ||
iti abhiyuktodāhtapramāṇe bhagavadicchāparyāyeṣu paṭhitāyāḥ prakṛteḥ `māyāṃ tadu prakṛtiṃ vidyānmāyinaṃ tu maheśvaram' (svaṃ.) iti śrutau māyātvokteḥ | mātrapade karmaṇi trannantam | paramātmanā icchayā nirmitaṃ svānpagajādikamiti sūtrārthaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ pūrvavat | icchārthe māyāpadasya prayogo'pi mahākavibhāravikṛtau kirātārjunīye---
atha svamāyākṛtamandirojjvalaṃ
jvalanmaṇiṃ vyomasadāṃ sanātanam |
surāṅkanā gopaticāpagopuraṃ
puraṃ vanānāṃ vijihīrṣayā jahuḥ || (8.1)
iti | svamāyayā-sveccāviseṣeṇeti mallināthaḥ | gopaticāpaḥ -- indradhaniḥ, tadvarṇasadṛśavarṇagopuraśobhitaṃ puraṃ jahariti saṃbandaḥ | `icchāmātraṃ prabhoḥsṛṣṭiriti sṛṣṭau viniśicitā' (māṃ) iti sṛṣṭisāmānyasya bhagavadicchāmūlakatvokteḥ | astu vā māyāpadaṃ vāsanāparaṃ tathāpi tannirmitasya ca satyatvāt svānpasya jāgkatpadārthavadrarthākriyākāritvāt | ata eva-jāgratīva svapne'pi saṃbhāvitanārīsaṃyogādinā caramadhātuskhalanaṃ saṃbhavati | `darśanaṃ sūcakaṃ tacca svakūpeṇa sat, asattu dṛśyam, ata eva strīdarśanasvarūpalṛsādhyāscaramadhātuvisargadayaḥ jāgradavasthāyāmanuvartante | strīsādhyāstu mālyavilepanadantakṣatādayo nānuvartante' iti bāmatī `sīcakaśca hi-' iti sūtre ubhayorvaiṣamyaṃ spaṣṭīkṛtam | ata eva ca maharṣayaḥ svapne paradārābhimarśanādeḥ pāpahetutvaṃ smarantaḥ smaranti saṃbhāvitataddoṣa,rihārāya prāyascittam, varṇināṃ yatīnāṃ ca prāyaścittaviṣeṣaṃ ca | ata eva ca -- ittamapuruṣalakṣaṇe `svapne'pyanyavadhūkatā' iti svānpaparadārakathābhāvadiḥ niveśitaḥ | yadi svānpamasat tarhi paradārābhimarśanādeḥ pāhetutvasmaraṇaṃ tasya prāyaścittasamraṇaṃ ca vyarthaṃ syāt | kiṃ ca svapnaṃ caramadātuniḥsaraṇena saṃdātavyādheḥ cikitsāṃ vidadhata āyurvedavida iti sarveṣāmanubhavasiddham, yadi svānpamasatsyāt tena vyādhirnotpadyeta tasyārthakriyākāritvāyogāttasya cikitsāvidānaṃ vyarthaṃ bhaveti | `yanme'dya retaḥ pṛthivīmaskān | yadoṣadhīrapyasarat yadāpaḥ | idaṃ tatpunarādade | dīrghāyutvāya varcase' iti (tai.ā.1.1.24) | pramādāt skannasya retasaḥ punarādānatatphaloktirnaṃ saṃgaccheteti jñeyam, iyaṃ śautiḥ | iyānviśoṣo jāgratsvānpalikāni, svānpavastūni tu sarvāṇi acirakālikānyeveti | mahākavikālidāsādibhiḥ ---
svanpo nu māyā nu matibhramo nu kliṣṭaṃ nu tāvatphalameva puṇyam |
asannivṛttyai tadatītameva manorathānāmataṭaprapātāḥ || (śā.6.10)
iti svānpamāyikapadārthayorbhedasya darśitatvena, loke'pyubhayoranayorbhedenaiva vyavahāreṇa svānpasya māyikatvamayuktam | etena--jāgratprapañcasya māyikatvaṃ samarthayamānena yuduktaṃ `prapañco'pi dīrghasvapna evāsmākamityāśayāt' iti tadapāstam | svāpnamāyikatvasyaiva durnirūpatvāt | kiṃ ca svāpnajāgaritavastunurvaidharmyasya `vaidharmyācca na svapnādivat' 9bra.sū.2.2.29) itisūtreṇa pradarśitatvāt svapnadṛṣṭāntena jāgaritavastuno na māyikatvaṃ śakyaṃ vaktuṃ vyāsamate, vaidharmyaṃ copapāditaṃ śrīśaṅkarācāryaprabhṛtatibhiḥ -- `nidrā glānaṃ tu me manaḥ tenaiṣā bhrāntirujbabhūva' iti, api ca--`smṛtireṣā yatsvāpnadarśanam, upalabdhibhrāntirudbabhūva' iti, api ca-`smṛtireṣā yatsvīpnadarśanam, upalabdhastu jāgaritadarśanam' ityādibhiḥ, `nidrādido,mūlaṃ svapnadarśanaṃ, jāgaritaṃ tu na tādṛśam' ityanyairuktadam | itaraiśca `svāpnamidaṃ na jāgaritam' ityādibādhatabhāvavattvadharmavaiṣamyāt na svapnadṛṣṭānto yukta ityuktam | evamevānyo'pyācāryā āhuḥ |
māyāmātretisūtre hi harīcchāśaktibodhakam |
māyāpadaṃ tatastena svāpno'rthastu na māyikaḥ ||
vāsanāvācakatve'pi na svāpnaṃ māyikaṃ bhavet |
sātyā hi vāsanā tasmānnāgeśoktaṃ na yujyate ||
nāsadrūpetiślokārthavicāraḥ
yadatra mañjūṣāyāṃ (1.pa)
nāsadrūpā nasadrūpā māyā naivobhayātmikā |
anirvācyā tato jñeyā mithyābhūtā sanātanī ||
iti bṛhannāradīyavacanaṃ svābhimatamāyāparatayā vyākhyāya, tena pratiṣiddhameva sadasadātmakatvamabhyupayan tattve virodhamāśaṅkya sattvamāropitaṃ paramārthatastu avāstavamiti vārayan tatra pramāṇatayā `yattatkāraṇamavyaktaṃ nityaṃ sadasadātmakam' (manu.1.11) iti mānavaṃ vacanamudāhṛtya tatra `sadi' tyasya āropitaṃ sattvamityarthavarṇanam, yacca--tasya vacanasyā'dyāparatvaṃ manvānaḥ `"avyaktapadavācyā sūkṣmā'vidya, vyavahārakale sā sthūlavidyāyāḥ kāraṇamiti tasyā eva kāraṇatvena nirdeśaḥ `avyaktaṃ kāraṇam' itīdamena vacanamiti pramāṇatvevodāharaṇam" tadubhayaṃ cintyam | tatra `sat--vedānta-siddhatvātsatsvabhāvam, ayat - pratyakṣādyagocaram' iti kullūkabhaṭṭādibhirvyākhyānāt | kiṃ ca ayaṃ ślokaḥ `āpo nārā' (1.10) iti pūrvaślokanirdiṣṭasya bhagavato nārāyaṇasya kāryabrajhasarṛṣṭikartṛtvādiguṇanirdhāraṇāya pravṛttaḥ | casya śrutyaikavedyatvāt uktasattvāsattvamupapadyate | nanu na vayaṃ tathā manyāmahe kiṃ tu avidyāsvarūpanirūpaṇārtha evaiṣa śloka iti cet, āstāṃ tāvadevamabhimāno bhavatāṃ, nāyaṃ śāstrārthavyavastāpanāya prabhavet | ślokastu nārāyaṇaguṇapratipādanapara eva | `taddisṛṣṭaḥ purūṣo loke tadbraṅotiṃ kīrtyate' ityuttarārdhe `tadvisṛṣṭa' iti kāryahabrajhaṇo nārāyaṇasṛṣṭatvokteḥ | nanu-brajhaṇo'vyaktapadavācyāvidyāsṛṣṭatvameva tenocyata iti tu na manohakam, tasyā acetanatvena sṛṣṭikartṛtvāyogāt | śrutyādayo hi ayetanasya pradhānasya kartṛtvaṃ vārayantyaḥ, nārāyaṇasyaiva kartṛtvaṃ kriyāsamabhivāyāhāreṇa pratipādayanti | `yo brajhāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvam, (śve) tasmādetadebrajha' (mu.) ityādayastu nārāyaṇa eva brahāṇasamṛjaditi viśiṣya vedayantīti pratipadyante vicakṣaṇāḥ | kiṃ ca udāhṛtaṃ nāradīyaṃ vacanaṃ śrīṣaṅkarācāryamatānuyānāmabhimate'nādyāsnarūpe, sahasadvilakṣaṇā'nirvacanīyasvarūpe ca pramāṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | na tu nāgeśābhimatamāyāsvarūpe, tatra `sanātanī, naivobhayātmikā | anirvācyā' ityukteḥ | ato vṛthā prāsyati nāgeśo'parādhyati ca mahatsu | avyaktaṃ pradhānamiti kasā, tanmūlabhūtaitacchlokavyākhyā ca cintyaiva, prakaraṇavirodhāt, nārāyaṇamahimavarṇanasya prakrāntatvāt |
nāgesamatamāyāyāṃ na pramāṇamidaṃ vacaḥ |
kiṃ tu mānaṃ bhavedetadadvaitācāryasaṃmate ||
ānādyavidyārūperthe cānurvācyatvalakṣaṇe |
sanātanītyādi paraśravaṇāditi bodhyatām ||
ṛte'rthamitiślokārthavicāraḥ
yacca svābhimatavāsanārūpamāyāyāṃ pramāṇatayā--
ṛte'rtaṃ yatpratīyeta na pratīyeta cātmani |
tadvidyādātmano māyāṃ yathābhāso yathā tamaḥ ||
(bhāga.2.9.33)
iti ślokamudāhṛtya, `yadātmani pratīyeta, yacca na pratīyate, tatpratyayā'pratyayarūpam, ātmano māyāṃ vidyāt, asadarthaprakāśanasyaiva māyātvāt' iti tadarthavarṇanaṃ, taccintyam | `yathātmamāyāyogena' (2.9.26) iti māyāsvarūpapraśnāt `vīryaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ devo māyayāvyasṛjat tridhā' (bhāga.2.10.13) ityādau tasyāḥ sṛṣṭisādhanatokteḥ, tataḥ paratra ślokeṣu māyāprayakteḥ | jagatsṛṣ‌ṭyādisādhanabhagavadāyattajñānāpavārakavastu atra māyāśabdavācyam | tasyāḥ sāmarthyamāhāyaṃ ślokaḥ ayamarthaḥ -- yat-yasmāt, ṛte'rtham-arthaṃ vināpi (tadabhāve'piti yāvat) pratīyeta - sati avidyamānamasattvaṃ bhāsate, ātmani ca vidyamānaṃ bhedapāratantryādi na pratīyeta-na bhāyāt, tat--anyathājñānā'jñānasādhanaṃ vastu, māyāṃ vidyāt-māyāpadavācyaṃ jāniyāt | avidyamānavastuviṣavakajñānaviṣaye ābhāsa iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ, vidyamānārthājñānaṃ t iti | ābhāso dvitvādiscandre, tamastu--jyotirmaṇḍalamadhyagato rāhuḥ yathāvasthitārthaviṣayakajñānāpahārakatvaṃ māyāyā bhagavataivoktam `māyayā|ñapahṛtajñānā' (gī.7.15) iti | īdṛśyapi māyā nivartate bhagavatpratipattyeti `daivī jhoṣā' iti bhagavataivoktam | bhāgavatavyākhyāneṣu bahudhā vyāsyāto'yaṃ ślokaḥ | saiṣā māyā vimohayantī bhaganadvimuṣān kāryākāryavivekarahitadān kurvantī akāryaṃ ca kārantī kuyoniṣi janayatīti bhavati sṛṣṭihetuḥ | kiṃ ca māyāśabdasyānekrthakatvāt īsvaraśaktāvapi māyāśabdaprayogadarśanāt tatsāmarthyapratipādanaparo'yaṃ śloka iti suvacam |
ṛte'rthaṃ yaditi śloko na pramāṇaṃ bhavediti |
māyāsvarūpe nāgeśasaṃmate pratipādyatām ||
śabdasya ekatvanirāsaḥ
yadatra mañjūṣāyāṃ pratamaparicchede vācakaśavdaṃ lakṣayitvā tasyaikatvamabhihitaṃ nāgeśena,, atredaṃ cintyam-bhagavatā bhāṣyakṛtā pāṇinīyasyāsya śāstrasya viṣayaprayojane nirūpayatā `atha śabdānuśāsanam | koṣāṃ śabdānā? laukikānāṃ vaidikānāṃ ca' | iti śāstraviṣayasya śabdasya bahapatvokternaikatvaṃ yuktam, na ca vyañjakānāṃ bahutvena tadabhiprāyakaṃ bhāṣyamiti vācyam, nāgośamate vācakasya sphoṭaṣyaiva satyatvena tadatiriktānāṃ vyañjakānāmasatyatvena granthādāvasatyasya viṣayatvena nirūpaṇāyogāt |
na ca `eka indo'nekasmin kratuśate āhūto yugapatsarvatra bhavati' itisarūpasūtrabhāṣyaṃ vācakasya śabdasyaikatvaṃ mānamiti vācyam, tasya bhāṣyasya indraśabdasya ekatve tātparyeṇa ghaṭapaṭādiśabdāntarābhāvā'sādakatvāt | idaṃ ca `itīndravat' itiyatra `sabdaprāduribhāvevyayībhāva' iti kaiyaṭodyotānusāreṇoktam | vastutaḥ tatra indraśabdasyārthaparatve'pi prakṛtānugumyaṃ susaṃpādam | tatprakāsastu--uttakamīmāṃsāyāṃ devatādhikaraṇe devatāyā vigrahavattve'pi `sāmarthyaviseṣāt sarvatra yugapatsaṃnidānamekasyaivenanjrasya, atha vā ekatraiva sthitamindrāddaivataṃ taistairtīyamānaṃ haviryugapadupatiṣṭhate, yathā bahubhiḥ samarpyamāṇā namaskriyādaya ekatra sthitānācāryādīn gacchanti' iti śrīśaṅkarācāryairuktadiśā bhāṣyārtho bodhyaḥ | bhāṣye `indra āhūta' ityukteḥ āhvānasya loke prāyeṇa cetenaviṣaya eva darśanāt atrārtha eva bhāṣyatātparyaṃ yuktam | anyathā `andraśabda uccarita' ityeva vadet | nanvevaṃ bhāṣyārthe varṇite, `itiśabdo vyartha iti cet , na' itīti bhinnaṃ padamuktaviṣayaviśeṣalābhārthamityavaiyarthyat |
kiṃ ca `śabdānāṃ pratipattau pratipadapāṭhaḥ kartavyaḥ, netyāha | anabhyupāya eṣa śabdānāṃ pratipattau pratipadapāṭhaḥ | evaṃ hi śrūyate bṛhaspatirindrāya divyaṃ varṣasahastraṃ pratipadoktānāṃ śabdānāṃ śabdapārāyaṇaṃ provāca, nāntaṃ jagama' iti paspaśāsthamahābhāṣyodāhṛtaśratyā bṛdaspatiriti tu na prāmāṇikānāṃ hṛdyam |śrutisiddamabādhitamarthamapalapitumīśvaro'pi nādhikaroti | prayuktānāmidam `anvākhaghyānam' iti bhāṣye, nityeṣu śabdeṣu kūṭasthairavicālibhirvarṇaipbhavitavyamanapāyopajanavikāribhiḥ' iti, `dādā ādyāntau' iti sūtrabhāṣye ca `paryuktānāṃ śabdeṣu' iti bahuvacavaprayogaśca nityānāṃ vācakaśabdānāṃ bahutve pramāṇam | `yenoccārite' ityādibhāṣye `yena, sa śabda' iti ekavacanaṃ tu jātyabhiprāyakam | yadi `yena' ityādibhāṣyavyākhyānakaiyaṭadiśā eka eva śabdaḥ śāstramevānārambhaṇīyaṃ syāt, `naikamudāhakaṇaṃ yogāraṃbhaṃ prayodayati' iti nyāyāt | na ca `vyañjakānuśāsanadvārā śāstraṃ sphoṭānuśāsanaṃ vyañjakāśca bahava iti na doṣa' iti vācyam tathā bhāṣyakṛdādibhiranukteḥ | teṣāṃ kalpitatvena āṇayati-vaṭṭayatītyādīnāmiva sphoṭānuśāsanāmityetaddarotsāritamiti prasiddhaśabdātiriktasphoṭasyaivāsiddhyā tatra ekatvādivicāro gaganakusumadalagaṇanakalpa eva | nanu ekaviṣayakatve śāstramanārambhaṇīyamityetanna yuktaṃ brajhaṇa ekatve'pi vādāntaśāstrārambhadarśanāditi cet, idamucyate - astyatra dṛṣṭāntavaiṣamyam | sarvādyakṣasya brajhaṇa ekatve'pi tatra vicāryamāṇānāṃ guṇānāṃ bahīnāṃ sattvena tadvicārāya śāstrārambho vyāyyaḥ sphoṭasya ta ekatvavādinastasya nirdharmakatvābhyupagamena akhṇḍe vicāryadharmāṇāmabhāvena śāstrāsambho vyarthaḥ | na ca brajhāpyakhaṇḍamiti vācyam, vedāntaśāstramārabhamāṇena maharṣiṇā brajhaṇo'khaṇḍatvānaṅkīrāt | pāṇinināpyācāryeṇa kvāpi brajhasphoṭayoraśaṇḍatvānukteḥ | nirūpitamidaṃ purastātpadādīnāmakhaṇḍatvanirasanāvasare | nanu sūtradvayabhāṣyasya `varṇādīnā sargādau sṛṣṭiḥ, pralaye nāśa' iti pakṣe idam, prayoge śrūyamāṇāḥ śabdā eva vācakā iti sūtre `achavā etayā''nupūrvyā'yaṃ śabjāntarānupadisati, prakṛtim, tato valādyārthadhātukaṃ, tataḥpaścādikāram yasamin tasyāgamabuddhirbhavati' iti sāgamarānāmādeśatvopapādanatṛtīyaprakārapradarśanabhāṣyasya `asatyaprakṛtipratyayopadeśeva satyasya vācakapadasya vyutpādanaṃ mate sphoṭātiriktasya satyatvābhāvena itavyāderavāntarasamudāyasyāpi asatyatvena tadbodhanadvārā vācakabodhanatātparyakatya athavā `yattāvadayaṃ sāmānyenopadeṣṭuṃ śaknoti tāvadupadiśati, prakṛtim......tenāyaṃ viśeṣaṇa śabdāntaraṃ samudāyaṃ pratipadyata' iti pūrvabhāṣyasyātthitiprasaṃgāt | `śabdāntaraṃ masudāyam' iti bhāṣyamādāya `tavyādyapekṣayā bhinnāmitavyādi' ita uddyote vyākhyānāt pūrvabhāṣyasyāpi kalpataśavda eva śāstraviṣaya iti nāgeśatātparyāt | yadvā-nāgaiśakṛtavyārītyā athaveti pakṣadvayabhāṣyayoḥ samānaviṣayakatvalābhāt `athavā' ityuttarabhaṣyasyānutthitprasaṃgaḥ | kiṃ ca `nityaṃ padame' iti kaiyaṭamādāya vyākaraṇā niṣpādyamityartaṃ' iti vyākhyānena kalpita eva sākṣādupadiśyate tena vācakasya bodanamiti alābhāt, pakṣadvayasyāpi samānaviṣayakatveti pakṣe kalpitasya sākṣādbodhanaṃ, tena vācakasyākhaṇḍasya bodhanamiti bhedaḥ iti pakṣadvasya vailakṣaṇyanirūpaṇāsaṃgatīḥ, nāgeśoktadiśā pakṣadvaye|pi kalpitasyaiva sākṣādbodhanamiti lābhātaḥ pāṇinernābhimataḥ sa pakṣaiti matvā pakṣāntaramāha athaveti | ayaṃ ca siddhāntina iti udyote | bhāṣye ayam-pāṇiniḥ, `tenāyam' ityatrāyaṃśabdo boddhṛparaḥ | ayaṃ ca pakṣo dādhetisūtrokta eva anena tavyabuddhiprasaṃgai itavyabuddhaḥ kāryā | tataḥ tadviśiṣṭo bhavitavyādiśavdo vācako bodhya iti bodyam | ayaṃ ca madhyamādhikārikaḥ | dvitoyo'thavetipakṣastu ūhāpohakuśalottamādhikārikaḥ | ayaṃ bhāṣyārthaḥ -- etayā''nupūrvyā--anenakrameṇa, ayaṃ--pāṇiniḥ, śabdāntaram--vācakaśabdam, upadiśati-bodhayatīti | prakṛtyādyupadeśamātreṇa svapratibhayaiva avāntarasamudāyopadeśamantarāpi śadastrīyasarvavisiṣṭasamudāyaṃ psatipadyata uttamādhikārīti bhāvaḥ | tataśca `nityeṣu' ityādi bhāṣyaparyālocanāyāṃ vācakaśabādasya bahutvameva yuktamiti bhāti |
vācakasya tu śabdasya naikatvaṃ bhāṣyasaṃmatam |
iti bhṣyaṃ samālocya saṃgirante sudhījanāḥ ||
sphoṭasyānārṣatvam
kiṃ ca `idaṃ ca śiṣṭasamācārātprakriyādaośāyāṃ svīkāryam, paramārthadaśāyāṃ tu--
pade na varṇa vidyante vākyeṣvavayavā na ca |
vākyātpadānāmatyantaṃ praviveko na kaścana ||'
iti vākyapadiyoktadiśā `sphoṭaṃ siddhāntayatāṃ kvāsya pūrvapakṣasyāvasara' iti `eoṅi' iti sūtraseṣe kaustubhe `vākyapadīyoktadiśā' ityuktyā, `tadvyatiriktaḥ sphoṭo nādavyaṅgyo vācako vistareṇa vākyapadīye vyavasthāpita' iti vaspaśāhnikakaiyaṭe `vākyapadīye' ityuktyā ca sphoṭavāde bharjaharivacanameva mūlaṃ na tu munitrayavacanamiti kaiyaṭādibhireva spaṣṭamabhidhānāt pāṇinīyasūtrānudāharaṇāt, sphoṭo na munitrayasaṃmata iti vadantīti kṛtaṃ vistareṇa |
sphoṭe mānaṃ kaiyaṭādau harivākyamudāhṛtam |
na pāṇinīyaṃ vacanaṃ tasmātso'nārṣa īryate ||
vācakasyākhaṇḍatvanirāsaḥ
yattu `akhaṇḍānyeva padāni' ityuktvā `idamapi' `ādyantau' iti `sātrabhāṣya' ityuktaṃ mañjūṣāyāṃ (pa.1) tadapeśalama | `aiuṇ' ādiṣu bahuṣu sūtreṣu `nityeṣu śabdaṣu kūṭasthairavicālibhivarṇairbhavitavyam' iti bhāṣye kūṭasthasya varṇaghaṭitatvoktyā sakhaṇḍatvameva bhāṣyakṛtsaṃmatatvāvagamāt | kvāpi bhāṣye'khaṇḍatvasya spaṣ‌ṭamanukteḥ | etena `tena ca bodhakasyākhaṇḍasya bodhavanam' iti `ādyantau' iti sūtrasthadvitīyāthavetipakṣabhāvavarṇanaṃ cintyam, tenāna ca bhāṣyeṇa tādṛśārtālābhāt | kiṃ ca ekhaṇḍatve nirdharmakatvāvaśyaṃbhāvena tasya vācakatvaṃ kathaṃ syāditi vibhāvyatām | nanu adhyasyate tatra taditi cet, tena tatra tannāstītyevet‌yuktaṃ bhavatīti jñeyam | `bhāṣye varṇapadena prakṛtipratyayāgamādaya' iti mañjūṣokteś‌ca sakhaṇḍatvaṃ śakyaṃ vaktum | `aiuṇ' ita sūtrasthanityeṣavityādibhāṣyaparyālocanāyāṃ varṇairityasyākṣarairityevārthe yukta iti bhāti | kalāyāṃ tu dvitīyā `athavā' iti bhāṣyaṃ, tatratyakaiyaṭoddyotau ca pramāṇatayopanyasya taiḥ akhaṇḍatvalābhaprakāro nopapāditaḥ | kiṃ ca `vākyādapoddhṛtya padānyanvākhyāyanta' iti bhāṣyaṃ śabdānāṃ sakhaṇḍatva eva pramāṇam, apoddharaṇasya samudāyādekaśā''dānarūpatvāt | `apoddharaṇaṃ-kalpanam, kalpanayā pravibhajya' iti kalādivyākhyānaṃ tu nākṣaramaryādālabhyamiti śabdārthatattvavido na sādhu manyante | aṅkādhikārasthāni sūtrāṇi sakhaṇḍatve pramāṇāni | akhaṇḍatvaṃ na vyāsādisaṃmatamiti pūrvaṃ nirūpitam |
vācakasya sakhaṇḍatve bhāṣyaṃ mānaṃ pradarśitam |
aśaṇḍatve vācakatvadharmastatra kathaṃ bhavet ||
sphoṭe bhāṣyasaṃmatinirāsaḥ
nanu `sphoṭaḥ śabdo, dhvaniḥ śabdaguṇa' iti taparasūtrabhāṣyokteḥ sphoṭo bhāṣyasaṃmata ārṣaḥ pāṇineśca saṃmata iti cet, bramūmaḥ-tatra sphoṭapadasya sphuṭyate vyajyata iti karmavyutpattyā vyaṅgyo nitya iti, sphuṭatyasmādartha iti vyutpattyā vācaka iti vāṣa़rthāt tena bhāṣyeṇa harikaiyaṭadikṣitanāgeśādibhirlakṣitalakṣaṇasphoṭāsiddheḥ | tathāhi-varṇānāmutpattivādinā mātrākālādinā hrasvadīrghādibhedavat drutādivattibhedenāpyakārādivyaktīnāṃ bhedāt yasyāṃ vṛttau taparakaraṇaṃ, tat tadanyavṛttimadakārādīnāṃ dīrghādīnāmina vyāvartakaṃ syāt, tataśca vṛttyantare aisādayo na prāpnupantītyāśaṅkite `vṛttyantare esādayo vaktavyā' iti śaṅkitrāśayānurodhena samādhimādāvabhidhāya vastuto varṇānāṃ nityatve vṛttibhedena nākārādivyaktibhedo'nubhūyate, ata aisādivacanaṃ na kāryamiti bodhayan `siddāntvavasthitā varṇā' iti sadṛṣṭāntaṃ samādhyantaramabhyadhāt bhṣyakāraḥ | tasyāyaṃ bhāvaḥ - bhinnabhinnapuruṣeṇa cirāciragamanaśīlena prāptaḥ klaptaparimāṇaviśeṣo'dhvā yathā na bhidyate, ekarūpa evāste, tathā varṇo'pi cirāciroccāraṇakartroccarito na bhidyata iti | nanu adhvanaḥ klṛptaparumāṇaviśeṣatayā na bhedaprasaṃgaḥ, varṇastu na tatāvidhaḥ, ato'yaṃ viṣamo dṛṣṭānta iti ukte'rthe'viścāsena punaḥsandihānaṃ prati dṛṣyāntāreṇa uktaṃ varṇanityatvaṃ draḍhayati bhāṣyakṛt evaṃ tarhītyādinā | atra sphoṭaśabdaḥ kaiyaṭakaustubhodyetamañjūṣāparyālocanāyāṃ vyaṅgyapara iti jñāyate | śabdaḥ - akārādivarṇaḥ, sphoṭaḥ - vyaṃgyaḥ, varṇānāṃ nityatvena tasya tattvam | dhvaniḥ-vaikṛtadhvaniḥ, vaikṛtatvaṃ ca-ālasyādiprayojyatvam, sa cavilaṃbitādivṛttiḥ, śabdaguṇaḥ-varṇasya svarūuābhivyaktyanantaraṃ vyajyamānatattadvarṇaniṣṭho dharmaḥ varṇasyopakārakaḥ, tasya tattvaṃ ca--tattadvṛtti visiṣṭatayā varṇopalabdhimātrahetutvam | ataḥ sa dharmo dīrghādivyāvartakahrasvatvādivat vṛttyantaraviśiṣiṭavarṇavyāvartako na bhavati | dṛṣṭāntaṃ vaktuṃ pṛcchati bhāṣye kathamiti | bheeryādyāghātavaditi | atrādhātaśabdo bhāvaghañntaḥ | bheryāāghāta iti ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaḥ | tatastṛtīyāntādvitiḥ | sphoṭa iti śeṣaḥ | sphoṭaḥ bherītāḍanajanyaśabdatulyo bhavatītyartaḥ | ayāṃ bhāvaḥ yathā viśatyādibhinnabhinnasaṃkhyākapadagamanasya bherīśabdabhedāpādakatvābhāvavat vṛttibhedasyāpi akārādivarṇavyaktibhedāpādakatvābhāvaḥ | tathaiva śabdasvarūpattavidāmanubhavāt | atra dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāṃntikaśabdayoranityatvavaiṣamye'pi ekavidhopalabdhiviṣayatāmātreṇa dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntirabhāva iti | bhāṣye dṛṣṭāntavākyatātparyamāha---tadyathā iti | bheryāgāta iti | atra bherīmāhantīti karmaṇyaṇ . upapadasamāsaḥ | āhatyeti | śabdaṃ śṛṇvanniti śeṣaḥ | akṣarārthaḥ spaṣṭaḥ | bhāṣye sphoṭa iti | asya dṛṣṭāntaśeṣatve `athāpi' ityādiḥ | vījātīyagamanaśīlapuruṣeṇa śruto'pītyarthaḥ | sphoṭastāvānevasa - bherīdaṇḍasaṃyogarūpatāḍanakāliparimāṇaviśiṣṭa eva, bhavati-śruyamāṇa ityādiḥ | kathaṃ sabdasya tāvaddaraṃ tathāvdhatvamityata āha bhāṣye-dhvanikṛtātviti | puruṣaniṣṭhavyāpāraviśeṣakṛtaḍhṛḍhataradatamasaṃyoganyadhvanipadābhidheyaśabdīyavijatīyadharmaprayojyetyarthaḥ | dṛṣṭāntaśeṣatve yojanākleśamasahamānaḥ kaiyaṭaḥ sphoṭastāvāneveti dārṣṭāntikopanyāsa ityāha atra kalpe evamityādiḥ | evaṃ bherīśabdavat, sphoṭo'pi-akārādirapi, tāvāneva utpaktikālika (abhivyaktikālika) hrasvatvādiviśiṣṭa eva, bhavativartate | na tatra bheda iti bhāvaḥ | bhāṣye vṛttistu na bhedāriṇītyāhadhvanūti | vṛddhiriti | hatvamityarthaḥ | idamupalakṣaṇamalpatvasyāpi iti | mahattvādi tu cirākṛcirakṛtoccāraṇanyānuṣpādidhvanikṛtamiti tātparyam | nāgeśastu `asti bherīśabde'pi sphoṭo'ta idaṃ vākyaṃ dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikobhayaparam' iti maṃjūṣodyotayorāha | tatpakṣe yathātathetyadhyāhṛtya bhāṣyavākyamāvṛtya yojanīyam | śabdārthatattvavidagresaro bhāṣyakāraḥ svānubhūtisiddhamartaṃ (varṇanityatvaṃ) antevāsijakayā bhāṣyakāra) -dhvaniriti |
dhvaniḥ sphoṭaśca śabdānāṃ dhvanistu khalu lakṣyate |
alpo mahāśca keṣāṃcidubhayaṃ tatsvabhāvataḥ ||
asyārthaḥ -- dhvaniḥ - vyañjakaḥ śabdaḥ, sphoṭaḥ - vyaṃgyaḥ śabdaḥ, alpo mahāśca-dhvaniviśeṣaḥ, idaṃ dvayam-cirāciropalabdhijanakatve alpatvamahatve | tādṛśaḥ - śabdānāṃ saṃbandhī, dhvani| - vaikṛtadhvaniḥ, lakṣyate-jñāyate | dhvaneḥ śabdasaṃbandhitvaṃ tadupalabdhijanakatvena uktadvividhaśabdasattve pramāṇamāha -- ubhayamiti | vyaṅgyatvaṃ vyañjakatvaṃ cetyubha.yam | svabhāvataḥ - svarūpeṇetyarthaḥ | śabdasya tādṛśasvabhāvatvāt na tatra pramāṇāntarāpekṣā | athāpi yogipratyakṣagocaro bhavati iti bhāvaṃḥ | tatra vyavasthāmāha--keṣāmiti | tadubhayamityanuṣaṅgaḥ | ayamarthaḥ - keṣāṃcit vyaktānāṃ śabdānāṃ tadubhayaṃ vyaṅgyatvaṃ vyañjakatvaṃ ca, avyaktānāṃ tu dhvanireveti | kaustubhe'pi idaṃ bhāṣyādi itthameva vyākhyātam | tataśca bhāṣye, kārikāyā ca sphoyapadaṃ vyaṃgya ityarthakaṃ, sphuṭyata iti karmaṇi ghañantaṃ bāhulakāditi | ayamatra niṣkarṣaḥ - idaṃ bhāṣyaṃ vṛttibhede vyaktibheda iti varṇānāṃ kāryatāvādinā codite varṇasya nityatvāt vṛttibhede'pi na bheda iti vyutpādanāya pravṛttaṃ, na tu vācakasya visakṣaṇasphoṭatvavyavasthāpanāyeti |
`athavā ubhayataḥ sphoṭamātraṃ nirdiśyate raśruterlatirbhavati' iti `eoṅ' sūtrabhāṣyamapi varṇaiṃkadeśasya grahaṇāgrahaṇavicāre, agrahaṇapakṣe klṛptamityādau latvāsiddiśaṅkāṃ parihartuṃ pravṛttamiti na haryādyabhimatasphoṭasiddhyanukūlam | tasya cāramarthaḥ -- ubhayatra `raḥ la' iti sthānyādeśayoḥ sphoṭamātram--svatantravarṇavarṇaikadeśobhayasādhāraṇadharmamātram | tādṛśaśca dharmo rephalakāranyataratatsadṛśavarṇaikadeśatadṛbhayavṛttiḥ ubhayorekarūpeṇa bhānaprayojakaḥ | sa tu na svatantravarṇabodhakapadena grahaṇaprayojakaḥ | ata eva `raśruterlaśrutirbhavati' iti vakṣyati | nirdiśyate vyaktidvārā sūtrakṛtā accāryace | evamuktau phalitamāha---raśruteriti | rasya śrutiḥ śravaṇaṃ yatra sa raśrutiḥ tasya raśruteḥ | evameva laśrutirityasyāpi vigrahaḥ | atha vā śrutiriti karmaṇi ktin śruyamāṇa ityarthaṃḥ | raḥ śrutiḥ śrūyamāṇo yatrati bahavrīhiḥ, śrūyamāṇarephādhikaraṇaṃ tataśca yo rephāvabhāsaḥ, tasya rephāva bhāsavataḥ lakārāvabhāsa ādeśo bhavatīti viśeṣṭārthaḥ | kṛp ityanayo rephavabhāsavattvāt tayoḥ kalpklap ityādaiśau krameṇa bhavataḥ, varṇatadekadeśayo rephāvabhāsa prayojakadharmavattvāt | ata eva atra sphoṭapadaṃ sphuṭati vikasate śabdaḥ asmāditi avabhāsajanakadharmaparamityāhuḥ | kaiyaṭastu antarbhūtānantarbhūtarephalakāravyaṅgyasāmānyaṃ sphoṭa ityāha | kaustubhe'pi--`eoṅ' sūtraśeṣa `kṛporola' ityatra `svatantrāsvatantrasādhāraṇī jātiranuvādye vidheye ca vivakṣita' iti vadatā dīkṣitena etatkaiyaṭārtha eva viśadīkṛtaḥ | nāgeśastu īdṛśajātisadbhāve mānābhāvena `rajāteriti vaktavye raśruteḥ' iti vadato bhāṣyakārasyāpi tādṛśajātisadbhāvo'saṃmatyanumānena ca ubhayasādhāāāraṇajātyaṅkīkāre varṇaikadeśagrahaṇapakṣa eva paryasānāt agrahaṇapapakṣopakrameṇa pravṛttsyāsya bhāṣyasya upakramavirodham, `narasiṃhatvādivat vilakṣaṇo'yaṃ varṇa' iti `raṣābhyām' (pā.sū.8.4.1) iti sūtrakaiyaṭavirodhaṃ ca pradarśya kvāpi vāstavarephābhāvena `rephābhāsavataḥ sphoṭasya lakārāvabhāsaḥ sphoṭo bhavati' iti `raśruteḥ' iti bhāṣyasyārthaṃ varṇayati | atredaṃ vaktavyam -- brajhātiriktasya vastusāmānyasya bhramaprakāratvena mithyātvaṃ vadato nāgeśasya mataṃ pāṇinyādyanabhimatamiti purastādasakṛdāveditam, ato'styeva pāṇinyādimate vāstavo rephādiḥ | ita evoktaṃ bhagavatā `raśruteḥ' iti, anyathā avabhāsasyetyādyeva brūyāt | `māyikatve'pi sarvasya nāsattvaṃ śaśaviṣāṇavaditi' sāre sārakṛtā, `paṃcakośādivattasmāt' iti kārikāvyākhyānaśeṣe, `na ca alikayā prakṛtipratyayakalpanayā kathaṃ vstavasphoṭabodhaḥ, tasyā alūkayā prakṛtipratyayakalpanayā kathaṃ vāstavasphoṭabodhaḥ, tasyā alīkatvāliddhervakṣyamāṇatvā' diti, `paṃcakośādivat prakṛtipratyayavibhāgasyāpi māyikatvena, nātyantāsacchaśaviṣāṇavadalīkatvam' iti tatraiva darpaṇakāreṇa cokteḥ, māyikapadārthasyāsatvābhāvena varṇatatsamudāyapadāderasattvābhāvāt | etena brajhātiriktaṃ sarvaṃ māyikamato'saditi tañjīṣoktamapāstam | na ca tena jagadasaditi noktamiti vācyam, śaktirajatāde bahistaddeśe cāsattvamupapādya `prapañco'pyevameva, mṛgatṛṣṇādidṛṣṭāntena prapañcasya śāstre varṇanāt | taduktaṃ paramārthasāra' (29.24) iti mañjīṣokteḥ mṛgatṛṣṇoodakaśr‌ṭāsattasya pūrvagranthe upapāditatvena jagato'pi taināsattvasyoktaprayatvāt | śaśaśṛṅgaktirūpyādyoraviśeṣa eva tanmate | `sadasadbhinnamanirvacanīyam' iti matakhaṇḍanāvasare `sadasadbhedādhikaraṇavastuno'prasiddheḥ' iti tenoktatvāt | yadi śuktirūpyādeḥ śaśaviṣāṇādiviśeṣaḥ tarhi bhedadvayādhikaraṇavastunaḥ sattvena advaitisaṃmatānirvacanīyalakṣaṇasya tatkṛkaśaṇḍanamasaṃgataṃ syāt | śuktirajatādisthala iva, atra bādhajñānānudayāt avabhāsetyuktirayuktā | na caivaṃ grahaṇapakṣa evāntarbhavatīti upakramavirodhastadavastha iti vācyam, grahaṇapakṣe tattvabuddhiḥ, agrahaṇapakṣe tatsadṛśatvabuddhariti bhedāt | mukhye saṃbhavati vinā mānaṃ sadṛśasya grahaṇaṃ na syāt | ataḥ `kṛporola' ityatra na mukhyavyaktinirdeśamātre sūtrakṛtastāpyaryam, kiṃ mukhyavyaktitatsadṛśavyaktyubhayorgrāhakadharmanirdaiśe | ata ubhayorgrahaṇaṃ bhavatīti bhāṣyāśayaḥ |
yadi varṇavattā padānāṃ na syāt rekhāgavayanyāyena sādhutvānuśāsakaṃ śāstraṃ śabdānāṃ nityatvapakṣe ityabhiyuktavyavahārāsaṃgatiḥ | rekhāgatagavayagatasaṃsthānasaṃnivśena gavayo bodhyate tathā nityaśabdagatagakāradivarṇavyañjakareśāvinyāsena nitye gavādiśabdo bodhyata iti hi tadarthaḥ | idaṃ jñeyam--asterleṭi edhīti rūperekhāsthānīyā'sadhātugatavarṇādarśanāt tena nyāyena śāstramanuśāsakamityetat na yuktamiti `ādyantau' iti sūtroddyoto na yuktaḥ | prakriyāvākye śāstrapravṛttyuttaraṃ niṣpannam edhītirekhāsthīnīyaṃ tena bodhyate, edhajñītyarthadhavācakaḥ śabdo nityaḥ, tatra rethāsthānīyagatavarṇopalambhāt, tena nyāyena śāstramanuśāsakamiti yuktadamiti | na ca nṛtvasiṃhatvānākrāntanṛsiṃhatvavat niravayavaṃ varṇāntaraṃ taditi vācyam, varṇāntaratve'pi niravayavatve mānābhāvāt | na hi nṛsiṃhasya naratvādyanavacchinnatvaṃ'pi niravayavatvamasti |
`tasyādita udāttamardhahrasvam' (pā.sū.1.2.32) `eco'pragṛjhasyādarāddhate pūrvasyārdhasyā''duttarasyedutau' (pā.sū.8.2.107) iti plutādikāryaviśeṣaṃ ca vidadhatā sūtrakṛtā, bhāṣyakṛtā ca `aicoścottarabhūyastvāt' iti vadatā, varṇaikadeśānāṃ varṇagrahaṇena graṅaṇā'grahaṇavicāraṃ kṛtavatā varṇānāṃ sāvayavatvābhyupagamāt | ata etoṣāṃ niravayavattvam. tatsamudāye samudāyatvabaddheratāttvikatvam, tatra avayavasadbhāvāśrayaṇaṃ ca bhrantimūlamityādinirūpaṇaṃ `varṇeṣvavayavā na ca' iti dṛṣṭāntena--
pade na varṇā vidyante vākyeṣvavayavā na ca |
vākyātpadānāmatyantaṃ praviveko na kaścana ||

iti vākyapadīyoktaṃ padāvakyayorniravayavattvaṃ ca nirmūlam | `nityeṣu śabdeṣu' ityādibhāṣye padavākyarūpaśabdeṣu varṇairbhavitavyamityukteḥ teṣāṃ ṣāvayavatvasya bhāṣyakṛtsaṃmatatvāt | na ca bhāṣyaṃ vyañjakaviṣayaṃ, vākyapadīyaṃ tu sphoṭaviṣayamiti vācyam, atiriktasphoyasvarūpasyaiva bhāṣyānārūḍhatvāt | bhāṣyādyanugraharahitārthapratipādakavākyapadīyaṃ kathaṃ pāṇinīyairupādeyaṃ syāt | na ca sāvayavatve janyatvaṃ syāditi na vityatvaṃ śbdānāmiti vācyam, triguṇātmakamūlaprakṛteḥ sāvayavāyā janyatvādarśanāt | śabdānāṃ nityatvapakṣe samudāyā'prasiddhicodyasyāpi nāvasaraḥ |
kiṃ ca `padāntasya' (pā.8.4.37) `sātpadādyoḥ' phā.8.3.111) `ṣaḥpratyayasya' (pā.1.3.6) `iṇaḥ ṣīdhvaṃluṅaliṭāṃ dhoṅkāt' (pā.8.3.78) ityādibhiḥ padāyavapratyayāvayananibandhanāni kryāṇi, `aluguttarapede' (pā.6.3.1) `uttarapadasya' (pā.7.3.10) `ekājuttarapade ṇaḥ' (pā.8.4.12) `pūrvadāt' (pā.8.3.106) `hṛdbhagasūddhvante pūrvapadasya ca' (pā.7.3.19) `taddhitārthottarapadasamāhāre ca' (pā.2.1.51) ityādibhiḥ samāsaṃ pūrvottarapadanibandhanakāryāṇi ca vidadhatā sūtrakṛtā padavākyayorapi sāvayavatvasya bodhitatvena tayorniravayavatvoktiḥ śāstrapravartakācāryānabhimataiva | ataḥ sā pramāṇakairvādartavāyā | kiṃ ca varṇādīnāṃ niravayavatvaṃ nāsti śavdeṣu' iti bhāṣyānugrahaḥ | tatra śabdeṣvityasya varṇapadavākyarūpaśabdeṣvityarthaḥ | etena yaduktaṃ samarthasūtraṃ kaiyaṭena `tattavato niravayavānyeva kevasamasatyapratriyāśrayeṇā'nvākhyānte' iti tadapāstam | `nityeṣu śabdeṣu varṇairbhavitavyam' iti bhāṣyokternityaśabdānāṃ sāvayavatvasiddheḥ, asātyārthānuśāsake śāstratvasyaiva durvacatvāt | tattvaviṣayakayathārthajñānajanakaśabdasamudāyo hi śāstramityācakṣate'bhjñāḥ | kiṃ ca asatyasyopadese tatraiva vartamānāḥ satyaṃ kadāpi na bhajeran, abhimatanagaraṃ yiyāsūnāṃ kāpathopadeśe tatraiva bhramanto yathā nagaraṃ kadāpi na prapnuvanti | kiṃ ca samarthasūtre śabdānāṃ niravayavattvaṃ nairaऱthakyaṃ ca nirācakre nāgeśaḥ kecittu pakṣopanyāsamukhena `upalabhyamānā'vayavānāṃ nairarthakye, samudāyasya niravayavatve ca mānābhāvaḥ, nityatvaṃ tu aniṣpādyatvamātreṇa vaktuṃ śakyam' iti granthena | etena-nityatvasāvayavatve parasparaparihākeṇaiva vartete iti niyamo neti bodhitam |
asti sphoṭapadaṃ bhāṣya eoṅataparasūtrayoḥ |
ādye tu tatpadaṃ dharmaparaṃ tatsūtrabhāṣyataḥ ||
dvitīye sphoṭabhinnasya nityaśabdasya vācakam |
iti nirṇīyate tasmānmañjūṣoktaṃ na yuḍyate ||
ato bhāṣye brajhasamasphoṭasiddhau na sādhakam |
iti bhāṣyavidāṃ pādapajhamāśritya bhāṣitam ||
sphoṭe bhāgavatasaṃmatinirāsaḥ
nanu bhagavatā vyāsena bhāgavate---
śṛṇoti ya imaṃ sphoṭaṃ supte śrotre ca śūnyadṛk |
yena vāgvayajyate yasya vyaktirākāśa ātmanaḥ || (12.6.5)
iti praṇavasya sphoṭatvokteḥ vyāsādimaharṣisaṃmataḥ sphoṭa iti cet, ucyate--sphoṭatyartho'smādanena veti apādāne, karaṇe vā ghañi niṣpannaḥ sphoṭaśabdo vācakaparyāyaḥ | yadvā--pūrvaṃ pralaktasya varṇatrayātmakasya praṇavasya `imaṃ sphoṭam' ityanuvādāt praṇavaparyāyaḥ saḥ | yadvā--`yena vāgvyajyata' ityuttarārdhe vakṣyamāṇatayā vedādyabhivyañjakatvena praṇavasya sphoṭatvoktiḥ, atastatra sphoyapadaṃ vyañjakamityarthakam | ślokārthastu--yaḥ paramātmā, śrotre supte--śrotren‌driye nirvyāpāre, śūnyadṛk-śūnye'pi sarvendriyavyāpārābhāve'pi, dṛk-jñānī, imaṃ sphoṭam-praṇavarūpaṃ svavācakaṃ śabdam, śṛṇoti | praṇavaḥ kīdṛśa ityata āha--yeneti | yena-praṇavena, vāk-sarve vedāḥ, yasya-praṇavasya, ātmana ākāśe-paramātmano hṛdayākāśe, iti śrīdharācryaḥ | paramātmaivaitaṃ śṛṇoti nānya iti etadbhāvaḥ | `suptaḥ śroteva śūnyadṛk' iti saṃhitāpāṭha iti kṛtvā, suptaḥ -suṣuptyavasthāmāpannaḥ, śūnyadṛkśūnyaḥ paramātmā, taṃ paśyatīti tathā, brajhā'parokṣi yo brajhajñānī, sasuptaḥ san, śroteva-paramātmeva, sphoṭaṃ śṛṇoti, iti vijayadhvajācāryaḥ | sphoṭasya śrotā paramātmā brajhajñānī ceti bhāvaḥ |
idamatra jñeyam -- prathamaskande caturthe'dhyāye saṃgraheṇa nirūpitaṃ vedādyadhigatikramaṃ,
paisādibhirvyāsaśiṣyairvedācāryairnahātmabhiḥ |
vedā vai katidhā vyastā etatsaumyā'bhudhehi naḥ ||
(bhāga.12.6.1)
ityādinā vistareṇa nirūpayatā śabdaprapañcasṛṣṭiṃ karavāṇīti saṃkalpavato bhagavato nārāyaṇasya hṛdayākāse kaścinnādo ghaṇṭāninādasaṃnibhaḥ icchāmātreṇa prathamaṃ pradurabhūt | tasmāccākṣarasamāmnāyādyabhivyaktidvārā vedāsarvamabhivyānat, sa ca praṇavo nārāyaṇasya vācakaḥ `yena vāgavyajyate, svadhāmno vācakaḥ sākṣāt brajhaṇaḥ paramātmana' ityādinirūpitam | na ca `parameṣṭhina' iti tatroktaṃ tena ca `parameṣṭhī pitāmahaḥ' iti kośāt canurmukhasyaiva grahaṇamiti vācyam, parameṣṭhipadasya paramātmavācakatvena sarvasya brajhakāraṇakatvasiddhāntenu ca parabrajhagrahaṇasyaivocitatvāt | parame sthāne tiṣṭhatīti parameṣṭhī, kin auṇādikaḥ | na ca `vṛtitirinirodhādvibhāvyata' itadi bhagavataiva pūrvamuktatvena sarvapratyakṣagocaraḥ sa iti vācyam, yogidṛṣṭyā tathokteḥ | yogino hi yogabalāt brajheva tamapi pratyakṣīkartuṃ samarthāḥ | yadvā yathā asmābhiḥ karṇapuṭe pihite nādaviśeṣo jñāyate, tathā praṇavavyañjako nādo vibhāvyate iti dṛṣṭāntoktiḥ, `alaukikaṃ lokadṛṣṭāntena bodhanīyam' iti sarvātāntrikāṇāṃ samayāt |
kiṃ ca bhāgavataparyālocanāyāṃ sarvapratyakṣāgocaranādābhivyaṅkyatvaṃ praṇāvākhyasphoṭasya pratīyate, vaiyākaraṇamanyaistu paraśrotragrājhavaikharīnādavyaṅgyatocyace, atoo vaiyākaraṇekteḥ | bhāgavatasya ca bhinnaviṣayatvena nedaṃ bhāgavataṃ teṣāmanukusam | etena `dhāryante yaistrayo bhāvā guṇānāmarthavṛttayaḥ ' iti sattvādiguṇādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ bhāvānāṃ (rūpaprapañcasya) sṛṣṭikathanāt nāmarūpobhayaprapañcopādanatvaṃ sphoṭasya bhagavatā vyāsevoktaṃ, tadanusāreṇa asmābhirapi tathoktamityapāstam | bhagavaduktapraṇavākyasphoṭasya bhavadabhimatasphoṭasya ca bhinnatvāt |
kiṃ ca parāpaśyantīmadhyamāvaikharībhedena caturvidhāvasthāviśiṣṭanādeṣu parākyanādasya śabgabrajhatvaṃ śabdārthobhayarūpaprapañcopādānatveneti `kriyāśaktipradhānāyāḥ śabdaśabdārthakāraṇam | prakṛterbindurūpiṇyāḥ śabdabrajhā'bhavatparā' iti vacanabalāt tena siddhāntitatvena, vaikharīvyaṅgyasya sphoṭasya kāryarūpatayā, kāraṇatvāyogāt tasya braṅatvānukteśca |
na ca `ekasyaiva sphoṭasya śabdabrajharūpasya sravaśabdatadarthobhayopādanatvenobhayorapi tatkāryayorupatvāt' iti sphoṭasya kāraṇatvamukta nāgeśeneti vācyam, tatra sphoṭapadasya parākhyāvācaka iti sāṃpradāyikairvyākhyānāt | ata eva `kriyāśaktipradhānasya' iti pramāṇodāharaṇaṃ saṃgacchate | pramāṇaṃ parā vāca eva śabdabrajhatvokteḥ kiṃ ca `guṇānāmartavṛttaya' ityatra tisṛṇāṃ vyāhṛtīnāmabhivyaktarebhidhānāt | nāmaprapañcanirūpaṇasyopakrameṇa ittaratra ca vedādināmaprapañcanirūpaṇadarśanena ca madhye rūpaprapañcasṛṣṭinirūpaṇaṃ prakramavirūddham | yadyatra rūpasṛṣṭirabhimatā syāt tarhi ādāvante vā cāṃ nirūpayet | `ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ, ' (tai.u.2) `tadaikṣata baha syāṃ prajāyeyeti tattejoosṛjata' (chā.6.2.3) `apa eva sasarjā''dau', `āpo nārā iti proktāḥ' ityādiśrutismṛtyādibhi rūpaprapañcasṛṣṭiparābhiḥ ākāśādirūpaparpañcasya śabdātsṛṣṭyanabhidhānācca, praṇavasya virūpaprapañcakāraṇatvāyogāt | yatra kvāpi praṇavāt rūpaprapañcasṛṣṭiḥ śrūyeta, smaryeta vā, tatra praṇavavācyaparamātmana eva sṛṣṭirucyata iti veditavyas |
