na ca `śabda iti cennā'ti prabhavātpratyakṣānumānābhyām' (bra.sū.1.3.28) iti sūtreṇa bhagavān bādarāyaṇo rūpaprapañcasyāpi śabdaprabhavatvamabhidhatta iti sphoṭasyoktaṃ jagatkāramatvaṃ yuktamiti vācyam, tatra `ataḥ prabhavāt' ityasya śabdādintrādyutpatterityakṣarārthe'pi śabdasya strakṣyamāṇapadārthākṛtismāraketvena tātparyāt | prajāpatiḥ sraṣṭukāmaḥ prathamaṃ vaidikānindrādīn śabdānanusaṃdadhānaḥ tadbodhitākṛtiviśeṣān smāraṃ smāraṃ tadanugatākāraviśeṣaviśeṣṭānindrādīn sṛjatīti śabdasya sahakārikāraṇatvameva sūtrakṛtā prābodhi | ata eva `janmādyasya yataḥ' (bra.sū.1.1.2) ityādinā sūtrakṛtā sarvasya kagṛtabrajhakāraṇakatvasiddhāntavyākopo na | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ śāṅkare `sabda iti cenna' iti sūtrabhāṣye | na ca sphoṭasyāpi brajhakāryatvāt brajhatvena voktasiddhāntavirodo'syamākamiti vācyam, brajhakāryatvāt brajhatvena noktasiddhāntavirodho'smākamiti vācyam, brajhakāryasāmānyasya brajhātve tadvatkāraṇatve ca mānābhāvena sphooṭasya prapañcakāraṇatvā'siddheḥ | na ca `tadananyatvam' iti kāraṇānanyatvaṃ kāryasyoktamiti vācyam, avivābhāvenaivānanyatvaṃ na tu svarūpata iti pūrvameva samāhitatvena sphoṭasya brajhatvāyogāt |
haryādisaṃmatasphoṭādanyo bhāgavatoditaḥ |
tasmānna mānaṃ tatsattvaṃ bhavedbhāgavatasmṛtiḥ ||
sphoṭaviṣaye naiyāyikasaṃmatinirāsaḥ
naiyāyikāstavat `asti padānāṃ padartheṣu saṃketaḥ na tu vākyasya vākyārthe, sa tu padāntarasamabhivyāhirarūpārāṃkṣayā apūrvo vākyā'śaktyaivotpadyate | vaiyākaraṇaṃmanyairnirūpito vilakṣaṇasvarūpaḥ sphoṭastu vāstyeva' iti vadantīti viditam |
mañjūṣākṛtadtu--prathamaparicchede `ṭhasaṃbandhacha pade vākye ca tadāha nyāyabhāṣyakāraḥ `samayajñānārtaṃ ca' ityādinā `vākyasphoṭo naiyāyikā'saṃmata iti parāstam" ityantena grantena tadbhāṣyopanyāsapūrvaṃ padeṣvina vākyeṣvapi aiśvarasamaya iti spaṣ‌ṭamevoktamiti bhāṣyābhiprāyaṃ varṇayitvā naiyāyikairapi vkye śaktiḥ, sphoṭaścā'ṅgī kriyata iti pratipādayan naiyāyikā api svānukūlā iti bodhanāya tadanabhimataṃ prameyaṃ tattātparyaviṣaya iti brate |
tatra `samayajñānārthamidaṃ padalakṣaṇāyā vāco'nvākhyānaṃ vyakaraṇam, vākyalakṣaṇāyā vāco'rthalakṣaṇama' iti bhāṣyam, anena--tanmate vākyaśaktiḥ, sphoṭaścāstīti śabdato'rthato vā kathaṃ labhya iti vicakṣaṇairvicāryam | kiṃ ca tatra (dvi.a.pra.ā.56.sūṃ) `samayapālanārthaṃ'......artho sakṣaṇam' iti pāṭho dṛśyate, tatra vyākaraṇaṃ padasādhutvabodhakam, arthaḥ -tatsamūhaḥ, lakṣaṇam-bhedakam, vākyābhāsāt sādhuvāktasya vyāvartakaṃ abādhitā'rtho vākyasādhutvabodhaka iti tadartaṃ tadvyākyātāra āhuḥ | kiṃ ca sphoṭasya `etena tāvadvarṇābhivyaṅgyaḥ tadvyañjakenaivopapatteriti' iti muktāvalyāṃ śabdakhaṇḍe dūṣitatvena, nyāyamañjaryāṃ (6.ā.) nyāyasūtrabhāṣyavārtikatātparyaṭīkāditātparyaṃ nirūpayatā jaṭantabhaṭṭena `nirmūlo'yaṃ vādo gandharvanagarasodara' iti atiriktasphoṭavādasya dūṣitatvena ca naiyāyikasaṃmataḥ sphoṭa iti kathaṃ saṃgacchate | idaṃ ca tadanuktatadanabhimatārthābhidhānamiti vidāṅkaruta |. etena--`arthalakṣaṇam' ityeva pāṭhamavalambya vyākaraṇāpadasyātrapi saṃbandhaḥ | tataśca vākyarūpavācaḥ arthabodhajanakatābodhakamityādivyākhyānaṃ nyāyabhāṣyatadvyākyānānavalokanamūlakameva | etena nāgeśo granthapāṭhamapyanyathāprakalpya svaikaṃ granthārtaṃ vaktīti vaktā niraṅkaśa iti ca spaṣṭaṃ jānantu |
sphoṭo gandharvanagarasodarastviti tārkikaiḥ |
dūṣitaḥ sa kathaṃ teṣāṃ mataḥ syāditi cintyatām ||
sphoṭasya brajhatvādinirāsaḥ
varṇapadavākyabhedena sphoṭastāvat trividhaḥ jātivyaktibhedena te punardvividāḥ iti ṣoḍhā saṃpannāḥ | akaṇḍapadasphoṭo'khaṇḍavākyasphoṭa iti dvaividhyena aṣṭau | tatra `vākyasphoṭo'tiniṣkarṣe tiṣṭhatīti matasthitiḥ' iti haryukteḥ vākyasphoṭo mukhyaḥ, tatrāpi jātisphoṭaḥ paramamukhyaḥ |
saṃbandibhedātsattaiva bhīdyamānā gavādiṣu |
jātirityucyate tasyāṃ sarve śabdā vyavasthitāḥ ||
tāṃ prātipadikārtaṃ ca dhātvarthaṃ ca pracakṣate |
sā nityā sā mahānātmā tāmāhustvatalādayaḥ ||
iti harikārikārthasaṃgrahaka--
satyā'satyau tu yau bhāgau pratibhāvaṃ vyavasthitau |
satyaṃ yattatra sā jātisarasatyā vyaktayo matāḥ || 70 ||
iti kārikayā nirūpayan dīkṣitaḥ -- " prativastu kācidanugatā sattā jātipadācyā'sti | sā ca sattā brajhaiva `ātmaivedaṃ sarvam' ityukteḥ | sattāparyāyaḥ tasyaśabdaḥ | jātipadavācyā sattā śabde'rthe ca vartate | tatra śabdakatvaṃ ca, lāghavāt | taduktam---
śakyatva iva śaktatve jāterlāghavamīkṣyatām |
aupādhiko vā bhedā'sti varṇānāṃ tāramandavat ||
iti | evaṃ ca--tattadvyaktyavacchinnaṃ brajhaiva jātiḥ brajhātiriktaṃ ca sadvastu nāstīti ātmaikatvasiddhiḥ | tataś‌ca śabdavicāre pravṛttasya aupaniṣade brajhaṇi vyutpattirūpajāyate | varāṭikānveṣaṇāya pravṛttasya cintāmaṇilābhavat" iti vyākaraṇasāstrādhyayanasya paramaṃ prayojanaṃ kaustubhe prathamāhnike'varṇayat |
atra vadanti--īdṛśamahāprayojanaṃ śabdavicāraṃ parihāya, śaṅkarācāryādayo mahānto vyācakṣāṇā brajhajñānaṃ muktisādhanamiti kuto vyavasthāpāyāmāsuriti prathamato vimarśanīyam | teṣāṃ tvayamāśayaḥ -- adhītavedavedāṅkāḥ karmādinā vīdhūtāntaḥkaraṇadoṣāḥ śuddhāntaḥkaraṇā `ātmā vā are' ityādiśruticoditātmopāsanayā brajhajñānavantaḥ pratpaparamātmānugrahāḥ tyaktadehā muktā bhavanti nā'nyopāyeneti |
nanu
dve brajhaṇī veditavye śabdabrajha paraṃ ca yat |
śabdabrajhaṇi niṣṇātaḥ paraṃ bpajhādhigacchati ||1
iti śabdavicāre pravṛtavye brajhādhigamarūpamokṣalābhāṣa़bhidhānāt yuktaṃ kaustubhoktamiti cet, na | tatra śrutau vā, smṛtau vā, śabrabrajhetyasya vedetyarthena, vedārthavucārāsaktasya brajhaprāptiriti tattātparyāt | ata eva `paraṃ ca yata' iti parabradhaṇo'pi vedyatvoktiḥ saṃgacchate | etena sphoṭasadbhāve vacanamidaṃ mānamiti nirastam | na ca bhāṣyakṛtā śāstrānuṣaṅkikaprayojananirūpaṇāvasare `catvāri śṛṅgāḥ', `saktumiva' ityābhyāṃ sādhuśabdajñānasya mahādevasāmyaṃ, brajhasāyujyaṃ ca bhavatīti nirūpitatvena mokṣaḥ phalaṃ śabdavicārasyetyuktaṃ bhavati, sāmyasāyujyayoreva mokṣatvāditi vācyam | yogo `tasya vācakaḥ praṇavaḥ, tadarthacintanam' iti sūtrābhyāmīścarasya vācakaḥ praṇavaḥ, praṇavapratipādyaguṇaviśiṣyatvena īśvaracintanaṃ muktisādhanamiti pratipādayataḥ patañjalaṃḥ śabdajñānena mokṣa ityatra na tātparyam, kiṃ tu śabdajñānena nirmalacittatvarūpasāmyam, brajhajñānasādhanavedāparthavicāramāsarthyapakaṃpacca bhavatīti śrutitātparyamiti bhāṣyāśayāt | ata eva--adhītasāṅgasarvavedādernāradasya śvateketośca `nāmavidevāsminnātmavit' (chā.7.2) iti ātmajñānābhāvaśravaṇam, `bhagavāṃstveva me tat bravītu' (chā.6.1.4) ityātmopadeśaprārthanaṃ ca saṃgacchate | yadi śabdajñānādevātmajñānasiddhiḥ, tarhi tayoradhītavyākaraṇatvena śabdavicārādeva brajhajñānasiddhau, śrutau tadabhāvaśravaṇaṃ, tatprārthanaṃ cāsaṃgataṃ syāt | ata eva bhāṣye vyākaraṇasya aṅgatvamevoktaṃ `pradhānamaṅgeṣu vyākaraṇam' iti | vedārthavicārasya sādhakamiti tadarthaḥ | ata eva ca vyākaraṇasāyāparavidyātvamuktvā parabrajhavidya anyoti `dve vidye veditavevye parā caivāparā ca' (1) ityādinoktaṃ muṇḍeke | ata eva ca `aiuṇ' ādīnāṃ māheśvarasūtrāṇāṃ brajhaparatayā vyākhyānena sanakādīnāṃ brajhajñānotpādanaṃ saphalam, anyathā māheśvarasūtramūlakapāṇinīvyākaraṇena śabdavicārādeva teṣāṃ brajhajñānāsaṃbhave tadvyarthaṃ syāt | kiṃ ca sphoṭasya brajhatvamityapi na yujyate | sphoṭavādino mate sphoṭātiriktavastuno'bhāvena asattvena ca brajha nāstīti siddhyā nirūśvaravādāntarbhāvaḥ, sphoṭasya brajhatvasiddhiḥ | brajhāvādimate tu brajhānyasya sphoṭasyāpyasattvāpattyā na tatsiddhiḥ | tataśca--`ātmaivedaṃ sarvam' iti sarvasyātmatvāt sphoṭasyāpi tattvamiti cintyam | etena īśvare tu na kiṃcinnirūpito bhedaḥ sarvarūpatvāttasyeti mañjūṣāyāmanūditagranthoktamapi cintyam | īśvarasya sarvarūpatve mānābhāvāt, na ca sphoṭa eva brajheti matamiti vācyam, tathārthakaśrutyādipramāṇābhāvāt | bhāṣyādau tathānukteśca | `na hi na hi sakṣati ḍukṛñkaraṇe'| na śabdaśāstrādibhitasya mokṣaḥ |
śabdabrajhaṇi niṣṇāto na niṣṇāyātpare yadi |
śramastasya śramaphalo jhadhenumiva rakṣataḥ || (bhāga.11.11.18)
ityādibhiḥ śabdavicāramātraratasya brajhādhigamamokṣatatsādhanajñānā bhāvasyā'bhiyuktairnirdhāritatvena kaustubhādyuktaṃ nādriyante parāparatātvavedino bhāṣyavidaśca |
sphoṭa eva vipratipanno bahubhiranaṅgīkārāt | kaiyaṭastu, `kvicit varṇasphoṭamāha, kvacit padasphoṭamāha, kvacit vākyasphoṭamāha, kvacit jātisphoṭamāha' ityevaṃ vadan bhāṣyasya sphoṭe tātparyamāha | tathātātparyavarṇanaṃ ca tattadbhāṣyākṣarānanuguṇameva | kiṃ ca sphoṭasvarūpaṃ, tasya vācakatvam, tasya brajhasvarūpatvaṃ ca yadi bhāṣyakṛdabhimataṃ bhavet, tadā `kiṃ punarime varṇā arthavantaḥ, āhosvit anarthakāṃ, kiṃ punarnityaḥ śabdaḥ, āhesvitkārtha' ityādinā yathā varṇādīnāmarthanattvādikaṃ savicāraṃ vyavasthāpitavanān bhāṣyakāraḥ tathā `kiṃ punardhvanirvācakaḥ, atha sphoṭa' iti pratramya, sphoṭasya vācakatvādikaṃ sarvaṃ vyavasthāpya tajjñānādapavarga ityeva vyavastāpayet | tathā na vyavastāpayāmāsa | tena manyāmahe kaustubādyuktaṃ na bhāṣyādisaṃmatamiti |
yadatra mate `advaitināṃ mate jāteranityatvena, nirāśrayajātisadbhāve mānābhāvācya, apāgātad yadagnerangitvamiti jāternāśaśravaṇācya' iti nāgeśoktadūṣaṇam, tatsarvaṃ jātisphoṭavādinastasya mate'pi samānam | kiṃ ca advaitināṃ mate jāteranikatyatve asmāraṃ kā hāniḥ ? bhāṣyakṛtā jāteranityatvānukteḥ | `vyaktiṣūtpādyamānāsvapi ākṛtīnāṃ nityatvāt na gavādiśabdeṣu kaścidvirodhaḥ' iti `śabda iti cenna' (bra.sū.1.3.17) iti sūtre śaṅkarācāryaiṃrjāternityatvokteḥ `advaitināṃ mate jāteranityatvāt' ityuktirayuktā | jātisphoṭavādināṃ prācāṃ mate jāterbrajhasvarūpatvena nityatvāt tasya nivṛttyayogāt `apāgāt' (chā.6.4) iti śrutyuktā nivṛttiḥ na jāteḥ, kiṃ tu angi padavyavahāryatvasya | ataḥ navyoktadūṣaṇaṃ na samyagiti |
sphoṭo brajha tatastasya vicārādbrajhadarśanam |
mokṣaṃ cānpoti pumāniti kaustubhakṛnmatam ||
na yuktaṃ tena tatrārthe nodāhāri munervacaḥ |
virūddhaṃ brajhamīmāṃsā paraśrutyādibhiryataḥ ||
ato brajhavidāṃ naitatsaṃmataṃ pāṇinerapi |
iti niścīyate brajha vicāracaturairjanaiḥ ||
nāgeśasaṃmatajātisphoṭanirāsaḥ
`jātisphoṭa eva paramasiddhāntaḥ jātistu anugatākārapratītijanako dharmaḥ, tasya tallakṣaṇatvopapattaye anugatāśaktigrahasiddhaye ca tattatkālatattadvarṇanurāgarūpabhedena sphoṭānantyam | ātmaikatvavādināmātmatvasya jātitvasuddhaye upādhivaśādātmanānātvavat tadvṛttitvena tasya jātitvam | tasyopādhyavacchinnatve vyaktisphoṭatvam | upādhyupalakṣitatve jātisphoṭatvam' | (ma.1.pa) iti nāgeśaḥ | atredaṃ vaktavyam--mahatā prayatnena sādhitaḥ sphoṭaḥ, kiṃ jātiḥ, utānyaḥ | ādyeṃ advaitināṃ mate jāteranityatvena, nirāśrayadātisadbhāve mānābhāvāccetyādi paraṃ prati taduktadūṣaṇaṃ tanmate'pyāpatati | antye tu jātisphoṭa iti siddāntabhaṅgaḥ | `advaitināmātmabhedavat' iti dṛṣṭānto'saṃgataḥ | teṣāṃ mate ātmano dravyatvamiti tatra jāterastitā yuktā | nāgeśamatasiddhasya sphooṭasya tu jātitvena tatra jāteraṅgīkārāyogāt, `jātau jāteranaṅgīkārāt' iti tāntrikanyāyāt . kiṃ ca loke bodhyavṛttinā'nugatākāradharmeṇa tathāpratītirityanubhavaḥ na tu bodhakavṛttitādṛśadharmeṇa . ata aupādhikānantasphooṭakalpanāprayāso viphala eva | kiṃ ca upādhisaṃbandhasya sārvakālikatve sphoyabhedasya niruktadharmāśrayatvasya ca sārvakālikatvena, sphoṭasyaikatvākhaṇḍatvabhaṅgaḥ | kādācitkatve nirūktadharmarūpajāterāśrayāsiddhiḥ | kiṃ ca evaṃkalpane sphoṭaniṣṭhajāterevāttāsābhena varṇādyatiriktatayā sādhitasya vaikharīvyaṅgyasya madhyamanādasya sphoṭasya nārthavattā sidhyatīti sphoṭo'rthavānitii pūrvekitiścintyā syāt | na ca tasyaiva sphoṭasya aupādhiko bhedaḥ bhinnavyaktivṛttitvaṃ ceti dātitvamiti noktadoṣāvakśa iti vācyam, evaṃ kalpane bhāṣyādyanugrahābhāvāt | kalpitā nāmasatvaṃ vadato mate vyaktibhedasya tadvṛttitvasya cāsattvena uktajātyasiddheḥ sphoṭātiriktasya sarvasya mithyātvana (asattvena) sphoṭānantyasaṃpādakasamagrīsaṃpado durlabhatvāt | atra pakṣe sphoṭavyavasthāparaharikaṭākṣo'pi mṛgyaḥ | bhāṣyādyananugṛhītapadārthasādhane prayatamānānāṃ pakṣaparigrahe lāghavagaurakacarcāpi śuṣkakolāhala evetyalam |
kālavarṇānurāgeṇa sphoṭānantyaṃ prakalṣte |
sphoṭatvaṃ vartate tatra tato jātiritīryate ||
ityevaṃ kalpane mānaṃ bhāṣyādau na pradṛśyate |
ato nāgeśavacanaṃ na yuktamiti sūrayaḥ ||
pātañjale hi bhāṣye loke vācakatvena prasiddhaśabdātiriktavilakṣaṇasphoṭasvarūpaṃ na nīrūpitam, nityatvaṃ tāvacchabdānāṃ tatra tatra pratipāditam | tena manyāmahe vācakānāṃ nityatā tāvat pṇinerbhāṣyakṛtaścābhimateti |
khaṇḍitaścāyaṃ sphoṭavādaḥ `pratītyapratītibhyāṃ na sphoṭātmakaḥ śabdaḥ' iti sūtreṇa sāṃkhyācāryaiḥ, tadunuyāyinopavarṣanāmnā cā''cāryeṇa | kiṃ ca śrīśaṅkarācāryaistadanuyāyibhiruttaramīmāṃsāyāṃ devatādhikaraṇe (8), kumārilabhaṭṭena tantrārtikaṃ, pārthasārathimiśreṇa śāstradīpikāyām `autvaktika' (1.1) iti pañṭamādhikaraṇe, jayantabhaṭṭena nyāyamañjaryām (6.ā.), viścanāthena muktāvalyāṃ śabdakhaṇḍe, śrīrāmānujamunitadanuyāyibhirbhāṣyatadvyākhyāśrutaprakāśikātattvaṭīkāsu jijñāsādikaraṇe, vādāntadeśikaḥ tattvamuktākalāpe ca, śrīmajjayatīrthasaṃyamīndraiḥ jijñāsādhikaraṇasudhāyāṃ, tattvanirṇayaṭīkāyāṃ ca sphoṭavādo bahudhā nirākṛtaḥ | na ca anyairdūṣaṇamātreṇa kakhamasmadācāryasiddhāntaḥ śakyastyaktumiti vācyam, bhāṣyādāvanukterapāṇinīyatvena ācāryasiddhāntābhāvāt |
dvaidhe bahūnāṃ vacanaṃ sameṣu guṇināṃ tathā |
guṇidvaidhe tu grājhaṃ ye guṇavattamā || (yā.va.vya.78)
iti vyavasthāpakanyāyena, sphoṭamanaṅgīkurvāṇānāṃ bahutvena, guṇitvena ca teṣāṃ vacanasyaiva grajhatvācca | yattu--`brajhākṣarasamudbhavaḥ' (gī.3.15) ityatra brajhapadaṃ sphoṭaparam, akṣarapadaṃ tadvyañjakaparamiti yādavaprakāśena vyākyānāt sphoṭo vyāsasaṃmata iti, pāṇinerapi saṃmata eveti, tanna | tatra brajhapadena karmakāraṇavastuno grājhatayā sphoṭasya kvāpi karmakāraṇatvānukteḥ | tatra-karma ṛtvigyajamānavyāpāraḥ, brajha-veda iti śāṅkarīye, brajha-prakṛtiriti rāmānujīyaṃ vyākhyānāt, anyaiśca-tatra brajha-paramātmaiva--
ahiṃsā samatā tuṣṭistapo dānaṃ yaśo'yaśaḥ |
bhavanti bhāvā bhūtānāṃ mattaeva pṛthagvidhāḥ || (gī.10.5)
iti gītāyāṃ paramātmanāṃ sakāśādeva dānādikarmasaṃbhavābhidhānāditi vyākhyānācca yādavaprakāśoktarenādaraṇīyatvat | na ca `akṣarāṇāmakārastvaṃ sphoṭastvaṃ varṇasaṃśrayaḥ' iti harivaṃśa hareḥ sphoṭatvokteḥ sphoṭasddhiriti vācyam, tena nāgeśādyabhimatā'siddheḥ, tathāhi-varṇasaṃśrayaḥ, tadātmaka iti yāvat, sphoṭaḥ - vācakatvadharmaḥ saḥ, tvam tvadadhīna ityarthaḥ | rājā rāṣṭramitivat sāmānādhikaraṇyena vyavahāra iti vacanatātparyeṇa nāgeśādyuktavilakṣaṇasphoṭasadbhāve tasya pramāṇatvābhāvāt | na ca `sūkṣmāmarthenā'pravibhaktatattvām' ityādi śritiḥ, `prāṇā'pānāntare devi' ityādi bhārataṃ ca tatra pramāṇamitadi śaktivāde mañjūṣāyāmuktamiti (4.20) vācyam | etayoḥ śrutismṛtyoḥ bhāṣyādāvānudāharaṇāttayoruktārthe'pramāṇatvāt | ata evoktaṃ `śrutismṛtī harugranthe sphoṣṭe' iti nāgeśena | ata eva coktaṃ bhāṭṭadīpikāyāṃ (1.3.11 a.) `śritiviruddhamidam' iti | śrutismṛtyoḥ sphoooṭapadāśravaṇāt tayoḥ sphoṭaparatvaṃ durvacam | śrutyarthastu--ekām - vibhāgātpūrvamekatvena sthitām, ātmani saṃniviṣṭāṃ-paramātmaikaparām, nānārūpām-anekaśākhām, ātmani saṃniviṣṭāṃ-paramātmaikaparām, nānārūpām-anekaśākhām, sūkṣmāṃ-ṇanantatvāt durvijñeyām, arthena apraviviktatattvāṃ-nitsaṃbaddhām, abhiṣyandamānām-abhimatārthupradām, tāṃ vācam-vedavāṇīm, anyāmiva-devatāntarapratipādanaparāmiva, anye-tattvārtānabhijñāḥ, viduḥ - jānanti' iti | `nārāyaṇaparā vedā' (śā.357.77) ityādi bhāratavacanādibhirvārāyaṇaparatvena nirṇītaṃ vedam, itihāsapurāṇādyapapabṛhaṇānabhijñā upakramāditātparyaliṅgānabhijñāśca pratīyamānadevatāntaraparaṃ manyanta iti bhāvaḥ | śabdānāṃ nābhyāditattadyeśasaṃbandhena paradivyavahāra ityeva bhāratavacanānāṃ tātparyam | ataḥ śrutibhāratayorna sphoṭaparatvam |
bhāṣye bhāśyakṛtā kvāpi sphoṭo'yaṃ na samarthitaḥ |
ācāryairvahubhiścāyaṃ sphoṭavādaḥ pradūṣitaḥ ||
śrutismṛtī na pramāṇaṃ tatra sphoṭapadāśruteḥ |
ataḥ kaiyaṭabhaṭṭojīnāgeśoktaṃ na sāṃpratam ||
sphoṭadūṣaṇāsaṃgatinirāsaḥ
nanu iyamapāṇinīyatā sphoṭasya, spaṣṭīkṛcā khalu granthakṛtaivosaṃhāre---
vaiyākaraṇanāgeśaḥ sphoṭāyanaṛṣermatam |
pariṣkṛtyoktavāstena prīyatāmumayā śivaḥ ||
ityannimadyena sphoṭāyanamataniṣkarṣa eva mayā kṛtaḥ ityabhidhānena | ato'tra bhavataḥ pravṛttiraphalā, ācūḍaṃ tadīgranthā'navalokanamūlā ceti manmaha iti cet ucyate---saṃpūrṇaṃ tadīyaṃ granthamavalokyaiva vayamatra pravṛttāḥ | tenopasaṃhre tathābhidhāne'pi ādau `vaiyākaraṇasiddhānta' iti sāmānyanirdeśena, tatra tatra mahābhāṣyavākyānāmudāharaṇena, kalā.yāṃ `pāṇinyādīnām' iti vaiyākaraṇapadavyākhyānena mudāharaṇena, kalāyāṃ `pāṇinyādīnām' iti vaiyākaraṇapadavyākhyānena ca, pāṇinerapyayaṃ sphoṭo'bhimata iti nāgeśāśayo vyajyate | upasaṃhāravākyasya tu sphoṭasya sphoṭāyanamunyupajñatāmātre tātparyam | yadyapāṇinīyatvaṃ nāgeśābhimataṃ, tarhi mahābhāṣyavākyāni nodāharet |
sphoṭasya tadupajñatāyāṃ mānaṃ tu--sphoṭo'yanaṃ pratipādyatayā āśraya ityanvarthanāmeda tasya ṛṣeriti `avaṅsphoṭāyanasya' (pā.sū.6.1) iti sūtraśekharoktireva | tarhi--kuto'yaṃ granthakāraḥ sphoṭe tadīyavacanaṃ pramāṇatayā nodājahāret codyasya samādhānaṃ sa eva nāgeśaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ | sphoṭāyanasyeti pāṭhapakṣe tu samādhānaṃ sa eva nāgeśaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ | sphoṭāyanasyeti pāṭhapakṣe tu sphoṭaprātipadikāt `naḍādibhyaḥ phak'(4.1.99) iti phak | atra pakṣesphoṭāyananāmā'nyaḥ kaścana ṛṣiḥsphoṭasya smartā iti `sphoṭāyana ṛṣermatam' iti nāgeśavacanatātadparyaṃ bodhyam | atha vā--sphoṭāyanapadāt bidāditvādañ, bidādirākṛtigaṇaḥ, nirvacanaṃ tu uktameveti | evaṃ ca--upakramopasaṃhārayorānuguṇyena prāptapāṇinīyatvasaṃśavyudāsāyāyamudyamaḥ |
ādausāmānyanirdeśāt bhāṣyodāharaṇādapi |
pāṇinīyo'thavā neti sphoṭe bhavati saṃśayaḥ ||
atasta tparihāreṇa pravṛttiḥ saphalā mama |
upasaṃhāravākyaṃ tu tadupajñatvabodhakam ||
śaktivicāraḥ
asmātpadādayamartho boddhavya itīśvarecchā śaktiḥ | tāmeva saṃketa iti vyavaharanti naiyāyikāḥ apare tu abhidhāyakatvamiti vadanti | etanmūlaka eva śabdārthayoḥ pratyāyya pratyāyakatvalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ,4 bodhyabodhakatvaṃ vācyavācakatvaṃ ceti śāstre vyavahāraḥ tatra jātau kāryanvite cārthe śaktiriti mīmāṃsakāḥ | ananvite jātyavacchinne'rthe iti naiyāyikāḥ | kvacijājātau, kvacidvyaktau iti vaiyākaraṇāḥ | nāgeśastu kvacidanvite'pītyāha | anvita evārtha ityabhiyuktāḥ | `vedāntinastṛ ṇanvite padānāṃ śakti' riti padārthadīpikāyāṃ kauṇḍabhaṭṭaḥ | atra pramāṇodāharaṇāni tattadgrantheṣu draṣṭavyāni | tatra ca śaktigkāhakaśiromaṇiḥ vṛddhavyavahāra eva bahusaṃmatā'nvitābhidhāne mānamiti vadanti | tathā hā--`gāmānaya' ityādi prayojakavṛddhavākyaśravaṇottaraṃ gavāyanādiprayojyavṛddhakartṛkavyāparaṃ paśyan pārśvastho bālaḥ prayojyo'yaṃ ānayanānvite gavi śaktiṃ gṛhītavānityanumāya prathamaṃ gopadasya ānayanānvite gavi śaktiṃ gṛṅṇāti, `gāṃ badhāne' ti vātyāntaraṃ śrutavato madhyamavṛddhasya gobandhanavyāpāraṃ paśyan ānayanāṃśapramoṣeṇa bandhanakriyānvite śaktiṃ gṛhṇan gavādipadārthe yoogyetarānvayayogyatā'stīti jānāti | tataḥ -- `gauḥ, ghaṭa, paṭaḥ' ityatiṅantāvākyaśravaṇe tatra kriyābodhakapadābhāvāt viseṣakriyāśravaṇe'piyogyakriyādītarānvite gavādau gopadādeḥ śaktiṃ gṛhṇāti, ityanuūvāt anvitārthavācakatvaṃ padānāṃ yuktam | prāthamiikaśaktigkahaviṣayāṇāṃ trayāṇāmanvayakriyātadviśeṣāṇāṃ madhye uktadiśā kriyātadviśeṣarūpāśadvayaparityāge bhinnabhinnakriyāvācakapadāśravaṇarūpakāraṇasattvepi anvayāṃśatyāge kāraṇābhāvaḥ | `ghaṭamānaya' ityukte kiṃ nīlaḥ, ata rakto ghaṭa ānetavyaḥ, `nīlamānaya' ityukte kiṃ nīlaḥ, uta rakto ghaṭa ānetavyaḥ, `nīsamānaya' ityukte kiṃ ghaṭaḥ uta paṭaḥ, `sundaramānaya' ityukte kiṃ puruṣaḥ atha ghaṭaḥ, `devadattamānaya' ityukte kiṃ paṇḍita athā'paṇḍitaḥ, atha brajhaṇaḥ, uta abrājhaṇaḥ, `devadatta' ityukte kiṃ karoti, `pacati' ityukte ka iti jījñāsodayaśca, anvitābhidhānaṃ draḍhayati | yadyanvito'rthe vācyo na bhaveti evaṃvidhajijñāsaiva nodiyāt | kiṃ cātra `kāraka' iti sūtrasthamahābhā,yānugraho'pyasti | tathā hi--- `anvarthasaṃjñā yathā vijñāyeta karotīti kārakamiti' iti bhāṣyam | atra kaiyaṭaḥ -- `sādhyatvena kriyaiva śabdātpratīyate kriyāyā nirvartakasya kārakasaṃjñā' iti | atrodyotaḥ -- `kri.yāyā eva śabdena sādhyatvapratīteḥ nirvartārthe kārakaśabde saṃbandhyākāṅakṣāyāṃ saiva saṃbadhyata iti bhāva' iti | `athavā yāvat brayāt kriyāyāmiti tāvatkāraka iti' iti bhāṣyam | sā hi kartrādīni viśiṣṭavyapadeśayuktāni karoti, viṣayatvaṃ ca, janakatvameva' ityudyotaḥ | atra saṃbandyākāṅakṣāyāṃ saiva saṃbandhyate, viṣayatvaṃma janakatvam' ityudyotoktyā, kriyākārakaparayorvyutpattipradarśanaparabhāṣyeṇa ca, kriyākārakapadāni sasaṃvandhakārthakāni, atooo niyataparasparākāṅakṣājanakārthavācakānīti anvitārthābhidhānasamarthāni bhavanti, etaddṛṣṭāntena sarvāṇi padāni tādṛśānīti śakyaṃ vaktum, sarvatrākāṅkṣā'stīti pūrvamupapāditam | anvayayoodyārthāntakaviṣayakākāṅkṣājanakārthābhidhānasamarthatvamanvitarthavācakatvam | tataśca anvitābhidhāne'sti bhāṣyādyanugraha iti bodhyam | ata eva mañjūṣāyāṃ `kvacitu padairitarānvitasvārthabodhanamastyeva...praviśa paṇḍīmityādau......viśiṣṭaśaktigkahakāle'nuniṣpādyavayavānāmapi tatra śaktigrahāt' ityuktam | `gehaṃ praviśa, piṇḍīṃ bhakṣaya' ityādivākyaiḥ visiṣṭārthaviṣayaśaktigrahāt iti mañjīṣārthaḥ | `igyaṇa' iti sūtrastha `praviśa piṇḍīm' utyāderapyevameva vyākhyānaṃ kṛtamudyote | evaṃ ca yogyetarānvitārthe padānāṃ śaktiriti bhāṣyasaṃmatamiti vadanti | `dvau brājhaṇau' ityādāviva paunaruktyaṃ bodhāvṛttiśceti doṣadvayaṃ suparihakamiti veditavyam |
tatra pratyāyakatvaṃ śaktirityetat bhāṣyakṛtāṃ saṃmataḥ pakṣaḥ | `yenoccāritena sāstrālāṅagūlakhurakakudaviṣāṇināṃ saṃpratyayo bhavati sa śabda' iti paspaśāyāṃ bhāṣyakṛtā arthasaṃpratyajanakatvaṃ śabdatvamiti śabdalakṣaṇanirūpaṇadvārā `pratyākatvaṃ' (artapratyāyakatvaṃ) śaktiriti śaktisvarūpasyāpi nirūpaṇāt | anena ca śabdārthayoḥ pratyāyyapratyāyakabhāvaḥ saṃbandha ityuktaṃ bhanati | sa ca saṃbandho nityaḥ `siddhe śabdārthasaṃbandha' iti vārtikabhāṣyābhyāṃ sabdārthatatsaṃbandhānāṃ nitadyatvabodhanāt | `autpattikastu śabdasyārthena saṃbandhaḥ' (jai.sū.) iti sūtrastha autpattakapadasya `padapadārthasaṃbandhasya nityatvamautpattikapadenoktam' iti pārthasārathimiśrairvyākyānāt tattatsaṃbandhanityatvaṃ jaiminyādīmapyabhimatamiti bodyam | tatra autpattikapadasya śabdābhivyaktikālika ityarthaḥ, bodhakatvaśaktiviśiṣṭaśabdasyaivābhivyaktiḥ, tataśca svābhāvika eva śabdārthayoḥ saṃbandha iti bhāṣyādibhāvaḥ evapameva manyante bahava ācāryāḥ saṃbandha iti bhāṣyarūḍhaḥ pakṣaḥ, bhāṣye'nukteḥ, `yenoccāritena arthasaṃpratyaya' iti bhāṣyeṇa cārthapratyāyakatvaṃ saṃbandha ityevāvagamāt | eta eva--`sabdārthapratyayānāmitaretarādhyāsātsaṃkīrṇa' (3.17) ityādi vibhūtipādīyayogasūtrabhāṣyatadvyākyāneṣaktopyadhyāso na śābdikairāśrayituṃ śakyaḥ, mahābhāṣye'nukteḥ | yogabhāṣyace vācaspatinā `saṃketopādhirekākāra pratyayo na tu tāttvika' ityuktatvena tayostādātmyaṃ na vāstavamityavadhe.yam , kiṃ ca ekākārapratyayo na tāttvika iti tatroktestanmūlakatādātmyamapi atāttvikameva | ñjūṣāyāṃ prātipadikārthacicāre tādātmyasya saṃbandhatvanirākaraṇācca | vistarastu sāravyākhyāne darpaṇe śaktivāde . vakṣyate caitatkiṃcit śābde śabdabhānavicāre'traiva | `artaṃ śṛṇu'1 ityādiprayogāstu lakṣaṇayā nirvoḍhavyāḥ | bhartṛharirapi manyate śabdānāṃ bodhakatvasaṃbandhaḥ svābhāvika iti tadīyakārikayā niūrūpayāmaḥ | tathā hi--
upakāraḥ sa yatrā'sti dharmastatrānumīyate |
śaktīnāmapyasau śaktirguṇānāmapyasau guṇaḥ ||
indriyāṇāṃ svaviṣaye jhanādiryogyatā yathā |
anādirarthaiḥ śabdānāṃ saṃbandho yogyatā tathā ||
samayādyogyatāsaṃvinmātāputrādiyogavat |
saṃbandha(ndhi)śabde śaṃbandho yogyatāṃ prati yogyatā || iti |
ayamarthaḥ-yatra, upakāraḥ-upakārasvabhāvaḥ, saḥ-saṃbandhaḥ, yatrā'sti, tatra, dharmaḥ-kāryajanakatvaśaktirūpo dharmaḥ, anumīyate | asau-saṃbandhaḥ, śaktīnāmapi--kāryajanakatvasāmarthyānāmapi, śaktiḥ - tatpradā (taduttejikā) | yathā dīpaḥ vastuprakāśakatvasāmartyavānapi vastusaṃbandhādeva vastu prakāśayati, tatā śabdaḥ svābhāvikaśaktimānapi arthasaṃbaddhaḥ arthamupasthāpayati | sa saṃbandhaśca tādātmyādhyāsa iti nāgesaḥ | anādivyavahārādirityanye | yathā indriyāṇāṃ svaviṣaye -- cakṣurādīnāṃ rūpādiviṣayakajñānajanane, yogyatā-sāmartāyaviseṣaḥ, anādiḥsvābhāvikī, tathā anādirarthaiḥ | saṃbando'pi, yogyatā - bodhajanakatāvacchedako dharmaḥ svābhāvikaḥ | ata evoktaṃ śabdaratne sarūpasūtre-- `anyathā vidyamānāyāḥ śakteḥ ekaśeṣaśāstrasahastreṇāpyupapādayitumaśakyam' iti | anena sabdānāmarthadhītanakatvasāmarthya svata evāsti, na saṃketādipuruṣaprayatnairutpādyam | puruṣayatnaratu vidyamānaśaktereva jñāpaka ityuktaṃ bhavati | saṃbandhaśabde--saṃbandhiśabde, (arthabodhakatvarūpasaṃbandavisi,ṭe), yogyatāṃ prati---bodhajanakatayāvacchedakadharmaṃ prati, (tannirūpaka iti yāvat) saṃbandaḥ -saṃkerādiḥ, yogyatā - arhatā, śabdārthayoḥ saṃbande'stīti tadarthaḥ | saṃbandha eva śabdaniṣṭhārthabodhakatvānādisaṃbandhānumāpaka iti bhāvaḥ | yogyatāsaṃvit - saṃbandhani,ṭhayogyatājñānaṃ ca, samayāt - asmadādivyahārāt . dṛṣṭāntamāhamātetyādi | arthaḥ spaṣṭaḥ | kārikāṇāṃ prācīnanavīnagrantheṣu yojanābhedadarśane'pi bodhakatvaśaktiḥ svābhāvikī, tadanumāpara īśvarasaṃketādiriti tātparyaṃ samānam |
tatra kaṣāṃcudācāryāṇāṃ matamanūdya anyakṛtadūṣaṇānuvādena na yuktamiti sūcayati kauṇḍabhaṭṭo būṣaṇe śaktivāde | tadanūdito'yaṃ dūṣaṇagkanthaḥ --`kvacitasaṃketasya, kvacicchakterjñānaṃ kāraṇamiti vyabhicārādekasyāpi tattathā na syāt, asmākaṃ punaḥ saṃketatvarūpeṇaiva tajjñānaṃ heturutu na vyabhicāraḥ, tacceśvarasaṃketasādāraṇam | na ca--asmadādīnāmeva sa tathāstu niyataśaktisādutvādyanurodhena bhagavata eva tatkalpanāt | sṛṣṭyādivyavahāre tvayāpi tasya śaktigrāhakatvenā'bhyupetavyatvāt | ubhayasiddheśvareccāṃ tvayāpi tasya śaktigrāhakatvenā'bhyupetavyatvāt | ubhayasiddheśvarecchāṃ tyaktvā'tiriktaśaktikalpane sagauravācca | tādṛśeśvarasaketitatvaṃ ca sādhutvaṃ tatparicchedakaṃ vyākaraṇāditi na tadvaiyarthyam | evaṃ ca sarvathāpi atiriktapadārthāntarakalpanamayuktamiti saṃkṣepaḥ | tasmādīśverecchaiva śaktiriti naiyāyikānusāriṇa' iti |
atra vadanti śrīmajjayatīrtānuyāyinaḥ -- kvacitsaṃketasya, kvacicchakterjñānaṃ kāranamityetat na śrīmajjayatīrtānāṃ rāddān‌taḥ, kiṃ tu sarvatra svābhāvikyā śaktyā bodhaḥ, saṃketastu tadanumāpakaḥ iti | ato noktaparasparavyatirekavyabhicārāvakāśaḥ | ata eva paddhatau teṣāṃ padārthasaṃbandho'pi svābhāvika evetyuktaṃ taiḥ | sudhāyāṃ ca svata eveti pratīkamādāya svābhāvikenaiveti coktam . sṛṣṭyādivyavahāre īśvarasaṃketo'pi svābhāvikaśakteranumāpaka eveti na tadā tasya svātantryeṇārthabodhakatvamiti tadāśayaḥ | etacca `athavā' ityādinottaragranthena spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ taireva nyāyasudāyām | nanvevaṃ `na vayaṃ kvacidapi sabdasya saṃketādvodhakatvaṃ nāsitīti brūmaḥ, kiṃ tu kvacitsvābhāvikyā saktyā bodhaḥ, kvacitsaṃketāt' iti kathamuktamiti cecchrūyatām-atra naiyāyikoktadūṣaṇasya, mīmāṃsakoktasamādhānānuvādo'yam | āśaṅki tavyabhicāraparihārastu śaktilakṣaṇārūpavṛttidvayamahgīkurvatā bhavateva kāryatāvacchedakakoṭau kāranavaiśiṣṭyaṃ niveśya svottaratvaniveśena supariharastaiḥ | nanu yavapaśabdasya priyaṅgo gauṇatvamityeva dṛś.yate tadīyeṣu granteṣu, na tu saṃketa iti cet ucyate-gauṇatvavyavahāro'pi yavavarāhidhikaraṇasiddhāntaniṣkarṣottarakālika eva, tataḥ pūrvaṃ yavaśabdasyāpi priyaṅgau saṃketa eveti mimāṃsakāsayaḥ | anyathobhayaprasiddhyoḥ samapradhānyābhāvena pūrvapakṣasyaivānutthityā yavavarāhādhikaraṇānutthitiprasaṃgaḥ | lāghavagauravacarcā tu śuṣkakolāhala mlecchavyavahārasya cirakālikatvena lapoke tatra gauṇatvavyavahārādarśanātsaṃketetyādyuktam |
idaṃ padasyārthaṃsya bodhakaṃ bhavitviti hīśvareccā''kāraḥ, tatra bodhakatvaṃ viṣayatayā bhāsata iti icchātiriktasvābhāvikasaṃbandharūpabodakatvamastīti pratiteḥ atiriktaśaktirastyeveti svīkartavyam | ataḥ śrīmajjayatīrtānāmanyeṣāṃ cācāryāṇāṃ matameva yuktam | īśvarasaṃketitadatvaṃ sādhutvamiti tu na yuktam | ādhunikapurūṣakṛtaṭi-ghuṭabhādisaṃjñānāmīśvarakṛtatvābhāvena sādhutvābhāvaprasaṅgāt . saṃketitvaṃ sādhutvamityuktau gāvyādīnanāmapi saṃketaśabdatvāt sādhutvāpātaḥ | ataḥ saṃketitivātiriktaḥ vyākaraṇaśāstravyaṅgyo'bhyudayaprayojako dharmaviseṣaḥ sādhutvam, anapabhraṣṭatvaṃ vā taditi vaktavyamiti saṃketi tatvasādhutvayoranyatvameva | evaṃ ca nuyāyikānusāriṇīṃ jayatīrthagranthadūṣaṇaṃ, kauṇḍabhaṭṭas‌ya tadanubhāṣaṇenānumodanaṃ ca jayatīrthoyasya `athavā' ityādyuttaragranthasya, grantāntarasya cā'navalokanibandhanamiti vidāṃkurvantu sūraya iti |
bodhakatvaṃ śabdaśaktiḥ sāpi svābhāvikī matā |
saṃketo vyañjakastasyā ityetadvahusaṃmatam ||
idameva mataṃ śrīmajjayatīrthamunerapi |
tadāśayamajānāno nyāyavit dūṣayannidam ||
modate kauṇḍabhaṭṭaśca dūṣaasyānubhāṣaṇāt |
tasmāttāvubhāvetau vijñeyau sthūladarśinau ||
lakṣaṇāvicāraḥ
nanu prayoganirvāhaṃ lakṣaṇayā kartuṃ na śaknuvanti śābdikāḥ, yato'yaṃ nāgeśo mañjūṣādvaye `sarvesarvārthavācakā' iti samayābhyupagamena gaṅjāpadasya tīre'pi śaktimabhyupetya lakṣaṇāvṛttiṃ nirākarot | bhūṣaṇatatsārakārāvapi tāṃ vṛttiṃ nirākurvātām | na ca `sarvesarvārtha-' iti samayo na bhāṣyakṛtsaṃmata iti vācyam | `kutvaṃ kasmānna bhavati' iti vṛddhacāhnikabhāṣyasaṃmato'yaṃ samaya iti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | tathā hi--tatra praśnāśayaṃ vivṛṇvatākaiyaṭena aijādipadānāṃ śāstravisayatvahetubhūtalaukikatvasādhakaḥ `ane1kaśakteḥ sabdasya'ityādinā śabdārthasaṃbandanityatvarūpaheturuktaḥ, nāgesena `sarvesarvārthavācakā' ityabhyupagamāditi pradīpāsayaṃ nirūpayatā kathamekasya śabdasyā'nekasaktitvamiti śaṅkā vāritā | tataśca praśnabhāṣyāśayavarṇanāvasare pradīpodyotayorevamukterbhāṣyānugṛhīto'yaṃ pakṣa ityavadhārayāmaḥ | evaṃ ca lakṣaṇāsthale'pi śaktyaiva nirvāhena sakṣaṇāvṛtterabhāvāt | iti cet, atrāhuḥ- `sarveṣāṃ sarvārthavācakatā tāvat na sarvānubhavasiddā, ato'nekaśakteḥ ityādinoktahetuḥ na laukikatvasādhanasamarthaḥ | ata eva anukaraṇatvājātitvādveti hetvantaramabhidhatte kaiyaṭaḥ | `apūrvāḥ saṃjñāḥ kriyanta iti pakṣe'pyāha' iti avātāritamidaṃ vākyamudyote | jātitvāditi trayīpakṣe | atra pakṣe saṃjñāśavdānāṃ jātiśabdeṣvantarbhāvaḥ teṣāmapūrvatvaṃ vetyetadanyo vicāraḥ | kiṃ ca anekaśaktitvaṃ vā'nāditvaṃ vetyetadanyo vicāraḥ | kiṃ ca anekaśaktitvaṃ haryādiśabdavat nānārthakatvābhāyupagamamātreṇopapadyata iti `sarve sarvārtha' ityabhyupagamo nāvasyakaḥ ato'tra samaye na bhāṣyānugrahaḥ | kiṃ ca yadi `sarve sarvārtha' iti pakṣa āśrīyate tarhi vyajyamānā api sarve'rthoḥ śaktyaiva pratīyanta iti vaktuṃ śakyamiti vyajyanāvṛttiḥ pralayaṃgatā syāt | na yeṣṭāpattiḥ, vyañjanāvṛttikavasyamāśrīyaṇiyeti mañjūṣāyāṃ (pra.pa) vyavasthāpitatvāt | abhidhālakṣaṇetyādivṛttivibhāgaṃ kurvatā śāstrakṛtāṃ bahūnāmācāryāṇāṃ kāvāyaprakāśakagṛnmammaṭabhaṭṭā2dīnāmālaṅkārikāṇāṃ ca saṃmatatvāt | na kevalaṃ pare,āmatra saṃmatiḥ kiṃ tu bhāṣyakṛtāmapi | tathā hi -- `kathaṃ tuṣajakasya patnī' iti `patnīva patnītyupamānāt' iti `patyurnavo yajñasaṃyoge' (4.1.33) iti sūtraṃ yajñasaṃbandhavadvācakapatnīśabdasya sādṛśyāt vṛṣalabhāryādiīnā prakāracatuṣṭayena siṃhādiśabdānāṃ māṇavakādau vṛttiṃ pradarśayanto bhāṣyakārā lakṣaṇāvṛttiṃ pṛthageveti manyanta iti spaṣṭameva viduṣāmityavehi | kiṃ ca tathā yogavācaspatye aktaṃ `sarve ca śabdāḥ sarvārthābhidhānasamarthā' (ma.4.15) iti. `sarnairarthaiḥ sarveṣāṃ ca śabdānāṃ saṃbandhaśca yogijñānahamya eva' iti mañjūṣokteḥ, paribhāṣenduśekhare `sarve sarvārthavācakā ityabhyupagamo'pi yogidṛṣṭyā nāsmaddaṣṭyā' ityādyukteśca ismadādīnāṃ gaṅgādipadaiḥ tīrādibodhaḥ śaktyeti na śakyate vaktumiti nāgeśenaivoktaprāyamiti lakṣaṇāvṛttirāśrayaṇīyaivā'smābhiḥ | kiṃ ca -- `sarve sarvārthavācakā' ityabhyupagacchatāmapi akṣaharyādipadavat vibhinnaśakyatāvacchedakāvacchinnanānārtavācakatvaṃ śabdasyetyevā'bhiprāyaḥ, tathā ca--gaṅkāpadaṃ pravāhatvena pravāhabodhakaṃ, tīratvena tīrabodhakamityeva vaktavyaṃ, na vu āropitapravāhatvena tīrabodhakamiti | tathā sati prasiddhaṃ tīrapadaṃ vihāya aprasiddhagaṅkāpadaprayogo'narthakaḥ | na ca tīrapadāt vivakṣitaśaityādyapratīteḥ tīre gaṅkātvamāropyate ārapitadharmapuraskāreṇa gaṅgāpadāt tīrabodhe śaityapāvanatvādipratītirghoṣe iti vācyam | `sarve sarvārtha-' ityanena bhavatāmativesamanorathā'lābhāt | āropāṅgīkāre lakṣaṇāyāmeva paryavasānācca | kiṃ ca -- `patnīva patnī' ityupamānāt, caturbhiḥ prakāraiḥ iti bhāṣyokteḥ, tatsāmīpyādinā śakyatāvacchedakasyāropa ityāropovateśca prayojanaṃ durnirūpaṃ śaktyā tīrīdibodhavādinā | na hi loke śaktyā padārtha bodhyanta iti vādinaḥ sāmīpyādidharmān nimittatayā āśrayanti | kiṃ ca -- `kibahunā yeṣāṃ pratyayānāmanyeṣāṃ ca śabdānāṃ yatrārthe śāstreṇa vidānaṃ teṣāmanyārthe tadarthe vānyeṣāṃ lakṣaṇayāpi sādhutvābhāvasya `pratyayaḥ, paraśca, puṃyogāt' iti sūtrabhāṣyato lābhāditi nāgesena `yena vidhiḥ' iti sūtraśekhare kṛtavyavasthā bhajyeta sarveṣāṃ sarvārthakatve | kiṃ ca `tadasyā'styasmin' iti sūtre asyāsminnityarthadvayopādānāt sarveṃ sarvārthanācakā iti na sūtrakṛtsaṃmataṃ yadi tat tadiṣṭaṃ syāt, tarhi anyatarasaminnarthe pratyayavidhāne'pi gomānityādeḥ `sarve sarvārtha' iti sarvārthavācakā iti na sūtrakṛtsaṃmataṃ yadi tat tadadiṣṭaṃ syāt, tarhi anyatarasminnarthe pratyayavidhāne'pi gomānityādeḥ `sarve sarvārta' iti samayāt ubhayārthakatvaṃ śakyaṃ vaktumityubhayopādāne na phalam | evaṃ ca gaṅgāyāṃ dhoṣa ityādiprayoganirvāhāya śābdikaiḥ lakṣaṇāvṛttirāśrayaṇīyaiva | ata eva -- `raghuyaduśabdayostadapatye lakṣaṇā, na subvibhaktau lakṣaṇā' ityādi śābdikānāṃ vyavahāraḥ svarasataḥ saṃgacchate | etena idṛśesthale aprasiddhaśaktiriti tātparyam | gauṇamukhyanyāyo'pi prasiddhyaprasiddhitātparyakaḥ | `lakṣaṇayā' ityādi vyavahāraḥ paramatarītyā iti kalpanaṃ kliṣṭameva | tataśca lakṣaṇāvṛttirneti vādaḥ sūtrabhāṣyakṛtanabhimata eveti bhāti |
lakṣaṇā neti vāde'yaṃ naiva bhāṣyakṛtāṃ mataḥ |
iti bruvanti sūdhiyo mahābhāṣyavimarsakāḥ ||
gotvādijātīnāṃ vyāpakatvanirāsaḥ
yaduktaṃ mañjūṣāyāṃ `sarvajātīnāṃ vyāpakatve'pi padārtānāṃ vicitraśaktitvātkāṃcideva jātīṃ kaścitpadārtho'bhivyanakti' ityādinā gotvādijātīnāṃ vyāpakatvamiti, tasyā'yamāsayaḥ - gotvādervyāpakatvenā'śvatvameva vyanaktiḥ gotvameva vyavakti nāsvatvam, tathā'śvavyaktiraśvatvameva vyanakti na gotvamiti gavādiśabedena nā'śvādibodhaḥ, gopadasya svavācyavṛttilokaprasiddhagotvamātrabodhajanakatvasāmarthyavattvaṃ natvaśvatvādibodhajanakaśaktimattvamiti | vyākyāne tu-upādhisvabhāvādityuktam | tatropādhiḥ ka iti cintyaḥ |
atra vadanti---gotvādijātīnāṃ vyāpakatvamityatra na pramāṇamupalabhyate, bhā,ye tu nityataiva proktā jāteḥ, na tu vyāpakatā | `nanu brajhaṇa eva pramāṇasiddhatayā sarve bhāvā brajhaṇaḥ śaktibhūtā na tu tato bhinnā' iti ma़jūṣayāṃ sarvadārthanāṃ brajhaśaktirūpatvokteḥ, tasyāśca vyāpakatvena tadrūpagotvāderapi vyāpakatvasiddhiriti cet, na | brajhaṇo nirdharmakatvāṅkīkartṛnāgeśamate śaktestadīyatvā'prasiddeḥ | vyāsapāṇinipatañjalīnāṃ nābhedavāditvamiti prāṅanirūpitatvena sarvabhāvānāṃ brajhaśaktirūpatvasya brajhānaneyatevasya ca vyāsādimunitrayāsaṃmatvācca | etena `brajhasattārūpāyā gotvajāteḥ' ityādi vyākhyānoktirapi nirastā | darśitabhedavādimunitrayamate gotvāderbrajhasattārūpatvābhāvāt | nirdharmakatvavādinā sattāyā brajhītvasya durnirūpatvāt | atra `brajhaṇa eva pramāṇasiddhatayā' ityuktyā brajhātiriktasarvabhāvānāṃ, gotvādijātīnāṃ ca brajhaśaktirūpatvamapyaprāmāṇikameveti siddhyediti vibhāvyam | `yadbhinneṣvabhinnaṃ, chinneṣvaccinnam, ākṛtirnāma sā' iti sarpasāhnikabhāṣyeṇa, `ākṛtyabhidhānādvā, na hi gaurityukte vyapavargaḥ gamyate śuklo nīla' iti sarūpasūtrabhā,yeṇa ca, tattatpadārthatāvacchedakaḥ śakyatāvacchadetatvena pratīyamāno jātipadavācyo gotvādirūpo dharmaḥ tattacchabadena pratīyata ityarthasyaiva lābhena tadbhaṣyato vyāpakatvā'lābhāt | yadi manyate bhavān vyāpakatvaṃ gotvādūnāṃ, tarhi prameyatvādivat vyāpakaṃ gotvamapi gopadaśakyatāvacchedakaṃ na syāt | vyāpakasya avacchedakatvāyogāt iti gopadaśakyatāvacchedako dharmaḥ sarvakavilokasaṃmataḥ kaścana saṃpādanīyo'tiprasaṅkavāraṇāya | sa ca na sulabhaḥ, mañjūṣāvyākyāyāṃ gotvādermahākattāvivarttavoktiḥ, dhātvarthavādama़ñjūṣāyāṃ bhūvādisūtrodyote ca, `jagatkāraṇasya sattārūpabrajhaṇo māyāsahakāreṇa kriyārūpā vivartā' at nāgeśoktiśca cintyā | evaṃ munitrayānukteḥ, pāṇinyādyanabhimataprameyajātaṃ kataṃ vaiyākaraṇasiddhāntavyapadesārhaṃ bhaveti | `ṣaṭbhāva vikārā bhavanti' iti niruktavacanasya niruktabhāṣye mahābhā,ye ca evamarthā'varṇanāt, kaiyaṭe bhāvasya kriyāyāṃ ityeva vyākyānāt, `bhāvapradhānamākhyātaṃ, sattvapradhānāni nāmāni' iti pūrvaṃ prastutavacanaikadeśabhāvabhedavivaraṇaparaitadvacanagatabhāvapadasya kriyet.yeva vyākhyānasyocitatvāt, bhūvādisūtre astyādīnāṃ kriyāvācakatvopapādanāyo panyastavacanagatabhāvapadasya padārthasamanetadhātvarthasya kriyetivyavahriyamāṇasya vakṣyamāṇajanmādayaḥ ṣaṭprabhedā ityeva tātparyamiti pacañjalerāśayāt, yāvaddravyabhāvisaditivyavahārahetubhūtātmadhāraṇarūpapadārthamātravṛttikriyāpadavyapadeśyasattāyā brajhavivartatve mānābhāvāt, patañjalimate brajhaṇo vivartakāraṇatvābhāvācya | nanu `tadbhāṣyakṛta' ityuttaratra mañjūṣokteḥ, sarvamidaṃ niruktabhāṣyakārasaṃmatamiti cet, na | niruktabhāṣye evamānupūrvīkavākyasya etadarthakavākyāntarasya vā'darśanāt tadbhāṣyakṛtsaṃmatamidamiti na viśvasimaḥ |
kecitu bhāvānāṃ ghaṭapaṭādīnāmarthanāṃ vikārāḥ ṣaḍiti vacanārthamāhu | ataḥ sarvāsāṃ jātīnāṃ vyāpakatvādyapāṇinīyameva |
idaṃ tvavadheyam--ye tvatra mañjūṣyāṃ nirūpitāḥ sphoṭatadekatvādayaḥ, prapañcamithyātvādayo viṣayāḥ te sarvepyapāṇinīyā abādarāyaṇīyāḥ | pāṇinīye bādarāyaṇīye ca tathārthakavacanānupalambhāt, jaganmithyātvādinirūpaṇaṃ ca `vaiyākaraṇasiddhānta' ityādipratijñāviruddhaṃ prapañcamithyātvādervaiyākaraṇasiddāntatvābhāvāt | kiṃ bahunā `kvacidādhanikagranthānusāreṇa likhitamasti' (ma.1.) iti granthakṛduktireva nirūpitavi,yāṇāmapāṇinīyatvaṃ prakaṭayati | tatoktyā prācāmācāryāṇāṃ grantānusāreṇa na viṣayā nirūpitā iti pratīterityalam |
gotvādijātervyāpitvaṃ brajhaśaktitva eva ca |
bhāvānāṃ brajharūpatve bhāṣye mānaṃ na dṛśyate ||
sphoṭe prapañcamithyātvapramukhe viṣaye tathā |
na pāṇinivaco vyakavacanaṃ ca na dṛśyace ||
ato nābhimataṃ vyāsapāṇinyorakhilaṃ tvidam |
iti vyāsādicaraṇaśaraṇā bruvate budhāḥ ||
śābde śabdabhāne gautamasaṃmatinirāsaḥ
śābdabodhe sarvatra śabdasya na bhānamiti naiyāyikānāṃ matamiti sarvaviditam | evaṃsthite'pi śabdaḥ śābde bhāsata iti nirūpya `andriyārthasaṃnikarṣotpannaṃ jñānamavyapadesyamavyabhicāri vyavasāyātmakaṃ pratyakṣam' (nyā.sū.1.1.4) iti pratyakṣalakṣaṇe `avyapadeśyatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ vadatā gautamena śābde śabdabhānasyāṅkīkārācca' iti gautamasaṃmatimāha mañjūṣāyām (pra.pa.32.20) | atredaṃ cittyam -- atra sūtre `avyapadeśyam' ityasya aśābdamityarthaḥ | arthapratyakṣakāle na śabdaḥ pravartate | śa6bdo na tatra pratyakṣaviṣayabhūtārthadhiyaṃ janayati | atastasyāśabdatvam | sābdabodhakāse tu śabdaḥ pravartate tadviṣayabhūtamarthamupasthāpayan taṃ janayati | ataḥ śābdatve tasyā | na tu sabdaviṣayakatveneti tadviśeṣaṇatātparyamiti nyāyasiddhāntaprameyaṃ tatsūtrabhāṣyādito labhyate | tathā hi--tatra avyapadeśyaviśeṣaṇakṛtyavicāraparaṃ bhāṣyam -- " yāvadarthaṃ vai vāmadheyaśabdāstairarthasaṃpratyayaḥ | arthasaṃpratyayācca vyavahāraḥ | tatredamindriyārthasaṃnirarṣādutpannamartajñānaṃ `rūpamiti vā rasa ityevaṃ vā' bhavati | rūparasaśabdāśca viṣayanāmadheyaṃ tena vyapadisyate jñānaṃ, rūpamiti jānīte, rasa itati jānīte | nāmadeyaśabdena vyapadisyamanaṃ sat śābdaṃ prasajyate | ata āha -- avyapadesyamiti | yadidamanupayukte (ajñāte) śabdārthasaṃbandhe'rthajñānaṃ na tannāmadeyaśabdena vyapadisyate, gṛhīte'pi ca śabdārthasaṃvandha asyārthasyāyaṃ śabdo nāmadheyamiti | yade tu--so'rthoo gṛjhate tadā tat pūrvasmādarthajñānānna viśiṣyate | tadarthavijñānaṃ tādṛgeva bhavati | tasya tvarthajñānāsyā'nyaḥ samākyāśabdo nāstīti yena pratīyamānaṃ vyavahārāya kalpeta | na cā'pratīyamānena vyabahāraḥ | tasmāt jñeyasyārthasya saṃjñāśabdeneti karaṇayuktena nirdiśyate `rūpamiti jñānaṃ, rasa iti jñānam' iti | tadevamarthajñānakāle sa na samākhyāśabdo vyapriyate, vyavahārakāle tu vyāpriyate | tasmādaśabdamarthajñānamindriyārthasaṃnnikarṣotpannamiti" iti | atra `yāvadartham' ityantaḥ samādānagranthaḥ | yāvadarthamityavyayībhāvaḥ `yāvadavadāraṇe' ityantaḥ samādhānagranthaḥ | yāvarthamityavyayībhāvaḥ `yāvadavadhāraṇe' iti sūtreṇa | yāvanto'rtā iti vigrahaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-śabdārthayostādātmyāt artapratītisthale sarvatra vācakaśabdasaṃvalitasyārthasya bhānam | tataśca śābde yathā śbasaṃvalitasyārthasya viṣayatayā arthavacchabdasyāpi viṣayatvena bhānāt śābdatvaṃ tathā pratyakṣe'pi śabdaviśiṣṭārthasya (viṣayatvena) bhānāt sabdaviṣayakatvena śābdatvaṃ syāt | astu kā no hāniriti cet tasya pramāṇān‌taratvaṃ na syāditi |
samādhānagranthasya tvayamāsayaḥ -- śabdārthauṃ vastuto bhinau | na tādātmyaṃ tayoḥ na ca tadāropyata iti vācyam, tatra phalābhāvāt | ataḥ kvacidapi jñāne na śabdasaṃvalitārthasya bhānaṃ kiṃ tu kevalārthasyaiva, tathaivānubhavāt | nanvevaṃ śābdesya bhānamiti na tasya śābdatvavyavahāraḥ syāditi cet, ucyate--śabdaviṣayakatvaṃ tādṛśavyavahāraṃ prati na prayojakaṃ, kiṃ cu śabdopasthāpitārthaviṣayakatvaṃ, tacca tatrāstīti tathā vyavahāra iti | tathā ca pratyakṣe indriyasaṃnikṛṣṭavastumātraṃ vi,yaḥ, na tu śabdopasthāpitaṃ (śabdasaṃvatitaṃ vā) vastu | atastasya śābdatvaṃ neti pramāṇāntaratvaṃ sustham | `arthabhānakāre1 sa na samākhyāśabdo vyapkiyate, vyavahārakāse tu vyapriyata' ityanena bhāṣyakṛtaivokto'yamartha iti | evaṃ ca--śābde śābdabhāvaṃ gautamādīnāṃ saṃmatamityuktirna samañjasā | etna---`sabdaviṣayakatvena pratyakṣasya śābdaviṣayakatvameva sābdatvaṃ na tu śabdajanyatvamiti tatraiva vācaspatumiśrairiti bhāṣyaṭīkāgranthapanyasya sābdasya śabdavi,yakatvaṃ tadabhimatamityuktiḥ parāstā | ubhayerapi tayorgranthayoscaudakāśayavarṇanaparatvena siddān‌tagranthatvābhāvāt śabdārtayostādātmyasya atraiva sūtre nyāyavārtikatātparyaṭīkāyāṃ vācaspatinā bahudā nirākaraṇāt | itavanaiyāyikairanabhyupagamācca |
pratyakṣe śabdo viṣayo netyuktimātreṇa śābde tadaṅgīkāryaṃ teneti ko'yaṃ niyamaḥ | gautamastu sābde bhāsata iti mahatā kaṇṭhena kvāpi nābhāṣi | jayantabhaṭṭo naiyāyikaśiromaṇiḥ | nyāyama़ñjaryāṃ (1.ā.) avyapadeśyapadakagṛtyavarṇanāvasare, anyatra ca prakaraṇe śābde śabdabhāvapakṣaṃ pratyācaṣṭe | tasmādidaṃ kathaṃ gautamābhimataṃ bhavet | kiṃ ca--`vyaktyākagṛtijātayastu padārthaḥ' (gau.sū.2.26.8) iti vyaktyādīnāṃ trayāṇameva padārthatvokteḥ, sabdasya triṣvanantarbhāvāt padatṛttyaviṣayatvena tasya śābde bhānaṃ gautamasaṃmatamityetanna śobhanam | asti śabdasyāpi vṛttiviṣayatvamiti tu na gautamasiddhāntaḥ kiṃ tu bhartṛhakeḥ,`grājhatvaṃ gkāhakatvaṃ ca' iti tadukteḥ | vṛttyaviṣayasyāpi sābde bhānamiti tu na sādhu manyante śāstrakārāḥ | yadi vṛttyaviṣayasyāpi bhānaṃ tarhi ghaṭamānayetyukte samavāyenopasthitākāśasyāpi sābde bhānaprasahkaḥ | kiṃ ca śābde śbadabhānamiti sābdikasiddhāntopyanārṣa eva yataste pāṇinyādyṛṣivacanamanudāhṛtya `na so'sti pratyayo loke yaḥ śabdānugamādṛta' ityanārṣaṃ vākyapadīyavacanamena pramāṇatayodāharanti | tatasca anārṣasiddhāntānisāreṇa ṛṣitātparyāvadharaṇaṃe viduṣāṃ manase na rocate | nanu arthena śabdasmaraṇāt smṛtasya tasya śābde bhānamiti cet, na | kadācit deśakālapuruṣāṇāmapi smaraṇasaṃbhavāt teṣāmapi bhānaprasaṅkāt | `unnitabhānapakṣastu na sarvasaṃmataḥ' iti dinakarīyaśabdakhaṇḍaḥ nāgeśena ma़ñjaṣāyāṃ (pa.1) nirākṛtāśca | kiṃ ca evaṃkalpane gotamavacanānigraho'pi ṛgyaḥ | ataḥ `śābde śabdabhānasya gautamenāṅgīkārācca' iti nāgesoktirna yuktā | anukaraṇe, saṃjñāsthale ca śabdasyaiva tathākalpanaṃ na yujyate |
pratyakṣe'vyapadeśyatvaviśeṣaṇaniveśanam |
śabdentiśiraskeṇa tadākārābhilāpanāt ||
śābdatvameva tasya syāditi śaṅkānivārakam |
atastena tadiṣṭārthasiddirnaiva bhaviṣyati ||
gautamo nāvadacchabdaḥ śāvde bhātīti kaṇṭhataḥ |
bhāṣyakārādayo nāhustasmātteṃṣāṃ na tanmatam ||
pratyaye śabdabhāne munitrayānabhimatatvanirūpaṇam
kiṃ ca `śābde'nyatra ca pratyaye śabdo bhāsata' ityetat ma़jūṣādāvuktaṃ na munitrayasaṃmatam, taistathānukteḥ | nanu `śabdapūrvakāscāsya saṃpratyaya' iti `svaṃ rūpam' iti sūtrabhāṣyasya `śabdaviśeṣaṇakascā'rthasaṃpratyaya' ityudyotakṛtā vyākyānāt `svaṃ rūpam' (pā.sū.1.1.38) iti sūtraṃ, tadbhāṣyaṃ ca tatra pramāṇaṃ, śabdaviśeṣaṇakatvaṃ ca śabdaprakārakatvaṃ, ghaṭatvādīnāṃ prakāratāvat śabdānāmapi sā, śabdaprakārikaiva sarvā dhīriti tadartha iti munitrayasaṃmatameveti cet, na | tadbhā,yākṣaramaryādayā tatā'rthalābhāt, kaiyaṭena tathā tadbhāṣyasyāvyakyātatvācca | nanu kastarhi bhā,yārta iti cet, ucyate---śabdajñānapūrvakaśca śroturarthapratyaya iti bhāṣyārthaḥ | tathā hi--śabdajñānapūrvako'rthasaṃpratyaya iti sarvalokasaṃpratipannam | śabdānupalambhe vā prayukte jñāte ca tasmin, jñāto'rthaḥ karma kurmo yathāniyogamityuttaradānācca | tataśca lokavyutpattyanusāreṇa `rājaśvaśurādyat' (pā.sū.4.1) `agnerḍhak' (pā.sū.4.2) ityādāvupāttarājādayaḥ śabdāḥ `paśurapatyaṃ devatā' ityādi śabdā iva arthaparā eva | athāpi arthe śāstrīyakāryāṇāṃ yadādipratyayānāṃ bādāt pratipadikādityadhitārācca rājādivācakaśabdasāmānyāt yadādiḥ prānpoti | na ca pratyāsattyā kāraṇatayopasthitarājādiśabda eva gṛjhate natvanyaḥ (paryāyādiḥ) iti vācyam, tannyāyāśrayaṇe pramāṇābhāvāt | ataḥ svarūpameva gṛjhata iti na śakyaṃ vaktumiti śaṅkāpanodanāya `svaṃ rūpam' iti sūtraṃ prarabhate pāṇiniḥ | asya tvayamartaḥ -- śabdasya-rājaśvaśurādityādāviccatisya rājāśabdasya, svam - svīyārthaviśiṣyam, rūpam | uccaritaśabdaniṣṭhānurvīvisiṣṭavarṇasadṛśavarṇaghaṭitaśabdasvarūpam, saṃjñā - bodhyaṃ saṃjñāpadamatra karmaṇi aṅantaṃ, svapadaṃ svīyārtharūpam | rūpapadamānupūrvīviśeṣaviśiṣṭaśabdaparaṃ bhāṣyatastathālābhāt iti | evaṃ ca--`rījaśvaśurāt' ityādau arthayuktarājādipadādeva yadādipravṛttiḥ | na tu inādipadebhyaḥ, nāpi nirarthakebhya iti arthavatparibhāṣāpyanenaiva siddhyatīti tātparyam |
atha vārtikabhāṣyamatam-arthopasthitau padajñānaṃ kāraṇamiti sarvasaṃpratipannam | tataśca pajārtopasthitihetutayā jñātasya śabdasya pratyāsannatvena taṃ parityajya viprakṛ ṣṭaśabdāntaragrahaṇe vinigamakābhāvātparyādīnāṃ taṃ parityajya viprakṛṣṭasabdāntaragrahaṇe vinihamakābhāvātparyāyādīnāṃ na grahaṇamiti kāraṇatayopasthitarājādipadādeva yadādikāryaṃ pravartate | inādiśabdebhyo yadādyadarśanameva pratyāsattinyāyā8yaṇe mānamiti sūtraṃ nārambhaṇīyamiti kātyāyanapatañjalī manyete | tathā ca vārtikabhāṣye--`na vā śabdapūrvako jharthe saṃpratyayaḥ tasmādarthas‌ya nivṛttiḥ' iti vārtikam | `na vā etatprayojanamasti, kiṃ kāraṇam, śabdapūrvako jharthe saṃpratyayaḥ, atasca śabdapūrvaḥ, yo'pi jhasāvāhūyate nāmnā, nāma ca yadānena nopalabdaṃ bhavati, tadā pṛcchati--kiṃ bhavānāheti, śabdapūrvakaścāsya saṃpratyayaḥ' iti bhāṣyam | ayamarthaḥ--etat-śabdamātrasya saṃjñāpratiṣedhārthatvam (paryāyādivyāvṛttyarthatvam) vārtike hu śabdo lokasitvadyotaka iti kṛtvā, anvayadṛṣṭāntena tadāha --- śabdapūrvako hīti | arthasaṃpratyayaḥ śabdajñānapūrvaka iti lokasiddha ityarthaḥ ukte'rthe vyatirekamāha-ātasceti ātaḥ tadārthamāha yo'pūti | ayamarthaḥ - yo'sau puruṣo loke kenacidāhūyamāna āhvāyakapuruṣoccaritasya śabdasya samyaganupalambhe kiṃ bhavānāheti inuyuṅkte iti | anena sabdajñānabhāve'rthapratyayo neti vyatirekadṛṣṭānta uktaḥ | itīti, hātāvayamitiḥ, ityanvayavyatirekadṛṣṭāntasadbhāvādetorityartaḥ | asya-upasabdhuḥ, saṃpratyayaḥ - jāyamāno'rthapratyayaḥ | śabdapūrvakaṣca - śabdajñānapūrvaka eva | pratijñātaṃ śabdajñānapūrvakatvamevānepasaṃhṛtam | etena idaṃ bhā,yaṃ yuktyantarabodhakaṃ śabdaviṣeṣaṇakamityarthakamityuktiḥ cintyā |. akṣaramaryādayā tādṛśārthālābhāt | yathā arthadhiyaḥ śabdajñānapūrvakatvamupapāditaṃ bhāṣyakṛtā tathā śabdaviśiṣaṇakatvasyānupādanena tādṛśārthakatvakalpanasyāyuktatvāt | na ca śabdasya prakāratayā bhānābhyupagama eva, ghaṭaḥ kalaśaityādito'bhedabodho bhavati, anyathā avaccedakadharmabhedābhāvena sa na syātad, dharmitāvacchedakatāsaṃvandhena taddharmāvacchannābhedabuddhiṃ prati taddharmabhedaḥ kāraṇamiti vyutpatteriti vācyam, `kākaḥ kākaḥ pikaḥ pikaḥ' ityādānina bodopapatteḥ1 | na caivaṃ lakṣaṇāprasaṃgo doṣa iti vācyam, vaiyākaraṇamate lakṣaṇāprasaṃgo doṣa iti vācyam, vaiyākaraṇamate lakṣaṇaiva neti mañjīṣādāvaktatvāt |
kiṃ ca śābde śabdabhānavādo sāravyākhyāne darpaṇe harivallabheaina `atra vadanti' ityādinā śaktivāde vistareṇa nirākṛtaḥ | kiṃ ca paraparārthayorabhedavādino'sya mate ubhayorekatvenaiva bhānāt viseṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvaḥ kathamupapadyeta |`tayorbhedo vāstavaḥ, abhedastvāropita' iti mañajūṣāyāmupakrame nirūpitatvena abhedo.़pi na vāstavaḥ | avāstavo'bhedaḥ kathaṃ śabdasaṃvalitasyaivārthasya bhānamityarthavyavasthāpako bhavet | kiṃ ca yasya tayostādṛśo'dhyavasāyaḥ tasāya tathābodhe'pi tadīyatādṛśādhyavasānānyeṣāṃ tathābodha iti vaktuṃ na śakm, ekasya bhrantatve anyairapi tathābhavitavyamityatra niyāmakābhāvāt | kiṃ ca prakāratayā bhānaṃ prati padanirūpitavṛttijanyopastitiḥ kāraṇaṃe na tu abhebuddhiḥ, saṃjñā'nukaraṇobhayātiriktasthale śabdasya vṛttijanyopasthitiviṣayatā nāstīti suprasuddham | kiṃ ca `śabdamātramarthasvarūpaṃ saṃbhavati śabdārthayorbhedāt' iti `bhāvaṃ tu bādarāyaṇaḥ' (bra.sū.1.3.33) iti sūtre śaṅkarācāryaiḥ, `svarūpabhedāt, upāyabhedāt, arthakriyābhedācca śabdārthayorabhedo na yuktaḥ' ityādinā `akṣaramambarāntadhṛteḥ' (bra.sū.1.3.10) iti sūtre nācaspatimiśraiśca śabdārthayorabhedasya dūṣitatvācca nābhedoo yuktisahaḥ | śaktivāde darpaṇe śabdārthayorabhedavādaḥ `tattu na sādhīya' ityādinā bahudhā dūṣitaḥ | kiṃ ca `mantravidevāsmi nātmavit, yadvai kiṃ caitadadhyagīṣṭhā nāmaivaitat' (chā 7.1.3) iti nāradasanatkumārasaṃvāde ṛgādinikhilanidyāvedino.़pi nāradasya, ātmajñānābhāvasya vedapuru,eṇa bodhitatvena sabdārthayorbheda eva śrutisiddha iti padapadārthayostādātmyaṃ śrutiviruddham | yadi tayostādātmyaṃ tarhi sati śabdajñānaṃ ātmajñānamapi jātamiti śakyaṃ vaktimiti `nātmavit' ityuktiḥ `nāmaivaitat' iti sāvadhāraṇoktiś‌ca na saṃgaccheta | `abhedastvāropita' at nāgeśādyuktyaiva sa neti spaṣṭaṃ sarveṣām |
nanu padapadārthayostādātmya eva `kiṃ yattatsāsnā......sa śabda' iti prasvabhāṣyopapattiḥ, anyathā `viṣāṇyartharūpaṃ sa śabda' iti praśnopasaṃhāro'nupapanna eva syāditi cet, na | loke `gaurayaṃ, śuklo'yam' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśadarśanena tayostādātmyaṃ manyamānasya praśnānuvādarūpatvenāsya bhāṣyasya śabdārthayostādātmyaṃ manyamānasya praśnānuvādarūpatnenāsya bhāṣyasya śabdārthayostādātmye tātparyābhāvāt | `netyāha dravyaṃ nāma tat' iti samādhānabhā,yeṇāpi tādātmyajñānameva nivāryate | tataśca praṣṭustādātmyabhramo vārito bhāṣyakṛtā | evameva `yatarhi' ityādi praśnasamādhānabhā,yatrayatātparyamiti na śabdārthayostādātmyaṃ bhāṣyakṛtsaṃmam | `loke gaurayaṃ śuklo'yam' ityādau gopadavācyo'yaṃ, śuklapadavācyo'yamityevārthaḥ na tu gopadābhinna ityavadheyam1 | yadapi ma़ñjūṣāyāmuktam--
udeti savitā tāmrastāmra evāstameti ca |
saṃpattau ca vipattau ca mahatāmekarūpatā ||
ityatra `tāmrapadaṃ vinā va tādṛśo bodhaḥ, padaprakārakabodāditi prakāsakṛt' iti | na tadyuktam, tatra `tāmrapadam' ityādi vākyasyaivānupalambhāt | tatratyaprakāśagranthataḥ `padaprakārakabodha' ityarthasyālābhācca | tathā hi--tatra tu `atra rakta evāstametīti yadi kriyate tadā padāntarapratipāditaḥ sa evārtho'rthantaratayeva pratibhāsamānaḥ pratīti sthagayati' iti vākyam | sthagayatītyasya niruṇaddhi itadyartaḥ | jhaṭiti notpādayati pratītim | nirodhasca visambonopasthitijananam | ata eva pradīpe `pratītiṃ vyavadadhīta' iti prakāśagkantho vyākyātaḥ | prakāśapradīpayorayamabhiprāyaḥ - yathā pūrvavākye yena padena pratipāditaḥ yo'rthaḥ, sa evārto vākyāntare'pi tenaiva padena pratipāditaḥ, jhaṭiti pratītiviṣayo bhavati, na tathā padāntareṇa pratipāditaḥ so'rthaḥ, kiṃ tu vilambona, anya iva pratītatvāt | tādṛsapratītanivṛttyuttaraṃ sa evārtha iti pratītirjāyata iti vilambaḥ | anyatvapratītau padāntaraprayogo hetuḥ paryāyatvajñānaṃ tannivṛttau kāraṇam | vilambamasahamānaḥ kavistadeva padaṃ punaḥ prāyuṅkta iti |
ata eva--evevāśabdau prāyuñjātāmubho, yadi padaprakārakatvaṃ prakāśakṛdiṣṭaṃ syāt tadā raktaśabdaprayoge tasya prakāratayā bhāne arthasya bhinnatvena `sa eva' ityuktiranya ivetyuktiścāsaṃgatā syāt, prakārabhedāt anyatvasya spaṣṭatvāt | kiṃ ca ekarūpatāyāmutsekānutsekayorabhāvo hetuḥ, na tu padaprakārakatvam | saṃpadi vipadi ca utsekādyabhāva eva mahatāmekarūpateti mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇamiti vadanti vipaṣcitaḥ | kiṃ ca bodhe padaprakārakatvāṅkīkāre punarukterdooṣatvoktirna saṃgacchate | samānārthapadāntaraprayoge hi punaruktiriti kāvyaprakāśakārādayaḥ | yadi nāgeśoktadiśā padasya prakāratvaṃ tadabhimataṃ, tadā avacchedakabhedādarthasya bhedena samānārthakatvābhāvāttasya doṣatvāprasaktiḥ | kiṃ ca `uktārthānāmaprayoga' iti bahusaṃmato nyāyo'pi nirviṣayatāmāpadyeta | tattacchabdasya prakāratve tattadarthasya bhedāduktārthakaśabdasyaiva daurlabhyāt | etena `svaṃ rūpam' iti sūtśekhare `tajjanyabodhe prakāratayā bhāsamānas‌ya rājan śabdsya' ityādyuktiḥ, `śabdāliṅgitārthasyaiva bhāvāt' iti pa़camīvibhaktyarthavicāre mañjūṣotkiḥ, anyeṣāṃ śābdikānāmīdṛśārthakoktisca tathārthakārṣamūlavacanābhāvāccintyā | nanu bodhasya padaprakārakatvābhāve ghaṭapadaśaktigrahavataḥ kalaśa ityanuvyavasāyapraṃsaṃgaḥ padārthasyaikatvāt | padasya prakāratve tu ghaṭakalaśapadārthayorbhedāt na tathāvidhānuvyavasāyaprasaṃga iti cet, ucyate -- svāvyavahitottaratvaniveśena paryāpadādbodhavāraṇavat tādṛśānuvyavasāyo'pi suśakavāraṇa iti | kiṃ ca ghaṭapadaśaktigrahavataḥ taduttarakṣaṇe kalaśa ityanuvyavasāyo na kasyāpi kadācidapi bhavati | tādṛśānuvyavasāyaṃ prati tatpadaśaktigrahasya kāraṇatvena prakṛte tadabhāvāt |
nanu `arthaṃvat śabdo'pi vṛttiviṣaya' iti `kṛttaddhita' iti sūtraratnagranthe śabdasyāpi śakyatvokteḥ tadasyāpi prakāratayā bhānaṃ yuktaṃ śakyopasthitasyārthasakya kathaṃcidbhānāvaśyakatvāditi cet, na tasya granthasyānukaraṇasthale śabdaḥ śakya ityarthāt | kiṃ ca śabdas‌ya prakāratvena bhāne tattacchabdaprakāratattadarthasya bhedāvasyaṃbhāvena samānārthaṃkaśabdasyaivābhāvenana `ekārtānāmapi virahūpāṇām' iti vārtikaṃ tadbhāṣyam, sūtrākṣaraireva vārtikārthalābhaprakāraprakarśikamanoramāditaṃ cāsaṃbhavaduktikaṃ syāt |
na so'sti pratyayo loke yaḥ śabdānugamādṛte |
anuviddhamiva jñānaṃ sarvaṃ śabdena bhāsate ||
grājhatvapaṃ gākāhakatvaṃ ca dve śaktī tejaso yathā |
tathaiva sarvaśabdānāmete pṛthagavasthite ||
ityādi tu harīvacanaṃ na trimunivacanamiti anārṣamiti vibhāvanīyam ṣa haruvacane ivetyukteḥ śabdānuviddhatvaṃ vāstavamiti pratīyate | anuviddhameveti kalāyāṃ sāvadhāraṇavyākhyānaṃ tu cintyameva | tathāvyākhyāne bhāṣyādyanugrahābhāvāt | ato na śabdaprakārakatvaṃ śābdasyeti vaktuṃ śakyam | grājhatvamanukaraṇasthale, ghakatvaṃ taditaratra astītyetāvanmātre tejodṛṣṭāntaḥ, na tu ekatra yugapadubhayasattve iti vatadatātparyam | kiṃ ca svaṃ rūpamiti sūtra eva `mantrādisahacarito yo'rtha' iti bhāṣyapratīkamādāya `sāhacaryaṃ ca śabdānuviddhasyaiva' iti pratīkamādāya `na so'sti iti haryuṃkteriti bhāva' iti kaiyaṭaṃ vyākyāya `tadbodyatvena sahacaritatvamityanye' iti bhāṣyabhāvamanyathā'varṇayat | atredamavadheyam-kaiyaṭoktārthe haruvacanaṃ pramāṇamupanyasya tatrārthe'viśvāsena bhāṣyasya bhāvāntaraṃ varṇayatā, śabdānuviddhatvaṃ na munitrayābhimatamityuktaṃ bhavati | tatra tathāvadan mañjūṣāyāṃ (1.pa) śaktivāde sābde, pratyayasāmānye ca, śabdāvanuviddhatvaṃ kataṃ siddāntayatīti vābhāvyaṃ sūribhiḥ |
pratyaye bhāsate śabda iti novāca bhāṣyakṛt |
na so'stipratyaya iti vacanaṃ tu na pāṇineḥ ||
atastadanusāreṇa yaḥ śābde śabdabhāsanam |
vadatyayaṃ pāṇinīyavarge na gaṇanāṃ bhajet ||
viralpalya avāltavatvakalpatvanirāsaḥ
yadatra ma़ñjūṣāyāmabhihitaṃ vastutattve vikalpāyogena prākipadikāryaviṣaye `ekaṃ dvikaṃ trikaṃ ceti catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ vastuni anekapakṣopanyāso vyavahāropapādakarārthānāmanāstavatvaṃ sūcitam' iti granthena vastunāmavāstavatvamiti | atrocyate--tathā - tathābhūte brajhaṇi āsaṃsāraṃ prasiddhānubhave bagavati bhave saṃdehaḥ kuta' iti kusumāñjalāvudayanācāryeṇa grantārambhe kṛtanānākoṭikalpānuvādena---
yaṃ saivāḥ samupāsate śiva iti brajheti vedāntino
buddhā bauddha iti pramāṇakuśaloḥ karteti naiyāyikāḥ |
arhannityatha jainaśāsanaratāḥ karmeti mīmāṃsakāḥ
so'yaṃ vo vidadhātu vāñchitaphalaṃ trailokyanātho hariḥ ||
iti kenacidvidvatkaviśekharatilakakṛtahariviṣayakaśivatvādiprakārakaviralpānuvādena, tattvabhūte brajhaṇi brajha jījñāsamānānāṃ saguṇātvanirguṇatvādivikalpodayena, loke purovartini padārthe `stāṇurvā puruṣo vā' ityādi vikalpodayena ca, sāstrakāraiḥ svasamīhitamarthaṃ siddhāntayitu tadaṅgatayā vikalpollekhanena, sūtrakṛtā pāṇinā `avah sphoṭāyanasya'(pā.sū.6.1.123) ityādi sūtreṣu prācāmācāryāṇāṃ nāmanirdesena avahādikāryeṣu nānāpakṣasūcanena, `vipradiṣedheparaṃkāryam' (pā.sū.1.4.2) ityatra bhāṣyaṃ vidhiniyamapakṣayorubhayorupanyāsena, dharmāśāstreṣu kāladeśavayo'vasthābhedena gurulaghudaṇḍaprāyaścitvidānena, pūrvatantre vrīhiyavapakṣayoḥ ṣoḍaśigrahaṇāgrahaṇapakṣayorupanyāsena, vaidyake ekasya rogasyānekacikitsāpradarśanena, gītāyāṃ dvādaśe saṃsārasāgarasamuttāraṇasamarthabhagavatprasādasaṃpādakabhagavaccittatvādyanekopāyapradarśanena ca ekatra viṣaye vikalpo vividhapakṣāścāpanyasanīyāḥ sāstrakāraiḥ svasiddhāntastāpanāyetyetat acāryaśailīti sarveṣāṃ viditacarameva | tatra kvāpi vi,yāṇāmanāstavatvānubhavo nodetikasyāt, evāstavānāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ vicārānarhatvena tadvicāraparaśāstrasyaiva vaiyarthyaṃ syāt | na dṛśyate hi loke prekṣakapūrvakārī avāstavasaśaviṣāṇavicārāya pravartamānaḥ purūṣaḥ |
nanu ekatvādiviralpaḥ prātipadikārthaviṣayaka iti tannaṣṭho bhavati na tu vācakatvaniṣṭhaḥ atohatoḥ pakṣavṛttitvābhāvena vācakatvāvāstavatvarūpasādyasādhako na syāditi doṣaḥ kasmānnokta iti na śaṅkyam | vikalpaviṣayārthanirūpitatvasaṃbandhena vācakatvaṃ kimekārthanirūpitaṃ vā, arthadvayanirūpitaṃ veti vikalpaparyavasāne na vikalpasya vācakatvavṛttitvaṃ susaṃpādamiti samādātuṃ śakyamiti sa noktaḥ |
idaṃ bodyam -- uktasthaleṣu, `aṅkaṃ ke'pi śaśaṅ‌kire'1 ityatra śyāmadharmiikāṅkatvādyullekhe `agnihotraṃ juhoti' ityādau kimagnipadaṃ karmanāmadheyam, uta agnirūpadevatāparam, ākaśaśabdaḥ kiṃ prasiddhākāśaparaḥ, atha paramātmaparaḥ iti karmabrajhamīmāṃsakānāṃ vyavahāre, `sthāṇurvā puruṣo vā' ityādilaukikavyavahāre ca, kasyacidapi kasmiṃścidapi padārthe'vāstatvajñānānudayena vikalpādikaṃ na vastuno'vastavatvasādakam | sāstrakārāsca svasvaśāstreṣvavāstavamartaṃ na pratipādayanti | kiṃ tu prakārāntaraṃ prakṛte na saṃbhavatūtyeva tatra tatra dhīmatāṃ dhīrudeti | śrīvyāsapāṇinī api kvāpisvatantre'vāstavamarthaṃ noktavantau, ato māyikamevārthaṃ vyāsapāṇinī abrūtāmiti vacanaṃ prāmāṇikairna śraddeyamityalam |
vikalpavi,ye loke'sattvabuddhirna jāyate |
ato vikalpo vastūvaॉnāṃ nāsattvaṃ sādhayediti ||
vadanti vastutattvajñāstasmādekatra vastuni |
vikalpaltadasattvasya kalpane na kṣamo bhavet ||
ārṣavacasāmapāramārthikatvotinirāsaraḥ
yadibhidhīyate mañjūṣāyāṃ `yadyapi ṛṣīṇāṃ darśavaṃ tattvaviṣayaṃ bhavati, tattvabhūtena vastunā vyavahārāsaṃbhavāt vyavahārakole te'dṛṣṭatattvasadṛśā1 eva nānāprakārairvyavaharanti,2 iti na tadvacasāpi padapadārthasatyatvaṃ mantavyam' iti, asyā'kṣarārthaṃ vyākyānato'vagamya saṃtoṣṭavyam tadīyaireva | anena ca sarve ārṣā grantā apāramārthikavastupratipādakā iti manyate nāgeśa iti vedituṃ prabhavanti sarve | ārṣāṇāmevaivadhatvaṃ, kiṃ vaktavyamaṃ rauruṣeyagkanthānām | evaṃ ca sarve'pi grantā apāramārthikā eveti tadbhāvaḥ | atrocyace-atra darśanapadena jñānagrahaṇe jñātārthavirūddārthapratipādakatvātte,āṃ granthasya āptimūlakatvābhāvenāgkājhatvaṃ syāt | yadi tena padena tadīyaśāstrasya grahaṇe tu ekasyaiva darśanasya tattvabhūtavastuviṣayakatvamathābhūtavastuviśayakatvamityetadviruddham | na ca brajha satyam, taditare'satyā iti tadgranthatātparyamiti na virodha iti vācyam tattadyarśane tādṛśoktyadarthanāt | yatra darśane brajha pratipādyate, tatra brajhātiriktatadyarśavasādhanani asatyānīti na pratipāditam | jaimitīyādidarśaneṣu brajhanirūpaṇameva neti evaṃ vibhāgāyogaḥ | ataḥ sarveṣu śāstreṣu pratipāditāni sarvāṇyapi tasyānyeva | kiṃ ca manuyājñavalkyaprabhṛtumunipraṇītasmṛtūnām, śrīmadrāmāyaṇabhāratayoritihāsayoḥ, śrībhāgavatādipurāṇānām, bodāyanāpastaṃbakātyāyanāśvalāyanagobhilaprabhṛtimaharṣipraṇītakalpasūtrāṇām, kapitapatañjasiprabhṛtipraṇītasāṃkhyayogādīnām, vākyapadīyasya, paramārthasāragranthasya, carakasya, pātañjalamahābhāśaṣyasya, tattadanuyāyimaharṣidesyatattadgranthavyākhyātṛvacavanānāṃ ca atattvārthapratipādakatvaṃ vyavasthāpya, tādṛśabhāratabhāgavataharuvaṃśamānavādismṛtugranthagatāni vacavāvi svasamīhitasphoṭasvarūpādisādhane pramāṇatayopanyasya sphoṭādiprameyajātaṃ sādhayatīti citrametat | idaṃ bodhyam--- tulyanyāyena māmakasyāpi granthasya apārāmārāthikapratipādakatā syāt, niścite tathā māmakīne atattvāvedakapramāṇamūlate grante kathaṃ prekṣāvatāṃ prāmāṇyabuddhirudayet, kathaṃ vā vacane mama vistrambhaḥ pramaṇāṇyaṃ, svasminnāptatvabuddhiḥ, upādeyatābuddhiści svagranthe, kathaṃ vā māṃ sabrajhacāriṇaḥ paṇḍitamatallikā madhyesabhaṃ paṇḍitagaṇe paṭheyurutyanālocyaiva, eva mavādīdayamiti sukṛtino nirṇayanti | sarve,āmṛṣivacanānāpāramārthikārthapratipādakatve ṛṣīṇāmasatyavāditve ca brajhasphoṭa pratipādakānāṃ granthānām, ātmaikatvapratipādakaparamārthasārasya carakādeśca tathātvena brajhādīnāṃ kathaṃ pāramārthikatvasiddhirasya mata iti vibhāvyam |
ṛṣivākyaṃ māyikārthadhamṛṣayo'satyavādinaḥ |
ityevaṃ vadatā'satyavādivākyabalādayam ||
sādhitaḥ sphoṭarūpo'rthaḥ kathaṃ prāmāṇiko bhavet |
samājaṃ munivaryāṇāṃ dūṣayanneṣa paṇḍitaḥ ||
iti pramāṇaśaraṇaiḥ suvibhāvyaṃ sudhījanaiḥ |
kathaṃ madhyesabhaṃ vidvadgaṇe'gaṇyata kovidaiḥ ||
mahatāṃ bhrāntatvoktinirāsaḥ

yadatra mañjūṣāyāṃ `navyanaiyāyikagranthe prakṛtipratyayārthavicāro vyākaraṇavāsanāvāsitāntaḥkaraṇatayā tatra vāstavatvabhrameṇeti bodhyam' iti granthena, pāṇineranyeṣāma cāvāstavaprakṛtipratyayārthaprativādakatvam, tadupajīvya tadarthavicāre pravṛttānāṃ maṇidīdhitiprabhṛti granthakṛtāṃ gaṅgeśopādhyāyaraghunāthabhaṭṭācāryaprabhṛtīnāṃ bhrāntatvam, `tade vibhaktyantāḥ' `padaṃ vyaktyākṛtijātayaḥ padārthaḥ' ityādinā gautamena prakṛtipratyayārthānāmavāstavatvaṃ dhvanitamapi teṣāṃ svagranthe'vāstavatvānuktyā teṣāṃ svācāryoktāvaviśvāsam, taduktitātparyānabhijñatvaṃ ca prakāśayati | `upadeśe'janunāsika' ityatra karaṇe ghañā vārtikārthalābhaprakāraṃ prakṛtipratyayārthavcāraṃ ca darśaऱyatāṃ bhāṣyakṛtādīnāmapi bhrānātatvaṃ sūcayati nāgeśa- | atra vaktavyam--- `vṛddhirādaic' iti sūtre bhagavān bhāṣyakāraḥ saṃbhāvitaprayoganiyamādipakṣaṃ nikasya `saṃjñāsaṃjñbhāvo'pi sāmarthyadeva siddhyatīti saṃjñādhikāraṃ kurvantaṃ vararuciṃ bodhayan, `pramāṇabhūta ācāryo darbhapavitrapāṇiḥ śucāvavakāśe prāṅmukha upavisya mahatā prayatnena sūtrāṇi praṇayati sma, tatrā'śakyaṃ varṇenāpyathakena bhanituṃ, kiṃ punariyatā sūtreṇaṭa iti bruvāṇaḥ, `pramāṇabhūta' ityanena ācajāryasya yathārthavaktutvādiguṇaṃ, pratārakatvādidoṣābhāvaṃ, `darbhapavitrapāṇiḥ' ityādinā paramāstikatvam, `mahatā prayatnena' ityanena unmādādyavasthārūpadoṣbhāvaṃ, prastutakāryaivyagracitttvam, śāstrasya ca pratipāditaguṇanadācāryapraṇītatvena ādāvuktarītyā1 mahāprayojanavattvam, tasmai prayodanāya sarvairavaśyopādeyatvapaṃ ca kūcayan sūtramidaṃ `daśadāḍimāni ṣaḍapūpā' utyādivat na nirarthako parṇarāśiḥ, ataḥ anyeṣāṃ akṣāṇāmasaṃbhavāt, sāmānādhikaraṇyena nirdeśācca ādaico vṛddhisaṃjñakā bhavantīti vākyārthaḥ paryavasyatīti sūtrakṛta ācāryasyāśayamāviṣkṛtya sūtrasya nairarthakyaśaṅkā pariharatisma | etādṛśaṃ pāṇinīyaṃ śāstramavāstavārthapratipādakamiti vadituṃ ko vā prabhavet |
evameva sarve-maharṣayaḥ tadanuyāyino'nye cācāryāḥ prāṇabhṛtsu paramānugraheṇa `athā'taḥ sāmayācāryikān dharmān vyāśyāsyāmaḥ, athā'to darśapūrṇamāsau vyākyāsyamaḥ' ityādibhiḥ śāstroktabrājhaṇyādiparamaprayojanalābhāya vāstavaśrautasmārtadharmānupadiśantyā pastambādaya iti tattadvyākhyātṛvacanaiḥ sarve'pi jānantyeva | kadācidapi te maharṣayastatkalpās‌ca avāstavamarthaṃ vāntīti na vadeyuḥ śabdapramāṇakāḥ | nāgeśastāvat vākyapadīyaparamārthasārādyanārṣagranthasantatapariśīlanajanyasaṃskārakaluṣitacetāḥ niraṅkuśo mahatāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ vacanamakhilamavāstavārthapratipādakam, tadanusāreṇa visayaṃ vyavasthāpayatāṃ bhrantatvaṃ vadan bhrānta eva bhrāntajanairmahanīyaḥ | na hi loke kaścitprekṣāpūrvakārī avāstavamarthaṃ pratipādayituṃ mahāntaṃ yatnamāsthito dṛsyate | āpi loke kascidvinekī janaḥ tatsādhanaparamātmānugrahakāmyayā cirakālaṃ tapasyati | naiva dṛsyate loke kavilokaḥ kascit śaśaviṣāṇādikaṃ avāstavamarthaṃ māmāṃsamānaḥ | nanu bhoḥ kimevaṃ vadanti bhavantiḥ, aindrajālikā avāstavamevārthaṃ darśayituṃ kṣudradevatāmupāsate yatante ca tadarthaṃ viseṣeṇa | tadviṣayakavcāraṃ ca vitanvate iti sarvalokaviditamidamiti cet, ucyate-aindrajālikena pradarśitaṃ vastu nāvāstavamitipañcaśikhācāryā vadantīti pūrvaṃ nirūpitam | acirakālatvamātraṃ tasya nāvāstavatvamityanye'pyācārya manyante | aindrajālikena samārādhitadevānugrahasaṃpāditaśaktiviśeṣeṇa samānīya dattaṃ phalādi bhakṣayanti janā ityarthakriyākāritvaṃ ca tasyeti nāvāstavatvamityeke iti |
kiṃ ca `nāmarūpe satyaṃ tābhyāmayaṃ prāṇaḥ chatraḥ, prāṇā vai satyaṃ te,āmeṣa satyam' iti śrutyantare brajhātirariktamastanāmarūpaprapañecajātasya vikāravargasya satyatvokteḥ nāgesektamayuktam | na ceyaṃ śrukireṣā bhūmavidyāyāṃ gautamādīnāṃ vākyānyudāhṛtyaḥ- ma़jūṣāyāṃ tatra tatra pramāṇatayā gautamādīnāṃ vākyānyudāhṛtya anenāvāstatvaṃ sūcitavān| dhvanayati, dhvanitamityādinā vyaṅgyamaryādayā gautamādīnāṃ vastuno'vāstavatve nāstītyeva tātparyamamiti varṇanāt tadīyeṣu grantheṣu tādṛśārthakaṃ vastunovāstavatve tātparyamiti varṇanāt tadīyeṣu grantheṣu tādṛśārthakaṃ spa,ṭavacanaṃ nāstītyeva prakṛtipratyayārthānāmavāstavatvaṃ dvanitamityetanna yuktamiti yathābhā,yaṃ tadīyasūtrārthanirūpaṇena nirūpayāmaḥ | tatā hi -- `ce vibhāktyantāḥ padam' (nyā.sū.1-a. 2-ā.60) `vyaktyākṛtijātisaṃndhau upapacārāt saṃśayaḥ' (1.2.61) iti sūtrakramaḥ | atra pūrvaprakaraṇena varṇānāṃ nityatvānityatvaṃ vicārya padasvarūpapadaśakyanirūpaṇāya idaṃ sūtradvayaṃ pravṛttam | astu1 varṇānāṃ nityatvamanityatvaṃ vā, padānyeva lore'rthabodanāya prayujyante | kiṃ tallakṣaṇamiti jijñāāsāyāṃ subantaṃ tiṅantaṃ ca padamiti tallakṣaṇamiityādyasūtrātparyam | dvitīyasya tu jātyādiṣu vācyatvaṃ nirṇetuṃ kiṃ sarvaṃ sarvatra vācyam, uta anyatamamiti saṃsayotpādakamiti tātparyamiti tadbhāṣyavido vadanti | idaṃ ca tatra bhā,yam-te yatādarśanaṃ vikṛtā varṇā vibhaktyantāḥ padasaṃrajñā bhavanti (60), tadarthe - avinābhāvavṛttiḥ saṃnidhiḥ, avinābhāvena vartamānāsu vyaktyākṛtijātiṣu gauriti prayujyate, tatra na jñāyate kimanyatamaḥ padārthaḥ, uta sarva (61) iti | atra sūtrabhā,yābhyāṃ prakṛtityārtasyā'vāstavatvaṃ kataṃ dhvanitamiti vibhāvayantu sudhuyaḥ | na ca vikṛtā ityasya vikāraṃ prāptā ityarthena vikārasāmānyasāyāvanāstavatvamiti naiyāyikānāṃ siddhāntābhāvāt tatsiddhāntānusāreṇaiva hi tadgranthārtho varṇanīyaḥ . anyathā so'rtho gautamābhimataḥ kataṃ syāt | vastutaḥ śāstravihitaukārādikāryaviśiṣṭā iti tadarthaḥ |
prakṛtipratyayārtheṣu vāstavatvadhiyā yadi |
kurvanvicāraṃ bhrāntaḥ syāt bhavedbhrāntasca bhāṣyakṛt ||
tadvicāraṃ prakurvāṇo bhrānta eva bhavānapi |
bhrāntoktiṃ ca samāśritya bhrāntena bhavatā kṛtaḥ ||
siddhāntaḥ kathamādarthumarhaḥ syāditi cintyatām |
kānane candrikeva syānnaṣphalo viddhi te śramaḥ ||

pāñcarātravcāraḥ
yaccoktaṃ mañjūṣāyāṃ proktārthakataddhitārthavicāre---
atyantamalinānāṃ tu prāṇināṃ vedamargataḥ |
pāñcarātrādayo mārgāḥ kālenaivopakārakāḥ ||
iti sūtrasaṃhitāvacanavyākyāvasare vedamārgahīnānāmatyantamalinānāṃ pāñcarātrādayo mārga iti udutānudutahomavadadhikāribhedena prāmāṇyaṃ teṣāmiti . atrocyate - evaṃvidārthakavacanasya pāṇinitantre'nupalambhāt bhāṣya evaṃvidhavyavasthā'darsanāccāpāṇinīyamidam | bhagavān vyāso'pi mahābhārate sāntiparvaṇi---
pāñcarātrasya kṛtsnasya vaktā tu bhagavān svayam |
pāñcarātravido ye tu yathākramaparā nṛpa |
ekāntabhānavopagatāste hariṃ praviśanti vai || (359)
ityādinā vaktā tu pāñcarātrasya bhagavān nārayaṇaḥ, tanmārganiṣṭhāstu bhagavata ekāntabhaktā bhavanto, bhagavato niratiśayaparamapremabhājanabhūtā labhante'bhīṣṭaṃ phalamiti pratipādayan, uparicarādayo viśuddātmāno vedāmārghābhiratāḥ sāttvataṃ (pāñcarātraṃ) vidhimāstāya bhagavantaṃ nārāyaṇamārādhayanta īpsitaṃ phalamalabhanteti itihāsakathanena,
śrutvā brajhamuṣādrudraḥ sa devyai kathayan punaḥ |
nārāyaṇānuśiṣṭā hi tadā devī sarasvatī |
viveśa tānṛṣīn sarvān lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā ||
(bhāra.śā.3.43)
ityādinā brajhādīnā proktṛtvaśrotṛtvanirūpaṇena vedavedināṃ tapasvināṃ maharṣīṇāṃ paramātmānugraheṇa pāñcarātre'dhikāriṇa iti spaṣṭamabravīt | tatra brajhadaya uparicarādayaśca malinā vedahīnāsceti kasya vā vāṇī vaktuṃ pravarteta | yatra kvāpi tantramidaṃ hīnādhikāramiti vāṇī yadi dṛśyate, sā tu mohanārtheti mantavyam, tatoktastu tatrā'satāṃ pravṛttirmābhūditi tātparyeṇa |
nanu `caturṣu vedeṣu paraṃ śreyo'labdhvā śāṇḍilya idaṃ śāstramadhigatavān' iti śāṇḍilyasya vedānādaroktiḥ vedanindanāvagamena pāñcarātraṃ hīnādhikārikamiti cet,ucyate--nātra śreyaso'prāptivacanaṃ vedānādaraṃ gamayati, kiṃ cu adhītasarvavedasyāpi na tāvanmātreṇa phalalābhaḥ, api tu
vedārthapūrakaṃ jñeyaṃ pāñcarātraṃ yato'kisam |
tatsaṃkṣepaśca gīteyaṃ tasmānnāsyāḥ samaṃ kvacit ||
iti brajhavaivarte,
vedena pāñcarātreṇa bhaktyā yajñena caiva hi |
dṛśyo'haṃ nānyathā dṛśyo varṣakoṭiśatairapi ||
iti vārāhe coktadiśā pāñcarātrābhyāsasahitenaiva vedacāreṇa phalaprāptiriti gamayati | ato'tra vedanindā pratīyate iti na śakyaṃ vaktum | `na hi nindā' iti nyāyena śāstre kṛtasamādānaṃ tu śaṅkatrāśayānurodena |
kiṃ ca vidyāntarādhigamanaṃ tadupadese vā na vidyāntaranindāyāṃ paryasyati, lokavedayoḥ tatādarśanāt | na hi loke vyākaraṇādhyayanopadeśau nyāyādinindātātparyakau dṛṣṭau | vede'pi na tathā dṛśyace | tathā hi-kāṭhake indrabharadvājasaṃvāde `ehi imaṃ viddhi | ayaṃ vai sarvavidyeti'1 | (kā.1.pra.43) `tasmai haitamangiṃ sāvitramuvāca'2 iti bhāradvājāyentrakṛtasāvitravidyopadeśo na sarve,ā vedānāṃ nindāṃ gamayati, bhāṣyakāraiśca tatra na tathā vyākyātam | chāndogye ca saptame nāradasanatkumārasaṃvāde sāṅgasarahasyasarvarūpaśreyo'lābhaśravaṇe'pi na tena vedādyayananindā gamyate | nīpi bhāṣyakārā ācāryāstathā vyācacakṣire | kiṃ ca---
(343.29)
vedaiscaturbhiḥ samitaṃ kṛtaṃ merau mahāgirau |
ṛgyajuḥ sāmabhirjuṃṣṭamatharvāṅgirasaistathā ||
idaṃ mahopaniṣadaṃ caturvedasamanvitam |
sāṃkyayogakṛtāntena pāñcarātrānuśabditam ||
nārāyaṇamukhodgītaṃ1... (348)
ityādinā bhārate śāntiparvaṇi sarvavedasahitasyaiva pāñcarātrasya ṛṣibhiḥ kṛtatvoktaḥ2
sarve pramāṇaṃ hi yathā tathā tacchāstramuttamam |
bhaviṣyati pramāṇaṃ vai etanmadanusāsanam || (344.41)
iti bhagavatsṛṣṭapadārthāntaraṇāmiva pāñcarātrasya prāmāṇyābhidhānācca, `bhārataḥ pañcamo vedaḥ' iti pañcamavedatvena smṛtabhārataviruddhavacanaṃ pramāṇamāsthāya pāñcarātrasya vedāmūlakatvenāprāmāṇyam, hīnādhiikārikatvena vāprāmāṇyamiti kalpanamayuktam | vedaviruddhasmṛteraprāmāṇyavat bhārataviruddhasūtramārganiṣṭhānāṃ vedahīnādhikārikatvamiti mañjūṣāyāṃ kṛtanirṇayaḥ, tattvakaustubhe tantrādhikāranirṇayaḥ, tattvakaustubhe tantrādhikāranirṇaye ca bhaṭṭojī dīkṣitakṛtaḥ patitādhikārikatvanirṇayaḥ, pāṣaṇḍitvanirṇayaśca, evaṃ jātīyako'nyakṛtanirṇayaśca nādartavyaḥ | bhārate'dhikāritayā nirdiṣṭānāṃ brajhādīnāmuparicarādīnāṃ, śaṇḍilyasya ca brajhabatyādipātityābhāvāt, vāsudevabhaktāgresaratayā teṣā pāṣaṇḍitvābhāvācca | `utpattyasaṃbhavāt' (bra.sū.2.2.42)ityādi catuḥsūtrīṃ pāñcarātraniṣedhaparatayā yojayadbhiḥ śrīśaṅkarācāryairupabṛṃhaṇatayā sūtavacanādīnāmanudāharaṇāt prāmāṇyasaṃdehācca | atha vā ye janāḥ nārāyaṇatadbhaktapradbeṣiṇāṃ pāñcarātre mahāśāstre sudṛḍhāmaprāmāṇyabuddhimutpādya tatrāpravṛttaye pravṛttāḥ pāñcarātrāprāmāṇyavādinyaḥ smṛtaya iti jānīhi |
